 1
yasyānandabhavena maṅgalakalāsaṃbhāvitena sphuradhāmnā siddharasāmṛtena karuṇāvīkṣāsudhāsindhunā |
bhaktānāṃ prabhavaprasaṃhṛtijarārāgādirogāḥ kṣaṇācchāntiṃ yānti jagatpradhānabhiṣaje tasmai parasmai namaḥ || 1.1 ||
ādimaścandrasenaśca laṅkeśaśca viśāradaḥ |
kapālī mattamāṇḍavyau bhāskaraḥ śūrasenakaḥ || 1.2 ||
ratnakośaśca śambhuśca sāttviko naravāhanaḥ |
indrado gomukhaścaiva kambalirvyāḍireva ca || 1.3 ||
nāgārjunaḥ surānando nāgabodhiryaśodhanaḥ |
khaṇḍaḥ kāpāliko brahmā govindo lampako hariḥ || 1.4 ||
saptaviṃśatisaṃkhyākā rasasiddhipradāyakāḥ |
rasāṅkuśo bhairavaśca nandī svacchandabhairavaḥ || 1.5 ||
manthānabhairavaścaiva kākacaṇḍīśvarastathā |
vāsudeva ṛṣiḥ śṛṅgaḥ kriyātantrasamuccayī || 1.6 ||
rasendratilako yogī bhālukī maithilāhvayaḥ |
mahādevo narendraśca vāsudevo harīśvaraḥ || 1.7 ||
eteṣāṃ kriyate'nyeṣāṃ tantrāṇyālokya saṃgrahaḥ |
rasānāmatha siddhānāṃ cikitsārthopayoginām || 1.8 ||
sūnunā siṃhaguptasya rasaratnasamuccayaḥ |
rasoparasalohāni yantrādikaraṇāni ca || 1.9 ||
śuddhyarthamapi lohānāṃ tantrādikaraṇāni ca |
śuddhiḥ sattvaṃ drutirbhasmakaraṇaṃ ca pravakṣyate || 1.10 ||
asti nīhāranilayo mahānuttaradiṅmukhe |
uttuṅgaśṛṅgasaṃghātalaṅghitābhro mahīdharaḥ || 1.11 ||
viśrāmāya viyanmārgavilaṅghanaghanaśramaḥ |
avatīrṇa iva kṣoṇīṃ śaradambumucāṃ gaṇaḥ || 1.12 ||
rāśirāśīviṣādhīśaphaṇāphalakarociṣām |
bhittvā bhuvamivottīrṇo yo vibhāti bhṛśonnataḥ || 1.13 ||
jvaladauṣadhayo yasya nitambamaṇibhūmayaḥ |
naktamuddāmataḍitāmanukurvanti vārmucām || 1.14 ||
kaṭake saṃcarantīnāṃ yasya kiṃnarayoṣitām |
pādeṣu dhāturāgeṇa lākṣākṛtyamanuṣṭhitam || 1.15 ||
avataṃsitaśītāṃśurācchāditadigambaraḥ |
yo guhādhigato lokairgirīśa iti gīyate || 1.16 ||
nimīlitadṛśo nityaṃ munayo yasya sānuṣu |
pratyakṣayanti giriśamavāṅmanasagocaram || 1.17 ||
śilātalapratihatairyasya nirjharaśīkaraiḥ |
ahanyapi nirīkṣante yakṣāstārāṅkitaṃ nabhaḥ || 1.18 ||
nīhārapavanodrekaniḥsahā yatra puruṣāḥ |
nijastrīṇāṃ niṣevante kucoṣmāṇaṃ nirantaram || 1.19 ||
saṃcaran kaṭake yasya nidāghe'pi divākaraḥ |
uddāmahimaruddhoṣmā na śītāṃśorvibhidyate || 1.20 ||
guhāgṛheṣu kastūrīmṛganābhisugandhiṣu |
gāyanti yatra kiṃnaryo gaurīpariṇayotsavam || 1.21 ||
cakāsti tatra jagatāmādidevo maheśvaraḥ |
rasātmanā jagattrātuṃ jāto yasmānmahārasaḥ || 1.22 ||
śatāśvamedhena kṛtena puṇyaṃ gokoṭibhiḥ svarṇasahasradānāt |
nṛṇāṃ bhavetsūtakadarśanena yatsarvatīrtheṣu kṛtābhiṣekāt || 1.23 ||
vidhāya rasaliṅgaṃ yo bhaktiyuktaḥ samarcayet |
jagattritayaliṅgānāṃ pūjāphalamavāpnuyāt || 1.24 ||
bhakṣaṇaṃ sparśanaṃ dānaṃ dhyānaṃ ca paripūjanam |
pañcadhā rasapūjoktā mahāpātakanāśinī || 1.25 ||
hanti bhakṣaṇamātreṇa pūrvajanmāghasambhavam |
rogasaṃghamaśeṣāṇāṃ narāṇāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 1.26 ||
pūrvajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ sadyo naśyati dehinām |
sugandhapiṣṭasūtena yadi śambhurvilepitaḥ || 1.27 ||
abhrakaṃ truṭimātraṃ yo rasasya parijārayet |
śatakratuphalaṃ tasya bhavedityabravīcchivaḥ || 1.28 ||
yaśca nindati sūtendraṃ śambhostejaḥ parātparam |
sa patennarake ghore yāvatkalpavikalpanā || 1.29 ||
rogibhyo yo rasaṃ datte śuddhipākasamanvitam |
tulādānāśvamedhānāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti śāśvatam || 1.30 ||
siddhe rase kariṣyāmi nirdāridryagadaṃ jagat |
rasadhyānamidaṃ proktaṃ brahmahatyādipāpanut || 1.31 ||
abhragrāso hi sūtasya naivedyaṃ parikīrtitam |
rasasyetyarcanaṃ kṛtvā prāpnuyātkratujaṃ phalam || 1.32 ||
udare saṃsthite sūte yasyotkrāmati jīvitam |
sa mukto duṣkṛtādghorātprayāti paramaṃ padam || 1.33 ||
mūrchitvā harati rujaṃ bandhanamanubhūya muktido bhavati |
amarīkaroti hi mṛtaḥ ko'nyaḥ karuṇākaraḥ sūtāt || 1.34 ||
suragurugodvijahiṃsāpāpakalāpodbhavaṃ kilāsādhyam |
śvitraṃ tadapi ca śamayati yastasmātkaḥ pavitrataraḥ sūtāt || 1.35 ||
rasabandha eva dhanyaḥ prārambhe yasya satatamitikaraṇā |
setsyati rase kariṣye mahīmahaṃ nirjarāmaraṇām || 1.36 ||
sukṛtaphalaṃ tāvadidaṃ sukule yajjanma dhīśca tatrāpi |
sāpi ca sakalamahītalatulanaphalā bhūtalaṃ ca suvidheyam || 1.37 ||
bhūtalavidheyatāyāḥ phalamarthāste ca vividhabhogaphalāḥ |
bhogāśca santi śarīre tadanityamato vṛthā sakalam || 1.38 ||
iti dhanaśarīrabhogānmatvānityānsadaiva yatanīyam |
muktau sā ca jñānāttaccābhyāsātsa ca sthire dehe || 1.39 ||
tatsthairye na samarthaṃ rasāyanaṃ kimapi mūlalohādi |
svayamasthirasvabhāvaṃ dāhyaṃ kledyaṃ ca śoṣyaṃ ca || 1.40 ||
kāṣṭhauṣadhyo nāge nāgo vaṅge'tha vaṅgamapi śulbe |
śulbaṃ tāre tāraṃ kanake kanakaṃ ca līyate sūte || 1.41 ||
amṛtatvaṃ hi bhajante haramūrtau yogino yathā līnāḥ |
tadvatkavalitagagane rasarāje hemalohādyāḥ || 1.42 ||
paramātmanīva satataṃ bhavati layo yatra sarvasattvānām |
eko'sau rasarājaḥ śarīramajarāmaraṃ kurute || 1.43 ||
sthiradehe'bhyāsavaśātprāpya jñānaṃ guṇāṣṭakopetam |
prāpnoti brahmapadaṃ na punarbhavavāsajanmaduḥkhāni || 1.44 ||
ekāṃśena jagadyugapadavaṣṭabhyāvasthitaṃ paraṃ jyotiḥ |
pādaistribhistadamṛtaṃ sulabhaṃ na viraktimātreṇa || 1.45 ||
na hi dehena kathaṃcidvyādhijarāmaraṇaduḥkhavidhureṇa |
kṣaṇabhaṅgureṇa sūkṣmaṃ tadbrahmopāsituṃ śakyam || 1.46 ||
nāmāpi dehasiddhe ko gṛhṇīyādvinā śarīreṇa |
yadyogagamyamamalaṃ manaso'pi na gocaraṃ tattvam || 1.47 ||
yajñāddānāttapaso vedādhyayanāddamātsadācārāt |
atyantabhūyasī kila yogavaśādātmasaṃvittiḥ || 1.48 ||
bhrūyugamadhyagataṃ yacchikhividyutsūryavajjagadbhāsi |
keṣāṃcitpuṇyadṛśāmunmīlati cinmayaṃ paraṃ jyotiḥ || 1.49 ||
paramānandaikarasaṃ paramaṃ jyotiḥsvabhāvamavikalpam |
vigalitasakalakleśaṃ jñeyaṃ śāntaṃ svasaṃvedyam || 1.50 ||
tasminnādhāya manaḥ sphuradakhilaṃ cinmayaṃ jagatpaśyan |
utsannakarmabandho brahmatvamihaiva cāpnoti || 1.51 ||
rāgadveṣavimuktāḥ satyācārā mṛṣārahitāḥ |
sarvatra nirviśeṣā bhavanti cidbrahmasaṃsparśāt || 1.52 ||
tiṣṭhantyaṇimādiyutā vilasaddehāḥ sadoditānandāḥ |
brahmasvabhāvamamṛtaṃ samprāptāścaiva kṛtakṛtyāḥ || 1.53 ||
āyatanaṃ vidyānāṃ mūlaṃ dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇām |
śreyaḥ paraṃ kimanyaccharīramajarāmaraṃ vihāyaikam || 1.54 ||
pratyakṣeṇa pramāṇena yo na jānāti sūtakam |
adṛṣṭavigrahaṃ devaṃ kathaṃ jñāsyati cinmayam || 1.55 ||
yajjarayā jarjaritaṃ kāsaśvāsādiduḥkhavivaśaṃ ca |
yogyaṃ tanna samādhau pratihatabuddhīndriyaprasaram || 1.56 ||
bālaḥ ṣoḍaśavarṣo viṣayarasāsvādalampaṭaḥ parataḥ |
yātaviveko vṛddho martyaḥ kathamāpnuyānmuktim || 1.57 ||
asminneva śarīre yeṣāṃ paramātmano na saṃvedaḥ |
dehatyāgādūrdhvaṃ teṣāṃ tadbrahma dūrataram || 1.58 ||
brahmādayo yatante tasmindivyāṃ tanuṃ samāśritya |
jīvanmuktāścānye kalpāntasthāyino munayaḥ || 1.59 ||
tasmājjīvanmuktiṃ samīhamānena yoginā prathamam |
divyā tanurvidheyā haragaurīsṛṣṭisaṃyogāt || 1.60 ||
śaile'smiñśivayoḥ prītyā parasparajigīṣayā |
sampravṛtte ca sambhoge trilokīkṣobhakāriṇi || 1.61 ||
vinivārayituṃ vahniḥ sambhogaṃ preṣitaḥ suraiḥ |
kāṅkṣamāṇaistayoḥ putraṃ tārakāsuramārakam || 1.62 ||
kapotarūpiṇaṃ prāptaṃ himavatkandare'nalam |
apakṣibhāvasaṃkṣubdhaṃ smaralīlāvilokinam || 1.63 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā lajjitaḥ śambhurvirataḥ suratāttadā |
pracyutaścaramo dhāturgṛhītaḥ śūlapāṇinā || 1.64 ||
prakṣipto vadane vahnergaṅgāyāmapi so'patat |
bahiḥ kṣiptastayā so'pi paridahyamānayā || 1.65 ||
saṃjātāstanmalādhānāddhātavaḥ siddhihetavaḥ |
yāvadagnimukhādreto nyapatadbhūrisārataḥ || 1.66 ||
śatayojananimnāṃstānkṛtvā kūpāṃstu pañca ca |
tadāprabhṛti kūpasthaṃ tadretaḥ pañcadhābhavat || 1.67 ||
raso rasendraḥ sūtaśca pārado miśrakastathā |
iti pañcavidho jātaḥ kṣetrabhedena śambhujaḥ || 1.68 ||
raso rakto vinirmuktaḥ sarvadoṣai rasāyanaḥ |
saṃjātāstridaśāstena nīrujā nirjarāmarāḥ || 1.69 ||
rasendro doṣanirmuktaḥ śyāvo rūkṣo'ticañcalaḥ |
rasāyino'bhavaṃstena nāgā mṛtyujarojjhitāḥ || 1.70 ||
devairnāgaiśca tau kūpau pūritau mṛdbhiraśmabhiḥ |
tadāprabhṛti lokānāṃ tau jātāvatidurlabhau || 1.71 ||
īṣatpītaśca rūkṣāṅgo doṣayuktaśca sūtakaḥ |
daśāṣṭasaṃskṛtaiḥ siddho dehaṃ lohaṃ karoti saḥ || 1.72 ||
athānyakūpajaḥ ko'pi sa calaḥ śvetavarṇavān |
pārado vividhairyogaiḥ sarvarogaharaḥ sa hi || 1.73 ||
mayūracandrikāchāyaḥ sa raso miśrako mataḥ |
so'pyaṣṭādaśasaṃskārayuktaścātīva siddhidaḥ || 1.74 ||
trayaḥ sūtādayaḥ sūtāḥ sarvasiddhikarā api |
nijakarmavinirmāṇaiḥ śaktimanto'timātrayā || 1.75 ||
etāṃ rasasamutpattiṃ yo jānāti sa dhārmikaḥ |
āyurārogyasaṃtānaṃ rasasiddhiṃ ca vindati || 1.76 ||
rasanātsarvadhātūnāṃ rasa ityabhidhīyate |
jarāruṅmṛtyunāśāya rasyate vā raso mataḥ || 1.77 ||
rasoparasarājatvādrasendra iti kīrtitaḥ |
dehalohamayīṃ siddhiṃ sūte sūtastataḥ smṛtaḥ || 1.78 ||
rogapaṅkābdhimagnānāṃ pāradānācca pāradaḥ |
sarvadhātugataṃ tejomiśritaṃ yatra tiṣṭhati || 1.79 ||
tasmātsa miśrakaḥ prokto nānārūpaphalapradaḥ |
evaṃbhūtasya sūtasya martyamṛtyugadacchidaḥ |
prabhāvānmānuṣā jātā devatulyabalāyuṣaḥ || 1.80 ||
tān dṛṣṭvābhyarthito rudraḥ śakreṇa tadanantaram |
doṣaiśca kañcukābhiśca rasarājo niyojitaḥ || 1.81 ||
tadāprabhṛti sūto'sau naiva sidhyatyasaṃskṛtaḥ |

[5 gatis]
jalago jalarūpeṇa tvarito haṃsago bhavet || 1.82 ||
malago malarūpeṇa sadhūmo dhūmago bhavet |
anyā jīvagatirdaivī jīvo'ṇḍādiva niṣkramet || 1.83 ||
sa tāṃśca jīvayejjīvāṃstena jīvo rasaḥ smṛtaḥ |


----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     rasasya pañcavidhagatimāha jalaga iti || 1.84 b;1
*     jalarūpeṇa dravatvena jalagaḥ jalena saha gamanaśīlaḥ bhavetrasasya cūrṇaprāyo'tisūkṣmāṃśa iti bhāvaḥ tvaritaḥ cañcalaḥ cāñcalyādityarthaḥ haṃsagaḥ haṃsavadgamanaśīlaḥ bhavetmalarūpeṇa malavattvātmalagaḥ malena saha miśritaḥ doṣasaṃśliṣṭaḥ bhavetsadhūmaḥ vahnidṛṣṭatvātdhūmagaḥ dhūmena saha gamanaśīlaḥ uḍḍayanasvabhāvaḥ bhavetanyā aparā pañcamītyarthaḥ daivī adṛśyarūpā jīvasya rasasya gatiḥ gamanamastīti śeṣaḥ tayā gatyā aṇḍātdeharūpakośātjīvaḥ ātmā iva niṣkrametrasa iti bhāvaḥ kena pathā dehātjīvo nirgacchati tatyathā na dṛśyate tathā pāradasya pañcamī gatirapi na jñātuṃ śakyate ityarthaḥ || 1.84 b;2
*     pūrvaśloke jīvagatiśabdena rasagatiriti pradarśitaṃ jīvaśabdasya rasārthatve hetumāha sa iti || 1.84 b;3
*     saḥ rasaḥ tān jīvān jīvayetjarāmaraṇādivināśanadvārā dīrghajīvanaṃ pradāpayettena hetunā rasaḥ jīvaḥ jīvayatīti vyutpattyā jīvanadāyakaḥ smṛtaḥ kathitaḥ || 1.84 b;4

____________________


catasro gatayo dṛśyā adṛśyā pañcamī gatiḥ || 1.84 ||
mantradhyānādinā tasya rudhyate pañcamī gatiḥ || 1.85 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     saṃprati pañcabhūtātmakasya tasya gatibhedena nāmabhedamāha jalaga iti || 1.85;1
*     yaḥ pārado jalarūpeṇa jalasvabhāvena gacchati sa jalago jalagativiśiṣṭa ityucyate || 1.85;2
*     tanmayāstu suvarṇādilohāḥ || 1.85;3
*     sthirasattvā rasādayo'pi tanmayāḥ || 1.85;4
*     bhūmimalakiṭṭayogenaiva teṣāṃ ghanatvaṃ sthiratvaṃ ca || 1.85;5
*     ata eva drutirūpeṇaiva teṣāṃ sthitiḥ sambhavati || 1.85;6
*     jalasvabhāvo'dhogamanarūpaḥ || 1.85;7
*     nirmaladrutirūpalohānāmapyadhogamanaṃ dṛśyata eva || 1.85;8
*     yaḥ pāradastvaritastvarayā vegena yukto nātyucchritamākāśe gacchati kiṃtu bhūmisaṃnihitākāśe bhuvi ca sa haṃsaga ityucyate || 1.85;9
*     haṃsa ivākāśe gacchatīti haṃsagaḥ || 1.85;10
*     sa tu mayūragaṇḍūpadādiṣu vyavasthitaḥ || 1.85;11
*     yastu malarūpeṇa kṛṣṇavarṇenāṃśena viśiṣṭo gacchati sa malaga ityucyate || 1.85;12
*     yaśca sadhūmo dhūmena dhūmasamānavarṇordhvarekhāsahitākāśagatyā saha vartata iti sadhūmaḥ sa dhūmaga ityucyate || 1.85;13
*     sa tu haritālahiṅgulamanaḥśilārasakagaurīpāṣāṇādiṣu vyavasthitaḥ || 1.85;14
*     te hi dhūmarodhātsphoṭaṃ kṛtvodgacchantītyanubhūyate || 1.85;15
*     nābhiyantrabhūdharalohamūṣādiyantrādinā nirdhūmajāraṇāyāmapi mahatā prayatnenāpi teṣāṃ sthiratvaṃ mīmāṃsyaṃ bhavati bhavennanveti || 1.85;16
*     anyā caturthī gatiḥ pāradasyāsti || 1.85;17
*     sā tu jīvagatirityucyate || 1.85;18
*     jīvasyevādṛśyā gatirjīvagatiḥ || 1.85;19
*     ata eva sā daivītyapyucyate || 1.85;20
*     tasyā devagaterivādṛśyatvāttayā yuktaḥ sa pārado'ṇḍājjīva ivādṛśyagatyā yuktaḥ svasthānānniṣkramennirgacchati || 1.85;21
*     aṇḍāddehasya nirgamanaṃ dṛśyate dehasahitasya jīvasya tu bahirnirgamanaṃ na dṛśyate || 1.85;22
*     bālasya cālanaśvāsocchvāsādikriyayā cānumīyate tadvadasyāpīti bhāvaḥ || 1.85;23
*     tayā gatyā yukta evāyaṃ jīvānprāṇino jīvayettena sa raso jīvanāmnā smṛtaḥ || 1.85;24
*     kārayettaṃ saṃskāranipuṇavaidyahastena rājā tatsamo vasumānvātmanaḥ prajānāṃ ca rakṣaṇārthamiti bhāvaḥ || 1.85;25

____________________


iti bhinnagatitvācca sūtarājyasya durlabhaḥ |
saṃskārastasya bhiṣajā nipuṇena tu rakṣayet || 1.86 ||
prathame rajasi snātāṃ hayārūḍhāṃ svalaṃkṛtām |
vīkṣamāṇāṃ vadhūṃ dṛṣṭvā jighṛkṣuḥ kūpago rasaḥ || 1.87 ||
udgacchati javātsāpi taṃ dṛṣṭvā yāti vegataḥ |
anugacchati tāṃ sūtaḥ sīmānaṃ yojanonmitam || 1.88 ||
pratyāyāti tataḥ kūpaṃ vegataḥ śivasambhavaḥ |
mārganirmitagarteṣu sthitaṃ gṛhṇanti pāradam |
patito darade deśe gauravādvahnivaktrataḥ || 1.89 ||
sa raso bhūtale līnastattaddeśanivāsinaḥ |
tāṃ mṛdaṃ pātanayantre kṣiptvā sūtaṃ haranti ca || 1.90 ||


________________________________________________________

 2
[aṣṭamahārasāḥ]
abhravaikrāntamākṣīkavimalādrijasasyakam |
capalo rasakaśceti jñātvāṣṭau saṃgrahedrasān || 2.1 ||
devyā rajo bhavedgandho dhātuḥ śukraṃ tathābhrakamiti kṣepakaḥ |

[abhra:: medic. properties]
gaurītejaḥ paramamamṛtaṃ vātapittakṣayaghnaṃ prajñābodhi praśamitarujaṃ vṛṣyamāyuṣyamagryam |
balyaṃ snigdhaṃ rucidamakaphaṃ dīpanaṃ śītavīryaṃ tattadyogaiḥ sakalagadahṛdvyoma sūtendrabandhi || 2.2 ||

[abhra:: production]
rājahastādadhastādyatsamānītaṃ ghanaṃ khaneḥ |
bhavettaduktaphaladaṃ niḥsattvaṃ niṣphalaṃ param || 2.3 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     tadevābhrakaṃ bhūmimadhye rājahastātsapādahastādadhastādadhobhāgasthaṃ yatkhanijaṃ ghanamabhrakaṃ vajrākhyaṃ tadeva pūrvoktaguṇam || 2.3;1
*     itaraṃ tu vāyujalamūtramalatāpakṛmisaṃbandhānniḥsāraṃ jñeyam || 2.3;2
*     idamupalakṣaṇaṃ sarveṣāṃ khanijadravyāṇām || 2.3;3
*     prāyasteṣāṃ hi bhūmigarbhasthānāmeva pūrṇātmaguṇatvāt || 2.3;4
*     atra ghanaśabdo'bhrakamātravācako'pi vajrābhrake paryavasyati || 2.3;5
*     tasyaiva yathoktaguṇatvāt || 2.3;6

____________________


[abhra:: subtypes]
pinākaṃ nāgamaṇḍūkaṃ vajramityabhrakaṃ matam |

[abhra:: subtypes:: colour]
śvetādivarṇabhedena pratyekaṃ taccaturvidham || 2.4 ||

[pināka]
pinākaṃ pāvakottaptaṃ vimuñcati daloccayam |
tatsevitaṃ malaṃ baddhvā mārayatyeva mānavam || 2.5 ||

[nāga]
nāgābhraṃ nāgavatkuryāddhvaniṃ pāvakasaṃsthitam |

[nāga:: medic. properties]
tadbhuktaṃ kurute kuṣṭhaṃ maṇḍalākhyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 2.6 ||

[maṇḍūka]
utplutyotplutya maṇḍūkaṃ dhmātaṃ patati cābhrakam |

[maṇḍūka:: medic. properties]
tatkuryādaśmarīrogamasādhyaṃ śastrato'nyathā || 2.7 ||

[vajra]
vajrābhraṃ vahnisaṃtaptaṃ nirmuktāśeṣavaikṛtam |
dehalohakaraṃ tacca sarvarogaharaṃ param || 2.8 ||
śvetaṃ raktaṃ ca pītaṃ ca kṛṣṇamevaṃ caturvidham |
śvetaṃ śvetakriyāsūktaṃ raktābhaṃ raktakarmaṇi |
pītābhamabhrakaṃ yattu śreṣṭhaṃ tatpītakarmaṇi || 2.9 ||
caturvidhaṃ varaṃ vyoma yadyapyuktaṃ rasāyane |
tathāpi kṛṣṇavarṇābhraṃ koṭikoṭiguṇādhikam || 2.10 ||

[abhra:: parīkṣā]
snigdhaṃ pṛthudalaṃ varṇasaṃyuktaṃ bhārato'dhikam |
sukhanirmocyapattraṃ ca tadabhraṃ śastamīritam || 2.11 ||

[grāsāyogyābhra]
sacandrikaṃ ca kiṭṭābhaṃ vyoma na grāsayedrasaḥ |
grasitaśca niyojyo'sau lohe caiva rasāyane || 2.12 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     grāsābhāve hyupoṣita iva rasaḥ kāryakaro na bhavedityāha sacandrikamiti || 2.12;1
*     sacandrikaṃ cākacikyasahitam || 2.12;2
*     kiṭṭābhaṃ prabhūtakiṭṭam || 2.12;3
*     grasitaśca grasitābhrasattva eva lohe svarṇādyutpādane lohamāraṇe rogavāraṇārthaṃ lohaprayoge ca niyojyaḥ || 2.12;4
*     tathā rasāyane jvarāṅkuśādi tattadrase rasāyane ca lakṣmīvilāsavajrapañjarādirūpe vakṣyamāṇa ityarthaḥ || 2.12;5
*     rasa īyate prāpyate'neneti vyutpattyātra rasaśabdena rasarasāyanayoḥ saṃgrahāt || 2.12;6

____________________


[abhra:: niścandrika:: medic. properties]
niścandrikaṃ mṛtaṃ vyoma sevyaṃ sarvagadeṣu ca |
sevitaṃ candrasaṃyuktaṃ mehaṃ mandānalaṃ caret || 2.13 ||
yairuktaṃ yuktinirmuktaiḥ pattrābhrakarasāyanam |
tairdṛṣṭaṃ kālakūṭākhyaṃ viṣaṃ jīvanahetave || 2.14 ||
sattvārthaṃ sevanārthaṃ ca yojayecchodhitābhrakam |
anyathā tvaguṇaṃ kṛtvā vikarotyeva niścitam || 2.15 ||

[abhra:: śodhana]
prataptaṃ saptavārāṇi nikṣiptaṃ kāñjike'bhrakam |
nirdoṣaṃ jāyate nūnaṃ prakṣiptaṃ vāpi gojale || 2.16 ||
triphalākvathite cāpi gavāṃ dugdhe viśeṣataḥ |

[abhra:: māraṇa]
tato dhānyābhrakaṃ kṛtvā piṣṭvā matsyākṣikārasaiḥ || 2.17 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     matsyākṣikā hilamocikā gaṇḍadūrvā vā || 2.17;1

____________________


cakrīṃ kṛtvā viśoṣyātha puṭedardhebhake puṭe |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     tatastadabhrakaṃ dhānyābhrakaṃ kṛtvā matsyākṣikārasenaikadinaparyantaṃ saṃmardya cakrīṃ vartulasthūlavaṭikāṃ vidhāyāvaśoṣya śarāvasaṃpuṭitaṃ kṛtvārdhe'bhrake puṭe puṭedardhe'bhrake puṭamardhagajapuṭaṃ tacca gajapuṭagatārdhabhāgaṃ vanopalaiḥ pūrayitvā bhavati || 2.18 b;1
*     puṭetpacedityarthaḥ || 2.18 b;2
*     pratipuṭamabhrakasya mardanaṃ tataścakrīṃ kṛtvā viśoṣya śarāvasaṃpuṭitaṃ kāryam || 2.18 b;3

____________________


puṭedevaṃ hi ṣaḍvāraṃ paunarnavarasaiḥ saha || 2.18 ||
kalāṃśaṭaṅkaṇenāpi saṃmardya kṛtacakrikam |
ardhebhākhyapuṭaistadvatsaptavāraṃ puṭetkhalu || 2.19 ||
evaṃ vāsārasenāpi taṇḍulīyarasena ca |
prapuṭetsaptavārāṇi pūrvaproktavidhānataḥ |
evaṃ siddhaṃ ghanaṃ sarvayogeṣu viniyojayet || 2.20 ||

[dhānyābhra]
cūrṇābhraṃ śālisaṃyuktaṃ vastrabaddhaṃ hi kāñjike |
niryātaṃ mardanādvastrāddhānyābhramiti kathyate || 2.21 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     dhānyābhravidhimāha cūrṇābhramiti || 2.21;1
*     cūrṇābhra abhracūrṇaṃ śālisaṃyuktamadyāpyatra śāliparimāṇaṃ noktaṃ tathāpi tantrāntarānusaraṇātpādamitaśālidhānyasahitaṃ boddhavyaṃ tathā ca rasendrasārasaṃgrahe |
*     pādāṃśaṃ śālisaṃyuktamabhrakaṃ kambalodare |
*     trirātraṃ sthāpayennīre tatklinnaṃ mardayeddṛḍham |
*     kambalādgalitaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ vālukārahitaṃ yat |
*     taddhānyābhramiti proktamabhramāraṇasiddhaye |
*     iti || 2.21;2
*     niryātaṃ nirgatam || 2.21;3
*     abhraṃ cūrṇitaṃ kṛtvā pādāṃśaśālidhānyena saha sthūlavastre baddhvā kāñjike tāvanmardanīyaṃ yāvadvastracchidrātślakṣṇaṃ vālukārahitaṃ satniryāti taddhānyābhraṃ smṛtam || 2.21;4

____________________


[dhānyābhra:: māraṇa]
dhānyābhraṃ kāsamardasya rasena parimarditam |
puṭitaṃ daśavāreṇa mriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
tadvanmustārasenāpi taṇḍulīyarasena ca || 2.22 ||

[abhra:: māraṇa]
pītāmalakasaubhāgyapiṣṭaṃ cakrīkṛtābhrakam |
puṭitaṃ ṣaṣṭivārāṇi sindūrābhaṃ prajāyate |
kṣayādyakhilarogaghnaṃ bhavedrogānupānataḥ || 2.23 ||

[abhra:: māraṇa]
vaṭamūlatvacaḥ kvāthaistāmbūlīpattrasārataḥ |
vāsāmatsyākṣikābhyāṃ vā mīnākṣyā sakaṭhillayā || 2.24 ||
payasā vaṭavṛkṣasya marditaṃ puṭitaṃ ghanam |
bhavedviṃśativāreṇa sindūrasadṛśaprabham || 2.25 ||

[abhra:: sattvapātana]
pādāṃśaṭaṅkaṇopetaṃ musalīrasamarditam |
rundhyātkoṣṭhyāṃ dṛḍhaṃ dhmātaṃ sattvarūpaṃ bhavedghanam || 2.26 ||

[abhra:: sattvapātana]
kāsamardaghanādhānyavāsānāṃ ca punarbhuvaḥ |
matsyākṣyāḥ kāṇḍavallyāśca haṃsapādyā rasaiḥ pṛthak || 2.27 ||
piṣṭvā piṣṭvā prayatnena śoṣayedgharmayogataḥ |
palaṃ godhūmacūrṇasya kṣudramatsyāśca ṭaṅkaṇam || 2.28 ||
pratyekamaṣṭamāṃśena dattvā dattvā vimardayet |
mardane mardane samyakśoṣayedraviraśmibhiḥ || 2.29 ||
pañcājaṃ pañcagavyaṃ vā pañcamāhiṣameva ca |
kṣiptvā golānprakurvīta kiṃcittindukato'dhikān || 2.30 ||

[pañcāja]
payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ mūtraṃ saviṭkaṃ cājamucyate |
adhaḥpātanakoṣṭhyāṃ hi dhmātvā sattvaṃ nipātayet || 2.31 ||

[abhra:: sattvapātana:: separationof sattva andkiṭṭa]
koṣṭhyāṃ kiṭṭaṃ samāhṛtya vicūrṇya ravakān haret |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     koṣṭhyāmavaśiṣṭasattvaṃ kācakiṭṭasaṃśliṣṭaṃ kaṇarūpaṃ durgrāhyaṃ tattu agnau śānte sati samāhṛtyaikīkṛtya bahirniṣkāsya saṃkuṭya vicūrṇya tatsaṃśliṣṭaṃ kaṇasattvaṃ haret || 2.32 b;1
*     kiṭṭātpṛthakkṛtya gṛhṇīyāt || 2.32 b;2

____________________


tatkiṭṭaṃ svalpaṭaṅkena gomayena vimardya ca || 2.32 ||
golānvidhāya saṃśoṣya gharme bhūyo'pi pūrvavat |
bhūyaḥ kiṭṭaṃ samāhṛtya mṛditvā sattvamāharet || 2.33 ||

[abhra:: sattva:: śodhana]
atha sattvakaṇāṃstāṃstu kvāthayitvāmlakāñjikaiḥ |
śodhanīyagaṇopetaṃ mūṣāmadhye nirudhya ca || 2.34 ||
samyagdrutaṃ samāhṛtya dvivāraṃ pradhamedghanam |
iti śuddhaṃ bhavetsattvaṃ yojyaṃ rasarasāyane || 2.35 ||

[abhra:: sattva:: mṛdūkaraṇa]
madhutailavasājyeṣu drāvitaṃ parivāpitam |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     parivāpitaṃ prakṣiptam || 2.36 b;1

____________________


mṛdu syāddaśavāreṇa sattvaṃ lohādikaṃ kharam || 2.36 ||

[abhra:: māraṇa]
paṭṭacūrṇaṃ vidhāyātha goghṛtena pariplutam |
bharjayetsaptavārāṇi cullīsaṃsthitakharpare || 2.37 ||
agnivarṇaṃ bhavedyāvadvāraṃ vāraṃ vicūrṇayet |
tṛṇaṃ kṣiptvā dahedyāvattāvadvā bharjanaṃ caret || 2.38 ||
tataḥ sagandhakaṃ piṣṭvā vaṭamūlakaṣāyataḥ |
puṭedviṃśativāreṇa vārāheṇa puṭena hi || 2.39 ||
punarviṃśativārāṇi triphalotthakaṣāyataḥ |
triphalāmuṇḍikābhṛṅgapatrapathyākṣamūlakaiḥ || 2.40 ||
bhāvayitvā prayoktavyaṃ sarvarogeṣu mātrayā |
sattvābhrātkiṃcidaparaṃ nirvikāraṃ guṇādhikam || 2.41 ||
evaṃ cecchatavārāṇi puṭapākena sādhitam |
guṇavajjāyate'tyarthaṃ paraṃ pācanadīpanam || 2.42 ||

[abhra:: māraṇa:: niścandrika]
gandharvapattratoyena guḍena saha bhāvitam |
adhordhvaṃ vaṭapatrāṇi niścandraṃ tripuṭaiḥ khagam || 2.43 ||
kṣudhaṃ karoti cātyarthaṃ guñjārdhamitisevayā |
tattadrogaharairyogaiḥ sarvarogaharaṃ param || 2.44 ||

[abhra:: sattva:: śodhana]
sattvasya golakaṃ dhmātaṃ sasyasaṃyuktakāñjike |
nirvāpya tatkṣaṇenaiva kuṭṭayellohapārayā || 2.45 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     lohapārayā lohamusalena || 2.45;1

____________________


sampratāpya ghanasthūlakaṇān kṣiptvātha kāñjike |
tatkṣaṇena samāhṛtya kuṭṭayitvā rajaścaret || 2.46 ||
goghṛtena ca taccūrṇaṃ bharjayetpūrvavattridhā |
dhātrīphalarasaistadvaddhātrīpatrarasena vā || 2.47 ||
bharjane bharjane kāryaṃ śilāpaṭṭena peṣaṇam |
tataḥ punarnavāvāsārasaiḥ kāñjikamiśritaiḥ || 2.48 ||
prapuṭeddaśavārāṇi daśavārāṇi gandhakaiḥ |
evaṃ saṃśodhitaṃ vyomasattvaṃ sarvaguṇottaram |
yatheṣṭaṃ viniyoktavyaṃ jāraṇe ca rasāyane || 2.49 ||

[abhra:: druti]
drutayo naiva nirdiṣṭāḥ śāstre dṛṣṭā api dṛḍham |
vinā śaṃbhoḥ prasādena na sidhyanti kadācana || 2.50 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     vallaḥ dviguñjā guñjātrayamiti līlāvatī sārdhaguñjeti rājanighaṇṭuḥ || 2.50;1

____________________


[abhra:: mṛta:: medic. properties, application]
vellavyoṣasamanvitaṃ ghṛtayutaṃ vallonmitaṃ sevitaṃ divyābhraṃ kṣayapāṇḍuruggrahaṇikāśūlāmakuṣṭhāmayam |
jūrtiṃ śvāsagadaṃ pramehamaruciṃ kāsāmayaṃ durdharaṃ mandāgniṃ jaṭharavyathāṃ vijayate yogairaśeṣāmayān || 2.51 ||

[vaikrānta:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality]
aṣṭāsraścāṣṭaphalakaḥ ṣaṭkoṇo masṛṇo guruḥ |
śuddhamiśritavarṇaiśca yukto vaikrānta ucyate || 2.52 ||

[vaikrānta:: subtypes:: colour]
śveto raktaśca pītaśca nīlaḥ pārāpatacchaviḥ |
śyāmalaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇaśca karburaścāṣṭadhā hi saḥ || 2.53 ||

[vaikrānta:: medic. properties]
āyuḥpradaśca balavarṇakaro'tivṛṣyaḥ prajñāpradaḥ sakaladoṣagadāpahārī |
dīptāgnikṛtpavisamānaguṇastarasvī vaikrāntakaḥ khalu vapurbalalohakārī || 2.54 ||
rasāyaneṣu sarveṣu pūrvagaṇyaḥ pratāpavān |
vajrasthāne niyoktavyo vaikrāntaḥ sarvadoṣahā || 2.55 ||

[vaikrānta:: myth. origin]
daityendro māhiṣaḥ siddhaḥ sahadevasamudbhavaḥ |
durgā bhagavatī devī taṃ śūlena vyamardayat || 2.56 ||
tasya raktaṃ tu patitaṃ yatra yatra sthitaṃ bhuvi |
tatra tatra tu vaikrāntaṃ vajrākāraṃ mahārasam || 2.57 ||

[vaikrānta:: localdistribution]
vindhyasya dakṣiṇe vāsti hyuttare vāsti sarvataḥ |

[vaikrānta:: nirukti]
vikṛntayati lohāni tena vaikrāntakaḥ smṛtaḥ || 2.58 ||

[vaikrānta:: subtypes:: colour]
śvetaḥ pītastathā rakto nīlaḥ pārāvataprabhaḥ |
mayūrakaṇṭhasadṛśaścānyo marakataprabhaḥ || 2.59 ||
dehasiddhikaraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ pīte pītaṃ site sitam |
sarvārthasiddhidaṃ raktaṃ tathā marakataprabham |
śeṣe dve niṣphale varjye vaikrāntamiti saptadhā || 2.60 ||
yatra kṣetre sthitaṃ caiva vaikrāntaṃ tatra bhairavam |
vināyakaṃ ca sampūjya gṛhṇīyācchuddhamānasaḥ || 2.61 ||

[vaikrānta:: medic. properties]
vaikrānto vajrasadṛśo dehalohakaro mataḥ |
viṣaghno rasarājaśca jvarakuṣṭhakṣayapraṇut || 2.62 ||

[vaikrānta:: śodhana]
vaikrāntakāḥ syustridinaṃ viśuddhāḥ saṃsveditāḥ kṣārapaṭūni dattvā |
amleṣu mūtreṣu kulattharambhānīre'thavā kodravavāripakvāḥ || 2.63 ||

[vaikrānta:: śodhana]
kulatthakvāthasaṃsvinno vaikrāntaḥ pariśudhyati |

[vaikrānta:: māraṇa]
mriyate'ṣṭapuṭairgandhanimbukadravasaṃyutaḥ || 2.64 ||

[vaikrānta:: māraṇa]
vaikrānteṣu ca tapteṣu hayamūtraṃ vinikṣipet |
paunaḥpunyena vā kuryāddravaṃ dattvā puṭaṃ tvanu |
bhasmībhūtaṃ tu vaikrāntaṃ vajrasthāne niyojayet || 2.65 ||

[vaikrānta:: sattvapātana]
mocamoraṭapālāśakṣāragomūtrabhāvitam |
vajrakandaniśākalkaphalacūrṇasamanvitam || 2.66 ||
tatkalkaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ lākṣācūrṇaṃ vaikrāntasaṃbhavam |
navasārasamāyuktaṃ meṣaśṛṅgīdravānvitam || 2.67 ||
piṇḍitaṃ mūkamūṣasthaṃ dhmāpitaṃ ca haṭhāgninā |
tatraiva patate sattvaṃ vaikrāntasya na saṃśayaḥ || 2.68 ||

[vaikrānta:: sattvapātana]
sattvapātanayogena marditaśca vaṭīkṛtaḥ |
mūṣāstho ghaṭikādhmāto vaikrāntaḥ sattvamutsṛjet || 2.69 ||

[vaikrānta:: formulations]
bhasmatvaṃ samupāgato vikṛtako hemnā mṛtenānvitaḥ pādāṃśena kaṇājyavellasahito guñjāmitaḥ sevitaḥ |
yakṣmāṇaṃ jaraṇaṃ ca pāṇḍugudajaṃ śvāsaṃ ca kāsāmayaṃ duṣṭāṃ ca grahaṇīmuraḥkṣatamukhān rogāñjayeddehakṛt || 2.70 ||
sūtabhasmārdhasaṃyuktaṃ nīlavaikrāntabhasmakam |
mṛtābhrasattvamubhayostulitaṃ parimarditam || 2.71 ||
kṣaudrājyasaṃyutaṃ prātarguñjāmātraṃ niṣevitam |
nihanti sakalānrogāndurjayānanyabheṣajaiḥ |
triḥsaptadivasairnṝṇāṃ gaṅgāmbha iva pātakam || 2.72 ||

[mākṣika:: myth. origin]
suvarṇaśailaprabhavo viṣṇunā kāñcano rasaḥ |
tāpyāṃ kirātacīneṣu yavaneṣu ca nirmitaḥ |

[mākṣika:: time of origin]
tāpyaḥ sūryāṃśusaṃtapto mādhave māsi dṛśyate || 2.73 ||

[mākṣika:: medic. properties]
madhuraḥ kāñcanābhāsaḥ sāmlo rajatasaṃnibhaḥ |
kiṃcitkaṣāyamadhuraḥ śītaḥ pāke kaṭurlaghuḥ |
tatsevanājjarāvyādhiviṣairna paribhūyate || 2.74 ||

[mākṣika:: subtypes]
mākṣiko dvividho hemamākṣikastāramākṣikaḥ |

[svarṇamākṣika]
tatrādyaṃ mākṣikaṃ kānyakubjotthaṃ svarṇasaṃnibham || 2.75 ||

[tāramākṣika]
tapatītīrasambhūtaṃ pañcavarṇasuvarṇavat |
pāṣāṇabahalaḥ proktastārākhyo'lpaguṇātmakaḥ || 2.76 ||
mākṣīkadhātuḥ sakalāmayaghnaḥ prāṇo rasendrasya paraṃ hi vṛṣyaḥ |
durmelalohadvayamelanaśca guṇottaraḥ sarvarasāyanāgryaḥ || 2.77 ||

[mākṣika:: śodhana]
eraṇḍatailaluṅgāmbusiddhaṃ śudhyati mākṣikam |

[mākṣika:: śodhana]
siddhaṃ vā kadalīkandatoyena ghaṭikādvayam |
taptaṃ kṣiptaṃ varākvāthe śuddhimāyāti mākṣikam || 2.78 ||

[mākṣika:: māraṇa]
mātuluṅgāmbugandhābhyāṃ piṣṭaṃ mūṣodare sthitam |
pañcakroḍapuṭairdagdhaṃ mriyate mākṣikaṃ khalu || 2.79 ||

[mākṣika:: māraṇa]
eraṇḍasnehagavyājairmātuluṅgarasena vā |
kharparasthaṃ dṛḍhaṃ pakvaṃ jāyate dhātusaṃnibham |
evaṃ mṛtaṃ rase yojyaṃ rasāyanavidhāvapi || 2.80 ||

[mākṣika:: sattvapātana]
triṃśāṃśanāgasaṃyuktaṃ kṣārairamlaiśca vartitam |
dhmātaṃ prakaṭamūṣāyāṃ sattvaṃ muñcati mākṣikam || 2.81 ||

[mākṣika:: sattva:: nāgagrāsa]
saptavāraṃ paridrāvya kṣiptaṃ nirguṇḍikārase |
mākṣīkasattvasaṃmiśraṃ nāgaṃ naśyati niścitam || 2.82 ||

[mākṣika:: sattvapātana]
kṣaudragandharvatailābhyāṃ gomūtreṇa ghṛtena ca |
kadalīkandasāreṇa bhāvitaṃ mākṣikaṃ muhuḥ |
mūṣāyāṃ muñcati dhmātaṃ sattvaṃ śulbanibhaṃ mṛdu || 2.83 ||

[mākṣika:: sattva:: properties]
guñjābījasamacchāyaṃ drutadrāvaṃ ca śītalam |
tāpyasattvaṃ viśuddhaṃ taddehalohakaraṃ param || 2.84 ||

[mākṣika:: sattva:: medicines]
mākṣīkasattvena rasendrapiṣṭaṃ kṛtvā vilīne ca baliṃ nidhāya |
saṃmiśrya saṃmardya ca khalvamadhye nikṣipya sattvaṃ drutimabhrakasya || 2.85 ||
vidhāya golaṃ lavaṇākhyayantre paceddinārdhaṃ mṛduvahninā ca |
svataḥ suśītaṃ paricūrṇya samyagvallonmitaṃ vyoṣaviḍaṅgayuktam || 2.86 ||
saṃsevitaṃ kṣaudrayutaṃ nihanti jarāṃ sarogāṃ tvapamṛtyumeva |
duḥsādhyarogānapi saptavāsarairnaitena tulyo'sti sudhāraso'pi || 2.87 ||
eraṇḍotthena tailena guñjākṣaudraṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇam |
marditaṃ tasya vāpena sattvaṃ mākṣīkajaṃ dravet || 2.88 ||

[vimala:: subtypes]
vimalastrividhaḥ prokto hemādyastārapūrvakaḥ |
tṛtīyaḥ kāṃsyavimalastattatkāntyā sa lakṣyate || 2.89 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     vimalasya bhedamāha vimala iti || 2.89;1
*     vimalaḥ mākṣikaviśeṣaḥ || 2.89;2
*     tattatkāntyā hemādīnāṃ prabhayā || 2.89;3
*     paryāyamuktāvalīkṛtā vimalaśabdena raupyamākṣikaṃ gṛhītam || 2.89;4

____________________


[vimala:: phys. properties]
vartulaḥ koṇasaṃyuktaḥ snigdhaśca phalakānvitaḥ |

[vimala:: medic. properties]
marutpittaharo vṛṣyo vimalo'tirasāyanaḥ || 2.90 ||

[vimala:: subtypes:: use]
pūrvo hemakriyāsūkto dvitīyo rūpyakṛnmataḥ |
tṛtīyo bheṣaje teṣu pūrvapūrvo guṇottaraḥ || 2.91 ||

[vimala:: śodhana]
āṭarūṣajale svinno vimalo vimalo bhavet |
jambīrasvarase svinno meṣaśṛṅgīrase'thavā |
āyāti śuddhiṃ vimalo dhātavaśca yathā pare || 2.92 ||

[vimala:: māraṇa]
gandhāśmalakucāmlaiśca mriyate daśabhiḥ puṭaiḥ || 2.93 ||

[vimala:: sattvapātana]
saṭaṅkalakucadrāvairmeṣaśṛṅgyāśca bhasmanā |
piṣṭo mūṣodare liptaḥ saṃśoṣya ca nirudhya ca || 2.94 ||
ṣaṭprasthakokilairdhmāto vimalaḥ sīsasaṃnibhaḥ |
sattvaṃ muñcati tadyukto rasaḥ syātsa rasāyanaḥ || 2.95 ||

[vimala:: sattvapātana]
vimalaṃ śigrutoyena kāṅkṣīkāsīsaṭaṅkaṇam |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     kāṅkṣī saurāṣṭramṛttikā || 2.96 b;1

____________________


vajrakandasamāyuktaṃ bhāvitaṃ kadalīrasaiḥ || 2.96 ||
mokṣakakṣārasaṃyuktaṃ dhmāpitaṃ mūkamūṣagam |
sattvaṃ candrārkasaṃkāśaṃ patate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 2.97 ||

[vimala:: sattva:: medic. use]
tatsattvaṃ sūtasaṃyuktaṃ piṣṭaṃ kṛtvā sumarditam |
vilīne gandhake kṣiptvā jārayettriguṇālakam || 2.98 ||
śilāṃ pañcaguṇāṃ cāpi vālukāyantrake khalu |
tārabhasmadaśāṃśena tāvadvaikrāntakaṃ mṛtam || 2.99 ||
sarvamekatra saṃcūrṇya paṭena parigālya ca |
nikṣipya kūpikāmadhye paripūrya prayatnataḥ || 2.100 ||
līḍho vyoṣavarānvito vimalako yukto ghṛtaiḥ sevito hanyāddurbhagakṛjjvarāñśvayathukaṃ pāṇḍupramehārucīḥ |
mūlārtiṃ grahaṇīṃ ca śūlamatulaṃ yakṣmāmayaṃ kāmalāṃ sarvānpittamarudgadānkimaparairyogairaśeṣāmayān || 2.101 ||

[śilājatu:: subtypes]
śilādhāturdvidhā prokto gomūtrādyo rasāyanaḥ |
karpūrapūrvakaścānyastatrādyo dvividhaḥ punaḥ |
sasattvaścaiva niḥsattvastayoḥ pūrvo guṇādhikaḥ || 2.102 ||

[śilājatu:: origin duringsummer]
grīṣme tīvrārkataptebhyaḥ pādebhyo himabhūbhṛtaḥ |
svarṇarūpyārkagarbhebhyaḥ śilādhāturviniḥsaret || 2.103 ||

[śilājatu:: froṃ gold]
svarṇagarbhagirerjāto japāpuṣpanibho guruḥ |
sasvalpatiktaḥ susvāduḥ paramaṃ tadrasāyanam || 2.104 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     tatrādau svarṇādibhya utpattibhedena trividhasya gomūtragandhinaḥ tasya lakṣaṇādikamāha svarṇeti || 2.104;1
*     tantrāntare tu utpattibhedena bhinnasya tasya cāturvidhyamuktaṃ yathā carake |
*     nātyuṣṇaśītaṃ dhātubhyaścaturbhyastasya saṃbhavaḥ |
*     hemno'tha rajatāttāmrātvaraṃ kālāyasādapi || 2.104;2
*     iti || 2.104;3
*     atra tu āyasaṃ noktaṃ tallakṣaṇamapi tatraiva draṣṭavyaṃ yathā |
*     yattu guggulusaṃkāśaṃ tiktakaṃ lavaṇānvitam |
*     vipāke kaṭu śītaṃ ca sarvaśreṣṭhaṃ tadāyasam || 2.104;4
*     gomūtragandhi sarveṣāṃ sarvakarmasu yaugikam |
*     rasāyanaprayogeṣu paścimaṃ tu viśiṣyate || 2.104;5
*     iti || 2.104;6
*     atra haimaśilājatuno paramarasāyanatvenoktatvādāyasasyāpi mukhyataḥ rasāyanaguṇatvena ekakāryakatvādāyasaṃ pṛthaṅnoktamiti mantavyam || 2.104;7

____________________


[śilājatu:: froṃ silver]
rūpyagarbhagirerjātaṃ madhuraṃ pāṇḍuraṃ guru |
śilājaṃ pittarogaghnaṃ viśeṣātpāṇḍurogahṛt || 2.105 ||

[śilājatu:: froṃ copper]
tāmragarbhaṃ girerjātaṃ nīlavarṇaṃ ghanaṃ guru |
śilājaṃ kaphavātaghnaṃ tiktoṣṇaṃ kṣayarogahṛt || 2.106 ||

[śilājatu:: parīkṣā:: śuddha]
vahnau kṣiptaṃ bhavedyattalliṅgākāramadhūmakam |
salile'tha vilīnaṃ ca tacchuddhaṃ hi śilājatu || 2.107 ||

[śilājatu:: medic. properties]
nūnaṃ sajvarapāṇḍuśophaśamanaṃ mehāgnimāndyāpahaṃ medaśchedakaraṃ ca yakṣmaśamanaṃ śūlāmayonmūlanam |
gulmaplīhavināśanaṃ jaṭharahṛcchūlaghnamāmāpahaṃ sarvatvaggadanāśanaṃ kimaparaṃ dehe ca lohe hitam || 2.108 ||

[śilājatu:: possessespropertiesof itssources]
rasoparasasūtendraratnaloheṣu ye guṇāḥ |
vasanti te śilādhātau jarāmṛtyujigīṣayā || 2.109 ||

[śilājatu:: śodhana]
kṣārāmlagojalairdhautaṃ śudhyatyeva śilājatu || 2.110 ||

[śilājatu:: śodhana]
śilādhātuṃ ca dugdhena triphalāmārkavadravaiḥ |
lohapātre vinikṣipya śodhayedatiyatnataḥ || 2.111 ||

[śilājatu:: śodhana]
kṣārāmlaguggulopetaiḥ svedanīyantramadhyagaiḥ |
sveditaṃ ghaṭikāmānācchilādhātu viśudhyati || 2.112 ||

[śilājatu:: māraṇa]
śilayā gandhatālābhyāṃ mātuluṅgarasena ca |
puṭitaṃ hi śilādhātu mriyate'ṣṭagiriṇḍakaiḥ || 2.113 ||

[śilājatu:: medic. application]
bhasmībhūtaśilodbhavaṃ samatulaṃ kāntaṃ ca vaikrāntakaṃ yuktaṃ ca triphalākaṭutrikaghṛtairvallena tulyaṃ bhajet |
pāṇḍau yakṣmagade tathāgnisadane meheṣu mūlāmaye gulmaplīhamahodare bahuvidhe śūle ca yonyāmaye || 2.114 ||
seveta yadi ṣaṇmāsaṃ rasāyanavidhānataḥ |
valīpalitanirmukto jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sukhī || 2.115 ||

[śilājatu:: sattvapātana]
piṣṭaṃ drāvaṇavargeṇa sāmlena girisaṃbhavam |
kṣiptvā mūṣodare ruddhvā gāḍhairdhmātaṃ hi kokilaiḥ |
sattvaṃ muñcecchilādhātuḥ śvasanairlohasaṃnibham || 2.116 ||

[karpūraśilājatu:: phys. properties]
pāṇḍuraṃ sikatākāraṃ karpūrādyaṃ śilājatu |

[karpūraśilājatu:: medic. properties]
mūtrakṛcchrāśmarīmehakāmalāpāṇḍunāśanam || 2.117 ||

[karpūraśilājatu:: śodhana]
elātoyena saṃbhinnaṃ siddhaṃ śuddhimupaiti tat |

[karpūraśilājatu:: māraṇa, sattvapātana]
naitasya māraṇaṃ sattvapātanaṃ vihitaṃ budhaiḥ || 2.118 ||

[sasyaka:: myth. origin]
pītvā hālāhalaṃ vāntaṃ pītāmṛtagarutmatā |
viṣeṇāmṛtayuktena girau marakatāhvaye |
tadvāntaṃ hi ghanībhūtaṃ saṃjātaṃ sasyakaṃ khalu || 2.119 ||

[sasyaka:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality]
mayūrakaṇṭhasacchāyaṃ bhārāḍhyamatiśasyate || 2.120 ||
dravyaṃ viṣayutaṃ yattaddravyādhikaguṇaṃ bhavet |
hālāhalaṃ sudhāyuktaṃ sudhādhikaguṇaṃ tathā || 2.121 ||

[mayūratuttha:: medic. properties]
niḥśeṣadoṣaviṣahṛdgadaśūlamūlakuṣṭhāmlapaittikavibandhaharaṃ paraṃ ca |
rasāyanaṃ vamanarekakaraṃ garaghnaṃ śvitrāpahaṃ gaditamatra mayūratuttham || 2.122 ||

[sasyaka:: śodhana]
sasyakaṃ śuddhimāpnoti raktavargeṇa bhāvitam || 2.123 ||
snehavargeṇa saṃsiktaṃ saptavāramadūṣitam |
dolāyantreṇa susvinnaṃ sasyakaṃ praharatrayam |
gomahiṣyājamūtreṣu śudhyate pañcakharparam || 2.124 ||

[tutthakharpara:: māraṇa]
lakucadrāvagandhāśmaṭaṅkaṇena samanvitam |
nirudhya mūṣikāmadhye mriyate kaukkuṭaiḥ puṭaiḥ || 2.125 ||

[kharpara:: sattvapātana]
sasyakasya tu cūrṇaṃ tu pādasaubhāgyasaṃyutam |
karañjatailamadhyasthaṃ dinamekaṃ nidhāpayet || 2.126 ||
andhamūṣāsyamadhyasthaṃ dhmāpayetkokilatrayam |
indragopākṛti caiva sattvaṃ bhavati śobhanam || 2.127 ||

[kharpara:: sattvapātana]
nimbudravālpaṭaṅkābhyāṃ mūṣāmadhye nirudhya ca |
tāmrarūpaṃ paridhmātaṃ sattvaṃ muñcati sasyakam || 2.128 ||

[kharpara:: sattvapātana]
śuddhaṃ sasyaṃ śilākrāntaṃ pūrvabheṣajasaṃyutam |
nānāvidhānayogena sattvaṃ muñcati niścitam || 2.129 ||
sattvametatsamādāya kharabhūnāgasattvabhuk |
tanmudrikā kṛtasparśā śūlaghnī tatkṣaṇādbhavet || 2.130 ||
carācaraṃ viṣaṃ bhūtaḍākinīdṛggataṃ jayet |
mudrikeyaṃ vidhātavyā dṛṣṭapratyayakārikā || 2.131 ||
rāmavatsomasenānīrmudrite'pi tathākṣaram |
himālayottare pārśve aśvakarṇo mahādrumaḥ |
tatra śūlaṃ samutpannaṃ tatraiva vilayaṃ gatam || 2.132 ||
mantreṇānena mudrāmbho nipītaṃ saptamantritam |
sadyaḥ śūlaharaṃ proktamiti bhālukibhāṣitam || 2.133 ||
anayā mudrayā taptaṃ tailamagnau suniścitam |
lepitaṃ hanti vegena śūlaṃ yatra kvacidbhavet |
sadyaḥ sūtikaraṃ nāryāḥ sadyo netrarujāpaham || 2.134 ||

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     capalaḥ yaśadaḥ dasteti loke || 2.134;1

____________________


[capala:: subtypes]
gauraḥ śveto'ruṇaḥ kṛṣṇaścapalastu caturvidhaḥ |
hemābhaścaiva tārābho viśeṣādrasabandhanaḥ || 2.135 ||
śeṣau tu madhyau lākṣāvacchīghradrāvau tu niṣphalau |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     athoddeśakramānurodhena tutthavarṇanāntaraṃ capalaṃ varṇayati gaura iti || 2.136 b;1
*     ayaṃ padārtho vyāvahārikanāmnāsmin deśe loke na prasiddhaḥ || 2.136 b;2
*     pāṣāṇaviśeṣo'yaṃ jasadakhanisaṃnihitabhūgarbha upalabhyata ityanumīyate || 2.136 b;3
*     āṅglabhāṣāyāṃ reḍiyamiti nāmnopalabdhaḥ padārtho'yameveti kecit || 2.136 b;4
*     kṛtrimastu paribhāṣādhyāye vakṣyamāṇaḥ || 2.136 b;5

____________________


[capala:: nirukti]
vaṅgavaddravate vahnau capalastena kīrtitaḥ || 2.136 ||

[capala:: medic. properties]
capalo lekhanaḥ snigdho dehalohakaro mataḥ |
rasarājasahāyaḥ syāttiktoṣṇamadhuro mataḥ || 2.137 ||

[capala:: phys. properties]
capalaḥ sphaṭikacchāyaḥ ṣaḍasrī snigdhako guruḥ |

[capala:: medic. properties]
tridoṣaghno'tivṛṣyaśca rasabandhavidhāyakaḥ || 2.138 ||

[capala:: elementof mahārasa]
mahāraseṣu kaiściddhi capalaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 2.139 ||

[capala:: śodhana]
jambīrakarkoṭakaśṛṅgaverairvibhāvanābhiścapalasya śuddhiḥ || 2.140 ||

[capala:: sattvapātana]
śailaṃ tu cūrṇayitvā tu dhānyāmlopaviṣairviṣaiḥ |
piṇḍaṃ baddhvā tu vidhivatpātayeccapalaṃ tathā || 2.141 ||

[rasaka:: subtypes]
rasako dvividhaḥ prokto durduraḥ kāravellakaḥ |
sadalo durduraḥ prokto nirdalaḥ kāravellakaḥ || 2.142 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     rasakasya dvaividhyaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ cāha rasaka iti || 2.142;1
*     rasakaḥ kharparītutthakaviśeṣaḥ yaduktaṃ bhāvaprakāśe |
*     kharparītutthakaṃ tutthādanyattadrasakaṃ smṛtam |
*     ye guṇāstutthake proktāste guṇāḥ rasake smṛtāḥ || 2.142;2
*     iti || 2.142;3
*     sadalaḥ sapattraḥ vaṃśapattraharitālavaditi bhāvaḥ || 2.142;4





*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     athoddeśakramaprāptaṃ rasakaṃ varṇayati rasaka iti || 2.142;1
*     rasako jasadopādānakhanijamṛttikā || 2.142;2
*     tatsattvaṃ jasadatulyamevotpadyate || 2.142;3

____________________


sattvapāte śubhaḥ pūrvo dvitīyaścauṣadhādiṣu |

[rasaka:: medic. properties]
rasakaḥ sarvamehaghnaḥ kaphapittavināśanaḥ |
netrarogakṣayaghnaśca lohapāradarañjanaḥ || 2.143 ||
nāgārjunena saṃdiṣṭau rasaśca rasakāvubhau |
śreṣṭhau siddharasau khyātau dehalohakarau param || 2.144 ||
rasaśca rasakaścobhau yenāgnisahanau kṛtau |
dehalohamayī siddhirdāsī tasya na saṃśayaḥ || 2.145 ||

[rasaka:: śodhana]
kaṭukālābuniryāsa āloḍya rasakaṃ pacet |
śuddhaṃ doṣavinirmuktaṃ pītavarṇaṃ tu jāyate || 2.146 ||

[rasaka:: śodhana]
kharparaḥ parisaṃtaptaḥ saptavāraṃ nimajjitaḥ |
bījapūrarasasyāntarnirmalatvaṃ samaśnute || 2.147 ||

[rasaka:: śodhana]
nṛmūtre vāśvamūtre vā takre vā kāñjike'thavā |
pratāpya majjitaṃ samyakkharparaṃ pariśudhyati || 2.148 ||

[rasaka:: śodhana]
naramūtre sthito māsaṃ rasako rañjayeddhruvam |
śuddhatāmraṃ rasaṃ tāraṃ śuddhasvarṇaprabhaṃ yathā || 2.149 ||

[rasaka:: sattvapātana]
haridrātriphalārālāsindhudhūmaiḥ saṭaṅkaṇaiḥ |
sāruṣkaraiśca pādāṃśaiḥ sāmlaiḥ saṃmardya kharparam || 2.150 ||
liptaṃ vṛntākamūṣāyāṃ śoṣayitvā nirudhya ca |
mūṣāmukhopari nyasya kharparaṃ pradhamettataḥ || 2.151 ||
kharpare prahṛte jvālā bhavennīlā sitā yadi |
tadā saṃdaṃśato mūṣāṃ dhṛtvā kṛtvā tvadhomukhīm || 2.152 ||
śanairāsphālayedbhūmau yathā nālaṃ na bhajyate |
vaṅgābhaṃ patitaṃ sattvaṃ samādāya niyojayet |
evaṃ tricaturairvāraiḥ sarvaṃ sattvaṃ viniḥsaret || 2.153 ||

[rasaka:: sattvapātana]
sābhayājatubhūnāganiśādhūmajaṭaṅkaṇam |
mūkamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ śuddhaṃ sattvaṃ vimuñcati || 2.154 ||

[rasaka:: sattvapātana]
lākṣāguḍāsurīpathyāharidrāsarjaṭaṅkaṇaiḥ |
samyaksaṃcūrṇya tatpakvaṃ godugdhena ghṛtena ca || 2.155 ||
vṛntākamūṣikāmadhye nirudhya guṭikākṛtim |
dhmātvā dhmātvā samākṛṣya ḍhālayitvā śilātale |
sattvaṃ vaṅgākṛtiṃ grāhyaṃ rasakasya manoharam || 2.156 ||

[rasaka:: sattvapātana]
yadvā jalayutāṃ sthālīṃ nikhanetkoṣṭhikodare |
sacchidraṃ tanmukhe mallaṃ tanmukhe'dhomukhīṃ kṣipet || 2.157 ||
mūṣopari śikhitrāṃśca prakṣipya pradhameddṛḍham |
patitaṃ sthālikānīre sattvamādāya yojayet || 2.158 ||

[rasaka:: sattva:: māraṇa]
tatsattvaṃ tālakopetaṃ prakṣipya khalu kharpare |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     kharpare mṛtkaṭāhe ityarthaḥ || 2.159 b;1

____________________


mardayellohadaṇḍena bhasmībhavati niścitam || 2.159 ||

[rasaka:: sattva:: mṛta:: medic. use]
tadbhasma mṛtakāntena samena saha yojayet |
aṣṭaguñjāmitaṃ cūrṇaṃ triphalākvāthasaṃyutam || 2.160 ||
kāntapātrasthitaṃ rātrau tilajaprativāpakam |
niṣevitaṃ nihantyāśu madhumehamapi dhruvam || 2.161 ||
pittaṃ kṣayaṃ ca pāṇḍuṃ ca śvayathuṃ gulmameva ca |
raktagulmaṃ ca nārīṇāṃ pradaraṃ somarogakam || 2.162 ||
yonirogānaśeṣāṃśca viṣamāṃśca jvarānapi |
rajaḥśūlaṃ ca nārīṇāṃ kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ ca hidhmikām || 2.163 ||
