 3
gandhāśmagairikāsīsakāṅkṣītālaśilāñjanam |
kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ cetyuparasāścāṣṭau pāradakarmaṇi || 3.1 ||
pārvatyuvāca |
gandhakasya tu māhātmyaṃ tadguhyaṃ vada me prabho || 3.2 ||
īśvara uvāca |

[sulfur:: myth. origin]
śvetadvīpe purā devi sarvaratnavibhūṣite |
sarvakāmamaye ramye tīre kṣīrapayonidheḥ || 3.3 ||
vidyādharādimukhyābhiraṅganābhiśca yoginām |
siddhāṅganābhiḥ śreṣṭhābhistathaivāpsarasāṃ gaṇaiḥ || 3.4 ||
devāṅganābhī ramyābhiḥ krīḍitābhirmanoharaiḥ |
gītairnṛtyairvicitraiśca vādyairnānāvidhaistathā || 3.5 ||
evaṃ saṃkrīḍamānāyāḥ prābhavatprasṛtaṃ rajaḥ |
tadrajo'tīva suśroṇi sugandhi sumanoharam || 3.6 ||
rajasaścātibāhulyādvāsaste raktatāṃ yayau |
tatra tyaktvā tu tadvastraṃ susnātā kṣīrasāgare || 3.7 ||
vṛtā devāṅganābhistvaṃ kailāsaṃ punarāgatā |
ūrmibhistadrajovastraṃ nītaṃ madhye payonidheḥ || 3.8 ||
evaṃ te śoṇitaṃ bhadre praviṣṭaṃ kṣīrasāgare |
kṣīrābdhimathane caitadamṛtena sahotthitam || 3.9 ||
nijagandhena tānsarvānharṣayansarvadānavān |
tato devagaṇairuktaṃ gandhakākhyo bhavatvayam || 3.10 ||
rasasya bandhanārthāya jāraṇāya bhavatvayam |
ye guṇāḥ pārade proktāste caivātra bhavantviti || 3.11 ||
iti devagaṇaiḥ prītaiḥ purā proktaṃ sureśvari |
tenāyaṃ gandhako nāma vikhyātaḥ kṣitimaṇḍale || 3.12 ||
[gandhakabhedāḥ]
sa cāpi trividho devi śukacañcunibho varaḥ |
madhyamaḥ pītavarṇaḥ syācchuklavarṇo'dhamaḥ priye || 3.13 ||

[gandhakabhedāḥ (2)]
caturdhā gandhako jñeyo varṇaiḥ śvetādibhiḥ khalu |
śveto'tra khaṭikāprokto lepane lohamāraṇe || 3.14 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     khaṭhikā khaḍu cāsiti nāmnā loke prasiddhā || 3.14;1

____________________


tathā cāmalasāraḥ syādyo bhavetpītavarṇavān |
śukapicchaḥ sa eva syācchreṣṭho rasarasāyane || 3.15 ||
raktaśca śukatuṇḍākhyo dhātuvādavidhau varaḥ |
durlabhaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇaśca sa jarāmṛtyunāśanaḥ || 3.16 ||

[gandhakaguṇāḥ]
gandhāśmātirasāyanaḥ sumadhuraḥ pāke kaṭūṣṇo mataḥ kaṇḍūkuṣṭhavisarpadadrudalano dīptānalaḥ pācanaḥ |
āmonmocanaśoṣaṇo viṣaharaḥ sūtendravīryaprado gaurīpuṣpabhavastathā kṛmiharaḥ satyātmakaḥ sūtajit || 3.17 ||

[sulfur:: origin from Bali]
balinā sevitaḥ pūrvaṃ prabhūtabalahetave || 3.18 ||
vāsukiṃ karṣatastasya tanmukhajvālayā drutā |
vasā gandhakagandhāḍhyā sarvato niḥsṛtā tanoḥ || 3.19 ||
gandhakatvaṃ ca samprāptā gandho'bhūtsaviṣaḥ smṛtaḥ |
tasmādbalivasetyukto gandhako'timanoharaḥ || 3.20 ||

[gandhakaśodhana]
payaḥsvinno ghaṭīmātraṃ vāridhauto hi gandhakaḥ |
gavyājyavidruto vastrādgālitaḥ śuddhimṛcchati || 3.21 ||
evaṃ saṃśodhitaḥ so'yaṃ pāṣāṇānambare tyajet |
ghṛte viṣaṃ tuṣākāraṃ svayaṃ piṇḍatvameva ca || 3.22 ||
iti śuddho hi gandhāśmā nāpathyairvikṛtiṃ vrajet |
apathyādanyathā hanyātpītaṃ hālāhalaṃ yathā || 3.23 ||

[sulfur:: śodhana]
gandhako drāvito bhṛṅgarase kṣipto viśudhyati |
tadrasaiḥ saptadhā bhinno gandhakaḥ pariśudhyati || 3.24 ||

[gandhakaśodhana (2)]
sthālyāṃ dugdhaṃ vinikṣipya mukhe vastraṃ nibadhya ca |
gandhakaṃ tatra nikṣipya cūrṇitaṃ sikatākṛti || 3.25 ||
chādayetpṛthudīrgheṇa kharpareṇaiva gandhakam |
jvālayetkharparasyordhvaṃ vanachāṇaistathopalaiḥ || 3.26 ||
dugdhe nipatito gandho galitaḥ pariśudhyati |
śatavāraṃ kṛtaṃ caiva nirgandho jāyate dhruvam || 3.27 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     matāntaramāha sthālyāmiti || 3.27;1
*     jvālayedityatra vahnimiti śeṣaḥ || 3.27;2
*     vanacchāṇaiḥ vanopalaiḥ || 3.27;3
*     upalaiḥ gṛhajātaśuṣkagomayapiṇḍaiḥ || 3.27;4

____________________


[śuddhagandhakaprayoga]
itthaṃ viśuddhastriphalājyabhṛṅgamadhvanvitaḥ śāṇamito hi līḍhaḥ |
gṛdhrākṣitulyaṃ kurute'kṣiyugmaṃ karoti rogojjhitadīrghamāyuḥ || 3.28 ||

[sulfur:: gandhataila]
kalāṃśavyoṣasaṃyuktaṃ gandhakaṃ ślakṣṇacūrṇitam |
aratnimātre vastre tadviprakīrya viveṣṭya tat || 3.29 ||
sūtreṇa veṣṭayitvātha yāmaṃ taile nimajjayet |
dhṛtvā saṃdaṃśato vartimadhyaṃ prajvālayecca tam |
druto nipatito gandho binduśaḥ kācabhājane || 3.30 ||

[sulfur:: gandhataila:: application]
tāṃ drutiṃ prakṣipetpattre nāgavallyāstribindukām |
vallena pramitaṃ svacchaṃ sūtendraṃ ca vimardayet || 3.31 ||
aṅgulyātha sapattrāṃ tāṃ drutiṃ sūtaṃ ca bhakṣayet |
karoti dīpanaṃ tīvraṃ kṣayaṃ pāṇḍuṃ ca nāśayet || 3.32 ||
kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ ca śūlārtigrahaṇīmatidurdharām |
āmaṃ vināśayatyāśu laghutvaṃ prakaroti ca || 3.33 ||

[sulfur:: gandhataila:: application]
ghṛtākte lohapātre tu vidrutaṃ śuddhagandhakam |
ghṛtāktadarvikākṣiptaṃ dviniṣkapramitaṃ bhajet |
hanti kṣayamukhān rogān kuṣṭharogaṃ viśeṣataḥ || 3.34 ||
kṣārāmlatailasauvīravidāhi dvidalaṃ tathā |
śuddhagandhakasevāyāṃ tyajedyogayutena hi || 3.35 ||
gandhakastulyamaricaḥ ṣaḍguṇatriphalānvitaḥ |
ghṛṣṭaḥ śamyākamūlena pītaścākhilakuṣṭhahā || 3.36 ||
tanmūlaṃ salile piṣṭaṃ lepayetpratyahaṃ tanau |
dṛṣṭapratyayayogo'yaṃ sarvatra prativīryavān |
śrīmatā somadevena samyagatra prakīrtitaḥ || 3.37 ||
dviniṣkapramitaṃ gandhaṃ piṣṭvā tailena saṃyutam |
athāpāmārgatoyena satailamaricena hi || 3.38 ||
vilipya sakalaṃ dehaṃ tiṣṭhedgharme tataḥ param |
takrabhaktaṃ ca bhuñjīta tṛtīye prahare khalu || 3.39 ||
bhajedrātrau tathā vahniṃ samutthāya tathā prage |
mahiṣīchagaṇaṃ liptvā snāyācchītena vāriṇā || 3.40 ||
tato'bhyajya ghṛtairdehaṃ snāyādiṣṭoṣṇavāriṇā |
amunā kramayogena vinaśyatyativegataḥ |
durjayā bahukālīnā pāmā kaṇḍuḥ suniścitam || 3.41 ||
gandhakasya prayogāṇāṃ śataṃ tanna prakīrtitam |
granthavistārabhītena somadevena bhūbhujā || 3.42 ||

[sulfur:: gandhataila]
athavārkasnuhīkṣīrairvastraṃ lepyaṃ tu saptadhā |
gandhakaṃ navanītena piṣṭvā vastraṃ lipedghanam || 3.43 ||
tadvartiṃ jvalitāṃ daṃśe dhṛtāṃ kuryādadhomukhīm |
tailaṃ patedadhobhāṇḍe grāhyaṃ yogeṣu yojayet || 3.44 ||

[sulfur:: śuddha:: medic. properties]
śuddhagandho haredrogānkuṣṭhamṛtyujarādikān |
agnikārī mahānuṣṇo vīryavṛddhiṃ karoti ca || 3.45 ||

[gairika:: subtypes]
pāṣāṇagairikaṃ caikaṃ dvitīyaṃ svarṇagairikam || 3.46 ||

[pāṣāṇagairika:: properties]
pāṣāṇagairikaṃ proktaṃ kaṭhinaṃ tāmravarṇakam |

[svarṇagairika:: properties]
atyantaśoṇitaṃ snigdhaṃ masṛṇaṃ svarṇagairikam || 3.47 ||

[svarṇagairika:: medic. properties]
svādu snigdhaṃ himaṃ netryaṃ kaṣāyaṃ raktapittanut |
hidhmāvamiviṣaghnaṃ ca raktaghnaṃ svarṇagairikam |

[pāṣāṇagairika:: medic. properties]
pāṣāṇagairikaṃ cānyatpūrvasmādalpakaṃ guṇaiḥ || 3.48 ||

[gairika:: śodhana]
gairikaṃ tu gavāṃ dugdhairbhāvitaṃ śuddhimṛcchati || 3.49 ||

[gairika:: sattva]
gairikaṃ sattvarūpaṃ hi nandinā parikīrtitam || 3.50 ||
kairapyuktaṃ patetsattvaṃ kṣārāmlaklinnagairikāt |
upatiṣṭhati sūtendramekatvaṃ guṇavattaram || 3.51 ||

[kāsīsa:: subtypes]
kāsīsaṃ vālukādyekaṃ puṣpapūrvamathāparam || 3.52 ||

[kāsīsa:: medic. properties]
kṣārāmlāgarudhūmābhaṃ soṣṇavīryaṃ viṣāpaham |
vālukāpuṣpakāsīsaṃ śvitraghnaṃ keśarañjanam || 3.53 ||

[puṣpakāsīsa:: medic. properties]
puṣpādikāsīsamatipraśastaṃ soṣṇaṃ kaṣāyāmlamatīva netryam |
viṣānilaśleṣmagadavraṇaghnaṃ śvitrakṣayaghnaṃ kacarañjanaṃ ca || 3.54 ||

[kāsīsa:: śodhana]
sakṛdbhṛṅgāmbunā klinnaṃ kāsīsaṃ nirmalaṃ bhavet || 3.55 ||

[kāsīsa:: sattvapātana]
tuvarīsattvavatsattvametasyāpi samāharet || 3.56 ||

[kāsīsa:: śodhana]
kāsīsaṃ śuddhimāpnoti pittaiśca rajasā striyāḥ || 3.57 ||
balinā hatakāsīsaṃ krāntaṃ kāsīsamāritam |
ubhayaṃ samabhāgaṃ hi triphalāvellasaṃyutam || 3.58 ||
viṣamāṃśaghṛtakṣaudraplutaṃ śāṇamitaṃ prage |
sevitaṃ hanti vegena śvitraṃ pāṇḍukṣayāmayam || 3.59 ||
gulmaplīhagadaṃ śūlaṃ mūlarogaṃ viśeṣataḥ |
rasāyanavidhānena sevitaṃ vatsarāvadhi || 3.60 ||
āmasaṃśoṣaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ mandāgniparidīpanam |
palitaṃ valibhiḥ sārdhaṃ vināśayati niścitam || 3.61 ||

[tuvarī]
saurāṣṭrāśmani sambhūtā sā tuvarī matā |
vastreṣu lipyate yāsau mañjiṣṭhārāgabandhinī || 3.62 ||

[tuvarī:: subtypes]
phaṭakī phullikā ceti dvitīyā parikīrtitā || 3.63 ||

[phaṭakī:: medic. properties]
īṣatpītā guruḥ snigdhā pītikā viṣanāśanī |
vraṇakuṣṭhaharā sarvakuṣṭhaghnī ca viśeṣataḥ || 3.64 ||

[phullikā:: medic. properties]
nirbhārā śubhravarṇā ca snigdhā sāmlāparā matā |
sā phullatuvarī proktā lepāttāmraṃ caredayaḥ || 3.65 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     tadbhedāvāha sphaṭikī pītiketi || 3.65;1
*     sphaṭikīlepācchatavāraṃ tāmrapatraṃ lohapatraṃ prati vā liptā satī tatpaścātcaretpāradena prayojyakartrā cārayedbhakṣitāṃ kārayedityarthaḥ || 3.65;2
*     yato jāraṇāyāmasyā viḍadravyatvenopayogāt || 3.65;3
*     antarbhāvitaṇyartho'tra caradhātuḥ || 3.65;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     lepāditi || 3.65;1
*     phullatuvarīlepena tāmraṃ lauhāṃśamujhati tāmre yaḥ lauhāṃśaḥ vidyate sa nirgacchatītyarthaḥ tāmraṃ lauhavatkāṭhinyaṃ gacchatītyartho vā || 3.65;2

____________________


kāṅkṣī kaṣāyā kaṭukāmlakaṇṭhyā keśyā vraṇaghnī viṣanāśanī ca |
śvitrāpahā netrahitā tridoṣaśāntipradā pāradajāraṇī ca || 3.66 ||

[tuvarī:: śodhana]
tuvarī kāñjike kṣiptvā tridinācchuddhimṛcchati || 3.67 ||

[tuvarī:: sattvapātana]
kṣārāmlairmarditā dhmātā sattvaṃ muñcati niścitam || 3.68 ||

[tuvarī:: sattvapātana]
gopittena śataṃ vārān saurāṣṭrāṃ bhāvayettataḥ |
dhamitvā pātayetsattvaṃ krāmaṇaṃ cātiguhyakam || 3.69 ||

[haritāla:: subtypes]
haritālaṃ dvidhā proktaṃ pattrādyaṃ piṇḍasaṃjñakam || 3.70 ||

[pattratālaka]
svarṇavarṇaṃ guru snigdhaṃ tanupattraṃ ca bhāsuram |
tatpattratālakaṃ proktaṃ bahupattraṃ rasāyanam || 3.71 ||

[piṇḍatālaka]
niṣpattraṃ piṇḍasadṛśaṃ svalpasattvaṃ tathāguru |
strīpuṣpaharaṇaṃ tattu guṇālpaṃ piṇḍatālakam || 3.72 ||

[haritāla:: medic. properties]
śleṣmaraktaviṣavātabhūtanutkevalaṃ ca khalu puṣpahṛtstriyaḥ |
snigdhamuṣṇakaṭukaṃ ca dīpanaṃ kuṣṭhahāri haritālamucyate || 3.73 ||

[haritāla:: śodhana]
snigdhaṃ kūṣmāṇḍatoye vā tilakṣārajale api vā |
toye vā cūrṇasaṃyukte dolāyantreṇa śudhyati || 3.74 ||

[haritāla:: aśuddha:: medic. properties]
aśuddhaṃ tālamāyughnaṃ kaphamārutamehakṛt |
tāpasphoṭāṅgasaṃkocaṃ kurute tena śodhayet || 3.75 ||

[haritāla:: śodhana]
tālakaṃ kaṇaśaḥ kṛtvā daśāṃśena ca ṭaṅkaṇam |
jambīrotthadravaiḥ kṣālyaṃ kāñjikaiḥ kṣālayettataḥ || 3.76 ||
vastre caturguṇe baddhvā dolāyantre dinaṃ pacet |
sacūrṇenāranālena dinaṃ kūṣmāṇḍaje rase |
svedyaṃ vā śālmalītoyaistālakaṃ śuddhimāpnuyāt || 3.77 ||

[haritāla:: śodhana]
madhutulye ghanībhūte kaṣāye brahmamūlaje |
trivāraṃ tālakaṃ bhāvyaṃ piṣṭvā mūtre'tha māhiṣe || 3.78 ||
upalairdaśabhirdeyaṃ puṭaṃ ruddhvātha peṣayet |
evaṃ dvādaśadhā pācyaṃ śuddhaṃ yogeṣu yojayet || 3.79 ||

[haritāla:: sattvapātana]
kulitthakvāthasaubhāgyamahiṣyājyamadhuplutam |
sthālyāṃ kṣiptvā vidadhyācca tvamlena chidrayoginā || 3.80 ||
samyaṅnirudhya śikhinaṃ jvālayetkramavardhitam |
ekapraharamātraṃ hi randhramācchādya gomayaiḥ || 3.81 ||
yāmānte chidramudghāṭya dṛṣṭe dhūme ca pāṇḍure |
śītāṃ sthālīṃ samuttārya sattvamutkṛṣya cāharet || 3.82 ||
sarvapāṣāṇasattvānāṃ prakārāḥ santi koṭiśaḥ |
granthavistārabhītyāto likhitā na mayā khalu || 3.83 ||

[haritāla:: sattvapātana]
palālakaṃ raverdugdhairdinamekaṃ vimardayet |
kṣiptvā ṣoḍaśikātaile miśrayitvā tataḥ pacet || 3.84 ||
anāvṛtapradeśe ca saptayāmāvadhi dhruvam |
svāṅgaśītamadhasthaṃ ca sattvaṃ śvetaṃ samāharet || 3.85 ||

[haritāla:: sattvapātana]
chāgalasyātha bālasya balinā ca samanvitam |
tālakaṃ divasadvaṃdvaṃ mardayitvātiyatnataḥ || 3.86 ||
yuktaṃ drāvaṇavargeṇa kācakupyāṃ vinikṣipet |
tridhā tāṃ ca mṛdā liptvā pariśoṣya kharātape || 3.87 ||
tataḥ kharparakacchidre tāmardhāṃ caiva kūpikām |
praveśya jvālayedagniṃ dvādaśapraharāvadhi |
kūpikaṇṭhasthitaṃ śītaṃ śuddhaṃ sattvaṃ samāharet || 3.88 ||

[haritāla:: sattvapātana]
palārdhapramitaṃ tālaṃ baddhvā vastre site dṛḍhe |
balinālipya yatnena trivāraṃ pariśoṣya ca || 3.89 ||
drāvite triphale tāmre kṣipettālakapoṭalīm |
bhasmanā chādayecchīghraṃ tāmreṇāveṣṭitaṃ sitam |
mṛdulaṃ sattvamādadyātproktaṃ rasarasāyane || 3.90 ||

[manaḥśilā:: subtypes]
manaḥśilā tridhā proktā śyāmāṅgī kaṇavīrakā |
khaṇḍākhyā ceti tadrūpaṃ vivicya parikathyate || 3.91 ||

[manaḥśilā:: śyāmāṅgī]
śyāmā raktā sagaurā ca bhārāḍhyā śyāmikā matā |

[manaḥśilā:: kaṇavīraka]
tejasvinī ca nirgaurā tāmrābhā kaṇavīrakā || 3.92 ||

[manaḥśilā:: khaṇḍa]
cūrṇībhūtātiraktāṅgī sabhārā khaṇḍapūrvikā |
uttaroktaguṇaiḥ śreṣṭhā bhūrisattvā prakīrtitā || 3.93 ||

[manaḥśilāguṇāḥ]
manaḥśilā sarvarasāyanāgryā tiktā kaṭūṣṇā kaphavātahantrī |
sattvātmikā bhūtaviṣāgnimāndyakaṇḍūtikāsakṣayahāriṇī ca || 3.94 ||

[manaḥśilā:: aśuddha:: medic. properties]
aśmarīṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ ca aśuddhā kurute śilā |
mandāgniṃ malabandhaṃ ca śuddhā sarvarujāpahā || 3.95 ||

[manaḥśilāśodhanam]
agastyapattratoyena bhāvitā saptavārakam |
śṛṅgaverarasairvāpi viśudhyati manaḥśilā || 3.96 ||

[manaḥśilā:: śodhana]
jayantībhṛṅgarājottharaktāgastyarasaiḥ śilām |
dolāyantre pacedyāmaṃ yāmaṃ chāgotthamūtrakaiḥ |
kṣālayedāranālena sarvarogeṣu yojayet || 3.97 ||

[manaḥśilāsattvapātana (1)]
aṣṭamāṃśena kiṭṭena guḍaguggulusarpiṣā |
koṣṭhyāṃ ruddhvā dṛḍhaṃ dhmātā sattvaṃ muñcenmanaḥśilā || 3.98 ||

[manaḥśilāsattvapātana (2)]
bhūnāgadhautasaubhāgyamadanaiśca vimarditaiḥ |
kāravallīdalāmbhobhirmūṣāṃ kṛtvātra nikṣipet || 3.99 ||
śilāṃ kṣārāmlaniṣpiṣṭāṃ pradhamettadanantaram |
kokilādvayamātraṃ hi dhmānātsattvaṃ tyajatyasau || 3.100 ||

[añjana:: subtypes]
sauvīramañjanaṃ proktaṃ rasāñjanamataḥ param |
srotoñjanaṃ tadanyacca puṣpāñjanakameva ca |
nīlāñjanaṃ ca teṣāṃ hi svarūpamiha varṇyate || 3.101 ||

[sauvīra]
sauvīramañjanaṃ dhūmraṃ raktapittaharaṃ himam |
viṣahidhmākṣirogaghnaṃ vraṇaśodhanaropaṇam || 3.102 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     sauvīraṃ kṛṣṇavarṇasurmā iti lokabhāṣāyām || 3.102;1

____________________


[rasāñjana]
rasāñjanaṃ ca pītābhaṃ viṣavaktragadāpaham |
śvāsahidhmāpahaṃ varṇyaṃ vātapittāsranāśanam || 3.103 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     rasāñjanamiti || 3.103;1
*     etadrasoditi brijabhāṣāyāmuttaradeśe prasiddham || 3.103;2
*     etadrītikiṭṭajanyaṃ dāruharidrākaṣāyājadugdhapākajanyaṃ tu rasāñjanamityapi vadanti || 3.103;3

____________________


[srotoñjana]
srotoñjanaṃ himaṃ snigdhaṃ kaṣāyaṃ svādu lekhanam |
netryaṃ hidhmāviṣachardikaphapittāsraroganut || 3.104 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     srotoñjanamiti || 3.104;1
*     śvetavarṇasurmā iti loke prasiddham || 3.104;2

____________________


[puṣpāñjana]
puṣpāñjanaṃ sitaṃ snigdhaṃ himaṃ sarvākṣiroganut |
atidurdharahidhmāghnaṃ viṣajvaragadāpaham || 3.105 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     puṣpāñjanamiti || 3.105;1
*     etadrītipuṣpajanyam || 3.105;2
*     jastaphūliti mahārāṣṭrabhāṣāyāṃ prasiddham || 3.105;3

____________________


[nīlāñjana]
nīlāñjanaṃ guru snigdhaṃ netryaṃ doṣatrayāpaham |
rasāyanaṃ suvarṇaghnaṃ lohamārdavakārakam || 3.106 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     nīlāñjanamiti || 3.106;1
*     nīlavarṇasurmā iti loke prasiddhaḥ || 3.106;2

____________________


[añjana:: śodhana]
añjanāni viśudhyanti bhṛṅgarājanijadravaiḥ || 3.107 ||

[añjana:: sattvapātana]
manohvāsattvavatsattvamañjanānāṃ samāharet || 3.108 ||

[srotoñjana:: phys. properties]
valmīkaśikharākāraṃ bhaṅge nīlotpaladyuti |
ghṛṣṭaṃ tu gairikacchāyaṃ srotojaṃ lakṣayedbudhaḥ || 3.109 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     valmīkaśikharākāraṃ valmīkavatunnatāvanatabahuśikharaviśiṣṭam || 3.109;1

____________________


[srotoñjana:: preparation forrasabandhana]
gośakṛdrasamūtreṣu ghṛtakṣaudravasāsu ca |
bhāvitaṃ bahuśastacca śīghraṃ badhnāti sūtakam || 3.110 ||

[rasāñjana:: śodhana]
sūryāvartādiyogena śuddhimeti rasāñjanam || 3.111 ||

[srotoñjana:: sattvapātana]
rājāvartakavatsattvaṃ grāhyaṃ srotoñjanādapi || 3.112 ||

[kaṅkuṣṭha]
himavatpādaśikhare kaṅkuṣṭhamupajāyate |

[kaṅkuṣṭha:: subtypes]
tatraikaṃ nālikākhyaṃ hi tadanyadreṇukaṃ matam || 3.113 ||

[nālika]
pītaprabhaṃ guru snigdhaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ kaṅkuṣṭhamādimam |

[reṇuka]
śyāmapītaṃ laghu tyaktasattvaṃ neṣṭaṃ hi reṇukam || 3.114 ||

[kaṅkuṣṭha:: origin froṃ diff. animals]
kecidvadanti kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ sadyojātasya dantinaḥ |
varcaśca śyāmapītābhaṃ recanaṃ parikathyate || 3.115 ||
katicittejivāhānāṃ nālaṃ kaṅkuṣṭhasaṃjñakam |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     matāntaramāha katiciditi || 3.116 b;1
*     katicidvadanti kiṃ tejivāhānāṃ prabalavegasāmarthyaviśiṣṭāśvānāṃ nālaṃ sadyojātānāṃ teṣāṃ nābhinālaṃ kaṅkuṣṭhamiti || 3.116 b;2

____________________


vadanti śvetapītābhaṃ tadatīva virecanam || 3.116 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha kramaprāptaṃ kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ varṇayati himavaditi || 3.116;1
*     sa ca pītavarṇaḥ || 3.116;2
*     ślakṣṇānekasacākacikyaphalakaviśiṣṭaḥ khanijaḥ kṣudrapāṣāṇaḥ prāṇijaśca || 3.116;3
*     ubhayamapi dvividhaṃ nalikā reṇukaśceti khanijabhedau || 3.116;4

____________________


rase rasāyanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ niḥsattvaṃ bahuvaikṛtam || 3.117 ||

[kaṅkuṣṭha:: medic. properties]
kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ tiktakaṭukaṃ vīryoṣṇaṃ cātirecanam |
vraṇodāvartaśūlārtigulmaplīhagudārtinut || 3.118 ||

[mahārasa, uparasa:: śodhana, sattvapātana]
sūryāvartakakadalī vandhyā kośātakī ca suradālī |
śigruśca vajrakando niraṅkaṇā kākamācī ca || 3.119 ||
āsāmekarasena tu lavaṇakṣārāmlabhāvitaṃ bahuśaḥ |
śudhyanti rasoparasā dhmātā muñcanti sattvāni || 3.120 ||

[kaṅkuṣṭha:: śodhana]
kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ śuddhimāyāti tridhā śuṇṭhyambubhāvitam || 3.121 ||

[kaṅkuṣṭha:: sattvapātana]
sattvākarṣo'sya na prokto yasmātsattvamayaṃ hi tat || 3.122 ||
bhajedenaṃ virekārthaṃ grāhibhiryavamātrayā |
nāśayedāmapūrtiṃ ca virecya kṣaṇamātrataḥ || 3.123 ||
bhakṣitaḥ saha tāmbūlairviricyāsūnvināśayet || 3.124 ||
barburīmūlikākvāthajīrasaubhāgyakaṃ samam |
kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ viṣanāśāya bhūyo bhūyaḥ pibennaraḥ || 3.125 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     gaurīpāṣāṇaḥ śuklapāṣāṇaḥ phulkhaḍi iti loke || 3.125;1



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     kampillaḥ kṣudrapāṣāṇaviśeṣaḥ kapileti nāmnā loke prasiddho gaurīpāṣāṇo dāruṇaviṣarūpo'yaṃ pāṣāṇaviśeṣaḥ somala iti mahārāṣṭrabhāṣāyāṃ prasiddhaḥ || 3.125;1

____________________


[sādhāraṇarasāḥ]
kampillaścapalo gaurīpāṣāṇo navasārakaḥ |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     vahnijāraḥ svanāmakhyātaḥ paścimasāgarasambhūta auṣadhiviśeṣaḥ || 3.126 b;1



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     agnijāro bahirarṇavojjhito viśiṣṭanakrajarāyuḥ || 3.126 b;1

____________________


kapardo vahnijāraśca girisindūrahiṅgulau || 3.126 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     girisindūraḥ parvatapāṣāṇodare raktavarṇaḥ padārthaviśeṣaḥ || 3.126;1
*     hiṅgulaścūrṇapāradaḥ svanāmnaiva loke prasiddhaḥ || 3.126;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     mṛddāraśṛṅgaṃ sīsakasamutpannaḥ pārvatīyadhātuviśeṣaḥ || 3.126;1



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     moddāraśṛṅgaṃ murdā¡śiṃgeti loke prasiddham || 3.126;1

____________________


modāraśṛṅgamityaṣṭau sādhāraṇarasāḥ smṛtāḥ |
rasasiddhakarāḥ proktā nāgārjunapuraḥsaraiḥ || 3.127 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     candrikāḍhyaḥ cākacakyaviśiṣṭaḥ sacandrābhravadujjvalakaṇābahulaḥ ityarthaḥ || 3.127;1

____________________


[kampillakaḥ]
iṣṭikācūrṇasaṃkāśaścandrikāḍhyo'tirecanaḥ |
saurāṣṭradeśe cotpannaḥ sa hi kampillakaḥ smṛtaḥ || 3.128 ||

[kampilla:: medic. properties]
pittavraṇādhmānavibandhanighnaḥ śleṣmodarārtikṛmigulmavairī |
mūlāmaśophajvaraśūlahārī kampillako recyagadāpahārī || 3.129 ||

[gaurīpāṣāṇakaḥ]
gaurīpāṣāṇakaḥ pīto vikaṭo hatacūrṇakaḥ |
sphaṭikābhaśca śaṅkhābho haridrābhastrayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 3.130 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     gaurīpāṣāṇasya paryāyān varṇabhedena traividhyaṃ cāha gaurīti || 3.130;1
*     pītavikaṭahatacūrṇakāḥ iti ime trayaḥ paryāyāḥ || 3.130;2
*     pūrva iti || 3.130;3
*     pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ haridrābhagaurīpāṣāṇātśaṅkhābhaḥ śaṅkhābhapāṣāṇātsphaṭikābhaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ || 3.130;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     trividhasya tasya svarūpamāha yaḥ kiṃcitpītavarṇo vikaṭakarāla āhataḥ saṃcūrṇarūpo bhavati sa gaurīpāṣāṇa ityucyate || 3.130;1
*     ayameva sphaṭikasamānacākacikyaviśiṣṭaḥ || 3.130;2
*     etadviśeṣaṇaṃ dvitīyabhedasyāpi sambhavati || 3.130;3
*     dvitīyaḥ śaṅkhābhaḥ so'pi cākacikyaviśiṣṭaḥ || 3.130;4
*     tṛtīyabhedāpanno yaḥ sa tu haridrābho haridrāsamānaḥ pūrṇapītavarṇaḥ || 3.130;5
*     trividhamadhya uttarottarātpūrvapūrvaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ || 3.130;6

____________________


[gaurīpāṣāṇa:: śodhana]
pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ guṇaiḥ śreṣṭhaḥ kāravallīphale kṣipet |
svedayeddhaṇḍikāmadhye śuddho bhavati mūṣakaḥ || 3.131 ||

[gaurīpāṣāṇa:: sattvapātana]
tālavadgrāhayetsattvaṃ śuddhaṃ śubhraṃ prayojayet || 3.132 ||

[gaurīpāṣāṇa:: medic. properties]
rasabandhakaraḥ snigdho doṣaghno rasavīryakṛt || 3.133 ||

[navasāra:: production]
karīrapīlukāṣṭheṣu pacyamāneṣu codbhavaḥ |
kṣāro'sau navasāraḥ syāccūlikālavaṇābhidhaḥ || 3.134 ||
iṣṭikādahane jātaṃ pāṇḍuraṃ lavaṇaṃ laghu |
taduktaṃ navasārākhyaṃ cūlikālavaṇaṃ ca tat || 3.135 ||

[navasāra:: alchem. properties]
rasendrajāraṇaṃ lohadrāvaṇaṃ jaṭharāgnikṛt |

[navasāra:: medic. properties]
gulmaplīhāsyaśoṣaghnaṃ bhuktamāṃsādijāraṇam |
viḍākhyaṃ ca tridoṣaghnaṃ cūlikālavaṇaṃ matam || 3.136 ||

[varāṭikā]
pītābhā granthikā pṛṣṭhe dīrghavṛttā varāṭikā |
rasavaidyairvinirdiṣṭā sā carācarasaṃjñikā || 3.137 ||

[varāṭika:: weight]
sārdhaniṣkabharā śreṣṭhā niṣkabhārā ca madhyamā |
pādonaniṣkabhārā ca kaniṣṭhā parikīrtitā || 3.138 ||

[varāṭikā:: medic. properties]
pariṇāmādiśūlaghnī grahaṇīkṣayanāśinī |
kaṭūṣṇā dīpanī vṛṣyā netryā vātakaphāpahā |

[varāṭikā:: alchem. properties]
rasendrajāraṇe proktā viḍadravyeṣu śasyate || 3.139 ||

[varāṭikā:: medic. propertiesof badspecimens]
tadanye tu varāṭāḥ syurguravaḥ śleṣmapittalāḥ || 3.140 ||

[varāṭikā:: śodhana]
varāṭāḥ kāñjike svinnā yāmācchuddhimavāpnuyuḥ || 3.141 ||

[agnijāra]
samudreṇāgninakrasya jarāyurbahirujjhitaḥ |
saṃśuṣko bhānutāpena so'gnijāra iti smṛtaḥ || 3.142 ||

[agnijāra:: medic. properties]
agnijārastridoṣaghno dhanurvātādivātanut |
vardhano rasavīryasya dīpano jāraṇastathā || 3.143 ||

[agnijāra:: śodhana]
tadabdhikṣārasaṃśuddhaṃ tasmācchuddhirna hīṣyate || 3.144 ||

[girisindūra]
mahāgiriṣu cālpīyaḥpāṣāṇāntaḥsthito rasaḥ |
śuṣkaśoṇaḥ sa nirdiṣṭo girisindūrasaṃjñayā || 3.145 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     girisindūrasya svarūpalakṣaṇamāha maheti || 3.145;1
*     himālayādibṛhatparvatāntarvartikṣudrapāṣāṇadvayamadhyanisṛtaḥ raktavarṇarasaviśeṣaḥ śuṣkībhūtaḥ girisindūra iti khyātaḥ || 3.145;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha girisindūramāha mahāgiriṣviti || 3.145;1
*     alpīyo'tyalpaḥ || 3.145;2
*     ayaṃ loke prāyo nopalabhyate || 3.145;3
*     kecittu kāmiyāṃ sindūra iti nāmnā prasiddho'yaṃ padārtho'tiraktavarṇa iti vadanti || 3.145;4
*     loke'tiprasiddhastu nāgasaṃbhava eva || 3.145;5
*     sa eva cātra grāhya iti rāvaṇamatam || 3.145;6
*     sindūro nāgasaṃbhava iti tadvacanāditi || 3.145;7

____________________


[girisindūra:: medic. properties]
tridoṣaśamanaṃ bhedi rasabandhanamagrimam |
dehalohakaraṃ netryaṃ girisindūramīritam || 3.146 ||

[hiṅgula]
hiṅgulaḥ śukatuṇḍākhyo haṃsapākastathāparaḥ || 3.147 ||

[hiṅgula:: śukatuṇḍa]
prathamo'lpaguṇastatra carmāraḥ sa nigadyate || 3.148 ||

[hiṅgula:: haṃsapāka]
śvetarekhaḥ pravālābho haṃsapākaḥ sa īritaḥ || 3.149 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     athoddeśakramaprāptaṃ hiṅgulaṃ varṇayati hiṅgula iti || 3.149;1
*     ayaṃ rasaḥ pāradakhanijamṛdviśeṣaḥ || 3.149;2
*     ata eva prathamādhyāya uktam |
*     tāṃ mṛdaṃ pātanāyantre pātayanti rasaṃ tataḥ |
*     iti || 3.149;3
*     kṛtrimo'pi loke dṛśyate pāradagandhakanavasāgarapākajanyaḥ || 3.149;4
*     khanijo'yaṃ dvividhaḥ || 3.149;5
*     tasya cottamasya lakṣaṇamāha śvetareṣa iti || 3.149;6
*     sa ca haṃsapāka iti nāmnā kathitaḥ pākena vyavasthitaḥ || 3.149;7
*     śvetaraktavarṇaviśiṣṭatvāt || 3.149;8
*     ayamuttamaḥ || 3.149;9
*     prathamastu hīnaśvetareṣo'lpaguṇaḥ || 3.149;10
*     sa tu carmāra iti nigadyate || 3.149;11

____________________


[hiṅgula:: medic. properties]
hiṅgulaḥ sarvadoṣaghno dīpano'tirasāyanaḥ |
sarvarogaharo vṛṣyo jāraṇāyātiśasyate || 3.150 ||

[hiṅgulākṛṣṭa]
etasmādāhṛtaḥ sūto jīrṇagandhasamo guṇaiḥ || 3.151 ||

[hiṅgulākṛṣṭa:: śodhana]
saptakṛtvārdrakadrāvairlakucasyāmbunāpi vā |
śoṣito bhāvayitvā ca nirdoṣo jāyate khalu || 3.152 ||

[hiṅgula:: gold-production]
kimatra citraṃ daradaḥ subhāvitaḥ kṣīreṇa meṣyā bahuśo'mlavargaiḥ |
evaṃ suvarṇaṃ bahugharmatāpitaṃ karoti sākṣādvarakuṅkumaprabham || 3.153 ||

[hiṅgula:: sattvapātana]
daradaḥ pātanāyantre pātitaśca jalāśraye |
tatsattvaṃ sūtasaṃkāśaṃ pātayennātra saṃśayaḥ || 3.154 ||

[mṛddāraśṛṅgaka]
sadalaṃ pītavarṇaṃ ca bhavedgurjaramaṇḍale |
arbudasya gireḥ pārśve jātaṃ mṛddāraśṛṅgakam || 3.155 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     mṛddāraśṛṅgasya lakṣaṇamutpattisthānaṃ cāha sadalamiti || 3.155;1
*     vaṃśapattraharitālavatpaṭalaviśiṣṭam || 3.155;2
*     mṛddāraśṛṅgasya paryāyādikaṃ nighaṇṭvādau anyatra vā kutrāpi granthe na paridṛśyate paraṃ tu asmaddeśe yanmudrāśaṅkha iti nāmnā prasiddhaṃ paścimadeśe tatmurdārśiṅiti nāmnā tatratyairabhidhīyate ato manye mṛddāraśṛṅgakaṃ mudrāśaṅkha eva iti || 3.155;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     gurjaramaṇḍale gurjaradeśe || 3.155;1
*     arbudagireḥ pārśve nāgakhanisthānabhūte ca jātamutpannaṃ yadviśiṣṭaṃ kṣudrapāṣāṇātmakaṃ dravyaṃ sadalapītavarṇātmakaṃ tanmṛddāraśṛṅganāmnā prathitaṃ bhavet || 3.155;2
*     tadguṇānāha sīsasattvamiti || 3.155;3
*     sīsasattvasyopādānaṃ kāraṇam || 3.155;4

____________________


[mṛddāraśṛṅga:: medic. properties]
sīsasattvaṃ guru śleṣmaśamanaṃ puṃgadāpaham |
rasabandhanamutkṛṣṭaṃ keśarañjanamuttamam || 3.156 ||

[sādhāraṇarasa:: śodhana]
sādhāraṇarasāḥ sarve mātuluṅgārdrakāmbunā |
trirātraṃ bhāvitāḥ śuṣkā bhaveyurdoṣavarjitāḥ || 3.157 ||
yāni kāni ca sattvāni tāni śudhyantyaśeṣataḥ |
dhmātāni śuddhivargeṇa milanti ca parasparam || 3.158 ||
iti karavālabhairavaḥ |

[rājāvarta]
rājāvarto'lparaktorunīlikāmiśritaprabhaḥ |
gurutvamasṛṇaḥ śreṣṭhastadanyo madhyamaḥ smṛtaḥ || 3.159 ||

[rājāvarta:: medic. properties]
pramehakṣayadurnāmapāṇḍuśleṣmānilāpahaḥ |
dīpanaḥ pācano vṛṣyo rājāvarto rasāyanaḥ || 3.160 ||

[rājāvarta:: śodhana]
nimbudravaiḥ sagomūtraiḥ sakṣāraiḥ sveditāḥ khalu |
dvitrivāreṇa śudhyanti rājāvartādidhātavaḥ || 3.161 ||

[rājāvarta:: śodhana]
śirīṣapuṣpārdrarasai rājāvartaṃ viśodhayet || 3.162 ||

[rājāvarta:: māraṇa]
luṅgāmbugandhakopeto rājāvarto vicūrṇitaḥ |
puṭanātsaptavāreṇa rājāvarto mṛto bhavet || 3.163 ||

[rājāvarta:: sattvapātana]
rājāvartasya cūrṇaṃ tu kunaṭīghṛtamiśritam |
vipacedāyase pātre mahiṣīkṣīrasaṃyutam || 3.164 ||
saubhāgyapañcagavyena piṇḍībaddhaṃ tu jārayet |
dhmāpitaṃ khadirāṅgāraiḥ sattvaṃ muñcati śobhanam || 3.165 ||

[gairika:: śodhana]
anena kramayogena gairikaṃ vimalaṃ bhavet |

[gairika:: sattvapātana]
kramātpītaṃ ca raktaṃ ca sattvaṃ patati śobhanam || 3.166 ||


________________________________________________________


 4
[maṇayaḥ]
maṇayo'pi ca vijñeyāḥ sūtabandhasya kārakāḥ || 4.1 ||
vaikrāntaḥ sūryakāntaśca hīrakaṃ mauktikaṃ maṇiḥ || 4.2 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     rājāvarto rāuṭī iti brijabhāṣāyām || 4.2;1


candrakāntastathā caiva rājāvartaśca saptamaḥ |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     garuḍodgārakaṃ tārkṣyaṃ marakata ityaparaparyāyadvayam || 4.3 b;1
*     pannā iti loke prasiddham || 4.3 b;2

____________________


garuḍodgārakaścaiva jñātavyā maṇayastvamī || 4.3 ||
----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     puṣparāgaṃ pukhrājiti loke prasiddham || 4.4 b;1



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     mahānīlamindranīlam || 4.4 b;1



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     padmarāgaṃ māṇikyam || 4.4 b;1

____________________


puṣparāgaṃ mahānīlaṃ padmarāgaṃ pravālakam |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     vaiḍūryaṃ tadyadākāśe sajalameghaśabdādaṅkuraviśiṣṭaṃ bhavati || 4.4 b;1
*     ata evāsyābhralohamiti paryāyāntaram || 4.4 b;2
*     etatpirojā iti loke prasiddham || 4.4 b;3

____________________


vaiḍūryaṃ ca tathā nīlamete ca maṇayo matāḥ |
yatnataḥ saṃgrahītavyā rasabandhasya kāraṇāt || 4.4 ||

[pañcaratna (?)]
padmarāgendranīlākhyau tathā marakatottamaḥ |
puṣparāgaḥ savajrākhyaḥ pañca ratnavarāḥ smṛtāḥ || 4.5 ||

[jewels:: navagraha]
māṇikyamuktāphalavidrumāṇi tārkṣyaṃ ca puṣpaṃ bhiduraṃ ca nīlam |
gomedakaṃ cātha vidūrakaṃ ca krameṇa ratnāni navagrahāṇām || 4.6 ||
grahānumaitryā kuruvindapuṣpapravālamuktāphalatārkṣyavajram |
nīlākhyagomedavidūrakaṃ ca krameṇa mudrādhṛtamiṣṭasiddhyai || 4.7 ||
rase rasāyane dāne dhāraṇe devatārcane |
surakṣyāṇi sujātīni ratnānyuktāni siddhaye || 4.8 ||

[māṇikya]
māṇikyaṃ padmarāgākhyaṃ dvitīyaṃ nīlagandhi ca || 4.9 ||

[māṇikya:: parīkṣā]
kuśeśayadalacchāyaṃ svacchaṃ snigdhaṃ mahatsphuṭam |
vṛttāyataṃ samaṃ gātraṃ māṇikyaṃ śreṣṭhamucyate || 4.10 ||

[māṇikya:: nīlagandhi]
nīlaṃ gaṅgāmbusambhūtaṃ nīlagarbhāruṇacchavi |
pūrvamāṇikyavacchreṣṭhamāṇikyaṃ nīlagandhi tat || 4.11 ||

[māṇikya:: parīkṣā]
randhrakārkaśyamālinyaraukṣyāvaiśadyasaṃyutam |
cipiṭaṃ laghu vakraṃ ca māṇikyaṃ duṣṭamaṣṭadhā || 4.12 ||

[māṇikya:: medic. properties]
māṇikyaṃ dīpanaṃ vṛṣyaṃ kaphavātakṣayārtinut |
bhūtavetālapāpaghnaṃ karmajavyādhināśanam || 4.13 ||

[mauktika:: parīkṣā]
hlādi śvetaṃ laghu snigdhaṃ raśmivannirmalaṃ mahat |
khyātaṃ toyaprabhaṃ vṛttaṃ mauktikaṃ navadhā śubham || 4.14 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     toyaprabhaṃ jalābhaṃ yatra tiṣṭhati tatra dūrato jalabhramotpādakamityarthaḥ yadvā jalavattaralacchāyaṃ lāvaṇyaviśiṣṭamiti yāvat || 4.14;1

____________________


[mauktika:: medic. properties]
muktāphalaṃ laghu himaṃ madhuraṃ ca kāntidṛṣṭyagnipuṣṭikaraṇaṃ viṣahāri bhedi |
vīryapradaṃ jalanidherjanitā ca śuktirdīptā ca paktirujamāśu haredavaśyam || 4.15 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     nirjalaṃ nirjaḍaṃ ḍalayoraikyātaśiśiramityarthaḥ uṣṇamiti yāvadyadvā vicchāyaṃ dṛśyate ca lāvaṇye jalaśabdopacāraḥ muktāphalasya taralacchāyā eva lāvaṇyaśabdabodhikā yaduktam |
*     muktāphaleṣu chāyāyāstaralatvamivāntarā |
*     pratibhāti yadaṅgeṣu tallāvaṇyamihocyate || 4.15;1
*     iti || 4.15;2
*     atra jalaśabdena muktāphalagatataralacchāyā bodhyā || 4.15;3

____________________


[mauktika:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality]
rūkṣāṅgaṃ nirjalaṃ śyāvaṃ tāmrābhaṃ lavaṇopamam |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     vikaṭaṃ viṣamagātram || 4.16 b;1

____________________


ardhaśubhraṃ ca vikaṭaṃ granthilaṃ mauktikaṃ tyajet || 4.16 ||

[mauktika:: medic. properties]
kaphapittakṣayadhvaṃsi kāsaśvāsāgnimāndyanut |
puṣṭidaṃ vṛṣyamāyuṣyaṃ dāhaghnaṃ mauktikaṃ matam || 4.17 ||

[pravāla:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality]
pakvabimbaphalacchāyaṃ vṛttāyatamavakrakam |
snigdhamavraṇakaṃ sthūlaṃ pravālaṃ saptadhā śubham || 4.18 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     pāṇḍuraṃ śvetapītamiśravarṇam || 4.18;1



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     dhūsaraṃ śvetakṛṣṇamiśravarṇam || 4.18;1

____________________


[pravāla:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality]
pāṇḍuraṃ dhūsaraṃ sūkṣmaṃ savraṇaṃ kaṇḍarānvitam |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     kaṇḍarānvitaṃ sirāvṛtam || 4.19 b;1

____________________


nirbhāraṃ śulbavarṇaṃ ca pravālaṃ neṣyate'ṣṭadhā || 4.19 ||

[pravāla:: medic. properties]
kṣayapittāsrakāsaghnaṃ dīpanaṃ pācanaṃ laghu |
viṣabhūtādiśamanaṃ vidrumaṃ netraroganut || 4.20 ||

[tārkṣya:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality]
haridvarṇaṃ guru snigdhaṃ sphuradraśmicayaṃ śubham |
masṛṇaṃ bhāsuraṃ tārkṣyaṃ gātraṃ saptaguṇaṃ matam || 4.21 ||

[tārkṣya:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality]
kapilaṃ karkaśaṃ nīlaṃ pāṇḍu kṛṣṇaṃ ca lāghavam |
cipiṭaṃ vikaṭaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ rūkṣaṃ tārkṣyaṃ na śasyate || 4.22 ||

[tārkṣya:: medic. properties]
jvaracchardiviṣaśvāsasaṃnipātāgnimāndyanut |
durnāmapāṇḍuśophaghnaṃ tārkṣyamojovivardhanam || 4.23 ||

[puṣparāga:: parīkṣā]
puṣparāgaṃ guru svacchaṃ snigdhaṃ sthūlaṃ samaṃ mṛdu |
karṇikāraprasūnābhaṃ masṛṇaṃ śubhamaṣṭadhā || 4.24 ||

[puṣparāga:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality]
niṣprabhaṃ karkaśaṃ rūkṣaṃ pītaṃ śyāmaṃ natonnatam |
kapiśaṃ kapilaṃ pāṇḍu puṣparāgaṃ parityajet || 4.25 ||

[puṣparāga:: medic. properties]
puṣparāgaṃ viṣacchardikaphavātāgnimāndyanut |
dāhakuṣṭhāsraśamanaṃ dīpanaṃ pācanaṃ laghu || 4.26 ||

[vajra:: subtypes]
vajraṃ ca trividhaṃ proktaṃ naro nārī napuṃsakam |
pūrvaṃ pūrvamiha śreṣṭhaṃ rasavīryavipākataḥ || 4.27 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     aṣṭaphalakamaṣṭadhāramastrādīnāmagrabhāgavatsūkṣmāgramityarthaḥ || 4.27;1

____________________


[puṃvajra]
aṣṭāsraṃ vāṣṭaphalakaṃ ṣaṭkoṇamatibhāsuram |
ambudendradhanurvāritaraṃ puṃvajramucyate || 4.28 ||

[strīvajra]
tadeva cipiṭākāraṃ strīvajraṃ vartulāyatam |

[napuṃsaka]
vartulaṃ kuṇṭhakoṇāgraṃ kiṃcidguru napuṃsakam || 4.29 ||
strīpuṃnapuṃsakaṃ vajraṃ yojyaṃ strīpuṃnapuṃsake |
vyatyāsānnaiva phaladaṃ puṃvajreṇa vinā kvacit || 4.30 ||

[vajra:: subtypes:: color]
śvetādivarṇabhedena tadekaikaṃ caturvidham |
brahmakṣatriyaviṭśūdraṃ svasvavarṇaphalapradam || 4.31 ||
uttamottamavarṇaṃ hi nīcavarṇaphalapradam |
nyāyo'yaṃ bhairaveṇoktaḥ padārtheṣvakhileṣvapi || 4.32 ||

[vajra:: medic. properties]
āyuḥpradaṃ jhaṭiti sadguṇadaṃ ca vṛṣyaṃ doṣatrayapraśamanaṃ sakalāmayaghnam |
sūtendrabandhavadhasadguṇakṛtpradīpanaṃ mṛtyuṃjayaṃ tadamṛtopamameva vajram || 4.33 ||

[jewels:: 5 doṣas]
gauratrāsaśca binduśca rekhā ca jalagarbhatā |
sarvaratneṣvamī pañca doṣāḥ sādhāraṇā matāḥ |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha sādhāraṇān sarvaratnadoṣānāha grāsastrāsaśceti || 4.34 -d;1
*     grāso grasitaikadeśatvam || 4.34 -d;2
*     trāsaḥ sabāhyābhyantaramalaviśiṣṭatvam || 4.34 -d;3
*     binduḥ prasiddhaḥ || 4.34 -d;4
*     sa cānekavidhaḥ || 4.34 -d;5
*     rekhā prasiddhā || 4.34 -d;6
*     jalagarbhatā || 4.34 -d;7
*     yatrāntaḥ koṭara ivāntaḥ śuṣiraviśiṣṭajalābhāso bhavati tādṛśatvam || 4.34 -d;8

____________________


kṣetratoyabhavā doṣā ratneṣu na laganti te || 4.34 ||

[vajra:: śodhana]
kulatthakvāthake svinnaṃ kodravakvathitena vā |
ekayāmāvadhi svinnaṃ vajraṃ śudhyati niścitam || 4.35 ||

[vajra:: māraṇa]
vajraṃ matkuṇaraktena caturvāraṃ vibhāvitam |
sugandhimūṣikāmāṃsairvartitairmardya veṣṭayet || 4.36 ||
puṭetpuṭairvarāhākhyaistriṃśadvāraṃ tataḥ param |
dhmātvā dhmātvā śataṃ vārānkulatthakvāthake kṣipet |
anyairuktaḥ śataṃ vārānkartavyo'yaṃ vidhikramaḥ || 4.37 ||

[vajra:: māraṇa]
kulatthakvāthasaṃyuktalakucadravapiṣṭayā |
śilayā liptamūṣāyāṃ vajraṃ kṣiptvā nirudhya ca || 4.38 ||
aṣṭavāraṃ puṭetsamyagviśuṣkaiśca vanotpalaiḥ |
śatavāraṃ tato dhmātvā nikṣiptaṃ śuddhapārade |
niścitaṃ mriyate vajraṃ bhasma vāritaraṃ bhavet || 4.39 ||
satyavāksomasenānīretadvajrasya māraṇam |
dṛṣṭapratyayasaṃyuktamuktavānrasakautukī || 4.40 ||

[vajra:: māraṇa]
viliptaṃ matkuṇasyāsre saptavāraṃ viśoṣitam |
kāsamardarasāpūrṇe lohapātre niveśitam || 4.41 ||
saptavāraṃ paridhmātaṃ vajrabhasma bhavetkhalu |
brahmajyotirmunīndreṇa kramo'yaṃ parikīrtitaḥ || 4.42 ||

[vajra:: māraṇa]
nīlajyotirlatākande ghṛṣṭaṃ gharme viśoṣitam |
vajraṃ bhasmatvamāyāti karmavajjñānavahninā || 4.43 ||

[vajra:: māraṇa]
madanasya phalodbhūtarasena kṣoṇināgakaiḥ |
kṛtakalkena saṃlipya puṭedviṃśativārakam |
vajracūrṇaṃ bhavedvaryaṃ yojayecca rasādiṣu || 4.44 ||

[vajra:: māraṇa]
tadvajraṃ cūrṇayitvātha kiṃciṭṭaṅkaṇasaṃyutam |
kharabhūnāgasattvena viṃśenāvartate dhruvam |
tulyasvarṇena taddhmātaṃ yojanīyaṃ rasādiṣu || 4.45 ||
triguṇena rasenaiva saṃmardya guṭikīkṛtam |
mukhe dhṛtaṃ karotyāśu caladdantavibandhanam || 4.46 ||
triṃśadbhāgamitaṃ hi vajrabhasitaṃ svarṇaṃ kalābhāgikaṃ tāraṃ cāṣṭaguṇaṃ sitāmṛtavaraṃ rudrāṃśakaṃ cābhrakam |
pādāṃśaṃ khalu tāpyakaṃ vasuguṇaṃ vaikrāntakaṃ ṣaḍguṇaṃ bhāgo'pyuktarasai raso'yamuditaḥ ṣāḍguṇyasaṃsiddhaye || 4.47 ||

[nīla]
jalanīlendranīlaṃ ca śakranīlaṃ tayorvaram || 4.48 ||

[jalanīla:: phys. properties]
śvaityagarbhitanīlābhaṃ laghu tajjalanīlakam |

[indranīla:: phys. properties]
kārṣṇyagarbhitanīlābhaṃ sabhāraṃ śakranīlakam || 4.49 ||

[nīla:: parīkṣā:: good]
ekacchāyaṃ guru snigdhaṃ svacchaṃ piṇḍitavigraham |
mṛdu madhye lasajjyotiḥ saptadhā nīlamuttamam || 4.50 ||

[jalanīla:: parīkṣā]
komalaṃ vihitaṃ rūkṣaṃ nirbhāraṃ raktagandhi ca |
cipiṭābhaṃ sasūkṣmaṃ ca jalanīlaṃ ca saptadhā || 4.51 ||

[nīla:: medic. properties]
śvāsakāsaharaṃ vṛṣyaṃ tridoṣaghnaṃ sudīpanam |
viṣamajvaradurnāmapāpaghnaṃ nīlamīritam || 4.52 ||

[gomeda]
gomedaḥsamarāgatvādgomedaṃ ratnamucyate || 4.53 ||

[gomeda:: parīkṣā]
susvacchagojalacchāyaṃ svacchaṃ snigdhaṃ samaṃ guru |

----------


*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     nirdalaṃ niṣpattramaśubhagomedasya sapaṭalatvādatra śubhagomedasya nirdalatvāvatāraṇam || 4.54 b;1

____________________


nirdalaṃ masṛṇaṃ dīptaṃ gomedaṃ śubhamaṣṭadhā || 4.54 ||

[gomeda:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality]
vicchāyaṃ laghu rūkṣāṅgaṃ cipiṭaṃ paṭalānvitam |
niṣprabhaṃ pītakācābhaṃ gomedaṃ na śubhāvaham || 4.55 ||

[gomeda:: medic. properties]
gomedaṃ kaphapittaghnaṃ kṣayapāṇḍukṣayaṃkaram |
dīpanaṃ pācanaṃ rucyaṃ tvacyaṃ buddhiprabodhanam || 4.56 ||

[vaiḍūrya:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality]
vaidūryaṃ śyāmaśubhrābhaṃ samaṃ svacchaṃ guru sphuṭam |
bhramacchubhrottarīyeṇa garbhitaṃ śubhamīritam || 4.57 ||

[vaiḍūrya:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality]
śyāmaṃ toyasamacchāyaṃ cipiṭaṃ laghu karkaśam |
raktagarbhottarīyaṃ ca vaidūryaṃ naiva śasyate || 4.58 ||

[vaiḍūrya:: medic. properties]
vaidūryaṃ raktapittaghnaṃ prajñāyurbalavardhanam |
pittapradhānarogaghnaṃ dīpanaṃ malamocanam || 4.59 ||

[ratna:: śodhana]
śudhyatyamlena māṇikyaṃ jayantyā mauktikaṃ tathā |
vidrumaṃ kṣāravargeṇa tārkṣyaṃ godugdhakaistathā || 4.60 ||
puṣparāgaṃ ca saṃdhānaiḥ kulatthakvāthasaṃpuṭaiḥ |
taṇḍulīyajalairvajraṃ nīlaṃ nīlīrasena ca |
rocanābhiśca gomedaṃ vaidūryaṃ triphalājalaiḥ || 4.61 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     lakucaḥ ḍahukaḥ || 4.61;1

____________________


[ratna:: māraṇa]
lakucadrāvasampiṣṭaiḥ śilāgandhakatālakaiḥ |
vajraṃ vinānyaratnāni mriyante'ṣṭapuṭaiḥ khalu || 4.62 ||

[ratna:: drāvaṇa]
rāmaṭhaṃ pañcalavaṇaṃ kṣārāṇāṃ tritayaṃ tathā |
māṃsadravo'mlavetaśca cūlikālavaṇaṃ tathā || 4.63 ||
sthūlaṃ kumbhīphalaṃ pakvaṃ tathā jvālāmukhī śubhā |
dravantī ca rudantī ca payasyā citramūlakam || 4.64 ||
dugdhaṃ snuhyāstathārkasya sarvaṃ saṃmardya yatnataḥ |
golaṃ vidhāya tanmadhye prakṣipettadanantaram || 4.65 ||
guṇavannavaratnāni jātimanti śubhāni ca |
bhūrje taṃ golakaṃ kṛtvā sūtreṇāveṣṭya yatnataḥ || 4.66 ||
punarvastreṇa saṃveṣṭya dolāyantre nidhāya ca |
sarvāmlayuktasaṃdhānaparipūrṇaghaṭodare || 4.67 ||
ahorātratrayaṃ yāvatsvedayettīvravahninā |
tasmādāhṛtya saṃkṣālya ratnajāṃ drutimāharet |
ratnatulyaprabhā laghvī dehalohakarī śubhā || 4.68 ||

[pearl:: drāvaṇa]
muktācūrṇaṃ tu saptāhaṃ vetasāmlena marditam |
jambīrodaramadhye tu dhānyarāśau vinikṣipet |
saptāhāduddhṛtaṃ caiva puṭe dhṛtvā drutirbhavet || 4.69 ||

[vajra:: drāvaṇa]
vajravallyantarasthaṃ ca kṛtvā vajraṃ nirodhayet |
amlabhāṇḍagataṃ svedyaṃ saptāhāddravatāṃ vrajet || 4.70 ||

[vaikrānta:: drāvaṇa]
śvetavarṇaṃ tu vaikrāntamamlavetasabhāvitam |
saptāhānnātra saṃdehaḥ kharagharme dravatyasau || 4.71 ||

[vaikrānta:: drāvaṇa]
ketakīsvarasaṃ grāhyaṃ saindhavaṃ svarṇapuṣpikā |
indragopakasaṃyuktaṃ sarvaṃ bhāṇḍe vinikṣipet |
saptāhaṃ svedayettasminvaikrāntaṃ dravatāṃ vrajet || 4.72 ||

[ratna:: drāvaṇa]
lohāṣṭake tathā vajravāpanātsvedanāddrutiḥ |
jāyate nātra saṃdeho yogasyāsya prabhāvataḥ || 4.73 ||
kurute yogarājo'yaṃ ratnānāṃ drāvaṇaṃ param || 4.74 ||

[drutīnāṃ dīrghakālarakṣaṇopāyaḥ]
kusumbhatailamadhye tu saṃsthāpyā drutayaḥ pṛthak |
tiṣṭhanti cirakālaṃ tu prāpte kārye niyojayet || 4.75 ||

[ratna:: goodproperties]
sūryādigrahanigrahāpaharaṇaṃ dīrghāyurārogyadam |
saubhāgyodayabhāgyavaśyavibhavotsāhapradaṃ dhairyakṛt || 4.76 ||
duśchāyācaladhūlisaṃgatibhavālakṣmīharaṃ sarvadā |
ratnānāṃ paridhāraṇaṃ nigaditaṃ bhūtādinirnāśanam || 4.77 ||


________________________________________________________


 5
śuddhaṃ lohaṃ kanakarajataṃ bhānulohāśmasāraṃ pūtīlohaṃ dvitayamuditaṃ nāgavaṃgābhidhānam |
miśraṃ lohaṃ tritayamuditaṃ pittalaṃ kāṃsyavartaṃ dhāturlohe luha iti mataḥ so'pyanekārthavācī || 5.1 ||

[gold:: subtypes]
prākṛtaṃ sahajaṃ vahnisambhūtaṃ khanisaṃbhavam |
raseṃdravedhasaṃjātaṃ svarṇaṃ pañcavidhaṃ smṛtam || 5.2 ||

[gold:: medic. properties]
āyurlakṣmīprabhādhīsmṛtikaramakhilavyādhividhvaṃsi puṇyaṃ bhūtāveśapraśāntismarabharasukhadaṃ saukhyapuṣṭiprakāśi |
gāṅgeyaṃ cātha rūpyaṃ gadaharamajarākāri mehāpahāri kṣīṇānāṃ puṣṭikāri sphuṭamatikaraṇaṃ vīryavṛddhiprakāri || 5.3 ||

[gold:: prākṛta]
brahmāṇḍaṃ saṃvṛtaṃ yena rajoguṇabhuvā khalu |
tatprākṛtamiti proktaṃ devānāmapi durlabham || 5.4 ||

[gold:: sahaja]
brahmā yenāvṛto jātaḥ suvarṇena jarāyuṇā |
tanmerurūpatāṃ yātaṃ suvarṇaṃ sahajaṃ hi tat || 5.5 ||

[gold:: vahnisaṃbhava]
visṛṣṭamagninā śaivaṃ tejaḥ pītaṃ suduḥsaham |
abhūtsarvaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ suvarṇaṃ vahnisaṃbhavam || 5.6 ||
etatsvarṇatrayaṃ divyaṃ varṇaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiryutam |
dhāraṇādeva tatkuryāccharīramajarāmaram || 5.7 ||

[gold:: khanija]
tatra tatra girīṇāṃ hi jātaṃ khaniṣu yadbhavet |
taccaturdaśavarṇāḍhyaṃ bhakṣitaṃ sarvarogahṛt || 5.8 ||

[gold:: aśuddha:: medic. properties]

[gold:: colouring(suvarṇasampādana)]

[gold:: māraṇa]
kṛtvā kaṇṭakavedhyāni svarṇapatrāṇi lepayet |
luṃgāṃbubhasmasūtena mriyate daśabhiḥ puṭaiḥ || 5.14 ||

[gold:: māraṇa]
drute vinikṣipetsvarṇe lohamānaṃ mṛtaṃ rasam |
vicūrṇya luṅgatoyena daradena samanvitam |
jāyate kuṃkumacchāyaṃ svarṇaṃ dvādaśabhiḥ puṭaiḥ || 5.15 ||

[gold:: bhasman:: medic. application]
etadbhasma suvarṇajaṃ kaṭughṛtopetaṃ dviguṃjonmitaṃ līḍhaṃ hanti nṛṇāṃ kṣayāgnisadanaṃ śvāsaṃ ca kāsārucim |
ojodhātuvivardhanaṃ balakaraṃ pāṇḍvāmayadhvaṃsanaṃ pathyaṃ sarvaviṣāpahaṃ garaharaṃ duṣṭagrahaṇyādinut || 5.19 ||

[gold:: amārita:: medic. properties]
balaṃ ca vīryaṃ harate narāṇāṃ rogavrajaṃ kopayatīva kāye |
asaukhyakāraṃ ca sadaiva hemāpakvaṃ sadoṣaṃ maraṇaṃ karoti || 5.20 ||

rajataṃ pūrvapūrvaṃ hi svaguṇairuttarottaram || 5.21 ||

[silver:: sahaja]
kailāsādyadrisambhūtaṃ sahajaṃ rajataṃ bhavet |
tatspṛṣṭaṃ hi sakṛdvyādhināśanaṃ dehināṃ bhavet || 5.22 ||

[silver:: khanija]
himālayādikūṭeṣu yadrūpaṃ jāyate hi tat |
khanijaṃ kathyate tajjñaiḥ paramaṃ hi rasāyanam || 5.23 ||

[silver:: pādarūpya]
śrīrāmapādukānyastaṃ vaṃgaṃ yadrūpyatāṃ gatam |
tatpādarūpyamityuktaṃ kṛtrimaṃ sarvaroganut || 5.24 ||

[silver:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality]

[silver:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality]
dāhe raktaṃ ca pītaṃ ca kṛṣṇaṃ rūkṣaṃ sphuṭaṃ laghu |
sthūlāṅgaṃ karkaśāṅgaṃ ca rajataṃ tyājyamaṣṭadhā || 5.26 ||

[silver:: medic. properties]
rūpyaṃ vipākamadhuraṃ tuvarāmlasāraṃ śītaṃ saraṃ paramalekhanakaṃ ca rūpyam |
snigdhaṃ ca vātakaphajijjaṭharāgnidīpi balyaṃ paraṃ sthiravayaskaraṇaṃ ca medhyam || 5.27 ||

[silver:: medic. properties(2)]
raupyaṃ śītaṃ kaṣāyāmlaṃ snigdhaṃ vātaharaṃ guru |
rasāyanavidhānena sarvarogāpahārakam || 5.28 ||

[silver:: śodhana]
taile takre gavāṃ mūtre hyāranāle kulatthaje |
kramānniṣecayettaptaṃ drāve drāve tu saptadhā |
svarṇādilohapatrāṇāṃ śuddhireṣā praśasyate || 5.29 ||

[silver:: aśuddha:: medic. properties]
āyuḥ śukraṃ balaṃ hanti tāpaviḍbandharogakṛt |
aśuddhaṃ na mṛtaṃ tāraṃ śuddhaṃ māryamato budhaiḥ || 5.30 ||

[silver:: śodhana]
nāgena ṭaṃkaṇenaiva vāpitaṃ śuddhimṛcchati |
tāraṃ trivāraṃ nikṣiptaṃ taile jyotiṣmatībhave || 5.31 ||

[silver:: śodhana]
kharpare bhasmacūrṇābhyāṃ paritaḥ pālikāṃ caret |
tatra rūpyaṃ vinikṣipya samasīsasamanvitam || 5.32 ||
jātasīsakṣayaṃ yāvaddhamettāvatpunaḥ punaḥ |
itthaṃ saṃśodhitaṃ rūpyaṃ yojanīyaṃ rasādiṣu || 5.33 ||

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     matāntaramāha kharpare iti || 5.33;1
*     bhasmacūrṇābhyāṃ sīsakabhasmaṭaṅkaṇacūrṇābhyāṃ pālikāmālavālam || 5.33;2
*     atrāyaṃ vidhiḥ nāgabhasmaṭaṅkaṇacūrṇe jalena piṣṭvā tatpiṇḍena mṛtkharparaṃ paritaḥ ālavālaṃ racayitvā tanmadhye samasīsacūrṇapiṣṭaraupyaṃ nikṣipya tāvatbhastrayā dhametyāvatsīsakṣayo na bhavediti || 5.33;3

[silver:: māraṇa]
lakucadravasūtābhyāṃ tārapatraṃ pralepayet |
ūrdhvādho gandhakaṃ dattvā mūṣāmadhye nirudhya ca || 5.34 ||
svedayedvālukāyantre dinamekaṃ dṛḍhāgninā |
svāṃgaśītāṃ ca tāṃ piṣṭiṃ sāmlatālena marditām |
puṭeddvādaśavārāṇi bhasmībhavati rūpyakam || 5.35 ||

[silver:: māraṇa]
mākṣīkacūrṇaluṃgāmlamarditaṃ puṭitaṃ śanaiḥ |
triṃśadvāreṇa tattāraṃ bhasmasājjāyatetarām || 5.36 ||

[silver:: nirutthīkaraṇa]
bhāvyaṃ tāpyaṃ snuhīkṣīraistārapatrāṇi lepayet |
mārayetpuṭayogena nirutthaṃ jāyate dhruvam || 5.37 ||

[silver:: māraṇa:: niruttha]
tārapatraṃ caturbhāgaṃ bhāgaikaṃ śuddhatālakam |
mardyaṃ jambīrajadrāvaistārapatrāṇi lepayet || 5.38 ||
śodhayedandhayantre ca triṃśadutpalakaiḥ pacet |
caturdaśapuṭairevaṃ nirutthaṃ jāyate dhruvam || 5.39 ||

[silver, gold:: drāvaṇa]
saptadhā naramūtreṇa bhāvayeddevadālikām |
taccūrṇāvāpamātreṇa drutiḥ syātsvarṇatārayoḥ || 5.40 ||

[silver:: medic. application]
bhasmībhūtaṃ rajatamamalaṃ tatsamau vyomabhānū sarvaistulyaṃ trikaṭu savaraṃ sāraghājyena yuktam |
līḍhaṃ prātaḥ kṣapayatitarāṃ yakṣmapāṇḍūdarārśaḥ śvāsaṃ kāsaṃ nayanajarujaḥ pittarogānaśeṣān || 5.41 ||

[copper:: subtypes]
mlecchaṃ nepālakaṃ ceti tayornepālakaṃ varam |
nepālādanyakhanyutthaṃ mlecchamityabhidhīyate || 5.42 ||

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     tāmrasya khanidvayaṃ vartate || 5.42;1

[copper:: nepāla:: parīkṣā]
susnigdhaṃ mṛdulaṃ śoṇaṃ ghanāghātakṣamaṃ guru |
nirvikāraṃ guṇaśreṣṭhaṃ tāmraṃ nepālamucyate || 5.44 ||

[copper:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality]

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     sadalaṃ sapattraṃ paṭalasaṃyutamiti yāvatdalaṃ joriti loke iti bhāvamiśraḥ || 5.45 b;1

____________________


rūkṣāṅgaṃ sadalaṃ tāmraṃ neṣyate rasakarmaṇi || 5.45 ||

[copper:: medic. properties]
tāmraṃ tiktakaṣāyakaṃ ca madhuraṃ pāke'tha vīryoṣṇakaṃ sāmlaṃ pittakaphāpahaṃ jaṭhararukkuṣṭhāmajantvantakṛt |
ūrdhvādhaḥ pariśodhanaṃ viṣayakṛtsthaulyāpahaṃ kṣutkaraṃ durnāmakṣayapāṇḍurogaśamanaṃ netryaṃ paraṃ lekhanam || 5.46 ||

[copper:: aśuddha:: medic. properties]
aśuddhaṃ tāmramāyurghnaṃ kāntivīryabalāpaham |
vāntimūrcchābhramotkledaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ śūlaṃ karoti tat || 5.47 ||
utkledabhedabhramadāhamohāstāmrasya doṣāḥ khalu durdharāste |
viśodhanāttadvigatasvadoṣaṃ sudhāsamaṃ syādrasavīryapāke || 5.48 ||

[copper:: śodhana]
tāmraṃ kṣārāmlasaṃyuktaṃ drāvitaṃ dattagairikam |
nikṣiptaṃ mahiṣītakre chagaṇe saptavārakam |
pañcadoṣavinirmuktaṃ bhasmayogyaṃ hi jāyate || 5.49 ||

[copper:: śodhana]
tāmranirmalapatrāṇi liptvā nimbvambusindhunā |
dhmātvā sauvīrakakṣepādviśudhyatyaṣṭavārataḥ || 5.50 ||

[copper:: śodhana]
nimbvambupaṭuliptāni tāpitānyaṣṭavārakam |
viśudhyantyarkapatrāṇi nirguṃḍyārasamajjanāt || 5.51 ||

[copper:: śodhana]
gomūtreṇa pacedyāmaṃ tāmrapatraṃ dṛḍhāgninā |
śudhyate nātra saṃdeho māraṇaṃ cāpyathocyate || 5.52 ||

[copper:: māraṇa]
jambīrarasasampiṣṭarasagandhakalepitam |
śulbapatraṃ śarāvasthaṃ tripuṭairyāti pañcatām || 5.53 ||

[copper:: māraṇa]
athavā māritaṃ tāmramamlenaikena marditam |
tadgolaṃ sūraṇasyāntā ruddhvā sarvatra lepayet || 5.54 ||
śuṣkaṃ gajapuṭe pacyātsarvadoṣaharaṃ bhavet |
vāntiṃ bhrāntiṃ virekaṃ ca na karoti kadācana || 5.55 ||

[copper:: māraṇa]
tāmrapatrāṇi sūkṣmāṇi gomūtre pañcayāmakam |
kṣiptvā rasena bhāṇḍe taddviguṇaṃ dehi gandhakam || 5.56 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     amlaparṇī cāṅgerī || 5.56;1

____________________


amlaparṇīṃ prapiṣyātha hyabhito dehi tāmrakam |
samyaṅnirudhya bhāṇḍe tamagniṃ jvālaya yāmakam |
bhasmībhavati tāmraṃ tadyatheṣṭaṃ viniyojayet || 5.57 ||

[copper:: medic. application]
sūtāddviguṇitaṃ tāmrapatraṃ kanyārasaiḥ plutam |
piṣṭvā tulyena balinā bhāṇḍamadhye vinikṣipet || 5.58 ||
channaṃ śarāvakeṇaitattadūrdhvaṃ lavaṇaṃ tyajet |
mukhe śarāvakaṃ dattvā vahniṃ yāmacatuṣṭayam || 5.59 ||
avacūrṇyaiva tacchulbaṃ vallamātraṃ prayojayet |
pippalīmadhunā sārdhaṃ sarvarogeṣu yojayet || 5.60 ||
śvāsaṃ kāsaṃ kṣayaṃ pāṇḍumagnimāṃdyamarocakam |
gulmaplīhayakṛnmūrcchāśūlapaktyarthamuttamam || 5.61 ||
doṣatrayasamudbhūtānāmayāñjayati dhruvam |
rogānupānasahitaṃ jayeddhātugataṃ jvaram |
rase rasāyane tāmraṃ yojayedyuktamātrayā || 5.62 ||

[somanātha]
śulbatulyena sūtena balinā tatsamena ca |
tadardhāṃśena tālena śilayā ca tadardhayā || 5.63 ||
vidhāya kajjalīṃ ślakṣṇāṃ bhinnakajjalasannibhām |
yantrādhyāyavinirdiṣṭagarbhayantrodarāntare || 5.64 ||
kajjalīṃ tāmrapatrāṇi paryāyeṇa vinikṣipet |
prapacedyāmaparyantaṃ svāṃgaśītaṃ vicūrṇayet || 5.65 ||
tattadrogaharānupānasahitatāmraṃ dvivallonmitaṃ saṃlīḍhaṃ pariṇāmaśūlamudaraṃ śūlaṃ ca pāṇḍujvaram |
gulmaplīhayakṛtkṣayāgnisadanaṃ mehaṃ ca mūlāmayaṃ duṣṭāṃ ca grahaṇīṃ hareddhruvamidaṃ śrīsomanāthābhidham || 5.66 ||

[iron:: subtypes]
muṃḍaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca kāṃtaṃ ca triprakāramayaḥ smṛtam || 5.67 ||

[muṇḍa:: subtypes]
mṛdu kuṇṭhaṃ kaḍāraṃ ca trividhaṃ muṇḍamucyate || 5.68 ||

[mṛdu:: phys. properties]
drutadrāvamavisphoṭaṃ cikkaṇaṃ mṛdu tacchubham || 5.69 ||

[kuṇṭha:: phys. properties]
hataṃ yatprasaredduḥkhāttatkuṇṭhaṃ madhyamaṃ smṛtam || 5.70 ||

[kaḍāra:: phys. properties]
yaddhataṃ bhajyate bhaṃge kṛṣṇaṃ syāttatkaḍārakam || 5.71 ||

[muṇḍa:: medic. properties]
muṇḍaṃ paraṃ mṛdulakaṃ kaphavātaśūlamūlāmamehagadakāmalapāṇḍuhāri |
gulmāmavātajaṭharārtiharaṃ pradīpi śophāpahaṃ rudhirakṛtkhalu koṣṭhaśodhi || 5.72 ||

[iron:: aśuddha:: medic. properties]
aśuddhalohaṃ na hitaṃ niṣevaṇādāyurbalaṃ kāntivināśi niścitam |
hṛdi prapīḍāṃ tanute hyapāṭavaṃ rujaṃ karotyeva viśodhya mārayet || 5.73 ||

[tīkṣṇaloha:: subtypes]
kharaṃ sāraṃ ca hṛnnālaṃ tārāvaṭṭaṃ ca vājiram |
kālalohābhidhānaṃ ca ṣaḍvidhaṃ tīkṣṇamucyate || 5.74 ||

[kharaloha:: phys. properties]
paruṣaṃ pogaronmuktaṃ bhaṃge pāradavacchavi |
namane bhaṅguraṃ yattatkharalohamudāhṛtam || 5.75 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     pogaraṃ kuñcitālakavattaraṅgāyitaṃ tena unmuktaṃ tadrahitamityarthaḥ || 5.75;1




*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     yatparuṣaṃ kaṭhoraṃ pogaronmuktaṃ pogaramalakavatkuṭilarekhāstābhirmuktaṃ tadrahitamityarthaḥ || 5.75;1

____________________

[sāra]
vegabhaṅguradhāraṃ yatsāralohaṃ tadīritam |
pogarābhāsakaṃ pāṇḍubhūmijaṃ sāramucyate || 5.76 ||

[hṛnnāla]
kṛṣṇapāṇḍuvapuścañcubījatulyorupogaram |
chedane cātiparuṣaṃ hṛnnālamiti kathyate || 5.77 ||

[pogara]
aṅgakṣayā ca vaṅgaṃ ca pogarasyābhidhātrayam |
cikuraṃ bhaṅguraṃ lohātpogaraṃ tatparaṃ matam || 5.78 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     kharalakṣaṇe pogaronmuktamityuktamataḥ pogarasya paryāyādikamāha aṅgakṣayeti || 5.78;1
*     tritvaṃ cātra pogaramapekṣya bodhyaṃ tena aṅgakṣayā vaṅgaṃ pogaraṃ ca ityekārtham || 5.78;2
*     pogarasya svarūpalakṣaṇamāha cikuramiti || 5.78;3
*     lohātlauhagātre ityarthaḥ || 5.78;4
*     atra saptamyarthe pañcamīti boddhavyaṃ yatcikuraṃ bhaṅguraṃ kuñcitakuntalavadbhaṅgīviśeṣaḥ ityarthaḥ || 5.78;5



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     pogaraśabdo'trāgataḥ || 5.78;1
*     sa cāpratisiddhārthaḥ || 5.78;2
*     ato bodhārthaṃ tatparyāyānāha aṅgachāyā ca vaṅgaṃ cikuraṃ ca iti pogarasyābhidhātrayaṃ nāmatrayamasti || 5.78;3
*     evaṃ ca nāmānyetāni viśiṣṭākāratejasa ityarthaḥ || 5.78;4
*     vaṅgasyeva rekhānāṃ śvetacchāyātvādvaṅgamiti saṃjñā || 5.78;5
*     kāntīnāṃ cikurākāratvātkeśākāratvāccikuramityapi nāma || 5.78;6
*     yaduktameva kharalohaṃ tadyadi lohādbhaṅguraṃ lohamayaghanaghātena bhaṅguraṃ bhavati paraṃtu pogaraṃ pogaraviśiṣṭaṃ dṛśyeta tarhi tatkharalohamapi paramuttamaṃ matam || 5.78;7

____________________


[vājira]
pogarairvajrasaṃkāśaiḥ sūkṣmarekhaiśca sāndrakaiḥ |
nicitaṃ śyāmalāṅgaṃ ca vājīraṃ tatprakīrtyate || 5.79 ||

[kālāyasa]
nīlakṛṣṇaprabhaṃ sāndraṃ masṛṇaṃ guru bhāsuram |
lohāghāte'pyabhaṅgātmadhāraṃ kālāyasaṃ matam || 5.80 ||

[tīkṣṇaloha:: khara:: medic. properties]
rūkṣaṃ syātkharalohakaṃ samadhuraṃ pāke'tha vīrye himaṃ tiktoṣṇaṃ kaphapittakuṣṭhajaṭharaplīhāmapāṃḍvartinut |
sadyaḥ śūlayakṛdgadakṣayajarāmehāmavātāpahaṃ dīptaṃ cātirasāyanaṃ balakaraṃ durnāmadāhāpaham || 5.81 ||
kharalohātparaṃ sarvamekaikasmācchatottaram || 5.82 ||

[kānta:: subtypes]
bhrāmakaṃ cumbakaṃ caiva karṣakaṃ drāvakaṃ tathā |
evaṃ caturvidhaṃ kāntaṃ romakāntaṃ ca pañcamam || 5.83 ||

[kānta:: subtypes:: mukha]
ekadvitricatuṣpañcasarvatomukhameva tat |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     tasya cokto vyastaḥ samasto vā bhedo'yaṃ jāraṇāyāṃ sakaladhātubhakṣaṇārthaṃ pāradasya mukharūpo bhavatītyāha ekadvitrīti || 5.84 b;1
*     kevalabhrāmakasattvasya prathamaṃ yathāvidhijāraṇenāpi pāradasya mukhaṃ bhavati || 5.84 b;2
*     tataḥ pāradaḥ sarvāṃllohādīn grasati || 5.84 b;3
*     ata ekabhedo'pi kaścitpāradamukhaṃ bhavati || 5.84 b;4
*     yasya tadekamukham || 5.84 b;5
*     bhrāmakacumbakayoḥ sattvadvayasyaikīkṛtasya yathāvidhijāraṇena pāradamukhaṃ kṛtaṃ cetsaṃyuktabhedadvayaviśiṣṭaṃ tatkāntaṃ dvimukhamucyate || 5.84 b;6
*     evamevārthastrimukhādau bodhyaḥ || 5.84 b;7
*     vijātīyadravyagrāsāntarasahitamapyetatprathamaṃ jāritaṃ cenmukhaṃ bhavatītyataḥ sarvatomukhamityuktam || 5.84 b;8
*     sajātīyavijātīyavyastasamastaṃ bhedaviśiṣṭametanmukhaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ || 5.84 b;9

____________________


[kānta:: subtypes:: colour]
pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ tathā raktaṃ trivarṇaṃ syātpṛthakpṛthak |
krameṇa devatāstatra brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ || 5.84 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     sparśavedhi sparśamātreṇaiva vedhakārakaṃ rasendrasya vedhākhyasaṃskārasaṃpādakaṃ vā ityarthaḥ || 5.84;1

____________________


sparśavedhi bhavetpītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ rasāyane |
raktavarṇaṃ tathā cāpi rasabandhe praśasyate || 5.85 ||
bhrāmakaṃ tu kaniṣṭhaṃ syāccumbakaṃ madhyamaṃ tathā |
uttamaṃ karṣakaṃ caiva drāvakaṃ cottamottamam || 5.86 ||

[bhrāmaka]
bhrāmayellohajātaṃ yattatkāntaṃ bhrāmakaṃ matam || 5.87 ||
cumbayeccumbakaṃ kāntaṃ karṣayetkarṣakaṃ tathā || 5.88 ||

[drāvaka]
sākṣādyaddrāvayellohaṃ tatkāntaṃ drāvakaṃ bhavet || 5.89 ||

[romakānta]
tadromakāntaṃ sphuṭitādyato romodgamo bhavet || 5.90 ||

[kānta:: subtypes:: mukha]
kaniṣṭhaṃ syādekamukhaṃ madhyaṃ dvitrimukhaṃ bhavet |
catuṣpañcamukhaṃ śreṣṭhamuttamaṃ sarvatomukham || 5.91 ||

[kānta:: subtypes:: medic. properties]
bhrāmakaṃ cumbakaṃ caiva vyādhināśe praśasyate |
rase rasāyane caiva karṣakaṃ drāvakaṃ hitam || 5.92 ||
madonmattagajaḥ sūtaḥ kāntamaṅkuśamucyate || 5.93 ||

[kānta:: collecting]
kṣetraṃ jñātvā grahītavyaṃ tatprayatnena dhīmatā |
mārutātapavikṣiptaṃ varjayennātra saṃśayaḥ || 5.94 ||

[kānta:: parīkṣā (?)]
pātre yasya prasarati jale tailabindurna lipto gandhaṃ hiṅgustyajati ca tathā tiktatāṃ nimbakalkaḥ |
pāke dugdhaṃ bhavati śikharākārakaṃ naiti bhūmau kāntaṃ lauhaṃ tadidamuditaṃ lakṣaṇoktaṃ ca nānyat || 5.95 ||

[kānta:: medic. properties]
kāntāyo'tirasāyanottarataraṃ svasthe cirāyuḥpradaṃ snigdhaṃ mehaharaṃ tridoṣaśamanaṃ śūlāmamūlāpaham |
gulmaplīhayakṛtkṣayāmayaharaṃ pāṇḍūdaravyādhinuttiktoṣṇaṃ himavīryakaṃ kimaparaṃ yogena sarvārtinut || 5.96 ||
samyagauṣadhakalpānāṃ lohakalpaḥ praśasyate |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena śuddhaṃ lohaṃ ca mārayet || 5.97 ||
nāyaḥ pacetpañcapalādarvāgūrdhvaṃ trayodaśāt || 5.98 ||
ādau mantrastataḥ karma kartavyaṃ mantra ucyate || 5.99 ||
omamṛtodbhavāya svāhā anena mantreṇa lohamāraṇam |
lakṣottaraguṇaṃ sarvaṃ lohaṃ syāduttarottaram |
kāntaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ tatra tadapyevaṃ guṇottaram || 5.100 ||

[iron:: śodhana]
śaśakṣatajasaṃliptaṃ trivāraṃ paritāpitam |
muṇḍādisakalaṃ lohaṃ sarvadoṣān vimuñcati || 5.101 ||

[iron:: śodhana:: giridoṣa]
kvāthyamaṣṭaguṇe toye triphalāṣoḍaśaṃ palam |
tatkvāthe pādaśeṣe tu lohasya palapañcakam || 5.102 ||
kṛtvā patrāṇi taptāni saptavāraṃ niṣecayet |
evaṃ pralīyate doṣo girijo lohasambhavaḥ || 5.103 ||

[iron:: śodhana:: giridoṣa]
sāmudralavaṇopetaṃ taptaṃ nirvāpitaṃ khalu |
triphalākvathite nūnaṃ giridoṣamayastyajet || 5.104 ||

[iron:: śodhana:: giridoṣa]
ciñcāphalajalakvāthādayo doṣamudasyati || 5.105 ||

[iron:: māraṇa:: vāritara]
yadvā phalatrayopetaṃ gomūtre kvathitaṃ kṣaṇam |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     recitaṃ srāvitam || 5.105;1

____________________


recitaṃ ghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ kṣiptvāyaḥ kharpare pacet || 5.106 ||
cālayellohadaṇḍena yāvatkṣiptaṃ tṛṇaṃ dahet |
piṣṭvā piṣṭvā pacedevaṃ pañcavāramataḥ param || 5.107 ||
dhātrīphalarasairyadvā triphalākvathitodakaiḥ |
puṭellohaṃ caturvāraṃ bhavedvāritaraṃ khalu || 5.108 ||

[iron:: māraṇa]
snehāktaṃ loharajo mūtre svarase'pi rātridhātrīṇām |
pṛthagevaṃ saptakṛtvo bharjitamakhilāmaye yojyam || 5.109 ||

[tīkṣṇaloha:: māraṇa]
tīkṣṇalohasya patrāṇi nirdalāni dṛḍhe'nale |
dhmātvā kṣipejjale sadyaḥ pāṣāṇolūkhalodare || 5.110 ||
kaṇḍayedgāḍhanirghātaiḥ sthūlayā lohapārayā |
tanmadhyātsthūlakhaṇḍāni ruddhvā malladvayāntare || 5.111 ||
dhmātvā kṣiptvā jale samyakpūrvavatkaṇḍayetkhalu |
taccūrṇaṃ sūtagandhābhyāṃ puṭedviṃśativārakam || 5.112 ||
puṭe puṭe vidhātavyaṃ peṣaṇaṃ dṛḍhavattaram |
evaṃ bhasmīkṛtaṃ lohaṃ tattadrogeṣu yojayet || 5.113 ||

[kānta:: medic. properties]
kāntāyaḥ kamanīyakāntijananaṃ pāṇḍvāmayonmūlanam |
yakṣmavyādhinibarhaṇaṃ garaharaṃ doṣatrayonmūlanam |
nānākuṣṭhanibarhaṇaṃ balakaraṃ vṛṣyaṃ vayaḥstambhanam |
sarvavyādhiharaṃ rasāyanavaraṃ bhaumāmṛtaṃ nāparam || 5.114 ||

[iron (gen.):: māraṇa]
hiṅgulasya palānpañca nārīstanyena peṣayet |
tena lohasya patrāṇi lepayetpalapañcakam || 5.115 ||
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacyātkaṣāyaistraiphalaiḥ punaḥ |
jambīrairāranālairvā viṃśatyaṃśena hiṅgulam || 5.116 ||
piṣṭvā ruddhvā pacellohaṃ taddravaiḥ pācayetpunaḥ |
catvāriṃśatpuṭairevaṃ kāntaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca muṇḍakam |
mriyate nātra saṃdeho dattvā dattvaiva hiṅgulam || 5.117 ||

[tīkṣṇaloha:: māraṇa]
atha pūrvoditaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ vasubhallakavāsayoḥ |
puṭitaṃ patratoyena triṃśadvārāṇi yatnataḥ |
śoṇitaṃ jāyate bhasma kṛtasindūravibhramam || 5.118 ||

[tīkṣṇaloha:: māraṇa]
yadvā tīkṣṇadalodbhūtaṃ rajaśca triphalājalaiḥ |
piṣṭvā dattvaudanaṃ kiṃciccakrikāṃ pravidhāya ca || 5.119 ||
śoṣayitvātiyatnena prapacetpañcabhiḥ puṭaiḥ |
raktavarṇaṃ hi tadbhasma yojanīyaṃ yathāyatham || 5.120 ||

[iron (gen.):: māraṇa:: niruttha]
matsyākṣīgandhabāhlīkairlakucadravapeṣitaiḥ |
vilipya sakalaṃ lohaṃ matsyākṣīkalkalepitam || 5.121 ||
bhastrābhyāṃ sudṛḍhaṃ dhmātvā triśūlīnirgamāvadhi |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     triśūlī triśūlavaccaṭikātrayamityarthaḥ || 5.122 b;1

____________________


athoddhṛtya kṣipetkvāthe triphalāgojalātmake || 5.122 ||
tasmādāhṛtya saṃtāḍya mṛtamādāya lohakam |
punaśca pūrvavaddhmātvā mārayedakhilāyasam || 5.123 ||
khaṇḍayitvā tato gandhaguḍatriphalayā saha |
puṭettriṃśativārāṇi nirutthaṃ bhasma jāyate || 5.124 ||

[iron (gen.):: māraṇa]
samagandhamayaścūrṇaṃ kumārīvāribhāvitam |
puṭīkṛtaṃ kiyatkālamavaśyaṃ mriyate hyayaḥ || 5.125 ||

[iron (gen.):: māraṇa]
jambīrarasasaṃyukte darade taptamāyasam |
bahuvāraṃ vinikṣiptaṃ mriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 5.126 ||

[iron (gen.):: māraṇa]
gomūtraistriphalā kvāthyā tatkaṣāyeṇa bhāvayet |
triḥsaptāhaṃ prayatnena dinaikaṃ mardayetpunaḥ || 5.127 ||
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacyāddinaṃ kvāthena mardayet |
divā mardyaṃ puṭedrātrāvekaviṃśaddināvadhi |
ekaviṃśatpuṭairevaṃ mriyate trividhaṃ hyayaḥ || 5.128 ||

[kāntaloha:: śodhana]
yatpātrādhyuṣite toye tailabindurna sarpati |
tāreṇāvartate yattatkāntalohaṃ tanūkṛtam || 5.129 ||
ayasāmuttamaṃ siñcettaptaṃ taptaṃ varārase |
evaṃ śuddhāni lohāni piṣṭānyamlena kenacit || 5.130 ||
mṛtasūtasya pādena praliptāni puṭānale |
pacettulyena vā tāpyagandhāśmaharatejasā || 5.131 ||
taptaṃ kṣārāmlasaṃliptaṃ śaśarakte nidhāpitam |
kāntalohaṃ bhavedbhasma sarvadoṣavivarjitam || 5.132 ||

[iron:: māraṇa:: vāritara]
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ dvidhā gandhaṃ khallena kṛtakajjalam |
dvayoḥ samaṃ lohacūrṇaṃ mardayetkanyakādravaiḥ || 5.133 ||
yāmadvayātsamuddhṛtya yadgolaṃ tāmrapātrake |
ācchādyairaṃḍapatraiśca yāmārdhe'tyuṣṇatāṃ vrajet || 5.134 ||
dhānyarāśau nyasetpaścāttridinānte samuddharet |
saṃpeṣya gālayedvastre satyaṃ vāritaraṃ bhavet |
kāntaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca muṃḍaṃ ca nirutthaṃ jāyate mṛtam || 5.135 ||

[metals:: māraṇa]
svarṇādīnmārayedevaṃ cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā ca lohavat |
siddhayogo hyayaṃ khyātaḥ siddhānāṃ sumukhāgataḥ || 5.136 ||
anubhūtaṃ mayā satyaṃ sarvarogajarāpaham |
triphalāmadhusaṃyuktaṃ sarvarogeṣu yojayet || 5.137 ||
etasmādapunarbhavaṃ hi bhasitaṃ lohasya divyāmṛtaṃ samyaksiddharasāyanaṃ trikaṭukīvellājyamadhvanvitam |
hanyānniṣkamitaṃ jarāmaraṇajavyādhīṃśca satputradaṃ diṣṭe śrīgiriśena kālayavanodbhūtyai purā tatpituḥ || 5.138 ||

[iron:: mṛta:: medic. properties]
lohaṃ jantuvikārapāṇḍupavanakṣīṇatvapittāmayasthaulyārśograhaṇījvarārtikaphajicchophapramehapraṇut |
gulmaplīhaviṣāpahaṃ balakaraṃ kuṣṭhāgnimāndyapraṇutsaukhyālambirasāyanaṃ mṛtiharaṃ kiṭṭaṃ ca kāntādivat || 5.139 ||
mṛtāni lohāni rasībhavanti nighnanti yuktāni mahāmayāṃśca |
abhyāsayogāddṛḍhadehasiddhiṃ kurvanti rugjanmajarāvināśam || 5.140 ||

[kānta:: optimal:: phys. properties]
pakvajambūphalacchāyaṃ kāntalohaṃ taduttamam || 5.141 ||

[tīkṣṇa:: drāvaṇa]
triḥsaptakṛtvo gomūtre jālinībhasmabhāvitam |
śoṣayettasya vāpena tīkṣṇaṃ mūṣāgataṃ dravet || 5.142 ||

[tīkṣṇa:: drāvaṇa]
suradālibhasma galitaṃ triḥsaptakṛtvo'tha gojale śuṣkam |
vāpena salilasadṛśaṃ karoti mūṣāgataṃ tīkṣṇam || 5.143 ||

[kānta:: drāvaṇa]
suradālibhavaṃ bhasma naramūtreṇa gālitam |
triḥsaptavāraṃ tatkṣāravāpātkāntadrutirbhavet || 5.144 ||

[iron (gen.):: drāvaṇa]
gaṃdhakaṃ kāṃtapāṣāṇaṃ cūrṇayitvā samaṃ samam |
drute lohe pratīvāpo deyo lohāṣṭakaṃ dravet || 5.145 ||

[iron (gen.):: drāvaṇa]
devadālyā dravairbhāvyaṃ gaṃdhakaṃ dinasaptakam |
tena pravāpamātreṇa lauhaṃ tiṣṭhati sūtavat || 5.146 ||

[iron:: aśuddha:: medic. properties]
aśuddhalohaṃ na hitaṃ niṣevaṇādāyurbalaṃ kāntivināśi niścitam |
hṛdi prapīḍāṃ tanute hyapāṭavaṃ rujaṃ karotyeva viśodhya mārayet || 5.147 ||
kiṭṭāddaśaguṇaṃ muṇḍaṃ muṇḍāttīkṣṇaṃ śatonmitam |
tīkṣṇāllakṣaguṇaṃ kāntaṃ bhakṣaṇātkurute guṇān |
tasmātkāntaṃ sadā sevyaṃ jarāmṛtyuharaṃ nṛṇām || 5.148 ||
āyuṣpradātā balavīryakartā rogaprahartā madanasya kartā |
ayaḥsamānaṃ na hi kiṃcidanyadrasāyanaṃ śreṣṭhatamaṃ hi jantoḥ || 5.149 ||

[maṇḍūra:: preparation]
akṣāṅgārairdhametkiṭṭaṃ lohajaṃ tadgavāṃ jalaiḥ |
secayedakṣapātrāntaḥ saptavāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
maṇḍūro'yaṃ samākhyātaścūrṇaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ prayojayet || 5.150 ||

[maṇḍūra:: preparation]
gomūtraistriphalā kvāthyā tatkvāthe secayecchanaiḥ |
lohakiṭṭaṃ susaṃtaptaṃ yāvajjīryati tatsvayam |
taccūrṇaṃ jāyate peṣyaṃ maṇḍūro'yaṃ prayojayet || 5.151 ||

[muṇḍa:: maṇḍūra:: medic. properties]
ye guṇā mārite muṇḍe te guṇā muṇḍakiṭṭake |
tasmātsarvatra maṇḍūraṃ rogaśāntyai prayojayet || 5.152 ||

[tin:: subtypes]
khurakaṃ miśrakaṃ ceti dvividhaṃ vaṃgamucyate |
khuraṃ tatra guṇaiḥ śreṣṭhaṃ miśrakaṃ na hitaṃ matam || 5.153 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     na hitamiti || 5.153;1
*     tatra nāgamiśraṇenāśuddhatvāt || 5.153;2

____________________


[khura:: mṛta:: parīkṣā]
dhavalaṃ mṛtaṃ snigdhaṃ drutadrāvaṃ sagauravam |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     niḥśabdaṃ pattrībhūtamapi śabdarahitaṃ pattrībhūtaṃ raṅgāntaraṃ yathā saśabdaṃ bhavati tathā na ityarthaḥ || 5.154 b;1

____________________


[miśraka:: phys. properties]
niḥśabdaṃ khuravaṃgaṃ syātmiśrakaṃ śyāmaśubhrakam || 5.154 ||

[tin:: medic. properties]
vaṅgaṃ tiktoṣṇakaṃ rūkṣamīṣadvātaprakopanam |
mehaśleṣmāmayaghnaṃ ca medoghnaṃ kṛmināśanam || 5.155 ||

[khura:: śodhana]
drāvayitvā niśāyukte kṣiptaṃ nirguṇḍikārase |
viśudhyati trivāreṇa khuravaṃgaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 5.156 ||

[miśraka:: śodhana]
amlatakravinikṣiptaṃ varṣābhūviṣatindubhiḥ |
kaṭphalāṃbugataṃ vaṃgaṃ dvitīyaṃ pariśudhyati || 5.157 ||

[metals:: śodhana]
śudhyati nāgo vaṃgo ghoṣo ravirātape'pi munisaṃkhyaiḥ |
nirguṇḍīrasasekaistanmūlarajaḥpravāpaiśca || 5.158 ||

[tin:: māraṇa]
satālenārkadugdhena liptvā vaṃgadalāni ca |
bodhiciṃcātvacaḥ kṣārairdadyāllaghupuṭāni ca |
mardayitvā caredbhasma tadrasādiṣu śasyate || 5.159 ||

[tin:: māraṇa]
pradrāvya kharpare vaṃgaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ rasaṃ kṣipet |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     bhāradvājasya vanakārpāsyāḥ || 5.160 b;1

____________________


svalpasvalpālakaṃ dattvā bhāradvājasya kāṣṭhataḥ |
mardayitvā caredbhasma tadrasādiṣu śasyate || 5.160 ||

[tin:: māraṇa]
palāśadravayuktena vaṃgapatraṃ pralepayet |
tālena puṭitaṃ paścānmriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 5.161 ||

[tin:: māraṇa]
bhallātatailasaṃliptaṃ vaṃgaṃ vastreṇa veṣṭitam |
ciṃcāpippalapālāśakāṣṭhāgnau yāti pañcatām || 5.162 ||

[tin:: formulations]
vaṃgabhasmasamaṃ kāntaṃ vyomabhasma ca tatsamam |
mardayetkanakāmbhobhirnimbapatrarasairapi || 5.163 ||
dāḍimasya mayūrasya rasena ca pṛthakpṛthak |
bhūpālāvartabhasmātha vinikṣipya samāṃśakam || 5.164 ||
gomūlakaśilādhātujalaiḥ samyagvimardayet |
tato guggulatoyena mardayitvā dināṣṭakam || 5.165 ||
viśoṣya paricūrṇyātha samabhāgena yojayet |
ghṛṣṭaṃ bandhūkaniryāsairnākulībījacūrṇakaiḥ || 5.166 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     nākulībījaṃ rāsnābījaṃ śālmalībījaṃ vā || 5.166;1

____________________


tataḥ kṣipetkaraṇḍāntarvidhāya paṭagālitam |
gotakrapiṣṭarajanīsāreṇa saha pāyayet || 5.167 ||
caturbhirvallakaistulyaṃ ramyaṃ vaṃgarasāyanam |
niścitaṃ tena naśyanti mehā viṃśatibhedakāḥ || 5.168 ||
śālayo mudgasūpaṃ ca navanītaṃ tilodbhavam |
paṭolaṃ tiktatuṇḍīraṃ takraṃ pathyāya śasyate || 5.169 ||

[lead:: śuddha:: parīkṣā]
drutadrāvaṃ mahābhāraṃ chede kṛṣṇaṃ samujjvalam |
pūtigandhaṃ bahiḥ kṛṣṇaṃ śuddhaṃ sīsamato'nyathā || 5.170 ||

[lead:: medic. properties]
atyuṣṇaṃ sīsakaṃ snigdhaṃ tiktaṃ vātakaphāpaham |
pramehatoyadoṣaghnaṃ dīpanaṃ cāmavātanut || 5.171 ||

[lead:: śodhana]
sinduvārajaṭākauntīharidrācūrṇakaṃ kṣipet |
drute nāge'tha nirguṇḍyāstrivāraṃ nikṣipedrase |
nāgaḥ śuddho bhavedevaṃ mūrchāsphoṭādi nācaret || 5.172 ||

[lead:: māraṇa]
tiryagākāracullyāṃ tu tiryagvaktraṃ ghaṭaṃ nyaset |
taṃ ca vaktraṃ vinā sarvaṃ gopayedyatnato mṛdā || 5.173 ||
bhṛṣṭayantrābhidhe tasmin pātre sīsaṃ vinikṣipet |
palaviṃśatikaṃ śuddhamadhastīvrānalaṃ kṣipet || 5.174 ||
drute nāge kṣipetsūtaṃ śuddhaṃ karṣamitaṃ śubham |
gharṣayitvā kṣipetkṣāramekaikaṃ hi palaṃ palam || 5.175 ||
arjunasyākṣavṛkṣasya mahārājagirerapi |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     mahārājagiriḥ śākaviśeṣaḥ rājaśākamitiyāvatsa tu laghusthūlabhedena dvividhaḥ atra ca sthūlo grāhyaḥ || 5.176 b;1

____________________


dāḍimasya mayūrasya kṣiptvā kṣāraṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 5.176 ||
evaṃ viṃśatirātrāṇi pacettīvreṇa vahninā |
vighaṭṭayandṛḍhaṃ dorbhyāṃ lohadarvyā prayatnataḥ || 5.177 ||
raktaṃ tajjāyate bhasma kapotacchāyameva vā |
nāgaṃ doṣavinirmuktaṃ jāyate'tirasāyanam || 5.178 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     atredaṃ kāryaṃ vakrākārāṃ cullīṃ kṛtvā tadupari ghaṭamekaṃ vakramukhaṃ kṛtvā sthāpayettato vaktramātraṃ vihāya kṛtsnaṃ ghaṭāvayavaṃ mṛllepenācchādayetbhṛṣṭayantrākhye'smin yantre viṃśatipalamānaṃ śuddhaṃ sīsakaṃ dattvā tīvrottāpena dravīkuryāttataḥ tasmin karṣapramāṇaṃ śodhitapāradaṃ prakṣipya darvyā ghaṭṭayetmiśrībhūte ca tasmin pratyekaṃ palamānamarjunādīnāṃ kṣāraṃ pṛthakpṛthakdattvā lauhadarvyā dṛḍhaṃ ghaṭṭayan tīvrāgninā viṃśatirātraṃ pacet || 5.178;1



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     tiryagākārā tiraścīnā yā dīrghā cullī āhāḻa iti mahārāṣṭrabhāsāyāṃ prasiddhā tasyāṃ ghaṭaṃ tiryagvaktrametādṛśaṃ ghaṭaṃ nyasedadhiśrayet || 5.178;1
*     taṃ ghaṭaṃ ca vaktraṃ vinā bāhye samaṃ tato'ṅgulasthūlaṃ mṛdā gopayellepayet || 5.178;2
*     bhṛṣṭayantrākhye tasmin viṃśatipalamitaṃ sīsaṃ vinikṣipet || 5.178;3
*     tasmin drute sati ekakarṣaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ tatra kṣiptvā tato darvyā vighaṭṭya itastataḥ saṃcālyaikībhūte satyarjunādīnāṃ pratyekaṃ kṣāraṃ palamitaṃ kṣipet || 5.178;4
*     tato dorbhyāṃ lohadarvyā vighaṭṭayaṃścālayan viṃśatirātraparyantaṃ tīvrāgninā pacet || 5.178;5
*     tena vidhinā nāgabhasma raktavarṇaṃ kapotacchāyaṃ kṛṣṇamiśraraktavarṇaṃ vā bhavet || 5.178;6
*     tāpasphoṭādikaratvarūpadoṣarahitaṃ bhasmībhūtaṃ nāgaṃ rasāyanaṃ bhavati || 5.178;7

____________________


[lead:: māraṇa]
hatamutthāpitaṃ sīsaṃ daśavāreṇa sidhyati |
tanmṛtaṃ sīsakaṃ sarvadoṣamuktaṃ rasāyanam || 5.179 ||

[lead:: māraṇa:: niruttha]
aśvatthaciṃcātvagbhasma nāgasya caturaṃśataḥ |
kṣipennāgaṃ pacetpātre cālayellohacāṭunā || 5.180 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     lohacāṭunā lohadarvyā || 5.180;1

____________________


yāmādbhasma taduddhṛtya bhasmatulyā manaḥśilā |
jambīrairāranālairvā piṣṭvā ruddhvā puṭe pacet || 5.181 ||
svāṃgaśītaṃ punaḥ piṣṭvā viṃśatyaṃśaśilāyutam |
amlenaiva tu yāmaikaṃ pūrvavatpācayetpuṭe |
evaṃ ṣaṣṭipuṭaiḥ pakvo nāgaḥ syātsunirutthitaḥ || 5.182 ||

[lead:: māraṇa:: niruttha]
śilayā ravidugdhena nāgapatrāṇi lepayet |
mārayetpuṭayogena nirutthaṃ jāyate tathā || 5.183 ||

[lead:: formulations]
evaṃ nāgodbhavaṃ bhasma tāpyabhasmārdhabhāgikam |
pādaṃ pādaṃ kṣipedbhasma śulbasya vimalasya ca || 5.184 ||
kāntābhrasattvayoścāpi sphaṭikasya pṛthakpṛthak |
sarvamekatra saṃcūrṇya puṭettriphalavāriṇā || 5.185 ||
triṃśadvanagiriṇḍaiśca triṃśadvāraṃ vicūrṇya tat |
vyoṣavellakacūrṇaiśca samāṃśaiḥ saha melayet || 5.186 ||
madhvājyasahitaṃ hanti pralīḍhaṃ vallamātrayā |
aśītivātajānrogāndhanurvātaṃ viśeṣataḥ || 5.187 ||
kapharogānaśeṣāṃśca mūtrarogāṃśca sarvaśaḥ |
śvāsaṃ kāsaṃ kṣayaṃ pāṇḍuṃ śvayathuṃ śītakajvaram || 5.188 ||
grahaṇīmāmadoṣaṃ ca vahnimāndyaṃ sudurjaram |
sarvānudakadoṣāṃśca tattadrogānupānataḥ || 5.189 ||

[brass:: subtypes]
rītikā kākatuṇḍī ca dvividhaṃ pittalaṃ bhavet || 5.190 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     tatpittalaṃ dvividham || 5.190;1
*     rītikā kākatuṇḍī ceti bhedāt || 5.190;2
*     tatra rītikāyā dvau bhedau || 5.190;3
*     kiṃcittāmrābhā svarṇavarṇā ca || 5.190;4

____________________


[rītikā:: parīkṣā]
saṃtāpya kāṃjike kṣiptā tāmrābhā rītikā matā || 5.191 ||

[kākatuṇḍī:: parīkṣā]
evaṃ yā jāyate kṛṣṇā kākatuṇḍīti sā matā || 5.192 ||

[rītikā:: medic. properties]
rītistiktarasā rūkṣā jantughnī sāsrapittanut |
pāṇḍukuṣṭhaharā yogātsoṣṇavīryā ca śītalā || 5.193 ||

[kākatuṇḍī:: medic. properties]
kākatuṇḍī gatasnehā tiktoṣṇā kaphapittanut |
yakṛtplīhaharā śītavīryā ca parikīrtitā || 5.194 ||

[brass:: rīti:: phys. properties]
gurvī mṛdvī ca pītābhā sārāṅgī tāḍanakṣamā |
susnigdhā masṛṇāṅgī ca rītiretādṛśī śubhā || 5.195 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     barbarā adhamā ityarthaḥ || 5.195;1

____________________


[rīti:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality]
pāṇḍupītā kharā rūkṣā barbarā tāḍanākṣamā |
pūtigandhā tathā laghvī rītirneṣṭā rasādiṣu || 5.196 ||

[rīti:: śodhana]
taptvā kṣiptvā ca nirguṇḍīrase śyāmārajo'nvite |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     śyāmārajo'nvite haridrācūrṇasaṃyute || 5.197 b;1

____________________


pañcavāreṇa saṃśuddhiṃ rītirāyāti niścitam || 5.197 ||

[rīti:: māraṇa]
nimbūrasaśilāgandhaveṣṭitā puṭitāṣṭadhā |
rītirāyāti bhasmatvaṃ tato yojyā yathāyatham || 5.198 ||

[rīti:: māraṇa]
tāmravanmāraṇaṃ tasyāḥ kṛtvā sarvatra yojayet || 5.199 ||

[brass:: pittalarasāyana]
mṛtārakūṭakaṃ kāntaṃ vyomasattvaṃ ca māritam |
trayaṃ samāṃśakaṃ tulyaṃ vyoṣaṃ jantughnasaṃyutam || 5.200 ||
brahmabījājamodāgnibhallātatilasaṃyutam |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     ajamodā yamānī antaḥparimārjanakatvāt || 5.201 b;1

____________________


sevitaṃ niṣkamātraṃ hi jantughnaṃ kuṣṭhanāśanam |
viśeṣācchvetakuṣṭhaghnaṃ dīpanaṃ pācanaṃ hitam || 5.201 ||

[brass:: drāvaṇa]
suvarṇarītikācūrṇaṃ bhakṣitaṃ veṣṭitaṃ punaḥ |
chāgena kṛṣṇavarṇena mattena taruṇena ca || 5.202 ||
talliptaṃ kharpare dagdhaṃ drutiṃ muñcati śobhanām |
caturdaśalasadvarṇasuvarṇasadṛśachaviḥ |
dehalohakarī proktā yuktā rasarasāyane || 5.203 ||

[bronśe:: production]
aṣṭabhāgena tāmreṇa dvibhāgakhurakeṇa ca |
vidrutena bhavetkāṃsyaṃ tatsaurāṣṭrabhavaṃ śubham || 5.204 ||

[bronśe:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality]
tīkṣṇaśabdaṃ mṛdu snigdhamīṣacchyāmalaśubhrakam |
nirmalaṃ dāharaktaṃ ca ṣoḍhā kāṃsyaṃ praśasyate || 5.205 ||

[bronśe:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality]
tatpītaṃ dahane tāmraṃ kharaṃ rūkṣaṃ ghanāsaham |
mandanādaṃ gatajyotiḥ saptadhā kāṃsyamutsṛjet || 5.206 ||

[bronśe:: medic. properties]
kāṃsyaṃ laghu ca tiktoṣṇaṃ lekhanaṃ dṛkprasādanam |
kṛmikuṣṭhaharaṃ vātapittaghnaṃ dīpanaṃ hitam || 5.207 ||
ghṛtamekaṃ vinā cānyatsarvaṃ kāṃsyagataṃ nṛṇām |
bhuktamārogyasukhadaṃ hitaṃ sātmyakaraṃ tathā || 5.208 ||

[bronśe:: śodhana]
taptaṃ kāṃsyaṃ gavāṃ mūtre vāpitaṃ pariśudhyati || 5.209 ||

[bronśe:: māraṇa:: niruttha]
mriyate gandhatālābhyāṃ nirutthaṃ pañcabhiḥ puṭaiḥ || 5.210 ||

[bronśe:: māraṇa]
trikṣāraṃ pañcalavaṇaṃ saptadhāmlena bhāvayet |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     ārakūṭaṃ pittalam || 5.210;1

____________________


kāṃsyārakūṭapatrāṇi tena kalkena lepayet |
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pakvaṃ śuddhabhasmatvamāpnuyāt || 5.211 ||

[vartaloha]
kāṃsyārkarītilohāhijātaṃ tadvartalohakam |
tadeva pañcalohākhyaṃ lohavidbhirudāhṛtam || 5.212 ||

[vartaloha:: medic. properties]
himāmlaṃ kaṭukaṃ rūkṣaṃ kaphapittavināśanam |
rucyaṃ tvacyaṃ kṛmighnaṃ ca netryaṃ malaviśodhanam || 5.213 ||
tadbhāṇḍe sādhitaṃ sarvamannavyañjanasūpakam |
amlena varjitaṃ cātidīpanaṃ pācanaṃ hitam || 5.214 ||

[vartaloha:: śodhana]
drutamaśvajale kṣiptaṃ vartalohaṃ viśudhyati || 5.215 ||

[vartaloha:: māraṇa]
mriyate gandhatālābhyāṃ puṭitaṃ vartalohakam |
teṣu teṣviha yogeṣu yojanīyaṃ yathāvidhi || 5.216 ||

[mercury:: preparation withmahārasasetc.]
jātimadbhirviśuddhaiśca vidhinā parisādhitaiḥ |
rasoparasalohādyaiḥ sūtaḥ sidhyati nānyathā || 5.217 ||
ratnāni lohāni varāṭaśuktipāṣāṇajātaṃ khuraśṛṅgaśalyam |
mahārasādyeṣu kaṭhoradehaṃ bhasmīkṛtaṃ syātkhalu sūtayogyam || 5.218 ||

[bhūnāga:: sattva]
vajrāṇāṃ drāvaṇārthāya sattvaṃ bhūnāgajaṃ bruve |
tadeva paramaṃ tejaḥ sūtarājendravajrayoḥ || 5.219 ||
dhautabhūnāgasambhūtaṃ mardayedbhṛṃgajadravaiḥ |
nimbūdravaiśca nirguṇḍyāḥ svarasaistridinaṃ pṛthak || 5.220 ||
taddrāvaṇagaṇopetaṃ saṃmardya vaṭakīkṛtam |
nirudhya dṛḍhamūṣāyāṃ dvidaṇḍaṃ pradhameddṛḍham || 5.221 ||
svataḥśītaṃ samāhṛtya paṭṭake viniveśya tat |
ravakān rājikātulyān reṇūnatibharānvitān || 5.222 ||
dvādaśāṃśārkasaṃyuktāndhamitvā ravakānharet |
prakṣālya ravakānāśu samādāya prayatnataḥ || 5.223 ||
vajrādidrāvaṇaṃ tena prakurvīta yathepsitam |
kharasattvamidaṃ proktaṃ rasāyanamanuttamam |
dvitrimūṣāsu caikasyāṃ sattvaṃ bhavati niścitam || 5.224 ||

[bhūnāga:: sattva]
bhujaṅgamānupādāya catuṣprasthasamanvitān |
suvarṇarūpyatāmrāyaskāṃtasaṃbhūtibhūmijān || 5.225 ||
prakṣālya rajanītoyaiḥ śītalaiśca jalairapi |
upoṣitaṃ mayūraṃ vā śūraṃ vā caraṇāyudham || 5.226 ||
krameṇa cārayitvātha tadviṣṭhāṃ samupāharet |
kṣārāmlaiḥ saha saṃpeṣya viśoṣya ca kharātape || 5.227 ||
tataḥ kharparake kṣiptvā bharjayitvā maṣīṃ caret |
maṣīṃ drāvaṇavargeṇa saṃyuktāṃ saṃpramarditām || 5.228 ||
nirudhya koṣṭhikāmadhye pradhamedghaṭikādvayam |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     khoṭaṃ rasajāraṇabandhanadravyaviśeṣaṃ bhūnāgasattvasya rasajārakatvātatra khoṭaśabdena ravakareṇurūpaṃ bhūnāgasattvaṃ bodhyam || 5.229 b;1

____________________


śītalībhūtamūṣāyāḥ khoṭamāhṛtya peṣayet || 5.229 ||
prakṣālya ravakānsūkṣmānsamādāya prayatnataḥ |
suvarṇamānavaddhmātvā ravaṃ kṛtvā niyojayet || 5.230 ||

[bhūnāga:: mudrikā (?)]
bhūnāgodbhavasattvamuttamamidaṃ śrīsomadevoditaṃ dattaṃ pādamitaṃ dviśāṇakanakenaikaṃ gatenormikām |
taddhautāmbuvilepitaṃ sthiracarodbhūtaṃ viṣaṃ netrarukśūlaṃ mūlagadaṃ ca karṇajarujo hanyātprasūtigraham || 5.231 ||

[tailapātana]
mūlānyuttaravāruṇyā jarjarīkṛtya kāṃjike |
kṣipedaṅkollabījānāṃ peśikāṃ jarjarīkṛtām |
tattailaṃ ghṛtavatstyānaṃ grāhyaṃ tattu yathāvidhi || 5.232 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     peṭakārī jyotiṣmatī || 5.232;1

____________________


[tailapātana]
saṃpeṣyottaravāruṇyāḥ peṭakāryā dalānyatha |
kāñjikena tatastena kalkena parimardayet || 5.233 ||
rajaścāṅkollabījānāṃ tadbaddhvā viralāmbare |
tadvilambyātape tīvre tasyādhaścaṣakaṃ nyaset |
tasminnipatitaṃ tailamādeyaṃ śvitranāśanam || 5.234 ||

[taila:: pātana]
aṅkollabījasambhūtaṃ cūrṇaṃ saṃmardya kāñjikaiḥ |
ekarātroṣitaṃ tattu piṇḍīkṛtya tataḥ param || 5.235 ||
svedayetkanduke yantre ghaṭikādvitayaṃ tataḥ |
tāṃ ca piṇḍīṃ dṛḍhe vastre baddhvā niṣpīḍya kāṣṭhataḥ || 5.236 ||
adhaḥpātrasthitaṃ tailaṃ samāhṛtya niyojayet |
evaṃ kandukayantreṇa sarvatailānyupāharet || 5.237 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     kākatuṇḍī śvetaguñjā || 5.237;1

____________________


aṅkolasyāpi tailaṃ syātkākatuṇḍyā samūlayā || 5.238 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     vākucī somarājī || 5.238;1
*     devadālī hastighoṣakaḥ || 5.238;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     karkoṭī karkoṭakaṃ kāṃkola iti bhāṣā || 5.238;1

____________________


[taila:: pātana]
vākucidevadālyośca karkoṭīmūlato bhavet || 5.239 ||

[taila:: viṣamuṣṭi]
apāmārgakaṣāyeṇa tailaṃ syādviṣamuṣṭijam || 5.240 ||

[taila:: jaipāla]
mūlakvāthaiḥ kumāryāśca tailaṃ jaipālajaṃ haret || 5.241 ||

[taila:: vākucī]
kvāthai raktāpāmārgasya vākucītailamāharet || 5.242 ||

[taila:: pātana]
kṛṣṇāyāḥ kākatuṇḍyāśca bījacūrṇāni kārayet |
kāntapāṣāṇacūrṇaṃ ca ekīkṛtya nirodhayet |
dhānyarāśigataṃ paścāduddhṛtya tailamāharet || 5.243 ||


________________________________________________________


 6
rasaśāstrāṇi sarvāṇi samālocya yathākramam |
sādhakānāṃ hitārthāya prakaṭīkriyate'dhunā || 6.1 ||
na krameṇa vinā śāstraṃ na śāstreṇa vinā kramaḥ |
śāstraṃ kramayutaṃ jñātvā yaḥ karoti sa siddhibhāk || 6.2 ||
ācāryo jñānavāndakṣo rasaśāstraviśāradaḥ |
mantrasiddho mahāvīro niścalaśivavatsalaḥ || 6.3 ||
devībhaktaḥ sadā dhīro devatāyāgatatparaḥ |
sarvāmnāyaviśeṣajñaḥ kuśalo rasakarmaṇi |
evaṃ lakṣaṇasaṃyukto rasavidyāgururbhavet || 6.4 ||
gurubhaktāḥ sadācārāḥ satyavanto dṛḍhavratāḥ |
nirālasyāḥ svadharmajñāḥ sadājñāparipālakāḥ || 6.5 ||
dambhamātsaryanirmuktāḥ kulācāreṣu dīkṣitāḥ |
atyantasādhakāḥ śāntā mantrārādhanatatparāḥ |
ityevaṃ lakṣaṇairyuktāḥ śiṣyāḥ syuḥ sūtasiddhaye || 6.6 ||
sahāyāḥ sodyamāstatra yathā śiṣyāstato'dhikāḥ |
kulīnāḥ svāmibhaktāśca kartavyā rasakarmaṇi || 6.7 ||
nāstikā ye durācārāścumbakā guruto'parāt |
vidyāṃ grahītumicchati cauryacchadmakhalotsavāt || 6.8 ||
na teṣāṃ sidhyate kiṃcinmaṇimantrauṣadhādikam |
kurvanti yadi mohena nāśayanti svakaṃ dhanam |
iha loke sukhaṃ nāsti paraloke tathaiva ca || 6.9 ||
tasmādbhaktibalādeva saṃtuṣyati yadā guruḥ |
tadā śiṣyeṇa sā grāhyā rasavidyātmasiddhaye |
hastamastakayogena varaṃ labdhvā susādhayet || 6.10 ||
ātaṅkarahite deśe dharmarājye manorame |
umāmaheśvaropete samṛddhe nagare śubhe || 6.11 ||
kartavyaṃ sādhanaṃ tatra rasarājasya dhīmatā |
atyantopavane ramye caturdvāropaśobhite || 6.12 ||
tatra śālā prakartavyā suvistīrṇā manoramā |
samyagvātāyanopetā divyacitrairvicitritā || 6.13 ||
tatsamīpe same dīpte kartavyaṃ rasamaṇḍapam |
atiguptaṃ suvistīrṇaṃ kapāṭārgalaśobhitam || 6.14 ||
dhvajachattravitānāḍhyaṃ puṣpamālāvilambitam |
bherīkāhalaghaṇṭādiśṛṅgīnādāvanāditam || 6.15 ||
bhūḥ samā tatra kartavyā sudṛḍhā darpaṇopamā |
tanmadhye vedikā ramyā kartavyā lakṣaṇānvitā || 6.16 ||
niṣkatrayaṃ hemapattraṃ rasendraṃ navaniṣkakam |
amlena mardayedyāmaṃ tena liṅgaṃ tu kārayet || 6.17 ||
dolāyantre sāranāle jambīrasthaṃ dinaṃ pacet |
talliṅgaṃ pūjayettatra suśubhairupacārakaiḥ || 6.18 ||
liṅgakoṭisahasrasya yatphalaṃ samyagarcanāt |
tatphalaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ rasaliṅgārcanādbhavet || 6.19 ||
brahmahatyāsahasrāṇi gohatyāścāyutāni hi |
tatkṣaṇādvilayaṃ yānti rasaliṅgasya darśanāt || 6.20 ||
sparśanātprāpyate muktiriti satyaṃ śivoditam |
āgneyyāṃ śrīghoreṇa mantrarājena cārcayet || 6.21 ||
aṣṭādaśabhujaṃ śubhraṃ pañcavaktraṃ trilocanam |
pretārūḍhaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ rasaliṅgaṃ vicintayet || 6.22 ||
tasyotsaṅge mahādevīmekavaktrāṃ caturbhujām |
akṣamālāṅkuśaṃ dakṣe vāme pāśābhayaṃ śubham |
dadhatīṃ taptahemābhāṃ pītavastrāṃ vibhāvayet || 6.23 ||
vāṅmayī śrīḥ kāmarājaśaktibījaṃ rasāṅkuśāyai namo dvādaśārṇaiṣā jñeyā vidyā rasāṅkuśā || 6.24 ||
anayā pūjayeddevīṃ gandhapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ |
nandībhṛṅgīmahākālakulīrān pūrvadikkramāt |
pūjayennāmamantraiśca praṇavādinamo'ntakaiḥ || 6.25 ||
eva nityārcanaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ rasasiddhaye || 6.26 ||
rasavidyā śivenoktā dātavyā sādhakāya vai |
yathoktena vidhānena guruṇā muditātmanā || 6.27 ||
sumuhūrte sunakṣatre candratārābalānvite |
kalaśaṃ toyasampūrṇaṃ hemaratnaphalairyutam || 6.28 ||
sthāpayedrasaliṅgāgre divyavastreṇa veṣṭitam |
gandhapuṣpākṣatairdhūpairnaivedyaiśca supūjayet || 6.29 ||
pūjānte havanaṃ kuryādyonikuṇḍe sulakṣaṇe |
tilājyaiḥ pāyasaiḥ puṣpaiḥ śatapuṣpādikaiḥ pṛthak || 6.30 ||
aghoreṇa rasāṅkuśyā homānte śiṣyamāhvayet |
kālinīśaktisaṃyuktaṃ rasasiddhiparāyaṇam || 6.31 ||
yasyāstu kuñcitāḥ keśāḥ śyāmā yā padmalocanā |
surūpā taruṇī bhinnā vistīrṇajaghanā śubhā || 6.32 ||
saṃkīrṇahṛdayā pīnastanabhāreṇa namritā |
cumbanāliṅgasparśakomalā mṛdubhāṣiṇī || 6.33 ||
aśvatthapattrasadṛśayonideśasuśobhitā |
kṛṣṇapakṣe puṣpavatī sā nārī kālinī smṛtā |
rasabandhe prayoge ca uttamā sā rasāyane || 6.34 ||
tadabhāve surūpā tu yā kācittaruṇāṅganā |
tasyā deyaṃ trisaptāhaṃ gandhakaṃ ghṛtasaṃyutam |
