karṣaikaikaṃ prabhāte tu sā bhavetkālinīsamā || 6.35 ||
evaṃ śaktiyuto yo'sau dīkṣayettaṃ gurūttamaḥ |
susnātamabhiṣiñceta mantreṇa kalaśodakaiḥ || 6.36 ||
aghorāmaṅkuśīṃ vidyāṃ dadhyācchiṣyāya sadguruḥ |
yathāśaktyā suśiṣyeṇa dātavyā gurudakṣiṇā || 6.37 ||
athājñayā gurormantraṃ lakṣaṃ lakṣaṃ pṛthagjapet |
oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ hrūmadyoratara prasphuṭa 2 prakaṭa 2 kaha 2 śamaya 2 jāta 2 daha 2 pātaya 2 oṃ hrīṃ hraiṃ hrauṃ hrūmaghorāya phaṭimamaghoramantraṃ tu auṃ kāmarājaśaktibījarasāṅkuśāyai ājñayā vidyāṃ rasāṅkuśām |
anayā pūjayeddevīṃ śaktimaṅkuśavidyayā || 6.38 ||
daśāṃśaṃ juhuyātkuṇḍe trikoṇe hastamātrake |
jātipuṣpaṃ trimadhvaktaṃ pūrṇānte kanyakārcanam || 6.39 ||
kṛtvātha praviśecchālāṃ śuddhāṃ liptāṃ savedikām |
ṣaṭkoṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tatra sindūreṇa dvihastakam || 6.40 ||
vedikāyāṃ likhetsamyaktadbahiścāṣṭapattrakam |
kamalaṃ caturasraṃ ca caturdvāraiḥ suśobhitam || 6.41 ||
karṇikāyāṃ nyasetkhallaṃ lohajaṃ svarṇalekhitam |
tanmadhye rasarājaṃ tu palānāṃ śatamātrakam |
pañcāśatpañcaviṃśadvā pūjayedrasaliṅgavat || 6.42 ||
vajravaikrāntavajrābhrakāntapāṣāṇaṭaṅkaṇam |
bhūnāgaḥ śaktayaścaitāḥ ṣaṭkoṇe pūjayetkramāt || 6.43 ||

[uparasa]
gandhatālakakāsīsaśilākaṅkuṣṭhabhūṣaṇam |
rājāvarto gairikaṃ ca khyātā uparasā amī |
pūjyā aṣṭadaleṣvete pūrvādīśānagaṃ kramāt || 6.44 ||

[mahārasa]
rasakaṃ vimalā tāpyaṃ capalā tutthamañjanam |
hiṅgulaṃ sasyakaṃ caiva khyātā ete mahārasāḥ |
pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ pattrāgreṣu prapūjayet || 6.45 ||
pūrvadvāre svarṇaraupye dakṣiṇe tāmrasīsake |
paścime vaṅgakāntau ca uttare muṇḍatīkṣṇake |
sarvametadaghoreṇa pūjayedaṅkuśānvitam || 6.46 ||
viḍaṃ kāñjikayantrāṇi kṣāramṛllavaṇāni ca |
koṣṭhī mūṣā vaṅkanālatuṣāṅgāravanopalāḥ || 6.47 ||
bhastrikā daṇḍikānekā śilā khalvānyulūkhalam |
svarṇakāropakaraṇaṃ samastatulanāni ca || 6.48 ||
mṛtkāṣṭhatāmralohotthapātrāṇi vividhāni ca |
divyauṣadhīnāṃ vargāśca rañjakasnehanāni ca |
etāni dvārabāhye tu mūlamantreṇa pūjayet || 6.49 ||
vāṅmāyā hrīṃ tataḥ kṣeṃ ca kṣmaśca pañcākṣaro manuḥ |
anena mantreṇa bhairavaṃ tatra pūjayet |
sarveṣāṃ rasasiddhānāṃ nāma saṃkīrtayettadā || 6.50 ||
vyālācāryaścandrasenaḥ subuddhirnaravāhanaḥ |
nāgārjuno ratnaghoṣaḥ surānando yaśodhanaḥ || 6.51 ||
indraśca māṇḍavyaścarpaṭī śūrasenakaḥ |
āgamo nāgabuddhiśca khaṇḍaḥ kāpāliko mataḥ || 6.52 ||
kāmāristāntrikaḥ śambhurlaṅkālampaṭaśāradau |
bāṇāsuro muniśreṣṭho govindaḥ kapilo baliḥ || 6.53 ||
ete sarve tu sūtendrā rasasiddhā mahābalāḥ |
caranti sarvalokeṣu nityā bhogaparāyaṇāḥ || 6.54 ||
saptaviṃśatisaṃkhyākā rasasiddhipradāyakāḥ |
vaidyāḥ pūjyāḥ prayatnena tataḥ kuryādrasārcanam || 6.55 ||
harṣayandvijadevānāṃ tarpayediṣṭadevatāḥ |
kumārīyoginīyogīśvarānmelakasādhakān |
tarpayetpūjayedbhaktyā yathāśaktyanusārataḥ || 6.56 ||
ityevaṃ sarvasambhārayuktaṃ kuryādrasotsavam |
sarvavighnapraśāntyarthaṃ sarvepsitaphalapradam || 6.57 ||
anyathā yo vimūḍhātmā mantradīkṣākramādvinā |
kartumicchati sūtasya sādhanaṃ guruvarjitaḥ || 6.58 ||
nāsau siddhimavāpnoti yatnakoṭiśatairapi |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena śāstroktāṃ kārayetkriyām || 6.59 ||
samyaksādhanasodyamā guruyutā rājājñayālaṃkṛtā nānākarmaparāṅmukhā rasaparāścāḍhyā janaiścārthitāḥ |
mātrāyantrasupākakarmakuśalāḥ sarvauṣadhe kovidāḥ teṣāṃ sidhyati nānyathā vidhibalācchrīpāradaḥ pāradaḥ || 6.60 ||
rasaśāstraṃ pradātavyaṃ viprāṇāṃ dharmahetave |
rājñe vaiśyāya vṛddhyarthaṃ dāsyārthamitarasya ca || 6.61 ||
gurau tuṣṭe śivastuṣyecchive tuṣṭe rasastathā |
rase tuṣṭe kriyāḥ sarvāḥ sidhyantyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 6.62 ||
rasavidyā dṛḍhaṃ gopyā māturguhyamiva dhruvam |
bhavedvīryavatī guptā nirvīryā ca prakāśanāt || 6.63 ||
na rogividitaṃ kāryaṃ bahubhirviditaṃ tathā |
rogiṇāṃ bahubhirjñātaṃ bhavennirvīryamauṣadham || 6.64 ||


________________________________________________________


 7
rasaśālāṃ prakurvīta sarvabādhāvivarjite |
sarvauṣadhimaye deśe ramye kūpasamanvite || 7.1 ||
yakṣatryakṣasahasrākṣadigvibhāge suśobhane |
nānopakaraṇopetāṃ prākāreṇa suśobhitām || 7.2 ||
śālāyāḥ pūrvadigbhāge sthāpayedrasabhairavam |
vahnikarmāṇi cāgneye yāmye pāṣāṇakarma ca || 7.3 ||
nairṛtye śastrakarmāṇi vāruṇe kṣālanādikam |
śoṣaṇaṃ vāyukoṇe ca vedhakarmottare tathā |
sthāpanaṃ siddhavastūnāṃ prakuryādīśakoṇake || 7.4 ||
padārthasaṃgrahaḥ kāryo rasasādhanahetukaḥ |
sattvapātanakoṣṭhīṃ ca jharatkoṣṭhīṃ suśobhanām || 7.5 ||
bhūmikoṣṭhīṃ calatkoṣṭhīṃ jaladroṇyo'pyanekaśaḥ |
bhastrikāyugalaṃ tadvannalike vaṃśalohayoḥ || 7.6 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     svarṇādimayyaḥ kuṇḍyo vartulapātrāṇi || 7.6;1

____________________


svarṇāyoghoṣaśulvāśmakuṇḍyaścarmakṛtāṃ tathā |
karaṇāni vicitrāṇi dravyāṇyapi samāharet || 7.7 ||
kaṇḍaṇī peṣaṇī svalpā droṇīrūpāśca vartulāḥ |
āyasāstaptakhallāśca mardakāśca tathāvidhāḥ || 7.8 ||
sūkṣmacchidrasahasrāḍhyā dravyagālanahetave |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     kaṭatrāṇi kaṣāyitacarmakhaṇḍāni || 7.9 b;1

____________________


cālanī ca kaṭatrāṇi śalākā hi ca kuṇḍalī || 7.9 ||

[sieve]
cālanī trividhā proktā tatsvarūpaṃ ca kathyate |
vaiṇavībhiḥ śalākābhirnirmitā grathitā guṇaiḥ |
kīrtitā sā sadā sthūladravyāṇāṃ gālane hitā || 7.10 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     iyaṃ catuṣkoṇā dīrghacālanī || 7.10;1
*     tatra dīrghāḥ śalākāstiraścīnāśca vaṃśamayyaścatuṣkoṇakāṣṭhapaṭṭikāchidreṣu bahirnirgatāgrāḥ sūtrabaddhāḥ kāryāḥ || 7.10;2

____________________


[sieve:: v.2]
cūrṇacālanahetośca cālanyanyāpi vaṃśajā || 7.11 ||
karṇikārasya śālmalyā harijātasya kambayā |
caturaṅgulavistārayuktayā nirmitā śubhā || 7.12 ||

[sieve:: kuṇḍalī]
kuṇḍalyaratnivistārā chāgacarmābhiveṣṭitā |
vājivālāmbarānaddhatalā cālanikā parā |
tayā pracālanaṃ kuryāddhartuṃ sūkṣmataraṃ rajaḥ || 7.13 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     kuṇḍalīṃ kuṇḍalākārāṃ vartulāṃ tṛtīyāṃ cālanīṃ vidadhyāt || 7.13;1
*     vājivālā aśvapucchakeśāḥ || 7.13;2
*     ambaraṃ vastram || 7.13;3
*     tadanyatareṇānaddhatalā baddhatalā || 7.13;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     kuṇḍalī cālanyā veṣṭanī || 7.13;1
*     aratnivistārā prasāritakaniṣṭhāṅgulibaddhamuṣṭihastapramāṇāyatā caturaṅgulavistṛtakarṇikārādivalkalanirmitā aratnivistāraveṣṭanī yuktā chāgacarmamaṇḍitā aśvapucchakeśasūkṣmavastraracitataladeśā sūkṣmataradravyacālanārthamaparavidhā cālanītyarthaḥ || 7.13;2

____________________


mūṣāmṛttuṣakārpāsavanopalakapiṣṭakam |
trividhaṃ bheṣajaṃ dhātujīvamūlamayaṃ tathā |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     śarkarā vālukā || 7.14 -d;1



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     śarkarā atikṣudrapāṣāṇaravakāḥ || 7.14 -d;1
*     tadapekṣayā sthūlāḥ śvetāḥ kaṇāḥ sitopalāḥ || 7.14 -d;2
*     śarkarāśabdena vālukāpi grāhyā || 7.14 -d;3

____________________


[śikhitra, śarkarā]
śikhitrā govaraṃ caiva śarkarā ca sitopalā || 7.14 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     sitopalā kaṭhinī phulakaḍī iti bhāṣā || 7.14;1

____________________


[charcoal]
śikhitrāḥ pāvakocchiṣṭā aṅgārāḥ kokilā matāḥ || 7.15 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     dagdhakāṣṭhakaṭhinakhaṇḍānāmeva śikhitrasaṃjñā kokilasaṃjñā ca || 7.15;1
*     saṃprati teṣāṃ sāmānyamavāntarabhedaṃ cāha śikhitrā iti || 7.15;2
*     pāvakena vahninā bhuktvā ucchiṣṭāḥ parityaktā aṅgārāḥ śikhitrāḥ kokilāśca matāḥ || 7.15;3
*     viśeṣastvitthaṃ vahninā bhuktvā svayaṃ tyaktāḥ śikhitrā haṭhātpratikūlavāyudhūlikṣepamṛttikādinipīḍanādinā yatnena vahnito viyojitā aṅgārāḥ kokilā matā iti ceti || 7.15;4
*     te prajvalitā jalena vinā nirvāṇāḥ kṛtā vahnimuktāḥ kṛtāḥ || 7.15;5

____________________


[charcoal:: kokila]
kokilāścetitāṅgārā nirvāṇāḥ payasā vinā || 7.16 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     śikhitralakṣaṇe kokilā uktāḥ ataḥ kokilāśabdasyārthamāha kokilā iti || 7.16;1
*     cetitāṅgārāḥ taptāṅgārāḥ payaso vinā svayaṃ nirvāṇāḥ śāntāḥ cette aṅgārāḥ kokilāḥ matāḥ kokilāḥ kaylā iti bhāṣā || 7.16;2

____________________


[driedcowdung]
piṣṭakaṃ chagaṇaṃ chāṇamupalaṃ cotpalaṃ tathā |
giriṇḍopalasāṭhī ca saṃśuṣkacchagaṇābhidhāḥ || 7.17 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     caṣakaṃ pānapātraṃ vāṭī glāsiti prasiddham || 7.17;1

____________________


kācāyomṛdvarāṭānāṃ kūpikā caṣakāni ca || 7.18 ||

[bottle]
kūpikā kupikā siddhā golā caiva giriṇḍikā || 7.19 ||

[caṣakaparyāyāḥ]
caṣakaṃ ca kaṭorī ca vāṭikā khārikā tathā |
kañcolī grāhikā ceti nāmānyekārthakāni hi || 7.20 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     kṣudraśiprāḥ kṣudrākārāḥ śuktayaḥ || 7.20;1

____________________


śūrpādiveṇupātrāṇi kṣudraśiprāśca śaṅkhikāḥ |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     śaṅkhikāḥ kṣudraśaṅkhamayapātrāṇi || 7.21 b;1



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     kṣurakā nāpitasya śastraviśeṣāḥ || 7.21 b;1



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     pākyo yavakṣāraḥ || 7.20;1

____________________


kṣuraprāśca tathā pākyaḥ yaccānyattatra yujyate |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     pālikā darvī || 7.21 b;1



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     karṇikā vilīti mahārāṣṭrabhāṣāyām || 7.21 b;1
*     sā cātra vartulākāradantapaṅktiśikharā grāhyā || 7.21 b;2

____________________


pālikā karṇikā caiva śākacchedanaśastrakāḥ || 7.21 ||
śālāsammārjanādyaṃ hi rasapākāntakarma yat |
tatropayogi yaccānyattatsarvaṃ paravidyayā || 7.22 ||
śrīrasāṅkuśayā sarvaṃ mantrayitvā samarcayet |
anyathā tadgataṃ tejaḥ parigṛhṇanti bhairavāḥ || 7.23 ||
rasasaṃcintakā vaidyā nighaṇṭujñāśca vārttikāḥ |
sarvadeśajabhāṣājñāḥ saṃgrāhyāste'pi sādhakaiḥ || 7.24 ||
rasapākāvasānaṃ hi sadāghoraṃ ca jāpayet || 7.25 ||
sodyamāḥ śucayaḥ śūrā baliṣṭhāḥ paricārakāḥ || 7.26 ||
dharmiṣṭhaḥ satyavāgvidvān śivakeśavapūjakaḥ |
sadayaḥ padmahastaśca saṃyojyo rasavaidyake || 7.27 ||
patākākumbhapāthojamatsyacāpāṅkapāṇikaḥ |
anāmādhastharekhāṅkaḥ sa syādamṛtahastavān || 7.28 ||
adeśikaḥ kṛpāmukto lubdho guruvivarjitaḥ |
kṛṣṇarekhākaro vaidyo dagdhahastaḥ sa ucyate || 7.29 ||
nigrahamantrajñāste yojyā nidhisādhane || 7.30 ||
baliṣṭhāḥ satyavantaśca raktākṣāḥ kṛṣṇavigrahāḥ |
bhūtatrāsanavidyāśca te yojyā balisādhane || 7.31 ||
nirlobhāḥ satyavaktāro devabrāhmaṇapūjakāḥ |
yaminaḥ pathyabhoktāro yojanīyā rasāyane || 7.32 ||
dhanavanto vadānyāśca sarvopaskarasaṃyutāḥ |
guruvākyaratā nityaṃ dhātuvādeṣu te śubhāḥ || 7.33 ||
tattadauṣadhanāmajñāḥ śucayo vañcanojjhitāḥ |
nānāviṣayabhāṣājñāste matā bheṣajāhṛtau || 7.34 ||
śucīnāṃ satyavākyānāmāstikānāṃ manasvinām |
saṃdehojjhitacittānāṃ rasaḥ sidhyati sarvadā || 7.35 ||
daśāṣṭakriyayā siddho raso'sau sādhakottamaḥ |
hā raso naṣṭamityuktvā sevetānyatra taṃ rasam || 7.36 ||
rasasiddho bhavenmartyo dātā bhoktā na yācakaḥ |
jarāmukto jagatpūjyo divyakāntiḥ sadā sukhī || 7.37 ||


________________________________________________________


 8
kathyate somadevena mugdhavaidyaprabuddhaye |
paribhāṣā rasendrasya śāstraiḥ siddhaiśca bhāṣitā || 8.1 ||

[dhanvantaribhāga]
ardhaṃ siddharasasya tailaghṛtayorlehasya bhāgo'ṣṭamaḥ saṃsiddhākhilalohacūrṇavaṭakādīnāṃ tathā saptamaḥ |
yo dīyeta bhiṣagvarāya gadibhirnirdiśya dhanvantariṃ sarvārogyasukhāptaye nigadito bhāgaḥ sa dhanvantareḥ || 8.2 ||

[rudrabhāga (ḍef.)]
bhaiṣajyakrīṇitadravyabhāgo'pyekādaśo hi yaḥ |
vaṇigbhyo gṛhyate vaidyai rudrabhāgaḥ sa ucyate || 8.3 ||

[viśvāsaghātaka (badphysician|lchemist)]
pragṛhyādhikarudrāṃśaṃ yo'samīcīnamauṣadham |
dāpayellubdhadhīrvaidyaḥ sa syādviśvāsaghātakaḥ || 8.4 ||

[kajjalī]
dhātubhirgandhakādyaiśca nirdravairmardito rasaḥ |
suślakṣṇaḥ kajjalābho'sau kajjalītyabhidhīyate || 8.5 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha kajjalīlakṣaṇamāha dhātubhiriti || 8.5;1
*     dhātavaśca |
*     svarṇaṃ rūpyaṃ ca tāmraṃ ca raṅgaṃ jasadameva ca |
*     sīsaṃ lohaṃ ca saptaite dhātavo girisaṃbhavāḥ |
*     iti || 8.5;2
*     ādyaśabdena haritālamanaḥśilādisaṃgrahaḥ || 8.5;3
*     cakāreṇopadhātusaṃgrahaḥ || 8.5;4
*     teṣāṃ bhāgo rasasamo viṣamo vā yathopadeśaṃ grāhyaḥ || 8.5;5
*     saiva kajjalī dravaṃ dattvā marditā cedrasapaṅkasaṃjñāṃ labhate || 8.5;6
*     kajjalyupayogaśca rasasindūrādividhānārthaṃ bodhyaḥ || 8.5;7
*     rasapaṅkopayogaṃ trailokyasundararasādividhānārthaṃ vakṣyati || 8.5;8

____________________


[rasapaṅka]
sadravā marditā saiva rasapaṅka iti smṛtā || 8.6 ||

[piṣṭī (1)]
arkāṃśatulyādrasato'tha gandhānniṣkārdhatulyāttruṭiśo'bhi khalle |
arkātape tīvratare vimardyātpiṣṭī bhavetsā navanītarūpā || 8.7 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha piṣṭīlakṣaṇamāha arkāṃśeti || 8.7;1
*     niṣkārdhatulyānniṣkārdharūpabhāgamitādityarthaḥ || 8.7;2
*     gandhakādarkāṃśatulyādrasato'rkaśabdo dvādaśasaṃkhyābodhakaḥ || 8.7;3
*     tatsaṃkhyākā ye'ṃśā bhāgā niṣkārdhātmakā bhāgāstattulyādrasātpāradāt || 8.7;4
*     niṣkārdhetyupalakṣaṇaṃ gṛhītakiṃcinmānasya || 8.7;5
*     tena gandhakasya yo bhāgastato dvādaśaguṇitaḥ pāradabhāgo'tra grāhya ityarthaḥ || 8.7;6
*     yathoktabhāgamubhayaṃ pṛthaggṛhītvā tīvre'rkātape lohe khalve truṭiśa īṣanmānena punaḥ punardattvā mardanānnavanītarūpā mṛdulā navanītākhyā ca piṣṭī bhavati || 8.7;7
*     atra navanītākhyo gandhako bhakṣyaḥ pāradaśca bhakṣakaḥ || 8.7;8
*     sa tu nānārūpapiṣṭīṣu samāna eva || 8.7;9
*     ato bhakṣyanāmnā bhedabodhakeneyaṃ navanītākhyetyabhidhīyate || 8.7;10
*     asyā nāmāntaraṃ gandhakapiṣṭīti || 8.7;11

____________________


[piṣṭī (2)]
khalle vimardya gandhena dugdhena saha pāradam |
peṣaṇātpiṣṭatāṃ yāti sā piṣṭīti matā paraiḥ || 8.8 ||

[pātanapiṣṭī]
caturthāṃśasuvarṇena rasena ghṛṣṭiṣaṣṭikā |
bhavetpātanapiṣṭī sā rasasyottamasiddhidā || 8.9 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha pātanapiṣṭīlakṣaṇamāha caturthāṃśeti || 8.9;1
*     caturthāṃśaṃ śuddhaṃ svarṇacūrṇaṃ pāradamadhye prakṣipya taptalohakhalve'mlarasena jambīrādijena yāmaparyantaṃ dviyāmaparyantaṃ vā mardanena saṃjātā cūrṇarūpā sā hemapiṣṭikāpi pātanopayogena siddhikaratvātpātanapiṣṭirityabhidhīyate || 8.9;2
*     tādṛśīṃ piṣṭiṃ kṛtvā pātanāyantre'dhasthapātrāntastala ūrdhvabhājane vā liptvā praharacatuṣṭayaparyantamagniyogenordhvaṃ pāradaṃ pātayet || 8.9;3
*     tadanantaraṃ pāradātpṛthagbhūtvādhobhāgasthatatsvarṇacūrṇamūrdhvalagnapāradaṃ ca yantrādbahirniṣkāsyaikīkṛtya punastaptalohakhalve'mlena rasena pūrvavanmardayitvā pūrvavatpātayet || 8.9;4
*     evaṃ śatadhā pātanena kṣīṇo gatirahito nirdoṣaḥ pāradaḥ sthiro'gnisaho bhavati || 8.9;5
*     tataśca bījādijāraṇakrameṇālpāyāsenaiva dehalohakaratvarūpottamasiddhipradaśca bhavatītyarthaḥ || 8.9;6

____________________


[kṛṣṭī]
rūpyaṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā rasagandhādibhirhatam |
samutthitaṃ ca bahuśaḥ sā kṛṣṭī hematārayoḥ || 8.10 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     jātarūpaṃ suvarṇam || 8.10;1
*     samutthitamiti antarbhūtaṇyarthaprayogastena samutthāpitaṃ śoṣitamityartha ūrdhvapātanāyantre sādhitamityartho vā || 8.10;2
*     sā utthāpanakriyā || 8.10;3
*     kṛṣṭīti || 8.10;4
*     svarṇaraupyayoḥ sā kriyā kṛṣṭīti bodhyam || 8.10;5
*     rasādibhiḥ saha svarṇaṃ vā raupyaṃ vā kenacinmārakadravyeṇa saṃmardya bahuśaḥ ātape śoṣayedathavā rasagandhādibhirmāritaṃ svarṇaṃ raupyaṃ vā bahuvāramūrdhvapātanayantreṇa samutthāpayetsā kriyā kṛṣṭī bodhyā || 8.10;6

____________________


piṣṭīṃ kṣipetsuvarṇāntarna varṇo hīyate tayā || 8.11 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     pūrvarītyā kṛtayā suvarṇaraupyayoranyatarakṛṣṭyā saha suvarṇaṃ saṃmardya puṭanena svarṇasya varṇānyatā na jāyate || 8.11;1

____________________


svarṇakṛṣṭyā kṛtaṃ bījaṃ rasasya parirañjanam || 8.12 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     bījaṃ nirvāpaṇaviśeṣeṇa ityādinā vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇo dhātuviśeṣāṇāṃ vicitrasaṃskāraviśeṣaḥ || 8.12;1
*     uktaprakāreṇaiva kṛtasvarṇakṛṣṭīnirmitaṃ bījaṃ pāradaṃ rañjayet || 8.12;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha kṛṣṭīlakṣaṇamāha rūpyamiti || 8.12;1
*     jātarūpyaṃ suvarṇam || 8.12;2
*     ādiśabdena mākṣikahiṅgulādiparigrahaḥ || 8.12;3
*     tairmāritaṃ punaḥ punaḥ pañcamitrasaṃskāreṇa prakṛtyavasthāpannaṃ kṛtam || 8.12;4
*     evaṃ saptavāraṃ daśavāraṃ votthāpitasvarṇatāraṃ ca krameṇa hemakṛṣṭī tārakṛṣṭī cābhidhīyate || 8.12;5
*     rasagandhādiyogena malavyapohanādujjvalamutkṛṣṭaṃ sārūpyaṃ svarṇaṃ tāraṃ cetyarthaḥ || 8.12;6
*     tāṃ svarṇakṛṣṭīṃ drute hīnavarṇasvarṇe kṣipet || 8.12;7
*     tena kṣepeṇa varṇo na hīyate tatsvarṇaṃ hīnavarṇaṃ na dṛśyate || 8.12;8
*     pūrṇavarṇaṃ dṛśyata ityarthaḥ || 8.12;9
*     evameva hīnavarṇatāre tārakṛṣṭyāḥ kṣepeṇāpi tāraṃ pūrṇavarṇaṃ bhavatītyartho'pi bodhyaḥ || 8.12;10
*     kṛtaṃ kalpitaṃ saṃskṛtamityarthaḥ || 8.12;11
*     bījaṃ śuddhaṃ svarṇotpādakaṃ lohadhātvādipāradasya pītavarṇatvakaraṃ bhavet || 8.12;12
*     rasahṛdaye'ṣṭamāvabodhe pāradasya kṛṣṭimapi pūjyapādā udājahruḥ || 8.12;13

____________________


[varalohakam]
tāmraṃ tīkṣṇasamāyuktaṃ drutaṃ nikṣipya bhūriśaḥ |
sagandhalakucadrāve nirgataṃ varalohakam || 8.13 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     varalohaprastutaprakāramāha tāmramiti || 8.13;1
*     tīkṣṇasamāyuktaṃ tīkṣṇalauhasaṃyuktam || 8.13;2
*     bhūriśaḥ saptavārānityarthaḥ || 8.13;3
*     tīkṣṇalauhaṃ tāmraṃ ca agnisaṃtāpena dravīkṛtya gandhakacūrṇamiśritalakucarase nikṣipedghanībhūtaṃ taddvayamuttolya punaḥ drāvayitvā pūrvarase nikṣipedevaṃ saptavārān || 8.13;4
*     drutamityatra mṛtamiti pāṭho mṛtaṃ bhasmībhūtam || 8.13;5
*     evaṃ prakriyayā tasmādutkṛṣṭalauhaṃ nirgamiṣyatīti || 8.13;6

____________________


[hemaraktī]
tena raktīkṛtaṃ svarṇaṃ hemaraktītyudāhṛtam || 8.14 ||
nikṣiptā sā drute svarṇe varṇotkarṣavidhāyinī |
tārasya rañjanī cāpi bījarāgavidhāyinī || 8.15 ||

[tāraraktī]
evameva prakartavyā tāraraktī manoharā |
rañjanī khalu rūpyasya bījānāmapi rañjanī || 8.16 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha hemaraktītāraraktyorlakṣaṇaṃ phalaṃ cāha tāmramiti || 8.16;1
*     tīkṣṇalohena samabhāgena saṃyuktamekīkṛtaṃ tāmraṃ bahuvāraṃ drutaṃ kṛtvā gandhakasahite lakucarase nirvāpayet || 8.16;2
*     tadvallohamiti khyātaṃ tārasya rañjanī drute tasminnikṣepeṇetyarthaḥ || 8.16;3
*     tāraraktīkaraṇārthaṃ tu varalohena tārameva dhamanenaikīkṛtya raktīkṛtaṃ kāryaṃ sāpi rūpyasya bījānāṃ ca rañjanī raktavarṇotpādikā || 8.16;4

____________________

[dala]
mṛtena vā baddharasena vānyallohena vā sādhitamanyaloham |
sitaṃ ca pītatvamupāgataṃ taddalaṃ hi candrānalayoḥ prasiddham || 8.17 ||
māsakṛtabaddhena rasena saha yojitam |
sādhitaṃ vānyalohena sitaṃ pītaṃ ca taddalam || 8.18 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     saṃprati tāradalasya svarṇadalasya ca lakṣaṇamāha mṛteneti || 8.18;1
*     mṛtena bhasmībhūtena pāradena yena kenaciddravyeṇa baddho yaḥ pāradastena baddhapāradena mṛtena lohena vā sādhitaṃ saṃskṛtamanyalohaṃ vijātīyaṃ lohaṃ sitatvamupāgataṃ prāptaṃ śvetavarṇaviśiṣṭamathavā pītatvamupāgataṃ prāptaṃ pītavarṇaviśiṣṭaṃ bhavet || 8.18;2
*     tatkrameṇa candradalamanaladalaṃ svarṇadalaṃ śāstre prasiddham || 8.18;3
*     analaḥ svarṇam || 8.18;4
*     kṛtrimarajataṃ kṛtrimasvarṇaṃ cetyarthaḥ || 8.18;5
*     ravā iti nāmnā loke prasiddham || 8.18;6

____________________


[śulbanāga]
mākṣikeṇa hataṃ tāmraṃ daśavāraṃ samutthitam |
tadvadviśuddhanāgaṃ hi dvitayaṃ taccatuṣpalam || 8.19 ||
nīlāñjanahataṃ bhūyaḥ saptavāraṃ samutthitam |
iti saṃsiddhametaddhi śulvanāgaṃ prakīrtyate || 8.20 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     śulvanāgamāha mākṣikeṇeti || 8.20;1
*     svarṇamākṣikeṇa saha māritaṃ tāmraṃ tathā sīsakaṃ ca pṛthakpṛthakdaśavāraṃ samutthāpitaṃ kṛtvā tayoḥ pratyekaṃ catuṣpalamādāya ekīkuryāttataḥ tadubhayaṃ bhūyaḥ nīlāñjanena saha bhasmīkṛtya saptavāraṃ samutthāpayedevaṃ ca tayoḥ śulvanāga iti saṃjñā jāyate iti || 8.20;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     saṃprati śulbanāgalakṣaṇamāha mākṣikeṇeti || 8.20;1
*     samabhāgamākṣikeṇa māritaṃ tāmraṃ pañcamitrasaṃskāreṇa samutthitaṃ kuryāt || 8.20;2
*     evaṃ daśavāraṃ kṛtvā tadvattāmravanmākṣikeṇaiva māritaṃ viśuddhaṃ nāgaṃ tadubhayaṃ mithaḥ samaṃ catuṣpalamitaṃ gṛhītvā punarnīlāñjanena samabhāgena māritaṃ pañcamitrasaṃskāreṇa punarutthāpitaṃ kuryāt || 8.20;3
*     evaṃ saptavāraṃ māraṇapūrvakotthāpanena saṃśuddhametaddravyadvaṃdvaṃ rasaśāstre śulbanāgamiti kīrtitam || 8.20;4

____________________


sādhitastena sūtendro vadane vidhṛto nṛṇām |
nihanti māsamātreṇa mehavyūhaṃ viśeṣataḥ || 8.21 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     tadupayogamāha sādhita iti || 8.21;1
*     taccūrṇaṃ pāradena samabhāgena śuddhena sahājamūtreṇa saṃmardya vajramūṣāyāṃ dhmānena jātaṃ khoṭaṃ śodhanagaṇena saha dhmānācchuddhaṃ kṛtvā taṃ khoṭabaddhaṃ pāradaṃ mukhamadhye yo dhārayettasya mehasamūhanāśo bhavet || 8.21;2

____________________


pathyāśanasya varṣeṇa palitavalibhiḥ saha |
gṛdhradṛṣṭirlasatpuṣṭiḥ sarvārogyasamanvitaḥ || 8.22 ||

[piñjarī]
lohaṃ lohāntare kṣiptaṃ dhmātaṃ nirvāpitaṃ drave |
pāṇḍupītaprabhaṃ jātaṃ piñjarītyabhidhīyate || 8.23 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     piñjarīlakṣaṇamāha lohamiti || 8.23;1
*     yathā jasadaṃ mūṣāyāṃ tāmre nikṣipya dhmānenaikībhūtaṃ patrajādyauṣadhīrase pītavargajarase vā nikṣepātpittalaṃ bhavati tadvadanyadapi tādṛgvarṇaṃ saṃkīrṇalohaṃ piñjarīvācyaṃ bhavati || 8.23;2
*     sā oṣadhīpatrajā śabdavācyā || 8.23;3
*     yasyā utpattau kāraṇaṃ bījasthāne patrameva bhavati || 8.23;4
*     cīritapattrā hrasvakṣupaviśeṣarūpā ceyamupavana utpadyate || 8.23;5

____________________


[candrārka]
bhāgāḥ ṣoḍaśa tārasya tathā dvādaśa bhāsvataḥ |
ekatrāvartitāstena candrārkamiti kathyate || 8.24 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     candrārkasaṃjñāmāha bhāgā iti || 8.24;1
*     bhāsvataḥ tāmrasya || 8.24;2
*     ekatrāvartitāḥ ekasminneva pātre yugapaddravīkṛtya āloḍitāḥ || 8.24;3
*     tena tathā āloḍanena || 8.24;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     candrārkaṃ lakṣayati bhāgā iti || 8.24;1
*     āvartitā dhmānenaikībhūtarasarūpā ityarthaḥ || 8.24;2
*     bhāsvatastāmrasya || 8.24;3
*     candrārkasya khoṭabaddharasena vedhātkanakotpattiśca rasasāre'bhihitā || 8.24;4

____________________


[nirvāpaṇam]
sādhyalohe'nyalohaṃ cetprakṣiptaṃ vaṅkanālataḥ |
nirvāpaṇaṃ tu tatproktaṃ vaidyairnirvāhaṇaṃ khalu || 8.25 ||
kṣipennirvāpaṇaṃ dravyaṃ nirvāhye samabhāgikam |
āvāhyaṃ vāpanīye ca bhāge dṛṣṭe ca dṛṣṭavat || 8.26 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha nirvāhalakṣaṇamāha sādhyaloha iti || 8.26;1
*     yasyāṃ kriyāyāṃ sādhyalohe nirvāhye lohe drute sati tasmiṃstatrānyalohaṃ vaṅkanālataḥ prakṣiptaṃ vaṅkanālajadhmānenaiva drutaṃ kṛtvā prakṣiptaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ || 8.26;2
*     vaṅkanāletyuktyā bhastrādijadhmānavyāvṛttiḥ || 8.26;3
*     nirvāhaṇamekīkaraṇamiti yāvat || 8.26;4
*     nirvāpaṇaṃ nirvāhaṇaṃ cetyanarthāntaram || 8.26;5
*     ekīkaraṇārthakanirvāpaṇaśabdaprayogastu saṃprati nopalabhyate || 8.26;6
*     kiṃtu sarvatra tadarthaṃ nirvāhaṇaśabda eva prayukto dṛśyate || 8.26;7
*     etacca nirvāhaṇaṃ prāyo bījādisaṃskārārthaṃ kriyate || 8.26;8
*     yathā pāradodare bījānāṃ garbhadrāvaṇayogyatāsaṃpādanārthaṃ nirvāhaṇasaṃskāraṃ vyājahāra rasahṛdaye |
*     mṛtanāgaṃ mṛtavaṅgaṃ mṛtavaraśulbaṃ mṛtaṃ tathā tīkṣṇam |
*     ekaikaṃ hemavare śatanirvyūḍhaṃ dravati garbhe ca || 8.26;9
*     iti || 8.26;10
*     ekaguṇasvarṇe nāgādyanyatamaṃ śataguṇanirvāhitaṃ kāryamityarthaḥ || 8.26;11
*     evaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ svarṇaṃ varabījaṃ bhavati || 8.26;12
*     atha nirvāpaṇadravyabhāgānuktisthāne taddravyasya kiyadbhāgaprakṣepaḥ kāryastadāha kṣipediti || 8.26;13
*     kṣipennirvāhayedityarthaḥ || 8.26;14
*     kṣepasāmānyādanuktāvāvāpadravyamānamapyāha āvāpyamiti || 8.26;15
*     āvāpalakṣaṇamasminnevādhyāye vakṣyati || 8.26;16
*     vāpanīye lohādye drute dravye || 8.26;17
*     tatra bhāge dṛṣṭa ukte tu dṛṣṭavaduktabhāgamitameva tannirvāhaṇadravyamāvāpadravyaṃ ca kṣipet || 8.26;18

____________________


[vāritaram]
mṛtaṃ tarati yattoye lohaṃ vāritaraṃ hi tat || 8.27 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     mṛtalohasya bodhakānāṃ vividhapāribhāṣikaśabdānāṃ lakṣaṇānyāha mṛtamiti || 8.27;1
*     tallohaṃ vāritaramucyate yanmṛtaṃ sattoye prakṣiptaṃ taratīti || 8.27;2

____________________


[rekhapūrṇa]
aṅguṣṭhatarjanīghṛṣṭaṃ yattadrekhāntare viśet |
mṛtalohaṃ taduddiṣṭaṃ rekhāpūrṇābhidhānataḥ || 8.28 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     rekhāpūrṇalauhalakṣaṇamāha aṅguṣṭheti || 8.28;1
*     tadrekhāntare tayoḥ tarjanyaṅguṣṭhayoḥ rekhāntare rekhāvakāśe aṅguṣṭhatarjanībhyāṃ gharṣaṇe kṛte yatra lauhe tayoraṅgulyoḥ rekhāsamūhaḥ aṅkito bhavedityarthaḥ || 8.28;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     tallohaṃ rekhāpūrṇamucyate yanmṛtamaṅguṣṭhatarjanīmadhye saṃmarditaṃ tayoḥ sūkṣmarekhāntaraṃ praviśediti || 8.28;1

____________________


[apunarbhava]
guḍaguñjāsukhasparśamadhvājyaiḥ saha yojitam |
nāyāti prakṛtiṃ dhmānādapunarbhavamucyate || 8.29 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     nirutthalauhalakṣaṇamāha guḍeti || 8.29;1
*     sukhasparśaḥ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ nirutthīkārakamitrapañcakavarge ṭaṅkaṇaśabdagrahaṇātyaduktaṃ rasendrasāre |
*     madhusarpistathā guñjā ṭaṅkaṇaṃ guggulustathā |
*     mitrapañcakametattu gaṇitaṃ dhātumelane || 8.29;2
*     iti || 8.29;3
*     mitrapañcakoktagugguloḥ kāryamatra guḍena saṃpādanīyamiti || 8.29;4
*     prakṛtiṃ svarūpam || 8.29;5
*     apunarbhavamapunarutthānaṃ nirutthamiti yāvat || 8.29;6



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha pūrṇamṛtaṃ tallohamapunarbhavamucyate || 8.29;1
*     yatpratyekaṃ samabhāgairguḍādibhiḥ samastaiḥ saha miśritaṃ piṇḍīkṛtaṃ mūṣāmadhye prakṣipya dhmānena prakṛtiṃ pūrvāvasthāmāmalohabhāvaṃ na prāpnuyāditi || 8.29;2

____________________


[ūnama, uttama]
tasyopari guru dravyaṃ dhānyaṃ copanayeddhruvam |
haṃsavattīryate vāriṇyuttamaṃ parikīrtitam || 8.30 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     athottamākhyamṛtalohalakṣaṇamāha yadvā pūrṇamṛtaṃ yallohaṃ vāritaraṃ svapṛṣṭha upanītaṃ dhānyaṃ dhārayati || 8.30;1
*     dhānyabhāraṃ sahata ityarthaḥ || 8.30;2
*     tatra lohe taddhānyaṃ kathaṃ tarati tadupamayāha yathā jale haṃsaviśeṣāstaranti tadvattathā dhānyabhārasahaṃ tanmṛtalohamuttamamiti nāmnā śāstre kīrtitam || 8.30;3
*     atra bahuṣu pustakeṣu ūnamaṃ parikīrtitamityapi pāṭhaḥ || 8.30;4
*     tasya svayamūnamūnabhāraṃ laghvapi yallohaṃ svāpekṣayā gurudravyaṃ gurudhānyaṃ vā māti sahata iti vyutpattyā kliṣṭārthabodhakaḥ sa pāṭho nātipriyaḥ || 8.30;5

____________________


[nirutthāpunarbhava]
raupyeṇa saha saṃyuktaṃ dhmātaṃ raupyeṇa cellaget |
tadā nirutthamityuktaṃ lohaṃ tadapunarbhavam || 8.31 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     nirutthasya lakṣaṇāntaramāha raupyeṇeti || 8.31;1
*     raupyaṃ lauhaṃ ca ekatra saṃsthāpya bhastrayā dhmāpanena yadi parasparaṃ miśrībhavettadāpi nirutthaṃ jñeyam || 8.31;2




*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     athāpunarbhavākhyamṛtalohasyaiva nirutthasaṃjñāprāpakaṃ lakṣaṇamāha raupyeṇeti || 8.31;1
*     saṃyuktaṃ melāpakamadhvājyaṃ dattvā mūṣāyāṃ saṃyojitaṃ na lagetna sajjetaikībhāvaṃ na prāpnuyādityarthaḥ || 8.31;2
*     ekībhāvaśca raupyamānavṛddhyā bodhyaḥ || 8.31;3
*     spaṣṭīkṛtaṃ caitadrasasaṃketakalikāyām |
*     lohamadhvājyagaṃ tāraṃ svapramāṇaṃ bhavedyadā |
*     tadā lohaṃ nirutthaṃ syādanyathā sādhayetpunaḥ || 8.31;4
*     iti || 8.31;5
*     tāraṃ śuddhatāram || 8.31;6

____________________


[bīja]
nirvāpaṇaviśeṣeṇa tattadvarṇaṃ bhavedyadā |
mṛdulaṃ citrasaṃskāraṃ tadbījamiti kathyate || 8.32 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     bījamāha nirvāpaṇeti || 8.32;1
*     pūrvanirdiṣṭasādhyalohe tattallohamārakānyalohanikṣeparūpanirvāhaṇākhyasaṃskāraviśeṣeṇa yadā sādhyalohaṃ prakṣiptalauhavarṇaṃ bhavettadā mṛdulaṃ sukhasparśaṃ citrasaṃskāramāhitāpūrvaguṇāntaraṃ tatbījamiti jñeyam || 8.32;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha jāraṇāsaṃskāre prakṣepārhasya bījasya lakṣaṇamāha nirvāhaṇeti || 8.32;1
*     nirvāhyate prakṣepeṇaikīkriyate'neneti vyutpattyā nirvāhaṇaśabdena nirvāhakaṃ dravyaṃ grāhyam || 8.32;2
*     prakṣepeṇa guṇaviśeṣotpādanapūrvakaikībhāvasāmānyācca tena śabdena vāpanadravyasyāpi saṃgrahaḥ kāryaḥ || 8.32;3
*     etadabhiprāyeṇaiva nirvāpaṇaviśeṣeṇetyapi pāṭho dṛśyate || 8.32;4
*     tena nirvāhaṇena nirvyūḍhaṃ yadbījopādānarasalohādi tattadvarṇaṃ nirvāhaṇadravyasya samānavarṇaṃ śāstranirdiṣṭavarṇaṃ ca bhavati || 8.32;5
*     vāpanadravyeṇa mṛdu ca bhavettathā sattvabhasmotpādakavidhibhyāṃ ca prāptamārdavam || 8.32;6
*     tathā kṛto vicitrasaṃskāro garbhadrāvako raktavargakaṣāye niṣecanarūpo melāpakadravyasaṃyogādiśca yasyetyevaṃguṇaviśiṣṭaṃ rasaśāstre siddhabījamityabhidhīyate || 8.32;7
*     bījaṃ dvividhaṃ pītaṃ sitaṃ ceti bhedāt || 8.32;8
*     tadapi pratyekaṃ dvividham || 8.32;9
*     akṛtrimaṃ kṛtrimaṃ ca || 8.32;10
*     yaddhi khanisambhūtaṃ śuddhaṃ svarṇaṃ rajataṃ vā tadakṛtrimam || 8.32;11
*     yattu dhāturasoparasasaṃyogakriyāviśeṣajanitasvarṇarajatotpādanayogyatāsaṃpannaṃ pītaṃ svarṇarītyādi śvetaṃ vaṅgādi tatkṛtrimamityācakṣate || 8.32;12
*     tadapi pratyekaṃ dvividham || 8.32;13
*     śuddhaṃ miśraṃ ca || 8.32;14
*     śuddhamekaikaṃ miśraṃ mithaḥ saṃkīrṇam || 8.32;15
*     punarapi sarvametattridhā bhavati || 8.32;16
*     kalpitaṃ rañjitaṃ pakvaṃ ca || 8.32;17
*     tatra tatkalpitaśabdavācyaṃ yacchuddharasoparasaśuddhamāritaṃ mithaḥ saṃyuktaṃ miśraṃ vā lohādidvaṃdvīkṛtamekaikaṃ sattvakaraṇavidhinā nirvāhaṇena dvaṃdvamelāpakavidhinā ca militaṃ śuddhaṃ jātamārdavaṃ tadevaikībhāvaṃ vrajati ca raktādivargeṣu secanena prāptavarṇaṃ rañjitasaṃjñakaṃ bhavati || 8.32;18
*     nirvāhaṇena hemaśeṣakṛtaṃ tāraśeṣakṛtaṃ vā yadbījaṃ bhūdharayantrādiṣu puṭitaṃ tatpakvabījaṃ vadanti || 8.32;19
*     evaṃ cedamuktalakṣaṇaṃ sarvabījānāṃ saṃgrāhakaṃ bodhyam || 8.32;20
*     atra rasoparasānāṃ śodhanaṃ tu sūryāvartādigaṇena kuryāt || 8.32;21
*     śaśaraktabhāvanayā kāntaṃ śudhyati || 8.32;22
*     sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ patraṃ ca lavaṇakṣārāmlaravisnuhīkṣīrairliptaṃ dhmātaṃ paścānnirguṇḍīrase bahuvāraṃ niṣecitaṃ sacchudhyati || 8.32;23
*     nāgavaṅgaghoṣatāmrāṇi tu prataptāni nirguṇḍīrasasekaistanmūlarajaḥpravāpaiśca śudhyanti || 8.32;24
*     sarvo'pi lohaḥ pratapto mākṣīkadaradavāpena bahuvāraṃ kṛtena śudhyati || 8.32;25
*     rasoparasānāṃ sattvāni mūloktavidhinā pātayet || 8.32;26
*     saṃprati bījanirvāhaṇavidhiṃ rasahṛdayoktaṃ vakṣyāmi || 8.32;27
____________________


[uttaraṇa]
idameva vinirdiṣṭaṃ vaidyairuttaraṇaṃ khalu |
saṃspṛṣṭalohayorekalohasya parināśanam || 8.33 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     uttaraṇamāha idamiti || 8.33;1
*     idaṃ saṃsṛṣṭalohayorityādinā vakṣyamāṇarūpamityarthaḥ || 8.33;2
*     uttaraṇamiti saṃjñāviśeṣaḥ || 8.33;3
*     saṃsṛṣṭeti miśritalauhayoḥ ekalohasya parināśanaṃ dhmāpanādikriyāviśeṣeṇa ekasmātanyatpṛthakkṛtya bhasmīkaraṇamekasmādanyasya bahirniṣkāśanaṃ vā || 8.33;4

____________________


[tāḍana]
pradhmātaṃ vaṅkanālena tattāḍanamudāhṛtam || 8.34 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     tāḍanamāha pradhmātamiti || 8.34;1
*     tatuttaraṇakriyāniṣpannaṃ saṃsṛṣṭalauhayorekaloham || 8.34;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha tāḍanasaṃjñāmāha saṃsṛṣṭeti || 8.34;1
*     parisādhanaṃ dhmānenāvaśeṣakārakaṃ yadvaṅkanālena pradhmātaṃ prakarṣeṇa dhmānaṃ kriyate tadrasaśāstre tāḍanaśabdena kathitam || 8.34;2
*     yathā ghoṣādvaṅgaṃ vināśya tāmrasyāvaśeṣārthaṃ dhmānaṃ tacca gārākoṣṭhyāṃ kāryam || 8.34;3

____________________


[dhānyābhra]
cūrṇābhraṃ śālisaṃyuktaṃ vastrabaddhaṃ hi kāñjike |
niryātaṃ mardanādvastrāddhānyābhramiti kathyate || 8.35 ||

[sattva]
kṣārāmladrāvakairyuktaṃ dhmātamākarakoṣṭhake |
yastato nirgataḥ sāraḥ sattvamityabhidhīyate || 8.36 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     sattvamāha kṣāreti || 8.36;1
*     kṣāraḥ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ lauhaśodhakatvātamlaḥ kāñjikādikaṃ drāvakaḥ guñjāṭaṅkaṇamadhvājyaguḍāḥ drāvakapañcakāḥ ityuktasvarūpaḥ taiḥ || 8.36;2
*     ākarakoṣṭhike koṣṭhikāyantre || 8.36;3
*     sāraḥ sthirāṃśaḥ prasādabhāga iti yāvat || 8.36;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     sattvalakṣaṇamāha kṣārāmleti || 8.36;1
*     kṣāro yavakṣārādiḥ || 8.36;2
*     amlaṃ jambīrarasādi || 8.36;3
*     drāvakaṃ guḍagugguluguñjādi || 8.36;4
*     drāvako gaṇo vakṣyamāṇaḥ || 8.36;5
*     pañcājaṃ pañcagavyādi || 8.36;6
*     matsyādipiṇḍīdravyaṃ ca tadyuktaṃ tena piṇḍīkṛtaṃ rasoparasādi dravyam || 8.36;7
*     ākarakoṣṭhaka ākaro vakṣyamāṇaḥ || 8.36;8
*     koṣṭhikā varṇanasthānagranthaḥ || 8.36;9
*     tatrokte yogye koṣṭhe koṣṭhayantre'ṅgārakoṣṭhyādau ca mūṣāyāṃ prakṣipya yadā bhastrāvaṅkanālādinā dhmātaṃ syāttadā tato dravyāddravarūpo yaḥ sāro nirgacchati pṛthagākāreṇa nipatati tatsattvamucyate || 8.36;10

____________________


[(eka)kolīsaka]
koṣṭhikāśikharāpūrṇaiḥ kokilairdhmānayogataḥ |
ākaṇṭhamanuprāptairekakolīsako mataḥ || 8.37 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     ekakolīsakamāha koṣṭhiketi || 8.37;1
*     koṣṭhikāyantrāgrabhāgaparyantamaṅgārairāpūrya dhmāpanavaśātmāraṇīyadravyaiḥ mūṣākaṇṭhaparyantamāgataiḥ upalakṣito yatkarma ekakolīsakākhyaḥ kriyāviśeṣo mataḥ || 8.37;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha dhmānakriyāyā mānaviśeṣajñānārthaṃ kṛtāyāḥ kolīsakasaṃjñāyā lakṣaṇamāha koṣṭhikāśikhareti || 8.37;1
*     śikharaparyantaṃ paripūrṇakokilānāṃ dhmānena mūṣākaṇṭhaparyantaṃ yadāpacayo bhavati tāvaddhmānasyaikakolīsaka iti saṃjñā || 8.37;2
*     asyā eva nāliśaketi paryāyāntaram || 8.37;3

____________________


[passendesḥolśfrḥolśkohle]
drāvaṇe sattvapāte ca mādhukāḥ khādirāḥ śubhāḥ |
durdrāve vaṃśajāste tu svedane bādarāḥ śubhāḥ || 8.38 ||

[hiṅgulākṛṣṭa]
vidyādharākhyayantrasthādārdrakadrāvamarditāt |
samākṛṣṭo raso yo'sau hiṅgulākṛṣṭa ucyate || 8.39 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha hiṅgulākṛṣṭarasamāha vidyādhareti || 8.39;1
*     vidyādharayantraṃ dvividhaṃ nirjalaṃ sajalaṃ ca || 8.39;2
*     tatra prathamaṃ kanakasundaraprabhṛtirasānāmūrdhvabhāge puṭanārthamupayujyate || 8.39;3
*     tacca nyubjordhvapātreṇa saṃpuṭitam || 8.39;4
*     yattu dvitīyamuttānapātraghaṭitaṃ tadatra vidyādharaśabdena grāhyam || 8.39;5
*     ārdrakamarditādityasyāgre hiṅgulāditi śeṣaḥ || 8.39;6

____________________


[ghoṣākṛṣṭa]
svalpatālayutaṃ kāṃsyaṃ vaṅkanālena tāḍitam |
muktaraṅgaṃ hi tattāmraṃ ghoṣākṛṣṭamudāhṛtam || 8.40 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     ghoṣākṛṣṭasya lakṣaṇamāha svalpeti || 8.40;1
*     tāḍitaṃ paridhmātam || 8.40;2
*     atra tālaḥ svalpaśabdena kāṃsyasya caturthāṃśena grāhyaḥ || 8.40;3
*     raṅgasya tāmrātpṛthagbhūtvā vināśārthaṃ tadupayogo bodhyaḥ || 8.40;4

____________________


[varanāga]
tīkṣṇanīlāñjanopetaṃ dhmātaṃ hi bahuśo dṛḍham |
kṛṣṇaṃ drutadrāvaṃ varanāgaṃ taducyate || 8.41 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     varanāgalakṣaṇamāha tīkṣṇeti || 8.41;1
*     atra viśeṣyapadollekhābhāve'pi varanāga iti saṃjñābalādeva tīkṣṇanīlāñjanopetamityatra nāgamiti viśeṣyapadaṃ śeṣaḥ bodhyaḥ || 8.41;2
*     tīkṣṇalauhanīlāñjanasaṃyuktaṃ yatsīsakaṃ bahuvāraṃ dṛḍhamādhmātaṃ satsukomalaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ drutadrāvaṃ ca syāttatsīsakaṃ varanāgaṃ bodhyam || 8.41;3
*     tīkṣṇamiti pṛthakpāṭhe nīlāñjanaṃ tīkṣṇalauhaṃ ca ityarthaḥ || 8.41;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     varanāgasya lakṣaṇamāha tīkṣṇamiti || 8.41;1
*     samabhāganīlāñjanasaṃyutaṃ tīkṣṇalohaṃ samabhāgena ṭaṅkaṇaṃ dattvāndhamūṣāyāṃ dṛḍhaṃ dhmātaṃ sadyadā nāgāpekṣayāpyatimṛdu kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ śīghradrāvaṃ ca bhavettadaitadvaranāgamucyate || 8.41;2
*     arkāpāmārgakadalībhasmatoyena lolayet || 8.41;3
*     tadvastragālitaṃ grāhyaṃ svacchatoyaṃ tadātape || 8.41;4
*     mṛdukṛṣṭaṃ drutadrāvamiti pāṭhe dhmātvā mūṣātaḥ kṛṣṭaṃ bahirākṛṣṭaṃ śītaṃ sadapi saṃjātamārdavamagniyogena śīghradrāvaṃ ca bhavedityarthaḥ || 8.41;5
*     nāgādvaraṃ śreṣṭhametadvaranāgamiti || 8.41;6
*     uttarapadasya pūrvanipātena siddho'yaṃ śabdaḥ || 8.41;7
*     asyopayogaṃ tvasthirāṇāṃ kharparādisattvānāṃ sthirīkaraṇārthaṃ pūjyapādā udājahrū rasasāre || 8.41;8
*     tatprakārastu dvitīyādhyāye'tra rasakasattvavidhivyākhyāyāṃ prakāśita eva || 8.41;9
*     nāgaṃ nīlāñjanopetamiti pāṭhastu prāmādika eva || 8.41;10
*     sattvānāṃ hi sthirīkaraṇe nāgasyānupayogāditi bodhyam || 8.41;11

____________________


[utthāpana]
mṛtasya punarudbhūtiḥ samproktotthāpanākhyayā || 8.42 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     utthāpanaśabdārthamāha mṛtasyeti || 8.42;1
*     pāradarasoparasalohādīnāmatimūrchitānāṃ prākṛtaguṇakriyāsahitānāṃ vā punarudbhūtiḥ punaḥ pūrvavatsthānāpannatvamutthāpanamityabhidhīyate || 8.42;2




*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     utthāpanāmāha mṛtasyeti || 8.42;1
*     punarudbhūtiḥ yantrādiyogena svarūpāpādanamityarthaḥ || 8.42;2

____________________


[ḍhālana]
drutadravyasya nikṣepo drave taḍḍhālanaṃ matam || 8.43 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     ḍhālanasaṃjñāṃ lakṣayati drutadravyasyeti || 8.43;1
*     yathā varalohakavidhau satīkṣṇatāmrasya dhmānena drutasya lakucadrāve nikṣepo'trādhyāye prāguktaḥ sa ḍhālanaśabdena paribhāṣyate || 8.43;2
*     atha capalo dvividhaḥ || 8.43;3
*     pāṣāṇaviśeṣaḥ kṛtrimo dhāturūpaśca || 8.43;4
*     tatra pāṣāṇaviśeṣo dvitīyādhyāya uktaḥ || 8.43;5

____________________


[capala ausBlei]
triṃśatpalamitaṃ nāgaṃ bhānudugdhena marditam |
vimardya puṭayettāvadyāvatkarṣāvaśeṣitam || 8.44 ||
na tatpuṭasahasreṇa kṣayamāyāti sarvathā |
capalo'yaṃ samādiṣṭo vārttikairnāgasambhavaḥ || 8.45 ||

[capala ausZinn]
itthaṃ hi capalaḥ kāryo vaṅgasyāpi na saṃśayaḥ || 8.46 ||

[capala fṃrrasabandha]
tatspṛṣṭahastasaṃspṛṣṭaḥ kevalo badhyate rasaḥ || 8.47 ||
sa raso dhātuvādeṣu śasyate na rasāyane |
ayaṃ hi kharvaṇākhyena lokanāthena kīrtitaḥ || 8.48 ||

[dhauta]
bhūbhujaṃgaśakṛttoyaiḥ prakṣālyāpahṛtaṃ rajaḥ |
kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ hi tatproktaṃ dhautākhyaṃ rasavādibhiḥ || 8.49 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     dhautamāha bhūbhujaṃgeti || 8.49;1
*     apahṛtamapacitaṃ niḥsāritamityarthaḥ || 8.49;2
*     rajaḥ puṭanādikāle tatsaṃlagnāṅgārādicūrṇam || 8.49;3
*     tatcapalībhūtaṃ nāgaṃ vaṅgaṃ ca || 8.49;4
*     bhūnāgamalarasaiḥ tanmalamiśrajalairvā pariśodhitamalādikaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ capalībhūtaṃ nāgaṃ vaṅgaṃ ca dhautanāgaṃ dhautavaṅgaṃ ca proktamiti niṣkarṣaḥ || 8.49;5



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     dhautasya lakṣaṇamāha bhūbhujaṅgeti || 8.49;1
*     bhūbhujaṅgā bhūnāgāḥ || 8.49;2
*     teṣāṃ śakṛnmṛdviśeṣarūpameva tatsaṃnidhāvupalabhyate || 8.49;3
*     etasyopayogastu kharasattvotpādanārthaṃ pañcamādhyāye prāgabhihita eva || 8.49;4

____________________


[dvaṃdvāna]
dravyayormardanādhmānāddvaṃdvānaṃ parikīrtitam || 8.50 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     dvandvānamāha dravyayoriti || 8.50;1
*     saṃsṛṣṭadravyadvayaṃ mardayitvā dhmāpanena dvandvānasaṃjñā jāyate || 8.50;2
*     dvandvānamityatra bandhanamiti pāṭhāntaram || 8.50;3

____________________


[bhañjinī]
bhāgāddravyādhikakṣepamanu varṇasuvarṇake |
dravairvā vahnikāgrāso bhañjanī vādibhirmatā || 8.51 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     bhañjanīmāha bhāgāditi || 8.51;1
*     varṇena suvarṇa iva tasmin varṇasuvarṇake rājapittale bhāgātmāraṇārthanirdiṣṭaprakṣepyabhāgamapekṣya dravyādhikakṣepaṃ dravyāṇāṃ prakṣepyadravyāṇāmadhikakṣepamadhikaprakṣepamanu paścādadhikaprakṣepānantaramityarthaḥ yaḥ vahnikāgrāsaḥ māraṇīyadravyagatavahninirvāpaṇaṃ vāthavā dravairjalādibhiḥ yaḥ vahnikāgrāsaḥ sa bhañjanīti saṃjñayā vādibhiḥ rasavādibhiḥ matā kathitā || 8.51;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     bhajanīlakṣaṇamāha bhāgādrūpyādhiketi || 8.51;1
*     anuvarṇasuvarṇake hīnavarṇasuvarṇe hemakṛṣṭiṃ dattvā śatāṃśavidhinā raktapītavarṇotkarṣārthaṃ yatamānena sādhakena pramādātkāraṇāntareṇa vā yadā rūpyasya yo bhāgaḥ śāstra uktastaṃ vihāya pramāṇāpekṣayādhikaḥ kṣipyate tādṛśakṣepaṃ kṛtvā yadā varṇikāhrāse prāgavasthitapītavarṇasyāpi hrāsaḥ kṣayo bhavati || 8.51;2
*     athavā dravyairvedhādāvanupadiṣṭadravyairvaṅganīlāñjanādibhiḥ saṃmīlanenāpi yo varṇikāhrāsaḥ sā rasaśāstre bhañjanīti kathyate || 8.51;3
*     hemakṛṣṭerlakṣaṇaṃ tu || 8.51;4
*     rūpyaṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā rasagandhādibhirhatam |
*     samutthitaṃ ca bahuśaḥ sā kṛṣṭī hematārayoḥ || 8.51;5
*     iti prāguktameva || 8.51;6
*     atra vidhau hemakṛṣṭiḥ || 8.51;7
*     rasadaradatāpyagandhakamanaḥśilārājavartakaṃ vimalam |
*     puṭamṛtaśulbaṃ tāre nirvyūḍhaṃ hemakṛṣṭiriyam || 8.51;8
*     iti || 8.51;9
*     rasadaradādīnāṃ puṭena mṛtaṃ yacchulbaṃ tattāre nirvāhitaṃ kuryāt || 8.51;10
*     śatāṃśavidhiśca |
*     aṣṭānavatirbhāgāstārastveko'pi kanakabhāgaḥ syāt |
*     sūtasyaiko bhāgaḥ śatāṃśavidhireṣa vikhyātaḥ || 8.51;11
*     iti rasahṛdaye || 8.51;12
*     tatra nirvyūḍhatārabhāgasyādhikakṣepeṇānu varṇasuvarṇe varṇanāśaḥ spaṣṭa eva || 8.51;13

____________________


[cullakā]
pataṅgīkalkato jātā lohe tāre ca hematā |
dināni katicitsthitvā yātyasau cullakā matā || 8.52 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     cullakāmāha pataṅgīti || 8.52;1
*     pataṅgīkalkataḥ pataṅgīkalkāntarityarthaḥ katiciddināni sthitvā lauhaṃ tāraṃ ceti śeṣaḥ tatra lauhe viśeṣataḥ tāre ca yā hematā svarṇasādṛśyaṃ jātā asau hematā cullakā yāti cullakā iti saṃjñāṃ labhate ityarthaḥ iti matā || 8.52;2
*     cullakā gilṭī iti loke || 8.52;3
*     yadvā lakṣaṇadvayamidaṃ tena pataṅgītyārabhya hematā ityantena ślokārdhena hematālakṣaṇaṃ dinānītyādiślokārdhena ca cullakālavaṇaṃ jñeyam || 8.52;4
*     asmin pakṣe vakṣyamāṇapataṅgīrāgākhyarañjakadravyaviśeṣasya kalkalepanena sarvalauhe viśeṣataḥ raupye hematā iti saṃjñā jāyate || 8.52;5
*     hemno bhāvaḥ iti hematā svarṇasādṛśyam || 8.52;6
*     tathā asau lauhatārayorhematā katiciddināni sthitvā pataṅgīkalke ityāśayaḥ cullakā yāti cullaketyākhyayā khyātiṃ yātītyarthaḥ || 8.52;7



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha cullikālakṣaṇamāha pataṅgīkalkata iti || 8.52;1
*     pataṅgī aśuddharasoparasādikṛtabījajīrṇaḥ pāradastadghaṭito yaḥ kalkastena jātaṃ yallohe tāmrādau gauravatejasvitvādiguṇasahitaṃ tāratvaṃ hematā vā kiṃcitkālaparyantaṃ sthitvā naśyati sā kriyā culliketi matā || 8.52;2
*     uktaṃ ca rasahṛdaye |
*     yaḥ punaretaiḥ kurute karmāśuddhairbhavedrasastasya |
*     avyāpakaḥ pataṅgī na rase rasāyane yojyaḥ || 8.52;3
*     iti || 8.52;4
*     etai rasoparasaiḥ || 8.52;5
*     karma jāraṇādikarma || 8.52;6
*     pataṅgī pakṣivadūrdhvagāmī || 8.52;7
*     pāradaghaṭitakalkastu yogataraṅgiṇyādigranthokto bodhyaḥ || 8.52;8
*     tathā coktaṃ taraṅgiṇyām |
*     pāradaṣṭaṅka ekasyā dvipalaṃ pītakharparam |
*     mardayetsudṛḍhaṃ tāvadraso yāvadvilīyate || 8.52;9
*     punarjambīranīreṇa guḍena ca samanvitam |
*     śoṣayeccātape piṣṭvā ślakṣṇaṃ kṛtvā ca dhāryate || 8.52;10
*     arkadugdhasya dātavyā bhāvanāstā yathā tathā |
*     asya kalkasya siddhasya bhāga ekaśca ṭaṅkaṇaḥ || 8.52;11
*     tāmraṃ bhāgatrayaṃ dattvā dhāmyatāmandhamūṣayā |
*     suvarṇaṃ divyatejaḥ syātkuṅkumādatiricyate || 8.52;12
*     iti || 8.52;13
*     evaṃ nāgārjunādigranthe tāratvotpādakakalko'pi draṣṭavyaḥ || 8.52;14

____________________


[pataṃgīrāga]
rañjitāddhi cirāllohāddhmānādvā cirakālataḥ |
viniryāsaḥ sa nirdiṣṭaḥ pataṅgīrāgasaṃjñakaḥ || 8.53 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     saṃprati kriyāviśeṣasiddhasyāciravināśino lohasthasya rāgasya saṃjñāmāha rañjitāditi || 8.53;1
*     atrāpi pataṅgikalkata ityanuvartanīyam || 8.53;2
*     tādṛśakalkena rañjitāllohāddhmānādiyatnena vinā kālāntare dhmānena sadyo vā yo rāgo viniryāti viyujya nirgacchati sa pataṅgīrāgasaṃjñako rasaśāstre khyātaḥ || 8.53;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     pūrvaśloke pataṅgī ityuktamataḥ tāmeva vivṛṇoti rañjitāditi || 8.53;1
*     cirāddīrghakālaṃ vyāpya rañjitādvakṣyamāṇaraktādivargānyatamavargeṇa rāgapariprāptādyasmātkasmādapi lauhādathavā cirakālataḥ sudīrghakālaṃ dhmānāddhmāpitādyasmātkasmādapi rañjitalauhādyaḥ viniryāsaḥ niḥsravaḥ sattvamiti yāvatnirgacchatīti śeṣaḥ sa pataṅgīrāgasaṃjñakaḥ nirdiṣṭaḥ || 8.53;2

____________________


[āvāpa, pratīvāpa, ācchādana]
drute dravyāntarakṣepo lohādye kriyate hi yaḥ |
sa āvāpaḥ pratīvāpastadevācchādanaṃ matam || 8.54 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     druta iti || 8.54;1
*     etadudāharaṇaṃ yathā |
*     suradālibhasma galitaṃ triḥsaptakṛtvo'tha gojalaṃ śuṣkam |
*     vāpena salilasadṛśaṃ karoti mūṣāgataṃ tīkṣṇam || 8.54;2
*     iti || 8.54;3
*     tathā |
*     suragopakadeharajaḥ suradāliphalaiḥ samāṃśakairdeyaḥ |
*     vāpo drute suvarṇe drutamāste tadrasaprakhyam || 8.54;4
*     iti || 8.54;5
*     rasaprakhyaṃ jalasadṛśamityarthaḥ || 8.54;6

____________________


[abhiṣeka]
drute vahnisthite lohe viramyāṣṭanimeṣakam |
salilasya parikṣepaḥ so'bhiṣeka iti smṛtaḥ || 8.55 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     abhiṣekamāha drute iti || 8.55;1
*     vahnisthite prajvalitacullyupari eva avasthite || 8.55;2
*     aṣṭanimeṣakamaṣṭavāramakṣṇornimīlanonmīlanātmakaṃ kālaṃ viramya dravībhavanānantaramapekṣya || 8.55;3

____________________


[nirvāpa]
taptasyāpsu vinikṣepo nirvāpaḥ snapanaṃ ca tat || 8.56 ||

[Zeitpunktfṃrāvāpa usw.]
pratīvāpādikaṃ kāryaṃ drute lohe sunirmale || 8.57 ||

[śuddhāvarta]
yadā hutāśo dīptārciḥ śuklotthānasamanvitaḥ |
śuddhāvartastadā jñeyaḥ sa kālaḥ sattvanirgame || 8.58 ||

[bījāvarta]
drāvyadravyanibhā jvālā dṛśyate dhamane yadā |
drāvasyonmukhatā seyaṃ bījāvartaḥ sa ucyate || 8.59 ||

[svaṅgaśītala]
vahnisthameva śītaṃ yattaduktaṃ svāṅgaśītalam || 8.60 ||

[bahiḥśīta]
agnerākṛṣya śītaṃ yattadbahiḥśītamucyate || 8.61 ||

[svedana]
kṣārāmlairauṣadhairvāpi dolāyantre sthitasya hi |
pacanaṃ svedanākhyaṃ syānmalaśaithilyakārakam || 8.62 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     svedanamāha kṣārāmlairiti || 8.62;1
*     auṣadhaiḥ tattallauhaśodhakadravyāṇāṃ svarasādibhirityarthaḥ || 8.62;2
*     malaśaithilyakārakaṃ svedanena mārdave jāte antarmalānāṃ pṛthakkaraṇaṃ vīkaraṇaṃ vā || 8.62;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     idānīmaṣṭādaśasaṃskārāṇāṃ krameṇa lakṣaṇamāha kṣārāmlairiti || 8.62;1
*     kṣārā yavakṣārasarjanaṭaṅkaṇāḥ || 8.62;2
*     amlaṃ kāñjikādi || 8.62;3
*     auṣadhaistryūṣaṇādibhiḥ sarpākṣyādibhiśca || 8.62;4
*     tāstu rasaratnākare parigaṇitā eva svedanādyupayogikāñjikavidhau || 8.62;5
*     tathā ca tadgranthaḥ |
*     nānādhānyairyathāprāptaistuṣavarjyairjalānvitaiḥ |
*     mṛdbhāṇḍe pūritaṃ rakṣa yāvadamlatvamāpnuyāt || 8.62;6
*     tanmadhye bhṛṅgarāṅmuṇḍī viṣṇukrāntā punarnavā |
*     mīnākṣī caiva sarpākṣī sahadevī śatāvarī || 8.62;7
*     triphalā girikarṇī ca haṃsapādī ca citrakam |
*     samūlaṃ kaṇḍayitvā tu yathālābhaṃ niveśayet || 8.62;8
*     pūrvāmlabhāṇḍamadhye tu dhānyāmlakamidaṃ smṛtam |
*     svedanādiṣu sarvatra rasarājasya yojayet || 8.62;9
*     iti || 8.62;10
*     sthitasya tryūṣaṇādikalkaliptavastrāvṛtabhūrjapatrapoṭalikāmadhye sthitasya pāradayetyarthaḥ || 8.62;11
*     tryūṣaṇādikalko'pi rasaratnākare'bhihitaḥ |
*     tryūṣaṇaṃ lavaṇaṃ rājī rajanī triphalādravam |
*     mahābalā nāgabalā meghanādā punarnavā || 8.62;12
*     meṣaśṛṅgī citrakaṃ ca navasāraṃ samaṃ samam |
*     etatsamastaṃ vyastaṃ vā pūrvāmlenaiva peṣayet || 8.62;13
*     tatkalkena lipedvastraṃ yāvadaṅgulamātrakam |
*     tanmadhye nikṣipetsūtaṃ baddhvā pacyāddinatrayam || 8.62;14
*     dolāyantre'mlasaṃyukte svedito jāyate rasaḥ |
*     prakṣālya kāñjikaiḥ soṣṇaistamādāya vimardayet || 8.62;15
*     iti || 8.62;16
*     malaśaithilyakārakaṃ malāśca dvādaśa doṣāḥ || 8.62;17
*     tacchaithilyajanakam || 8.62;18
*     śithilānāmeva hi teṣāṃ mocayituṃ śakyatvāt || 8.62;19
*     dvādaśa doṣāśca viṣaṃ vahnirmalaśceti naisargikāstrayaḥ || 8.62;20
*     nāgaja ekaḥ || 8.62;21
*     vaṅgaja ekaḥ || 8.62;22
*     tathā bhūmijo girijo vārjaḥ || 8.62;23
*     nāgajau dvau || 8.62;24
*     vaṅgajau ca dvau || 8.62;25
*     iti sapta kañcukākhyā ityevaṃ dvādaśa doṣā devaiḥ prārthitamaheśvareṇa pārade saṃyojitā bhavanti || 8.62;26

____________________


[mardana]
uditairauṣadhaiḥ sārdhaṃ sarvāmlaiḥ kāñjikairapi |
peṣaṇaṃ mardanākhyaṃ syādbahirmalavināśanam || 8.63 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     mardanamāha uditairiti || 8.63;1
*     uditairauṣadhaiḥ tatra tatroktabheṣajadravyāṇāṃ svarasaiḥ kvāthairvā ityarthaḥ || 8.63;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     uditairiti || 8.63;1
*     vakṣyamāṇamardanavidhyuktairgṛhadhūmādyauṣadhaiḥ sarvāmlairamlavetasapramukhairamlavargoktairdravyaiḥ || 8.63;2
*     teṣāṃ rasenetyarthaḥ || 8.63;3
*     tathā kāñjikairapi pāradasya yatpeṣaṇaṃ tridinaparyantaṃ kṛtaṃ tadbahirmalavināśanaṃ bhavati || 8.63;4
*     bahirmalaḥ svedenāntarviśliṣṭo bhūtvā pāradadehādbahiḥ saṃśliṣṭo rāgato naisargikadoṣaṃ vihāya navavidho yo malastadvināśako bhavatīti || 8.63;5
*     kevalaṃ svedamardanābhyāṃ na sarvathā bhujakañcukadoṣanāśanaṃ bhavati || 8.63;6

____________________


[mūrchana]
mardanādiṣṭabhaiṣajyairnaṣṭapiṣṭatvakārakam |
tanmūrchanaṃ hi vaṅgāhibhujakañcukanāśanam || 8.64 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     ato mardanapūrvakamagniyogena nāśaṃ kṛtvā pāradasya yatpiṣṭatvotpādanaṃ tanmūrchanasaṃskāranāmnāha mardanādiṣṭeti || 8.64;1
*     mardanārthaṃ vakṣyamāṇamūrchanasaṃskāravidhau kathitairgṛhakanyādibhirnaṣṭapiṣṭatvakārakaṃ yatkarma mardanapūrvakapācanarūpaṃ tanmūrchanamityabhidhīyate || 8.64;2
*     samāsato lakṣaṇaṃ tu dattagrāsasya grāsarahitasya vā gālanapātanavyaktirekeṇa pūrvāvasthāpannatvam || 8.64;3
*     tacca mardanapākābhyāṃ bhavatīti mardanottaraṃ yantrapuṭānyatareṇa pāko'pyaṅgatvena bodhya iti dvau yaugikau || 8.64;4
*     bhūjaśabdena bhūjadoṣaprabhṛtītyartho bodhyaḥ || 8.64;5
*     te ca kañcukā āvarakāḥ sapta doṣā vaṅgāhidoṣapramukhāḥ || 8.64;6
*     te ca mardanāgnibhyāṃ naśyanti || 8.64;7
*     vaṅgaśabdena vaṅgajadoṣo grāhyaḥ || 8.64;8
*     ahiśabdena nāgajadoṣaśca || 8.64;9
*     doṣāṇāṃ nānātvena vividhadṛḍhaśithilasaṃsargatāratamyena tannāśārthaṃ vividhopāyapradarśanamucitameveti na mardanasaṃskāreṇa mūrchanasya gatārthateti śaṅkyamiti bhāvaḥ || 8.64;10
*     tanmūrchanaṃ hi vaṅgāhibhūjakañcukanāśanamiti pāṭhaṃ gṛhītveyaṃ vyākhyā || 8.64;11
*     doṣatrayavināśanamiti pāṭhe tu naisargikadoṣanāśe sutarāṃ bahirdoṣanāśa ityabhiprāyaḥ || 8.64;12
*     ata evāyaṃ pāṭho rasahṛdayaṭīkāyāṃ caturbhujamiśritairādṛtaḥ || 8.64;13
*     naisargikadoṣetaradoṣāṇāṃ vāraṇāya pūrvoktamardanasaṃskāro naisargikadoṣavāraṇāyāyamiti vyavasthāyāḥ sukaratvāt || 8.64;14
*     evaṃ ca vaṅgāhibhūjakañcukanāśanamiti pāṭho na manorama iti bodhyam || 8.64;15



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     mūrchanamāha mardanāditi || 8.64;1
*     iṣṭabhaiṣajyaiḥ śodhakāditvena abhimatauṣadhaiḥ || 8.64;2
*     naṣṭapiṣṭatvakārakaṃ peṣaṇena svarūpanāśātyanmūrtibaddhatvam || 8.64;3
*     vaṅgeti || 8.64;4
*     nāgavaṅgādidoṣanāśanam || 8.64;5
*     etattu rasamuddiśya uktaṃ dhātvantarāṇāmapi svasvadoṣanāśanamiti ādipadena bodhyam || 8.64;6

____________________


[utthāpana]
svedātapādiyogena svarūpāpādanaṃ hi yat |
tadutthāpanamityuktaṃ mūrchāvyāpattināśanam || 8.65 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     utthāpanamāha svedeti || 8.65;1
*     svedaḥ ūrdhvapātanādinā svedanamātapaḥ raudrasaṃtāpaḥ ādinā mardanādīnāṃ grahaṇam || 8.65;2
*     svarūpāpādanaṃ prakṛtyavasthāpanam || 8.65;3
*     mūrchāvyāpattināśanaṃ mūrchanakriyājanitasvarūpadhvaṃsarūpavipattivāraṇam || 8.65;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     svedeti || 8.65;1
*     svedaḥ kāñjikayā kṣārāmlalavaṇaiśca dolāyantre svedanam || 8.65;2
*     ātapayogaḥ kāñjikādimarditarasasya kācapātre sūryatāpasthe dhāraṇam || 8.65;3
*     ādiśabdena sāgnikacullīsthe kaṭāhe vā pāradasya tasya dhāraṇamityādi || 8.65;4
*     tena pāradasya mūrchitasya yaccāñcalyatejasvitvagauravaviśiṣṭatvarūpamātmarūpaṃ tatpratiprāpaṇaṃ tadutthāpanamityucyate || 8.65;5

____________________


[naṣṭapiṣṭi]
svarūpasya vināśena piṣṭatvādbandhanaṃ hi tat |
vidvadbhirnirjitaḥ sūto naṣṭapiṣṭiḥ sa ucyate || 8.66 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     bandhanamāha svarūpasyeti || 8.66;1
*     piṣṭamiṣṭabhaiṣajyaiḥ peṣaṇaṃ piṣṭamiti bhāvoktaḥ tasya bhāvaḥ || 8.66;2
*     tattvātsvarūpasya vināśena svakīyaśubhratvacapalatvādirūpāpāyena yadrūpāpādanamiti śeṣastadbandhanaṃ vahninānucchidyamānatvaṃ mūrtibaddhatvamiti vā jñeyamiti śeṣaḥ || 8.66;3
*     asyaiva naṣṭapiṣṭiriti saṃjñāntaramāha vidvadbhiriti || 8.66;4
*     nirjito bandhanena naṣṭasvarūpaḥ yadvā nirjito mṛtaḥ sa sūtaḥ vidvadbhirnaṣṭapiṣṭirucyate || 8.66;5

____________________


[pātana]
uktauṣadhairmarditapāradasya yantrasthitasyordhvamadhaśca tiryak |
niryātanaṃ pātanasaṃjñamuktaṃ vaṅgāhisamparkajakañcukaghnam || 8.67 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     pātanātrayamāha uktauṣadhairiti || 8.67;1
*     niryātanaṃ śodhanādyarthaṃ yatkadarthanamityarthaḥ || 8.67;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha pātanasaṃskārasya lakṣaṇamāha uktauṣadhairiti || 8.67;1
*     vakṣyamāṇapātanavidhau nirdiṣṭatriphalādibheṣajaiḥ saha marditapāradasya tata ūrdhvapātanādiyantrasthitasya yanniryāpaṇamūrdhvādhastiryakprāpaṇaṃ tatpātanamucyate || 8.67;2
*     tena vaṅganāgajā yaugikadoṣā bhūjādisaptakañcukāśca sarvathā naśyanti || 8.67;3

____________________


[rodhana]
jalasaindhavayuktasya rasasya divasatrayam |
sthitirāsthāpanī kumbhe yāsau rodhanamucyate || 8.68 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     rodhanamāha jaleti || 8.68;1
*     jalasaindhavābhyāṃ saha kumbhamadhye divasatrayaṃ rasasya yā āsthāpanī ā samyaksthāpanī ṣaṇḍhadoṣanāśanapūrvakaṃ svavīrye sthāpanakāriṇī kriyeti śeṣaḥ asau sthitiḥ sthāpanaṃ rodhanamityucyate || 8.68;2
*     idaṃ hi svedanādikriyājanitakadarthanena ṣaṇḍhabhāvaprāptasya rasasya taddoṣanāśapūrvakavīryaprakarṣādhānārthaṃ jñātavyam || 8.68;3
*     granthāntare'sya bodhanamiti saṃjñā nirdiṣṭā || 8.68;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     rodhanaṃ lakṣayati jaleti || 8.68;1
*     kumbhe vakṣyamāṇe ghaṭayantre || 8.68;2
*     āpyāyanī puṣṭikarī || 8.68;3
*     atra saindhavamayamūṣāsaṃpuṭitaṃ kṛtvaikaviṃśatidinaparyantaṃ bhūdharapuṭanaṃ sṛṣṭyambujaiḥ saha mardanaṃ ca kṛtvā tataḥ paramiti vākyaśeṣo bodhyaḥ || 8.68;4

____________________


[niyamana]
rodhanāllabdhavīryasya capalatvanivṛttaye |
kriyate pārade svedaḥ proktaṃ niyamanaṃ hi tat || 8.69 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     niyamanamāha rodhanāditi || 8.69;1
*     svedaḥ svedanam || 8.69;2
*     yadyapyatra svedanārthaṃ dravyanirdeśo na kṛtaḥ tathāpi adhyetṝṇāṃ vijñānārthaṃ granthāntaroktaṃ tannirdiśyate tathā ca rasendracintāmaṇau |
*     sarpākṣīciñcikākanyābhṛṅgārakanakāmbubhiḥ |
*     dinaṃ saṃsveditaḥ sūto niyamātsthiratāṃ vrajet || 8.69;3
*     iti || 8.69;4
*     niyamanaṃ capalatvaparihārapūrvakasthiratvasaṃpādanam || 8.69;5



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     labdhavīryasya pāradasyeti śeṣaḥ || 8.69;1
*     svedaḥ pāradagarbhitamūṣāṃ bhūmimadhye gūḍhāṃ kṛtvā bhūmyupari karīṣāgnirityarthaḥ || 8.69;2

____________________


[dīpana]
dhātupāṣāṇamūlādyaiḥ saṃyukto ghaṭamadhyagaḥ |
grāsārthaṃ tridinaṃ svedo dīpanaṃ tanmataṃ budhaiḥ || 8.70 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     dīpanamāha dhātviti || 8.70;1
*     grāsārthaṃ ghanahemādīnāmiti śeṣaḥ || 8.70;2
*     svedaḥ dolāyantre kāñjikādinā rasasya iti śeṣaḥ || 8.70;3
*     dīpanaṃ grāsaśaktisaṃjananakriyāviśeṣaḥ || 8.70;4
*     rasendracintāmaṇikāreṇa dīpanadravyāṇi anyavidhānyuktāni yathā |
*     kāsīsaṃ pañcalavaṇaṃ rājikā maricāni ca |
*     bhūśigrubījamekatra ṭaṅgaṇena samanvitam || 8.70;5
*     āloḍya kāñjike dolāyantre pākāddinaistribhiḥ |
*     dīpanaṃ jāyate samyaksūtarājasya jāraṇe || 8.70;6
*     athavā citrakadrāvaiḥ kāñjike tridinaṃ pacet || 8.70;7
*     iti || 8.70;8



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha dīpanasaṃskāraṃ lakṣayati dhātupāṣāṇeti || 8.70;1
*     dhātavastāmrapatrādayaḥ || 8.70;2
*     pāṣāṇāḥ sphaṭikā mayūratutthakāsīsādayaḥ || 8.70;3
*     mūlāni sarpākṣyādimūlikāḥ || 8.70;4
*     ādyaśabdena kṣāralavaṇodakādi grāhyam || 8.70;5
*     ghaṭamadhyago ghaṭayantramadhyagaḥ pāradastridinaparyantaṃ svedyo bhavati yasmin karmaṇi taddīpanamiti khyātam || 8.70;6
*     atha jāraṇāyāmayathābalamayathākramaṃ ca grāsadānenājīrṇadoṣātpārade vikriyā syāditi grāsamānavicāro'vaśyaṃ kāryaḥ || 8.70;7

____________________


[grāsamāna]
iyanmānasya sūtasya bhojyadravyātmikā mitiḥ |
iyatītyucyate yāsau grāsamānaṃ samīritam || 8.71 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     grāsamānamāha iyanmānasyeti || 8.71;1
*     iyanmānasya etāvatparimāṇasya sūtasya saṃbandhavivakṣayā ṣaṣṭhī iyanmite sūte ityarthaḥ yā iyatī mitiḥ etāvatparimāṇaṃ bhojyadravyāṇāmiti śeṣaḥ iti ucyate bhojyadravyātmikā grasanīyasvarṇādidravyāṇāṃ mānanirdeśarūpā asau uktiḥ grāsamānaṃ samīritamiyanmānaḥ sūtaḥ iyanmānaṃ svarṇādidravyaṃ grasituṃ samarthaḥ evaṃrūpamānanirdeśaḥ grāsamānaṃ jñeyam || 8.71;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     ato grāsamānākhyasaṃskārasya lakṣaṇamāha iyanmānasyeti || 8.71;1
*     iyacchabdo'tra saṃskāryapāradasya gṛhītamānaviśeṣapalādivācakaḥ || 8.71;2
*     tādṛśamānamitapāradasyeyaccatuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśādimitābhrakasattvabījādyātmakaṃ dravyaṃ bhakṣaṇāya dattaṃ cetsukhena cīrṇaṃ jīrṇaṃ ca syāditi niścitā yā bījādermitistadgrāsamānaṃ khyātam || 8.71;3

____________________


[jāraṇā]
grāsasya cāraṇaṃ garbhe drāvaṇaṃ jāraṇaṃ tathā |
iti trirūpā nirdiṣṭā jāraṇā varavārttikaiḥ || 8.72 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     jāraṇātrayamāha grāsasyeti || 8.72;1
*     grāsasya grāsayogyasya svarṇāderityarthaṃ cāraṇaṃ rasāntaḥ kṣepaṇaṃ garbhe drāvaṇaṃ rasāntaḥ taralībhavanaṃ jāraṇaṃ viḍayantrādiyogena dravībhūtagrāsasya pākaḥ || 8.72;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha jāraṇāṃ lilakṣayiṣuḥ prathamaṃ sādhāraṇāṃstadavasthābhedān salakṣaṇān samāsata āha grāsasyeti || 8.72;1
*     śreṣṭhai rasavārttākuśalaiḥ sarvavidhā jāraṇā trirūpā triprakārā kathitā bhavati || 8.72;2
*     tatra grāsasya cāraṇaṃ pāradakartṛkagrāsabhakṣaṇakaraṇam || 8.72;3
*     garbhadrāvaṇaṃ grastabījādeḥ pāradodare drutikaraṇam || 8.72;4
*     jāraṇaṃ ca pārada ekībhāvo'nantadṛḍhasaṃbandhena || 8.72;5
*     eṣāṃ lakṣaṇānyanupadaṃ vakṣyati || 8.72;6

____________________


[jāraṇa:: subtypes:: grāsa, ...]
grāsaḥ piṇḍaḥ pariṇāmastisraścākhyāḥ parā punaḥ || 8.73 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     jāraṇāyā aparaṃ traividhyamāha grāsa iti || 8.73;1
*     grāsaḥ kavalīkaraṇarūpajāraṇā piṇḍaḥ kardamavatpiṇḍībhavanarūpajāraṇā || 8.73;2
*     parīṇāmaḥ avasthāntaraprāpaṇarūpajāraṇā || 8.73;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     punarapi jāraṇāyā avasthākṛtanāmāntarāṇi trīṇyāha grāsaḥ piṣṭiḥ parīṇāmaśceti || 8.73;1
*     tatra grāso grasanam || 8.73;2
*     piṣṭiḥ svedanamardanāgnyādibhiḥ pāradodare drutagrāsasya pāradasahitasya śuṣkaścūrṇaḥ || 8.73;3
*     pariṇāmārthastu prāgukta eva || 8.73;4
*     punarjāraṇā dvividhā bhavati || 8.73;5
*     samukhā nirmukhā ceti bhedena || 8.73;6

____________________


samukhā nirmukhā ceti jāraṇā dvividhā punaḥ || 8.74 ||

[nirmukhajāraṇā]
nirmukhā jāraṇā proktā bījādānena bhāgataḥ || 8.75 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     tatrādau nirmukhajāraṇāmāha nirmukheti || 8.75;1
*     niḥ nāsti mukhaṃ vakṣyamāṇacatuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśaḥ bījaprakṣeparūpamukhalakṣaṇaṃ yatra jāraṇāyāṃ tādṛśī || 8.75;2
*     bījeti || 8.75;3
*     bhāgataḥ yathābhāgaṃ tattaddravyajāraṇāyāṃ nirdiṣṭabhāgamanatikramya vakṣyamāṇacatuḥṣaṣṭibhāgātnyūnatayā adhikatayā vā ityarthaḥ bījādānena śuddhasvarṇaraupyātmakabījagrahaṇam || 8.75;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     tatrālpavaktavyatvātprathamaṃ nirmukhāyā eva lakṣaṇamāha nirmukheti || 8.75;1
*     catuḥṣaṣṭibhāgamitaṃ bījaṃ prathamaṃ yatra pāradodare na dīyate kiṃtu kevalaṃ śuddhadhātvādigrāsa eva sṛṣṭitrayanīrakaṇāvāsanauṣadhimardanādyupāyairjāryate sā jāraṇā nirmukhetyucyate || 8.75;2
*     rasoparasādimṛdudravyajāraṇe tasyā upayogaḥ kāryaḥ || 8.75;3

____________________


[bīja (def.)]
śuddhaṃ svarṇaṃ ca rūpyaṃ ca bījamityabhidhīyate || 8.76 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     mukhakaraṇopayogibījaśabdārthamāha śuddhamiti || 8.76;1
*     śuddhamakṛtrimamuttamaṃ khanijaṃ svarṇaṃ rūpyaṃ vaitacchāstroktaśuddhyā suśuddhaṃ kṛtvātra grāhyam || 8.76;2
*     etena kṛtrimanirvyūḍhamahābījādivyāvṛttiḥ kṛtā || 8.76;3

____________________


[samukhajāraṇā]
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśato bījaprakṣepo mukhamucyate || 8.77 ||

[samukharas-jāraṇā]
evaṃ kṛte raso grāsalolupo mukhavān bhavet |
kaṭhinānyapi lohāni kṣamo bhavati bhakṣitum |
iyaṃ hi samukhā proktā jāraṇā mṛgacāriṇā || 8.78 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     uktabījasya yathākramaṃ svarṇotpādārthaṃ raupyajananārthaṃ ca pāradasya catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśena pāradodare jāraṇārambhe prakṣepaḥ kāryaḥ || 8.78;1
*     evaṃ kṛte tasmin bīje pāradodare yathāvidhi cīrṇe jīrṇe ca sati pāradaḥ satvaraṃ grāsabhakṣaṇaṃ karotīti śāstre sa raso mukhavānityucyate || 8.78;2
*     tatkṛtaṃ yatkaṭhinalohādibhakṣaṇaṃ sā jāraṇā samukhetyuktā || 8.78;3
*     mṛgacāriṇā mṛgasahitaṃ vane carati saṃsāraṃ vihāya viriktena kenacidvaravārtikena || 8.78;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     samukhajāraṇāmāha evamiti || 8.78;1
*     evaṃ kṛte catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśato bījaprakṣepe kṛte || 8.78;2
*     mukhavān samukhaḥ || 8.78;3
*     mṛgacāriṇā tadākhyena kenacidrasatantrakṛtā || 8.78;4

____________________


[rākṣasavaktravat]
divyauṣadhisamāyogātsthitaḥ prakaṭakoṣṭhiṣu |
bhuñjītākhilalohādyaṃ yo'sau rākṣasavaktravān || 8.79 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     rākṣasamukharasamāha divyauṣadhīti || 8.79;1
*     divyauṣadhiḥ manaḥśilā || 8.79;2
*     prakaṭakoṣṭhiṣu vivṛtānanakoṣṭhikāyantreṣu || 8.79;3
*     bhuñjīta graset || 8.79;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha mahāmukhapāradasya lakṣaṇamāha divyauṣadhīti || 8.79;1
*     somalatādikāścatuḥṣaṣṭidivyauṣadhyastatsamāyogastadrasena kṛtamardanādinā kṛtasaṃskāraḥ || 8.79;2
*     tena prakāśamūṣāsvapi sthito'gnisahaḥ pārado dhmānena kaṭhinaṃ mṛdu sarvaṃ lohādi yadā bhunakti asau mahāmukhavānityucyate || 8.79;3

____________________


[cāraṇā]
rasasya jaṭhare grāsakṣapaṇaṃ cāraṇā matā || 8.80 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     jāraṇābhedacāraṇālakṣaṇamāha rasasyeti || 8.80;1
*     jaṭhare madhye ityarthaḥ grāsakṣapaṇaṃ grāsasya grāsārhalauhādeḥ kṣapaṇaṃ kṣayamāpādanaṃ rasena saha ekīkaraṇamityarthaḥ || 8.80;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     pūrvoktacāraṇādīnāṃ lakṣaṇamāha rasasyeti || 8.80;1

____________________


[garbhadruti]
grastasya drāvaṇaṃ garbhe garbhadrutirudāhṛtā || 8.81 ||

[bāhyadruti]
bahireva drutiṃ kṛtvā ghanasattvādikaṃ khalu |
jāraṇāya rasendrasya sā bāhyadrutirucyate || 8.82 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     garbhadrutiprasaṅgena bāhyadrutimāha bahiriti || 8.82;1
*     bahireva cullyuparisthakaṭāhādau na tu rasagarbhe ityarthaḥ || 8.82;2
*     ghanasattvādikamabhrasattvādikamatrādipadena svarṇasattvādīnāṃ grahaṇaṃ bodhyam || 8.82;3




*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     bahireva drutīkṛtyeti || 8.82;1
*     abhrakasattvadrutiḥ prāguktaiva dvitīyādhyāye || 8.82;2
*     jāraṇāya taptakhalvamadhye dīyata iti vākyaśeṣaḥ || 8.82;3

____________________


[druti (substance)]
nirlepatvaṃ drutatvaṃ ca tejastvaṃ laghutā tathā |
asaṃyogaśca sūtena pañcadhā drutilakṣaṇam || 8.83 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     druteḥ pañcadhābhedānāha nirlepatvamiti || 8.83;1
*     nirlepatvaṃ niḥ niścayena niḥśeṣeṇa vā lepatvaṃ liptatvaṃ drāvāntareṇa saha niḥśeṣeṇa ekībhavanamityarthaḥ yadvā niḥ nirgataḥ lepaḥ liptapadārthaḥ malādiryasmāttattvaṃ pṛthagbhūtamalādikamityarthaḥ || 8.83;2
*     drutatvaṃ viśeṣeṇa dravībhavanam || 8.83;3
*     tejastvaṃ svarūpāpekṣayā tejobhūyastvam || 8.83;4
*     laghutā svābhāvikagurutvamapekṣya lāghavam || 8.83;5
*     asaṃyogaśca sūtena pāradena saha pṛthaktayā avasthānam || 8.83;6

____________________


[druti (process)]
auṣadhādhmānayogena lohadhātvādikaṃ tathā |
saṃtiṣṭhate dravākāraṃ sā drutiḥ parikīrtitā || 8.84 ||

[jāraṇā (saṃskāra)]
drutagrāsaparīṇāmo viḍayantrādiyogataḥ |
jāraṇetyucyate tasyāḥ prakārāḥ santi koṭiśaḥ || 8.85 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     jāraṇābhedajāraṇāmāha druteti || 8.85;1
*     viḍaḥ vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ yantraṃ koṣṭhikādikaṃ tadādiyogataḥ atrādipadena mūṣāpuṭādīnāṃ grahaṇaṃ drutasya garbhe taralitasya grāsasya svarṇādeḥ parīṇāmaḥ paripākaḥ svātmani abhedarūpeṇa pariṇamanam || 8.85;2
*     rasendracintāmaṇau tu jāraṇā hi pātanagālanavyatirekeṇa ghanahemādigrāsapūrvakaṃ pūrvāvasthāpannatvamityanena yatprakārāntaraṃ jāraṇālakṣaṇamuktaṃ tatcāraṇākhyajāraṇābhiprāyeṇa bodhyam || 8.85;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha jāraṇālakṣaṇamāha drutagrāseti || 8.85;1
*     biḍayantrādiyogena pāradodare dravībhūtasya grāsasya bījāderyaḥ parīṇāmo'vināśidṛḍhatarasaṃbandhena pāradena sahaikībhāvaḥ sā jāraṇetyucyate || 8.85;2
*     tasyāḥ prakārāḥ koṭiśo'tibahavaḥ santi || 8.85;3
*     yantrādīnāṃ bahuvidhatvāt || 8.85;4
*     te ca prakārāḥ patrajāraṇāsattvajāraṇādrutijāraṇābījajāraṇāmahābījajāraṇāsiddhabījajāraṇāsadhūmajāraṇānirdhūmajāraṇābālajāraṇāvṛddhajāraṇābaddhajāraṇābhūcarījāraṇākhecarījāraṇāprabhṛtayo rasārṇavādau savistaraṃ bodhyāḥ || 8.85;5
*     dolāyantrasomānalayantracakrarājayantravālukāyantrajalakūrmayantrasthalakūrmayantramūṣāprabhṛtīni jāraṇayantrādīni vidyāt || 8.85;6
*     taptakhalve mardanaṃ tu sarvatra prākkāryameva || 8.85;7
*     ādiśabdena ṣaḍbindukīṭasahitamardanasṛṣṭitrayādisaṃgrahāḥ || 8.85;8

____________________


[viḍa]
kṣārairamlaiśca gandhādyairmūtraiśca paṭubhistathā |
rasagrāsasya jīrṇārthaṃ tadviḍaṃ parikīrtitam || 8.86 ||

[rañjana]
susiddhabījadhātvādijāraṇena rasasya hi |
pītādirāgajananaṃ rañjanaṃ parikīrtitam || 8.87 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     ato'bhrapattrajāraṇottaraṃ rāgasaṃskāraṃ lakṣayati susiddhabījeti || 8.87;1
*     susiddhaṃ yathāvidhi sādhitaṃ yadbījamabhrakasattvadhātvādicūrṇaṃ tajjāraṇena pāradasya yā bījadhātvādyanurūpā varṇotpattistadrañjanam || 8.87;2
*     tatra bījasādhanavidhistu siddhabījalakṣaṇavyākhyāyāṃ prāgukta eva || 8.87;3
*     jāraṇāvidhistu triṃśattame'dhyāye sphuṭībhaviṣyati || 8.87;4
*     ādiśabdo maṇirasoparasādiparigrahaḥ || 8.87;5
*     rāgadravyodāharaṇaṃ vidhiśca rasahṛdaye || 8.87;6
*     yathā |
*     jīrṇābhrako rasendro darśayati ghanānurūpiṇīṃ chāyām |
*     kṛṣṇāṃ raktāṃ pītāṃ sītāṃ tathā saṃkarairmiśrām || 8.87;7
*     tathā |
*     krāmati tīkṣṇena rasastīkṣṇena ca jīryate kṣaṇādgrāsaḥ |
*     hemno yonistīkṣṇaṃ rāgān gṛhṇāti tīkṣṇena || 8.87;8
*     tadapi ca daradena hataṃ hatvā vā mākṣikeṇa ravisahitam |
*     vāsitamapi vāsanayā ghanavaccāryaṃ ca jāryaṃ ca |
*     vāsanayā vāsanauṣadhena vāsitaṃ bhāvitamityarthaḥ || 8.87;9
*     tathā |
*     kāntaṃ vā tīkṣṇaṃ vā kāñcīṃ vā vajrasasyakādīnām |
*     ekatamaṃ sarvaṃ vā rasarañjane saṃkaro'pīṣṭaḥ || 8.87;10
*     kāñcīṃ svarṇamākṣikam || 8.87;11
*     ādiśabdena hīrakacapalādaya upadhātavaścaikatra militā apīṣṭāḥ || 8.87;12
*     kuṭile balamadhikaṃ rāgastīkṣṇe tu pannage snehaḥ || 8.87;13
*     rāgasnehabalāni tu kamale śaṃsanti dhātuvidaḥ || 8.87;14



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     rañjanamāha susiddheti || 8.87;1
*     śodhitasvarṇaraupyābhyāṃ tathā jīrṇatāmrādiyogena kriyāviśeṣamāśritya rasasya yatpītādirāgajananamityanvayaḥ || 8.87;2

____________________


[sāraṇā]
sūte satailayantrasthe svarṇādikṣepaṇaṃ hi yat |
vedhādhikyakaraṃ lohe sāraṇā sā prakīrtitā || 8.88 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     sāraṇālakṣaṇamāha sūta iti || 8.88;1
*     sāraṇākhyatailenārdhāṃśaṃ saṃbhṛtaṃ yatsāraṇāyantraṃ tatra sthite pārade yatsvarṇādikṣepaṇaṃ svarṇādibījanāgavaṅgānāṃ yatkṣepaṇaṃ vedhādhikyasiddhyarthaṃ kriyate sā sāraṇeti rasaśāstra uktā || 8.88;2
*     pratisāraṇānusāraṇeti ca tadbhedau || 8.88;3
*     uktaṃ caitadrasahṛdaye |
*     sāritavartitasūtaḥ samānabījena milati yaḥ sāryaḥ |
*     dviguṇena pratīsāryaḥ sa cānusāryaśca triguṇena || 8.88;4
*     iti || 8.88;5
*     dviguṇamapi suvarṇameva || 8.88;6
*     vedhādhikyaṃ vedho bhittvāntaḥpraveśaḥ so'dhiko bhavati || 8.88;7
*     pāradasya tāmradalādau śarīrasthadhātvādiṣu ca sarvato vyāptiḥ || 8.88;8
*     sāraṇātailaṃ pañcamādhyāyavyākhyāyāṃ prāguktameva || 8.88;9
*     yantrādhyāye sāraṇāyantraṃ sāraṇāvidhiśca sphuṭībhaviṣyati || 8.88;10



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     sāraṇāmāha sūte iti || 8.88;1
*     satailayantrasthe tailapūrṇāndhamūṣāmadhyasthite sūte rase yatsvarṇādikṣepaṇaṃ svarṇādiprakṣepaḥ lohe tāmrādau ityarthaḥ vedhādhikyakaraṃ vedhasya svarṇādijananarūpavedhaśakteḥ ādhikyakaramātiśayyajanakam || 8.88;2
*     uktaṃ ca rasendracintāmaṇau |
*     atha sāraṇocyate || 8.88;3
*     andhamūṣā tu kartavyā gostanākārasaṃnibhā || 8.88;4
*     saiva chidrānvitā madhye gambhīrā sāraṇocitā || 8.88;5
*     asyāmeva mūṣāyāṃ tattailamapagatakalkavimalamāpūryamasminnadhikamadhastāddrutabījaprakṣepasamakālameva samāvarjanīyaḥ sūtavarastadanu sadyo mūṣānanamācchādanīyam || 8.88;6
*     etattailāktakhaṇḍagranthibandhena aruṇāsitabījābhyāmaśvanāsārakarmaṇā militaścetsāritaḥ samyaksaṃyamitaḥ vijñātavyaḥ || 8.88;7
*     sāraṇārthaṃ tailamapi tatraivānveṣṭavyaṃ vistarabhayānnoddhṛtamiti || 8.88;8

____________________


[vedha]
vyavāyibheṣajopeto dravye kṣipto rasaḥ khalu |
vedha ityucyate tajjñaiḥ sa cānekavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ || 8.89 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     sāraṇālakṣaṇe vedhādhikyakaramityuktaṃ kastāvatvedha ityapekṣāyāmāha vyavāyīti nāgavallīkumārikādhustūrādivyavāyiguṇavadbheṣajasaṃyuktaḥ ityarthaḥ || 8.89;1
*     vyavāyilakṣaṇaṃ śārṅgadhare yathā |
*     pūrvaṃ vyāpyākhilaṃ kāyaṃ tataḥ pākaṃ ca gacchati |
*     vyavāyi tadyathā bhaṅgā phenaṃ cāhisamudbhavam |
*     iti || 8.89;2
*     dravye svarṇādirūpeṇa pariṇinamayiṣau lohe || 8.89;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha krāmaṇasya dravyasya sarvadā vedhasahopayogitvena samāsato viśeṣaṇamukhena krāmaṇalakṣaṇasahitaṃ vedhalakṣaṇamekena ślokenāha vyavāyīti || 8.89;1
*     dravye śatavedhādau yathābhāgaṃ gṛhīte mūṣāyāṃ drute kṛte prataptamātre vā tāmrarajatādau sādhyadravye kṣipto rasaḥ pārado yasmin karmaṇi sa vedha ityucyate || 8.89;2
*     sa ca pāradaḥ kīdṛśaḥ || 8.89;3
*     vyavāyibheṣajopetaḥ || 8.89;4
*     yatsevitamātraṃ tatkṣaṇa eva śarīrāntaḥsthasarvadhātuṣu sahasā sabāhyābhyantaraṃ vyāpnoti paścātpākaṃ prāpnoti tadvyavāyi krāmaṇetyaparaparyāyaṃ ca bodhyam || 8.89;5
*     tādṛśabheṣajopeto yogavāhī pārado'pi sevitaścetsahasā sarvaṃ dehaṃ sāntaraṃ vyāpnoti na kevalaṃ koṣṭha eva sthitiṃ karoti || 8.89;6
*     na vā dhātuṣu kathaṃcitprāpto'pi bahiḥ saṃlagna eva tiṣṭhatīti || 8.89;7
*     lohāntaḥ praviśedyena dravyayogena pāradaḥ |
*     sadyastatkrāmaṇamiti kathitaṃ rasasiddhidam |
*     iti krāmaṇalakṣaṇapārthakyena bodhyam || 8.89;8

____________________


[vedhabhedāḥ]
lepaḥ kṣepaśca kuntaśca dhūmākhyaḥ śabdasaṃjñakaḥ || 8.90 ||

[lepavedha]
lepanaṃ kurute lohaṃ svarṇaṃ vā rajataṃ tathā |
lepavedhaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ puṭamatra ca saurakam || 8.91 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     teṣu lepavedhalakṣaṇamāha lepanamiti || 8.91;1
*     lepanaṃ vedhasāmarthyāpādakakriyāviśeṣasiddharasenetyāśayaḥ || 8.91;2
*     atra lepavedhe || 8.91;3
*     saurakaṃ puṭaṃ dadyāditi śeṣaḥ sūryapakvaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ || 8.91;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha vedhabhedānāha lepa iti || 8.91;1
*     lepeneti || 8.91;2
*     yasminvedhe pārado lepena lohaṃ pattrīkṛtaṃ tīkṣṇatāmrādi svarṇaṃ karoti rajataṃ vā karoti sa lepavedha ityuktaḥ || 8.91;3
*     atra lepottaraṃ varṇotkarṣārthaṃ yatpuṭaṃ deyaṃ bhavati tatpuṭaṃ saukaraṃ varāhapuṭaṃ kāryam || 8.91;4
*     asmin saṃskāre jīrṇacaturthāṃśārdhāṃśasamādibhāgairjīrṇabīja eva sārito rasa upayoktavyaḥ || 8.91;5

____________________


[kṣepavedha]
prakṣepaṇaṃ drute lohe vedhaḥ syātkṣepasaṃjñitaḥ || 8.92 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     kṣepavedhalakṣaṇamāha prakṣepaṇamiti || 8.92;1
*     galitasvarṇetaralauhe rasaprakṣepeṇa yatsuvarṇīkaraṇaṃ sa kṣepākhyavedho jñeyaḥ || 8.92;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha kṣepavedhamāha prakṣepaṇamiti || 8.92;1
*     dhmānena mūṣāyāṃ lohe tāmrādau drute sati krāmaṇadravyakalkasahitasya pāradasya yatprakṣepaṇaṃ sa vedhaḥ kṣepa iti khyātaḥ || 8.92;2
*     atra pakṣāntaramapyuktaṃ rasasāre krāmaṇakalkasahitalohe dhāmyamāne kevalaṃ pāradaṃ kṣipettatreti || 8.92;3

____________________


[kuntavedha]
saṃdaṃśadhṛtasūtena drutadravyāhṛtiśca yā |
suvarṇatvādikaraṇaṃ kuntavedhaḥ sa ucyate || 8.93 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     kuntavedhamāha saṃdaṃśeti || 8.93;1
*     saṃdaṃśaḥ sāṃḍāśī iti khyātaḥ saṃdaṃśayantreṇa mūrtibaddhasūtaṃ dhṛtvā drutalauhe nimajjanena tatsamparkātsvarṇādirūpeṇa pariṇatasya lauhasya yadāharaṇaṃ sa kuntavedhasaṃjñako jñeyaḥ || 8.93;2

____________________


[dhūmavedha]
vahnau dhūmāyamāne'ntaḥ prakṣiptarasadhūmataḥ |
svarṇādyāpādanaṃ lohe dhūmavedhaḥ sa ucyate || 8.94 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     dhūmavedhamāha vahnāviti || 8.94;1
*     sadhūmavahnimadhye vedhasamartharasanikṣepeṇa yo dhūmaḥ nirgacchati tatsamparkādyatkiṃcillohasya svarṇādirūpeṇa yā pariṇatiḥ sa dhūmavedhaḥ || 8.94;2


*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     dhūmavedhalakṣaṇamāha vahnāviti || 8.94;1
*     dhūmāyamāne vahnāvantarmūṣāntaḥsthatadvahnimadhye prakṣipto yo rasaḥ pāradastatsaṃbandhidhūmasyordhvasthāpitatāmrādipatre saṃparkādyatsvarṇarajatāpādanaṃ sa dhūmavedha ityabhidhīyate || 8.94;2

____________________


[śabdavedha]
mukhasthitarasenālpalohasya dhamanātkhalu |
svarṇarūpyatvajananaṃ śabdavedhaḥ sa kīrtitaḥ || 8.95 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     śabdavedhamāha mukheti || 8.95;1
*     dhamanātśabdoccāraṇādityarthaḥ phutkārādityartho vā || 8.95;2
*     dhama dhvāne ityasmātlyuḥ || 8.95;3
*     mukhamadhye vedhasamartharasaguṭikāṃ saṃsthāpya puro lauhakhaṇḍamekaṃ dhṛtvā śabdoccāraṇe kṛte phutkāre datte vā tatlauhakhaṇḍaṃ svarṇādirūpeṇa pariṇatam || 8.95;4
*     dhamanātityatra damanātiti pāṭhe mukhamadhye vedhasamartharasaguṭikāṃ saṃsthāpya kṣudralauhakhaṇḍaṃ tannimne nidhāya ca pīḍane kṛte adhaḥsthalauhakhaṇḍaṃ yatra vedhe svarṇādirūpeṇa pariṇametsa śabdavedhaḥ ityarthaḥ || 8.95;5

____________________


[udghāṭana]
siddhadravyasya sūtena kāluṣyādinivāraṇam |
prakāśanaṃ ca varṇasya tadudghāṭanamīritam || 8.96 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     udghāṭanamāha siddhadravyasyeti || 8.96;1
*     siddhadravyasya mṛtalauhāderityarthaḥ sūtena kriyāviśeṣaniṣpannasūtasaṃyogena yatkāluṣyādinivāraṇaṃ mālinyādiproñchanaṃ varṇasya ca prakāśanamaujjvalyasaṃpādanaṃ yadvā sūtena sūtasaṃyogena siddhadravyasya māritalohādeḥ kāluṣyādinivāraṇaṃ yayā kriyayā dravyāntarasaṃyogajanitamālinyādināśanaṃ syādityevaṃ yojanīyam || 8.96;2
*     siddhadravyasya sūtena ityatra siddhadravyeṇa sūtasya iti pāṭhe mṛtalauhādisaṃyogadvārā sūtasya yatkāluṣyādinivāraṇamityarthaḥ || 8.96;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     sūtena sabāhyābhyantaravyāptyaikībhūtasya siddhadravyasya suvarṇatvādiprāptasya tāmrādilohātmakadravyasya tadekībhūtasūtasya ca yathāsambhavaṃ yatkāluṣyādi tannivārakaṃ daśāṃśena yaccūrṇaprakṣepādi karma tadudghāṭanamitīritam || 8.96;1
*     kāluṣyaṃ malasaṃkīrṇatvam || 8.96;2
*     ādiśabdena chattritvapataṅgitvadurdrāvitvadurmelitvādidoṣaḥ saṃgrāhyaḥ || 8.96;3

____________________


[svedana]
kṣārāmlairauṣadhaiḥ sārddhaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ ruddhvātiyatnataḥ |
bhūmau nikhanyate yatnātsvedanaṃ saṃprakīrtitam || 8.97 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     svedanamāha kṣārāmlairiti || 8.97;1
*     auṣadhaiḥ svedanīyauṣadhaiḥ || 8.97;2
*     bhāṇḍaṃ rasapūrṇabhāṇḍamityarthaḥ || 8.97;3
*     atra uṣṇavīryaiḥ kṣārāmlaiḥ saha bhūmyadhaḥsthāpanena taduṣmaṇā anagnisvedo bodhyaḥ || 8.97;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     sāraṇottaraṃ rasāyanaṃ kartuṃ kāmayamānena sādhakena pāradasya kartavyau svedasaṃnyāsākhyasaṃskārau lakṣayati kṣārāmlairiti || 8.97;1
*     auṣadhairbhṛṅgarāṅmuṇḍī viṣṇukrāntā ityādibhiḥ svedalakṣaṇavarṇanāvasare prāguktaiḥ || 8.97;2
*     taiśca sārdhaṃ saha pāradaṃ bhāṇḍamadhye pidhānasaṃdhirodhanādiyatnena ruddhvā tadbhāṇḍaṃ bhūmimadhye nikhanyate yasmin karmaṇi tatsvedanaṃ saṃprakīrtitam || 8.97;3

____________________


[saṃnyāsa]
rasasyauṣadhayuktasya bhāṇḍaruddhasya yatnataḥ |
mandāgniyutacullyantaḥ kṣepaḥ saṃnyāsa ucyate || 8.98 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     saṃnyāsamāha rasasyeti || 8.98;1
*     auṣadhayuktasya kṣārāmlauṣadhayuktasyetyarthaḥ || 8.98;2
*     mandāgniyutacullyantaḥ mṛdvagniviśiṣṭacullīmadhye na tu tīvrāgniyutacullyāmityarthaḥ || 8.98;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     athauṣadhayuktasya pāradasya mṛnmayabhāṇḍasaṃpuṭitasya mandavahniyuktacullīmadhye kṣiptvā yatpuṭanaṃ sa saṃnyāsa ityucyate || 8.98;1

____________________


[svedanasaṃnyāsaguṇāḥ]
dvāvetau svedasaṃnyāsau rasarājasya niścitam |
guṇaprabhāvajanakau śīghravyāptikarau tathā || 8.99 ||
rasanigamamahābdheḥ somadevaḥ samantātsphuṭataraparibhāṣānāmaratnāni hṛtvā |
vyaracayadatiyatnāttairimāṃ kaṇṭhamālāṃ kalayatu bhiṣagagryo maṇḍanārthaṃ sabhāyām || 8.100 ||
bhavetpaṭhitavāro'yamadhyāyo rasavādinām |
rasakarmāṇi kurvāṇo na sa muhyati kutracit || 8.101 ||


________________________________________________________


 9
atha yantrāṇi vakṣyante rasatantrāṇyaśeṣataḥ |
samālocya samāsena somadevena sāmpratam || 9.1 ||
svedādikarma nirmātuṃ vārttikendraiḥ prayatnataḥ |

[yantra:: nirukti]
yantryate pārado yasmāttasmādyantramiti smṛtam || 9.2 ||

[dolāyantra]
dravadravyeṇa bhāṇḍasya pūritārdhodakasya ca |
mukhasyobhayato dvāradvayaṃ kṛtvā prayatnataḥ || 9.3 ||
tayostu nikṣipeddaṇḍaṃ tanmadhye rasapoṭalīm |
baddhvā tu svedayedetaddolāyantramiti smṛtam || 9.4 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     dolāyantramāha dravadravyeṇeti || 9.4;1
*     dravadravyeṇa kāñjikādisvedanadravyeṇa || 9.4;2
*     mukhasyeti || 9.4;3
*     bhāṇḍakandharāyāḥ prāntadvaye chidradvayaṃ kṛtvetyarthaḥ || 9.4;4
*     tayoḥ chidrayoḥ || 9.4;5
*     tanmadhye daṇḍamadhye || 9.4;6
*     svedanārheṇa kāñjikādinā kenacitdraveṇa bhāṇḍārdhamāpūrya bhāṇḍakandharāprāntadvaye chidradvayaṃ kṛtvā tanmadhye daṇḍamekaṃ nidhāya tasmin rasapoṭṭalīṃ baddhvā ca evaṃ lambayetyathā bhāṇḍasthadrave sā nimajjetparaṃ tu bhāṇḍaṃ na spṛśediti niṣkarṣaḥ || 9.4;7

____________________


[svedanīyantra]
sāmbusthālīmukhe baddhavastre pākyaṃ niveśayet |
pidhāya pacyate yatra svedanīyantramucyate || 9.5 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha patrapuṣpādīnāmatimṛdudravyāṇāṃ svalpasvedārthaṃ yantrāntaramāha sāmbusthālīti || 9.5;1
*     sthālīṃ caturthāṃśajalena pūritodarāṃ jalenārdhapūritodarāṃ vā kṛtvā tasyā mukhamā samantādvastreṇa baddhaṃ yathā syāttathā mukhābaddhe vastre pākyaṃ svedyamatikomalaṃ dravyaṃ vinikṣipya nyubjaśarāvādinā pidhāyācchādya yatra pacyate tatsvedanīyantramityucyate || 9.5;2
*     asyaiva yantrasya kandukasaṃjñāṃ vakṣyati || 9.5;3

____________________


[pātanāyantra]
aṣṭāṅgulaparīṇāhamānāhena daśāṅgulam |
caturaṅgulakotsedhaṃ toyādhāraṃ galādadhaḥ || 9.6 ||
adhobhāṇḍe mukhaṃ tasya bhāṇḍasyopari vartinaḥ |
ṣoḍaśāṅgulavistīrṇapṛṣṭhasyāsye praveśayet || 9.7 ||
pārśvayormahiṣīkṣīracūrṇamaṇḍūraphāṇitaiḥ |
liptvā viśoṣayetsaṃdhiṃ jalādhāre jalaṃ kṣipet |
cullyāmāropayedetatpātanāyantramucyate || 9.8 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     athordhvapātanāyantramāha aṣṭāṅguleti || 9.8;1
*     atha nyubjasthāpyasya viśālapṛṣṭhasya mṛtpātrasyāṅgulādadho'ṅgulamitapṛṣṭhabhāgādadhobhāge toyādhāraḥ kāryaḥ || 9.8;2
*     katham || 9.8;3
*     aṣṭāṅgulaparīṇāhamaṣṭāṅgulagarbhavistāraṃ yathā syāttathā || 9.8;4
*     tathānāhena bandhena saha daśāṅgulaṃ yathā syāttathā bandha ālavālaṃ tatpradeśaṃ saṃgṛhya daśāṅgulavistāramityarthaḥ || 9.8;5
*     evaṃ ca bandhasthaulyamaṅguladvayamitaṃ garbhavistāraścāṣṭāṅgulamitaḥ kārya ityavatiṣṭhate || 9.8;6
*     na ha bandhane || 9.8;7
*     caturaṅgulocchrāyaṃ yathā syāttathā toyādhāraḥ pṛṣṭhe kārya ityarthaḥ || 9.8;8
*     tādṛśādhāraviśiṣṭasya ṣoḍaśāṅgulavistīrṇapṛṣṭhasyoparivartinastasya bhāṇḍasyāsye'dho bhāṇḍamukhaṃ praveśayet || 9.8;9
*     tato mukhapārśvayormahiṣīkṣīrādibhiḥ saṃdhiṃ liptvā viśoṣayet || 9.8;10
*     cūrṇaḥ sudhā śvetaṃ lepadravyamityarthaḥ || 9.8;11
*     pātanāyantramīritam || 9.8;12
*     ūrdhvapātanāyantramuktamityarthaḥ || 9.8;13
