*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     pātanāyantrasya svarūpamāha aṣṭāṅguleti || 9.8;1
*     parīṇāhaḥ vistāraḥ paridhiriti yāvat || 9.8;2
*     ānāhaḥ aunnatyaṃ dairghyamiti yāvat || 9.8;3
*     utsedhaḥ upacitiḥ sthaulyamiti yāvat || 9.8;4

____________________


[adhaḥpātanayantra]
athordhvabhājane liptasthāpitasya jale sudhīḥ |
dīptairvanopalaiḥ kuryādadhaḥ pātaṃ prayatnataḥ || 9.9 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     adhaḥpātanayantramāha asyordhveti || 9.9;1
*     jalādhāravihīnasya vaiparītyena jale sthāpitasyāsyoktayantrasya saṃbandhi yadūrdhvabhājanaṃ tatra liptasthāpitasya lepaṃ kṛtvā sthāpitasya pāradasya yantropari dīptairvanopalairadhaḥpātaṃ kuryāt || 9.9;2
*     ityetadadhaḥpātanāyantramabhihitam || 9.9;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     adhaḥpātanāyantre pātanakramamāha atheti |
*     atha uktarītyā yantranirmāṇānantaramūrdhvabhājane uparisthādhomukhabhāṇḍodare ityarthaḥ ādau liptaṃ paścātsthāpitaṃ tasya liptasthāpitasya rasasyeti śeṣaḥ jale adhobhāṇḍasthite iti śeṣaḥ dīptaiḥ vanotpalaiḥ vanyakarīṣāgnibhiḥ || 9.9;1

____________________


[kacchapayantra]
jalapūrṇapātramadhye dattvā ghaṭakharparaṃ suvistīrṇam |
tadupari viḍamadhyagataḥ sthāpyaḥ sūtaḥ kṛtaḥ koṣṭhyām || 9.10 ||
laghulohakaṭorikayā kṛtaṣaṇmṛtsaṃdhilepayācchādya |
pūrvoktaghaṭakharparamadhye'ṅgāraiḥ khadirakolabhavaiḥ || 9.11 ||
svedanato mardanataḥ kacchapayantrasthito raso jarati |
agnibalenaiva tato garbhe dravanti sarvasattvāni || 9.12 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     kacchapayantraṃ dvividhaṃ jalakacchapaṃ sthalakacchapaṃ ca || 9.12;1
*     tatredānīṃ jalakacchapamāha jalapūrṇeti || 9.12;2
*     ghaṭakharparaṃ khaṇḍitamukhaṃ sacchidraṃ ghaṭakhaṇḍamuttānaṃ dattvā tadupari koṣṭhīṃ mūṣāṃ chidre kiṃcitpraviṣṭabudhnāṃ nīrāviyoginīmuttānāṃ sudṛḍhaṃ niścalaṃ saṃsthāpya koṣṭhīmabhitaḥ kuḍyaṃ vidadhyāt || 9.12;3
*     koṣṭhyāṃ biḍaṃ tanmadhye pāradaṃ ca dattvā sa samyaksthāpyaḥ || 9.12;4
*     tato laghulohakaṭorikayā nyubjayā taṃ pāradaṃ koṣṭhyāmācchādya mṛtkarpaṭādinā saṃdhiṃ ruddhvā tadghaṭakharparamaṅgāraiḥ karīṣādimiśraiḥ pūrṇaṃ kuryātpūrṇaṃ tadghaṭakharparamaṅgāraiḥ karīṣatuṣamiśraiḥ || 9.12;5
*     garbhadravantīti sthāne garbhadrutiḥ sarvalohānāmiti pāṭhaḥ || 9.12;6
*     evaṃ rītyā kacchapayantrasthaḥ pāradaḥ svedanato vahnitāpena mardanato biḍādinā saha pākāvasare tatraiva jātena mardanena drutaṃ grāsaṃ tridinaṃ jarati || 9.12;7
*     evaṃ caitadyantraṃ garbhadrutipūrvakaṃ jāraṇopayogīti bhāvaḥ || 9.12;8
*     atra jalapūrṇapātraṃ bhūmāveva nikhātaṃ kṛtvā tanmukhe sacchidramuttānaṃ śarāvaṃ dattvā tatra chidre nīrāviyoginīṃ mūṣāṃ kācavilepitāṃ dhṛtvā tatra pāradasyādhastādupariṣṭācca gandhakaṃ dattvā pidhāyoparyupalāgninā gandhakaṃ jārayanti kecit || 9.12;9
*     kiṃcaitadghaṭakaśarāve chidrasaṃsthitāṃ pakvamūṣāṃ kṛtvā tasyāmaṣṭāṃśabiḍāvṛtaṃ pāradaṃ dhṛtvā lohapātryāṃ saṃruddhaṃ mudritaṃ ca kṛtvā taduparyaṣṭāṅgulamānāṃ vālukāṃ vinikṣipya haṭhāttadupari dhmānena dhmātaṃ tadgarbhasambhūtaṃ rasaṃ māyūrapittaliptaṃ kāñcanaṃ grāsayantīti || 9.12;10
*     sthalakūrmayantraṃ tu kiṃcidgartāyukte bhūtale tathaiva ghaṭakharparaṃ nyubjaṃ nidhāya saṃdhilepādi kṛtvā tadupari sarvataḥ pārśvabhāge ca puṭaṃ dadyāditi || 9.12;11
*     tatra gartāyāṃ vanasūraṇādikandodare biḍaṃ tanmadhye sagrāsaṃ pāradaṃ dattvā tatkandaśakalenaivācchādya mṛtkarpaṭādināveṣṭya saṃśoṣya dhārayet || 9.12;12
*     pṛṣṭhe cāgniṃ saṃtataṃ dinatrayaṃ dadyāt || 9.12;13
*     antarāntarā kandaparivartanaṃ kāryaṃ yathā kando na dahyeta || 9.12;14
*     evaṃ sthalakūrmayantreṇa jāraṇaṃ vidadhyāditi || 9.12;15
*     kande dagdhaprāye sati anyatrānyatra kande pūrvavattaṃ pāradaṃ dhṛtvā pācayet || 9.12;16

____________________


[dīpikāyantra]
kacchapayantrāntargatamṛnmayapīṭhasthadīpikāsaṃsthaḥ |
yasminnipatati sūtaḥ proktaṃ taddīpikāyantram || 9.13 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     dīpikāyantramāha kacchapeti || 9.13;1
*     vakṣyamāṇakacchapayantramadhyavartimṛnmayaghaṭakharpararūpapīṭhopari pradīpamekaṃ saṃsthāpya tatra sūtaṃ sthāpayetkacchapayantrādhaḥ vahniṃ ca dadyātevaṃ pradīpasthasūtaḥ kacchapayantre patati tatdīpikākhyaṃ yantraṃ jñeyam || 9.13;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha dīpikāyantramāha kacchapayantreti || 9.13;1
*     jalapūrṇapātramadhye ghaṭakalaśamuttānaṃ nidhāya tatra koṣṭhīmakṛtvā tatsthāne mṛnmayapīṭhaṃ kṛtvā tatra dīpikāṃ nidhāya dīpaṃ prajvālya nāgasvarṇabījasahitaṃ gandhakamanaḥśilāharītālasahitaṃ ca pāradaṃ mardanena piṣṭīkṛtaṃ poṭalikāyāṃ baddhvā kacchapākāranyubjamṛtpātrodare tailamagnāṃ tāṃ poṭalīṃ dīpajvālopari yathā syāttathāvalambitāṃ baddhvā tannyubjaṃ pātraṃ nyubjaṃ nidhāya dīpoṣmaṇā nāgaṃ bhakṣayitvā pārado jalapātre'dhaḥ patati yasmin yantre taddīpikāyantramuktam || 9.13;2
*     nāgajāraṇārthasya yantrasyopayogo bodhyaḥ || 9.13;3
*     tato jīrṇanāgaṃ pāradaṃ bhasmamūṣāyāṃ dhmānena viyuktaṃ nāgaṃ kṛtvā tasminrase sādhakāvarabījaṃ jārayantīti || 9.13;4
*     dīpikāsaṃstho dīpikopari samyaksthita ityarthaḥ || 9.13;5

____________________


[ḍekīyantra]
bhāṇḍakaṇṭhādadhaśchidre veṇunālaṃ vinikṣipet |
kāṃsyapātradvayaṃ kṛtvā sampuṭaṃ jalagarbhitam || 9.14 ||
nalikāsyaṃ tatra yojyaṃ dṛḍhaṃ taccāpi kārayet |
yuktadravyairvinikṣiptaḥ pūrvaṃ tatra ghaṭe rasaḥ || 9.15 ||
agninā tāpito nālāttoye tasminpatatyadhaḥ |
yāvaduṣṇaṃ bhavetsarvaṃ bhājanaṃ tāvadeva hi |
jāyate rasasaṃdhānaṃ ḍekīyantramitīritam || 9.16 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     ḍekīyantramāha bhāṇḍakaṇṭhāditi || 9.16;1
*     bhāṇḍasya kaṇṭhanimne chidramekaṃ vidhāya tatra vaṃśanirmitaṃ nalamekaṃ saṃyojya sthāpayet || 9.16;2
*     anyacca jalapūrṇakāṃsyapātradvayena saṃpuṭamekaṃ kṛtvā bhāṇḍakaṇṭhasthanalāgraṃ tatra praveśya dṛḍhaṃ sandhirodhaṃ kuryāt || 9.16;3
*     tato bhāṇḍamadhye nirdiṣṭadravyaiḥ saha rasaṃ kṣiptvā agnijvālā deyā tena nālacchidrānusārī rasaḥ kāṃsyapātramadhyasthajale patati || 9.16;4
*     evaṃ yāvatsarvaṃ pātramuṣṇaṃ bhavettāvatkuryāt || 9.16;5
*     rasasaṃdhānaṃ rasanirgamanamityarthaḥ || 9.16;6



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha ḍhekīyantramāha bhāṇḍakaṇṭhāditi || 9.16;1
*     adhaśchidra upakaṇṭhaṃ kṛte chidre jalagarbhitaṃ svāduśītajalagarbhitam || 9.16;2
*     yuktadravyaiḥ śuddhiyogyaiḥ kāñjikādibhiḥ pātanopayogidravaiśca saha saṃyukto raso ghaṭe sacchidropakaṇṭhe vahnisthite mṛnmayaghaṭe pūrvaṃ vinikṣiptaḥ kāryaḥ || 9.16;3
*     bhājanaṃ sampuṭaghaṭakamadhobhājanamuṣṇaṃ yāvadbhavedghaṭe tāvadagniḥ kārya ityarthaḥ || 9.16;4
*     rasasaṃdhānaṃ pāradasya samyagādhānaṃ sthāpanamityarthaḥ || 9.16;5
*     etadyantrasamaṃ degayantraṃ rasasāre'bhihitam |
*     bṛhadbhāṇḍaṃ samādāya kukṣau ca chidrasaṃyutam |
*     bhāṇḍe kācaghaṭīṃ kṣiptvā mukhaṃ chidre niyojayet || 9.16;6
*     tadāsye kācanālaṃ syāttacca nālamadhomukham |
*     ruddhvā tadā tayoḥ saṃdhimardhaṃ ca nālakaṃ dihet || 9.16;7
*     bhāṇḍaṃ vālukayāpūrya tasya dvāraṃ nirudhya ca |
*     vahneḥ prajvālanaṃ tāvadyāvattannālakaṃ dravet || 9.16;8
*     śītaṃ yantraṃ samuttārya ghaṭīṃ prakṣālayettataḥ |
*     drāvaṃ kṣiptvā punardadyātpunaḥ pāko vidhīyate || 9.16;9
*     evaṃ raṅgadrutiḥ proktā degayantre'tiśobhanā |
*     tālo nāgaḥ śilācauraḥ śulvo haṃsaśca gandhakaḥ || 9.16;10
*     ete raṅgaṃ vimuñcanti vidrumaṃ gairikaṃ tathā |
*     vālukādegayantreṇa picchakeśā dravanti ca || 9.16;11
*     iti || 9.16;12

____________________


[jāraṇāyantra]
lohamūṣādvayaṃ kṛtvā dvādaśāṅgulamānataḥ |
īṣacchidrānvitāmekāṃ tatra gandhakasaṃyutām || 9.17 ||
mūṣāyāṃ rasayuktāyāmanyasyāṃ tāṃ praveśayet |
toyaṃ syātsūtakasyādha ūrdhvādho vahnidīpanam || 9.18 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     jāraṇāyantramāha lohetyādi dvābhyām || 9.18;1
*     vitastimānaṃ lauhamayamūṣādvayaṃ nirmāya tayorīṣacchidrānvitāyāmekasyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ gandhakaṃ saṃsthāpayettataḥ rasagarbhāyāmanyasyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ pūrvoktāṃ sagandhakamūṣāṃ praveśya sūtagarbhamūṣādho jalaṃ sthāpayitvā ūrdhvādhaśca vahniṃ prajvālayediti || 9.18;2

____________________


[jāraṇāyantra (2)]
rasonakarasaṃ bhadre yatnato vastragālitam |
dāpayetpracuraṃ yatnādāplāvya rasagandhakau || 9.19 ||
sthālikāyāṃ pidhāyordhvaṃ sthālīmanyāṃ dṛḍhāṃ kuru |
saṃdhiṃ vilepayedyatnānmṛdā vastreṇa caiva hi || 9.20 ||
sthālyantare kapotākhyaṃ puṭaṃ karṣāgninā sadā |
yantrasyādhaḥ karīṣāgniṃ dadyāttīvrāgnimeva vā || 9.21 ||
evaṃ tu tridinaṃ kuryāttato yantraṃ vimocayet |
taptodake taptacullyāṃ na kuryācchītalāṃ kriyām || 9.22 ||
na tatra kṣīyate sūto na ca gacchati kutracit |
anena ca krameṇaiva kuryādgandhakajāraṇam || 9.23 ||

[vidyādharayantra]
yantraṃ vidyādharaṃ jñeyaṃ sthālīdvitayasampuṭāt |
cullīṃ caturmukhīṃ kṛtvā yantrabhāṇḍaṃ niveśayet || 9.24 ||
tatrauṣadhaṃ vinikṣipya nirundhyādbhāṇḍakānanam |
yantraṃ vidyādharaṃ nāma tantrajñaiḥ parikīrtitam || 9.25 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     vidyādharayantramāha yantramiti || 9.25;1
*     sthālīdvitayasampuṭāthaṇḍikādvayakṛtapuṭaviśeṣāt || 9.25;2
*     tadeva vivṛṇoti cullīmiti || 9.25;3
*     adhobhāṇḍe auṣadhaṃ nikṣipya bhāṇḍāntareṇa mukhaṃ pidhāya cullyāṃ caturmukhyāṃ saṃsthāpya ca jvālā deyā || 9.25;4
*     koṣṭhīyantramiti saṃjñāntaramasya || 9.25;5
*     granthāntare tu vidyādharayantralakṣaṇamanyavidhaṃ tattu agre svayameva vakṣyati || 9.25;6



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     vidyādharayantramāha || 9.25;1
*     tatra prathamaṃ tatsvarūpaṃ samāsata uktvānantaraṃ savistaramāha yantramiti || 9.25;2
*     sthālīdvitayasaṃpuṭāditi || 9.25;3
*     samasthūlānāmeva saṃpuṭaṃ bhavati na tu mahadatyalpayoriti || 9.25;4
*     iti vakṣyamāṇaḥ sthālīyantrādasyākṛtiviśeṣaḥ sphuṭīkṛtaḥ || 9.25;5
*     atha vistareṇāha cullīṃ caturmukhīmiti || 9.25;6
*     sarvatra sāmyenāgnipravṛttyarthaṃ caturmukhīmityuktam || 9.25;7
*     tatra yantrabhāṇḍaṃ niveśayet || 9.25;8
*     nirundhyāt || 9.25;9
*     nyubjamukhena pātrāntareṇa saṃpuṭet || 9.25;10
*     etadyantraṃ vidyādharanāmnā śāstre parikīrtitam || 9.25;11
*     asyopayogastu saumyavīryadravyāṇāṃ mṛdvagninā pākārthaṃ bodhyaḥ || 9.25;12
*     ata eva vakṣyati pañcadaśādhyāye kanakasundararase dattvā vidyādhare yantre puṭedāraṇyakopalairiti || 9.25;13

____________________


[somānalayantra]
ūrdhvaṃ vahniradhaścāpo madhye tu rasasaṃgrahaḥ |
somānalamidaṃ proktaṃ jārayedgaganādikam || 9.26 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     somānalayantramāha ūrdhvamiti || 9.26;1
*     gaganādikamabhrādikam || 9.26;2
*     sthālīmadhye rasamūṣāṃ saṃsthāpya śarāveṇa mukhaṃ pidhāya mṛdvastreṇa sandhiṃ liptvā ca jalapūrṇasthālyantaropari sthālīṃ tāṃ sthāpayetśarāvopari karīṣāgniṃ ca dadyāditi || 9.26;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha somānalayantraṃ samāsata āha ūrdhvaṃ vahniriti || 9.26;1
*     madhya iti || 9.26;2
*     adhaḥpātre jalaṃ tadupari uttānaṃ śarāvādi dattvā tatra laghumūṣāmuttānāṃ dhṛtvā tanmadhye biḍaṃ biḍamadhye sagrāsaṃ pāradaṃ ca dattvā dṛḍhaṃ pidhānena pidhāya tadupari kharparaṃ dattvā tatrāgnirdeyaḥ || 9.26;3
*     iti vidhinā gaganasattvādikaṃ jārayediti || 9.26;4
*     etadeva yantraṃ sanābhinālaṃ kṛtvāgnimadho dattvā nābhimadhye pāradaṃ sagrāsaṃ dattvā jārayediti prakārāntareṇa rasasāre'bhihitam || 9.26;5
*     tathā ca tatpāṭhaḥ |
*     somānalasya yantrasya nābhimadhye rasaṃ kṣipet |
*     dattvāṣṭāṃśaṃ biḍaṃ nābhiṃ kiṃcidamlena pūrayet || 9.26;6
*     kṣārāmlaṃ tryūṣaṇaṃ mūtraṃ rājikā śigru citrakam |
*     nālaṃ prapūrayedebhirnābheścopari sādhakaḥ || 9.26;7
*     dadyādupari yantrasya sacchidraṃ samabudhnakam |
*     chidre ca dorakaṃ baddhvā amlaiḥ kharparapūraṇam || 9.26;8
*     rasopari patedbinduḥ stokaṃ stokaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
*     yantrādhaḥ sthāpayedagnimahorātratrayaṃ budhaḥ || 9.26;9
*     tridinairjīryate grāsaṃ sūtako nātra saṃśayaḥ |
*     iti || 9.26;10
*     sacchidraṃ samabudhnakamiti kharparasya viśeṣaṇam || 9.26;11
*     nālaṃ tu kṣārādipūritaṃ kṛtvā kharparacchidrasaṃmukhamevocchritaṃ kāryamiti bhāvaḥ || 9.26;12
*     etadeva yantraṃ nābhirahitaṃ kṛtvā vaiparītyenāgnijalasthāpanena prāptāgnīṣomākhyaṃ pāradabandhakaraṃ bhavatītyapi tadgranthe evābhihitam |
*     athādho'gniruparyāpo madhye deyo raseśvaraḥ |
*     agnīṣomākhyayantreṇa badhyate saptarātrataḥ || 9.26;13
*     iti || 9.26;14

____________________


[garbhayantra]
garbhayantraṃ pravakṣyāmi piṣṭikābhasmakārakam |
caturaṅguladīrghāṃ ca tryaṅgulonmitavistarām || 9.27 ||
mṛnmayīṃ sudṛḍhāṃ mūṣāṃ vartulaṃ kārayenmukham |
loṇasya viṃśatirbhāgā bhāga ekastu gugguloḥ || 9.28 ||
suślakṣṇaṃ peṣayitvā tu vāraṃ vāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
mūṣālepaṃ dṛḍhaṃ kṛtvā lavaṇārdhamṛdambubhiḥ || 9.29 ||
karṣettuṣāgninā bhūmau svedayenmṛdu mānavit |
ahorātraṃ trirātraṃ vā rasendro bhasmatāṃ vrajet || 9.30 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     garbhayantramāha garbhayantramiti || 9.30;1
*     piṣṭikā naṣṭapiṣṭīkṛtaḥ rasaḥ ityarthaḥ || 9.30;2
*     loṇasya lavaṇasya loṇasya ityatra lohasya iti pāṭhāntaraṃ na yuktaṃ lavaṇārdhamṛdambubhiḥ iti vakṣyamāṇavākyavirodhāt || 9.30;3
*     atra vāraṃ vāramiti padaṃ peṣayitvā tathā punaḥ punariti padaṃ mūṣālepamityanena yojanīyam || 9.30;4
*     lavaṇārdhamṛdambubhiriti sahārthe tṛtīyā tena lavaṇārdhamṛdambubhiḥ saha loṇaguggulū peṣayitvā iti tathā viṃśatibhāgalavaṇāpekṣayā mṛdo'rdhatvamiti ca bodhyam || 9.30;5
*     karṣetātape śoṣayet || 9.30;6



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha garbhayantraṃ pratijānīte garbhayantramiti || 9.30;1
*     yatra sarvaṃ yantraṃ bhūmyudara evāvasthitaṃ vidadhyāttadgarbhayantraṃ nāma || 9.30;2
*     tadeva spaṣṭīkaroti caturaṅguleti || 9.30;3
*     caturaṅguladīrghādilakṣaṇāṃ mūṣāṃ vidhāya tanmukhaṃ vartulaṃ kārayet || 9.30;4
*     samyakpidhānasaṃdhānārthaṃ yathoktabhāgaṃ lohakiṭṭaguggulayorgṛhītvā bhāgatrayaṃ mṛdo gṛhītvaikabhāgātmakaṃ lavaṇaṃ gṛhītvā sarvametajjalena saṃmardya tena mūṣāṃ sāntarbahirvilipya tatra dhātvādikṛtapiṣṭikāṃ saṃbhṛtya samyakpidhāya bhūmimadhyagāṃ gajapuṭaparyāptāṃ gartāṃ karīṣamiśratuṣairardhapūritāṃ kṛtvā tatra mūṣāṃ dhṛtvā tadupari garākaṇṭhadaghnaṃ karīṣatuṣaireva sampūryāvaśiṣṭagartāṃ mṛttikayā sampūryāntarvāyupraveśārthaṃ kiṃcidaṅgulīsamaṃ chidraṃ vidhāyāgniṃ dattvā svedayet || 9.30;5
*     evaṃ nirvāṇāgnituṣādiyuktyā niṣkāsya punastuṣādipūraṇādisarvamantarāntarā kuryādahorātraparyantaṃ trirātraparyantaṃ veti || 9.30;6
*     atra govarapuṭaṃ bhūmāveva deyam || 9.30;7
*     taduktaṃ rasarājasundare || 9.30;8
*     cūrṇīkṛtakarīṣāgnau bhūmāveva tu yatpuṭam |
*     dīyate tattu kṛtibhirgovaraṃ samudāhṛtam || 9.30;9
*     iti || 9.30;10

____________________


[haṃsapāka]
kharparaṃ sikatāpūrṇaṃ kṛtvā tasyopari nyaset |
aparaṃ kharparaṃ tatra śanairmṛdvagninā pacet || 9.31 ||
pañcakṣāraistathā mūtrairlavaṇaṃ ca viḍaṃ tataḥ |
haṃsapākaṃ samākhyātaṃ yantraṃ tadvārttikottamaiḥ || 9.32 ||

[vālukāyantra]
sarasāṃ gūḍhavaktrāṃ mṛdvastrāṅgulaghanāvṛtām |
śoṣitāṃ kācakalaśīṃ triṣu bhāgeṣu pūrayet || 9.33 ||
bhāṇḍe vitastigambhīre vālukāsupratiṣṭhitā |
tadbhāṇḍaṃ pūrayettribhiranyābhiravaguṇṭhayet || 9.34 ||
bhāṇḍavaktraṃ maṇikayā saṃdhiṃ limpenmṛdā pacet |
cullyāṃ tṛṇasya cādāhānmaṇikāpṛṣṭhavartinaḥ |
etaddhi vālukāyantraṃ tadyantraṃ lavaṇāśrayam || 9.35 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     vālukāyantramāha sarasāmiti || 9.35;1
*     sarasāṃ pāradagarbhām || 9.35;2
*     gūḍhavaktrāṃ saṃkīrṇamukhīm || 9.35;3
*     mṛditi || 9.35;4
*     mṛdvastreṇa mṛlliptavastreṇa aṅgulaghanamaṅgulamānotsedhaṃ yathā tathā āvṛtām || 9.35;5
*     triṣu bhāgeṣu pūrayetpācyarasādinā kācakalasyāḥ bhāgatrayaṃ pūrayedityarthaḥ || 9.35;6
*     triṣu bhāgeṣviti avacchede saptamī || 9.35;7
*     bhāṇḍe ityatra kācakalasīṃ sthāpayediti śeṣaḥ || 9.35;8
*     vālukābhiḥ kiyān bhāgaḥ pūrayitavya ityapekṣāyāmāha tadbhāṇḍamiti || 9.35;9
*     tribhirityatra || 9.35;10
*     bhāgairiti śeṣaḥ || 9.35;11
*     tṛtīyā cātra abhede bhāṇḍasya bhāgatrayamityarthaḥ pūrayetvālukayeti śeṣaḥ anyābhiḥ vālukābhirityarthaḥ avaśiṣṭāṃśaṃ pūrayediti śeṣaḥ || 9.35;12
*     avaguṇṭhayedityasya maṇikayā ityanena sambandhaḥ maṇikayā śarāveṇa || 9.35;13
*     ayamarthaḥ saṃkīrṇamukhīṃ kācakūpikāṃ mṛdvastreṇāṅgulotsedhamālipya śoṣayitvā ca tasyā bhāgatrayaṃ rasenāpūrayettato vitastipramāṇagabhīre vālukayā tribhāgapūrṇe bhāṇḍamadhye tāṃ niveśya ūrdhvaṃ vālukayā ācchādayettataśca śarāveṇa bhāṇḍavaktraṃ pidhāya mṛttikayā saṃdhiṃ liptvā ca tāvatpacetyāvatśarāvopari nyastaṃ tṛṇaṃ na dahediti || 9.35;14
*     tulyalakṣaṇatvātvālukāyantraprasaṅge lavaṇayantramapyatidiśati taditi || 9.35;15
*     taduktaprakāraṃ yantraṃ lavaṇāśrayamapi bhavatīti śeṣaḥ lavaṇayantramapi vālukāyantraṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ || 9.35;16



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     vālukāyantraṃ dvividhamāha surasāmiti || 9.35;1
*     surasāṃ granthikoṭararekhādyabhāvena sulakṣaṇām || 9.35;2
*     gūḍhavaktrāmakarālamukhāmadrimṛtkarpaṭādinā trivāraṃ saptavāraṃ vā kṛtenāṅgulasthūlena veṣṭitāmātape saṃśoṣitāṃ kācaghaṭīmudarasya bhāgacatuṣṭayaṃ parikalpya triṣu bhāgeṣu pūrayet || 9.35;3
*     tasyā garbhe bhāgatrayaṃ kajjalyādinā bheṣajena pūrayet || 9.35;4
*     evaṃ sampūritāṃ tāṃ vitastigambhīre bhāṇḍe mṛnmaye viśālakharpare pūritavālukāmadhye sthāpitāṃ kuryāt || 9.35;5
*     pūraṇīyavālukāyā mānamapyāha tadbhāṇḍaṃ pūrayediti || 9.35;6
*     bhāṇḍodarasyāpi bhāgacatuṣṭayaṃ parikalpya tadbhāṇḍaṃ vālukayā tribhirbhāgairmitayā tribhāgapūraṇaparyāptayā pūrayetsaṃbharet || 9.35;7
*     anyābhiravaśiṣṭaikabhāgamitābhirvālukābhiḥ kācakalaśīmabhitaḥ prakṣepeṇāvaguṇṭhayet || 9.35;8
*     kaṇṭhaparyantamācchāditāṃ kuryādityarthaḥ || 9.35;9
*     bhāṇḍamukhaṃ ca maṇikayā viśālanyubjamṛtpātreṇācchādya saṃdhilepādi kṛtvā cullyāṃ pacet || 9.35;10
*     tatpacanakālamānamāha tṛṇasyeti || 9.35;11
*     maṇikāpṛṣṭhe prakṣiptatṛṇadāhaparyantaṃ pacet || 9.35;12
*     tṛṇadāhetyupalakṣaṇam || 9.35;13
*     sindūrarasādau raktavarṇotpattiparyantamapi pākasyāvaśyakatvāt || 9.35;14
*     sa ca varṇo jñeyaḥ || 9.35;15
*     kūpīmukhādudgataraktavarṇabāṣpadarśaneneti bodhyam || 9.35;16

____________________


[vālukāyantra (2)]
pañcāḍhavālukāpūrṇabhāṇḍe nikṣipya yatnataḥ |
pacyate rasagolādyaṃ vālukāyantramīritam || 9.36 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     anyavidhaṃ vālukāyantramāha pañceti || 9.36;1
*     catvāriṃśaccharāvamitavālukāpūrṇabhāṇḍe ityarthaḥ || 9.36;2
*     atra vālukāmadhye eva rasagolādikaṃ sthāpayetna tu kācakūpyāmiti viśeṣaḥ || 9.36;3

____________________


[lavaṇayantra (1)]
evaṃ lavaṇanikṣepātproktaṃ lavaṇayantrakam || 9.37 ||

[lavaṇayantra (2)]
antaḥkṛtarasālepatāmrapātramukhasya ca |
liptvā mṛllavaṇenaiva saṃdhiṃ bhāṇḍatalasya ca || 9.38 ||
tadbhāṇḍaṃ paṭunāpūrya kṣārairvā pūrvavatpacet |
evaṃ lavaṇayantraṃ syādrasakarmaṇi śasyate || 9.39 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     antaḥ kṛtarasālepa iti || 9.39;1
*     ālepa ityupalakṣaṇam || 9.39;2
*     mṛdbhāṇḍe kharparasamāne viśālamukhe'ntastale nyubjaṃ sthāpanīyaṃ yallaghu tāmrapātraṃ tatpāradādigolakopari nyubjaṃ nidhāya mṛllavaṇādinā bhāṇḍatalatāmrapātramukhasaṃdhiṃ vilipya tayantrabhāṇḍaṃ lavaṇakṣārādyanyatamenāpūrya pidhānena pidhāya praharaparyantaṃ śālisphuṭanaparyantaṃ praharaparyantaṃ vā pacet || 9.39;3
*     evaṃvidhamapi yantraṃ lavaṇayantrākhyametadrasakarmaṇi pākamūrchanādikarmaṇi praśastaṃ syāt || 9.39;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     lavaṇayantrasya prakārāntaramāha antariti || 9.39;1
*     antastāmrapātramadhye ityarthaḥ kṛtaḥ rasena ālepaḥ yatra tathābhūtaṃ yattāmrapātraṃ tasya mukhaṃ tasya rasaliptodaratāmrapātramukhasya tathā bhāṇḍatalasya tāmrapātramukhopari sthāpitabhāṇḍādhobhāgasya || 9.39;2
*     tadbhāṇḍamuparisthabhāṇḍam || 9.39;3
*     pūrvavattṛṇasya cādāhādityarthaḥ || 9.39;4
*     tāmrapātrodare rasaṃ nikṣipya tadupari lavaṇapūrṇaṃ kṣārapūrṇaṃ vā bhāṇḍamekaṃ vinyasettato mṛllavaṇena sandhiṃ ruddhvā pacetiti || 9.39;5

____________________


[nālikāyantra]
lohanālagataṃ sūtaṃ bhāṇḍe lavaṇapūrite |
niruddhaṃ vipacetprāgvannālikāyantramīritam || 9.40 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     nālikāyantramāha loheti || 9.40;1
*     lauhamayanālamadhye pāradamāpūrya chidrarodhaṃ kṛtvā ca lavaṇapūritabhāṇḍāntaḥ nālaṃ taṃ nirundhyāttato maṇikayā bhāṇḍavaktramācchādya ālipya ca sandhiṃ tāvatpacetyāvatśarāvoparisthaṃ tṛṇaṃ na dahetiti || 9.40;2

____________________


[bhūdharayantra]
vālukāgūḍhasarvāṅgāṃ garte mūṣāṃ rasānvitām |
dīptopalaiḥ saṃvṛṇuyādyantraṃ tadbhūdharāhvayam || 9.41 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha bhūdharayantramāha vāluketi || 9.41;1
*     garte rājahastamātragambhīre garte hastamātrāyāmavistāre caturasre vartule vā tādṛśe garte rasānvitāṃ pāradagarbhitāṃ mallamūṣāṃ vālukāgūḍhasarvāṅgāṃ nidhāya gartakaṇṭhaparyantaṃ vālukayā prapūryopari kiṃcitpārśve ca dīptavanyopalaiḥ saṃvṛṇuyādācchādayet || 9.41;2
*     samantato vanyopalairācchādya jvālayedityarthaḥ || 9.41;3
*     atra pāradasya yantraṇamātraṃ vālukāyāṃ bhūmāveva vahnistūparyeva garbhayantre sarvameva bhūgarbhe || 9.41;4
*     bhūdharapuṭe tu na vālukāsaṃbandhaḥ || 9.41;5
*     iti trayāṇāṃ yantrapuṭānāṃ viśeṣo bodhyaḥ || 9.41;6

____________________


[puṭayantra]
śarāvasampuṭāntasthaṃ karīṣeṣvagnimānavit |
paceccullyāṃ dviyāmaṃ vā rasaṃ tatpuṭayantrakam || 9.42 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     puṭayantramāha śarāveti || 9.42;1
*     karīṣeṣu pacetathavā agnimānaviddviyāmaṃ cullyāmaṅgāreṣu pacedyojyam || 9.42;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha puṭayantramāha śarāveti || 9.42;1
*     bhūmitale karīṣarāśiṃ kṛtvā tatra śarāvasaṃpuṭitaṃ pāradaṃ ca dhṛtvāgnimānavidvaidyo ghāṭikādvayaparyantaṃ pacet || 9.42;2
*     atyantasaṃnihitāgnisaṃyogāt || 9.42;3
*     athavā cullyāṃ karīṣāgniṃ dattvādhiśritakharpare śarāvasaṃpuṭitaṃ rasaṃ dhṛtvā kharparamukhamācchādya yāmaparyantaṃ dviyāmaṃ vā pacet || 9.42;4
*     dūratarāgnisaṃyogāt || 9.42;5
*     evaṃ nyūnādhikaḥ pacanakālo rasayogidravyādyudgamaśālitvāvayavaśaithilyakāṭhinyādyanurodhena svabuddhyaiva tarkya iti bhāvaḥ || 9.42;6

____________________


[koṣṭhīyantra]
ṣoḍaśāṅgulavistīrṇaṃ hastamātrāyataṃ samam |
dhātusattvanipātārthaṃ koṣṭhīyantramiti smṛtam || 9.43 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     koṣṭhīyantramāha ṣoḍaśeti || 9.43;1
*     atra samamiti padena militabhāṇḍadvayasya ṣoḍaśāṅgulatvādi bodhyamevaṃ ca vitastipramāṇadīrghasya aṣṭāṅgulavistīrṇasya ca adhobhāṇḍasya mukhopari tāvanmānaṃ bhāṇḍāntaramadhomukhaṃ saṃsthāpya adho dṛḍhāṅgārairbhastrayā dhamettena dhātusattvaṃ nirgacchatīti || 9.43;2
*     atrisaṃhitāyāmetacchlokānantaramasyaivāparāṃśaḥ paṭhitaḥ tadyathā || 9.43;3
*     paripūrṇaṃ dṛḍhāṅgārairadho vātena koṣṭhake |
*     mātrayā jvālamārgeṇa jvālayecca hutāśanam || 9.43;4
*     iti || 9.43;5



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha koṣṭhīyantramāha ṣoḍaśāṅguleti || 9.43;1
*     dhātusattveti || 9.43;2
*     viśiṣṭadhātūnāṃ tāpyagairikavimalādīnāmanudgamaśālināṃ mṛdūnāṃ ca sattvasya nipātārthametadyantraṃ vidyāt || 9.43;3
*     asyāṃ koṣṭhyāṃ madhya ucitāṃ dhātugarbhitāṃ vṛntākādimūṣāṃ saṃsthāpya kokilairmūṣāmuparyuparibhāvena pārśvataśca sampūrya yathocitaṃ dhamet || 9.43;4
*     laghubhastrikādinetyarthaḥ || 9.43;5
*     koṣṭhīyantre sattvanipātanārhā dhātava udāhṛtāśca rasasāre |
*     vaikrāntaṃ sasyakaṃ tāpyaṃ kāntābhre vimalaṃ giriḥ |
*     gairikā tutthakaṃ caiva rājāvarto viśeṣataḥ || 9.43;6
*     eṣāmuparasānāṃ ca koṣṭhe sattvanipātanam |
*     iti || 9.43;7

____________________


[valabhīyantra]
yatra lohamaye pātre pārśvayorvalayadvayam |
tādṛksvalpataraṃ pātraṃ valayaprotakoṣṭhakam || 9.44 ||
pūrvapātropari nyasya svalpapātre parikṣipet |
rasaṃ saṃmūrchitaṃ sthūlapātramāpūrya kāñjikaiḥ || 9.45 ||
dviyāmaṃ svedayedeva rasotthāpanahetave |
etatsyādvalabhīyantraṃ rase ṣāḍguṇyakārakam |
sūkṣmakāntamaye pātre rasaḥ syādguṇavattaraḥ || 9.46 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     valabhīyantramāha yatreti || 9.46;1
*     valayadvayaṃ valayaṃ kaḍā āṃṭā iti khyātam || 9.46;2
*     tādṛklohamayam || 9.46;3
*     valayeti || 9.46;4
*     valaye bṛhatpātrasthavalaye protamābaddhaṃ koṣṭhaṃ madhyadeśo yasya tādṛśam || 9.46;5
*     ayamartho bṛhadākāraṃ kāntalauhamayaṃ pātramekaṃ nirmāya tasyāntargalādadhaḥpārśvadvaye valayadvayaṃ saṃyojanīyaṃ kṣudrākāramaparamapi tathāvidhaṃ pātramekaṃ kṛtvā bṛhatpātrasthe valaye aspṛṣṭatalabhāgaṃ yathā tathā ābadhya tatra mūrchitarasaṃ parikṣipetkāñjikena sthūlapātraṃ ca pūrayediti || 9.46;6
*     rase ṣāḍguṇyakārakaṃ ṣāḍguṇyaṃ vidyā vitarko vijñānaṃ smṛtistatparatā kriyā ityuktaṣaḍguṇapradaśaktijanakamevaṃvidharasopayoktā uktaṣāḍguṇyasiddhiṃ labhate ityarthaḥ || 9.46;7



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha khalacarīyantramāha yatreti || 9.46;1
*     yantrabhūte lohapātra ābhyantaropakaṇṭhe pārśvayoravasañjanārthaṃ valayadvayaṃ kāryam || 9.46;2
*     valayamadhye tu praveśārhakoṣṭhakaṃ lauhaṃ svalpapātramanyadvidhāya mūrchitarasagarbhitaṃ tatsvalpapātraṃ praveśya valayayoravasajjitaṃ kṛtvā sthūlapātre kāñjikaṃ prabhūtaṃ dattvā cullyāṃ mandāgninā praharaparyantaṃ svedayet || 9.46;3
*     tena pārada utthāya kāñjikadrave praviśya tiṣṭhati ata evāsya yantrasya jalāhāryayantramityapi nāmāntaraṃ kāñjikajalena svalpapātrādrasasya hriyamāṇatvāditi || 9.46;4
*     mūrchito rasa utthāya khale'ṅgaṇe carituṃ śaknotyaneneti vyutpattyā khalacarītyapi nāma || 9.46;5
*     atra yatsvalpaṃ pātraṃ tatkāntamayaṃ kṛtvā yantre tatpātramadhye pārado dhṛtaścedatiśayena guṇavānbhavati || 9.46;6
*     uktaṃ ca rasārṇave |
*     na sūtena vinā kāntaṃ na kāntena vinā rasaḥ |
*     kāntasūtasamāyogātprayogo dehadhārakaḥ || 9.46;7
*     iti || 9.46;8

____________________


[tiryakpātanayantra]
kṣipedrasaṃ ghaṭe dīrghanatādhonālasaṃyute |
tannālaṃ nikṣipedanyaghaṭakukṣyantare khalu || 9.47 ||
tatra ruddhvā mṛdā samyagvadane ghaṭayoratha |
adhastādrasakumbhasya jvālayettīvrapāvakam || 9.48 ||
itarasminghaṭe toyaṃ prakṣipetsvādu śītalam |
tiryakpātanametaddhi vārttikairabhidhīyate || 9.49 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     tiryakpātanayantramāha kṣipediti || 9.49;1
*     dīrghanatādhonālasaṃyute dīrghākāraṃ nataṃ nimnābhimukhaṃ ca yatadhonālaṃ tena saṃyute || 9.49;2
*     ayaṃ vidhiḥ ekasmin ghaṭe rasaṃ nidhāya tasya udarādho dīrghamadholambitaṃ nālamekaṃ saṃyojya dvitīyaghaṭodarasthacchidre nālāgraṃ praveśya mṛdvastreṇa sandhimālipya ca ghaṭayormukhamapi tathā limpetrasakumbhādhaśca tīvrāgniṃ prajvālayetdvitīyaghaṭe svāduśītalaṃ jalaṃ ca prakṣipetiti || 9.49;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha tiryakpātanayantramāha kṣipedrasamiti || 9.49;1
*     natādhonāleti natopakaṇṭhacchidrasaṃlagnādhonālasaṃyute || 9.49;2
*     anyaghaṭaḥ pārśve nimnadeśe sthitaḥ || 9.49;3
*     tato ghaṭayorvadane ruddhvā pidhānābhyāṃ pidhāya saṃdhilepādi kṛtvādho ghaṭayorvadanayoradhaḥsthitanalikāyojitacchidrayorapi saṃdhilepaṃ kṛtvā jvālayet || 9.49;4
*     ghaṭāntare tāpotpattiparyantaṃ pāradaśuddhyarthametadyantrasyopayogo bodhyaḥ || 9.49;5

____________________


[pālikāyantra]
caṣakaṃ vartulaṃ lauhaṃ vinatāgrordhvadaṇḍakam |
etaddhi pālikāyantraṃ balijāraṇahetave || 9.50 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     pālikāyantramāha caṣakamiti || 9.50;1
*     caṣakaṃ darvīsamānaṃ pātram || 9.50;2
*     tattu na tiryagdaṇḍaṃ kiṃtu vinatāya ucchrito daṇḍo yasya tathoktam || 9.50;3
*     etadyantraṃ sadhūmagandhakajāraṇopayogi tathā bhairavanāthoktaparpaṭyādividhau copayokṣyate || 9.50;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     pālikāyantramāha caṣakamiti || 9.50;1
*     vinatamagraṃ yasya tatnyubjīkṛtāgrabhāgamūrdhvamuccaiḥkṛtaṃ daṇḍaṃ yasya tadunnatadaṇḍaṃ vinatāgraṃ ca tadūrdhvadaṇḍaṃ ceti vinatāgrordhvadaṇḍakaṃ lauhaṃ lohamayaṃ vartulaṃ golākāraṃ caṣakaṃ kaṭorikā kāryamiti śeṣaḥ || 9.50;2
*     caṣakaśabdasya madyapānapātrārthakatve'pi pātramātrārthakatvaṃ prāgeva pradarśitam || 9.50;3
*     balijāraṇahetave gandhakajāraṇārtham || 9.50;4

____________________


[ghaṭayantra]
catuṣprasthajalādhāraścaturaṅgulikānanaḥ |
ghaṭayantramidaṃ proktaṃ tadāpyāyanakaṃ smṛtam || 9.51 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     ghaṭayantramāha caturiti || 9.51;1
*     ṣoḍaśaśarāvamitajaladhāraṇasamarthaḥ caturaṅgulavistṛtamukhaviśiṣṭaśca ghaṭaḥ ghaṭayantramiti niṣkarṣaḥ || 9.51;2
*     āpyāyanakaṃ rasādīnāṃ tarpaṇaṃ ṣāḍguṇyasaṃpādanamiti yāvat || 9.51;3
*     āpyāyanakamityatra utthāpanakamiti pāṭhe rasotthāpanasādhanamityarthaḥ || 9.51;4

____________________


[iṣṭikāyantra]
vidhāya vartulaṃ gartaṃ mallamatra nidhāya ca |
vinidhāyeṣṭakāṃ tatra madhyagartavatīṃ śubhām || 9.52 ||
gartasya paritaḥ kuryātpālikāmaṅgulocchrayām |
garte sūtaṃ vinikṣipya gartāsye vasanaṃ kṣipet || 9.53 ||
nikṣipedgandhakaṃ tatra mallenāsyaṃ nirudhya ca |
mallapālikayormadhye mṛdā samyaṅnirudhya ca || 9.54 ||
vanopalaiḥ puṭaṃ deyaṃ kapotākhyaṃ na cādhikam |
iṣṭikāyantrametatsyādgandhakaṃ tena jārayet || 9.55 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     iṣṭakāyantramāha vidhāyeti || 9.55;1
*     mallaṃ śarāvapātram || 9.55;2
*     pālikāmālavālam || 9.55;3
*     golākāraṃ gartamekaṃ kṛtvā tatra śarāvaṃ saṃsthāpya tadupari madhyacchidrāmiṣṭakāmekāṃ vinyasetiṣṭakāgartaṃ paritaḥ aṅgulimitonnatamālavālamekaṃ ca kuryāttata iṣṭakārandhre pāradaṃ vinikṣipya randhramukhe vastraṃ tadupari gandhakaṃ ca vinyasya śarāvāntareṇa ruddhvā śarāvālavālayoḥ saṃdhiṃ mṛdā samyagālipya ca vanyakarīṣaiḥ kapotākhyapuṭaṃ dadyāditi niṣkarṣaḥ || 9.55;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atheṣṭikāyantraṃ lakṣayati nidhāya vartulāmiti || 9.55;1
*     mallaṃ gambhīrodaraṃ kṣudraṃ mṛnmayaṃ pātraṃ tadgartāmadhye saṃsthāpya tatraikāmiṣṭikāṃ madhyagartavatīṃ ca nidhāyeṣṭikāghaṭakagartasya paritaḥ samantato'ṅgulocchrāyāṃ pālikāmālavālaṃ vidhāya tadgarte pāradaṃ kṣiptvā tadgartamukhe vastraṃ prasārya tacca dṛḍhaṃ baddhvā tadupari samabhāgaṃ gandhakaṃ dattvā nyubjamallena gartamukhaṃ ruddhvā mallapālimadhyabhāgaṃ mṛdā samyagruddhvopari vanyopalaiḥ kapotapuṭaṃ punaḥ punardeyam || 9.55;2
*     iṣṭikayā pāradasya yantraṇādetadyantramiṣṭikāyantranāmnā kathitam || 9.55;3
*     tena nirdhūmagandhakajāraṇaṃ kuryāt || 9.55;4
*     jīrṇe ca gandhake pārado raktavarṇo bhavet || 9.55;5
*     atra drutapārade gandhakajāraṇāyāṃ gartāsye vastrācchādanaṃ vastropari gandhakaṃ ca dadyāditi vidhikramaḥ || 9.55;6
*     yatra tu baddhasya rasasya gandhakajāraṇā kartavyā syāttadā tu pāradaṃ vastreṇa baddhvā tasyādhastādupariṣṭācca gandhakaṃ dattvā jārayedityanukto'pi viśeṣo bodhyaḥ || 9.55;7

____________________


[hiṅgulākṛṣṭividyādharayantra]
sthālikopari vinyasya sthālīṃ samyaṅnirudhya ca |
ūrdhvasthālyāṃ jalaṃ kṣiptvā vahniṃ prajvālayedadhaḥ |
etadvidyādharaṃ yantraṃ hiṅgulākṛṣṭihetave || 9.56 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     hiṅgulākṛṣṭividyādharayantramāha sthālikoparīti || 9.56;1
*     sthālīmityatra ūrdhvamukhīmiti śeṣo bodhyaḥ || 9.56;2
*     atra hiṅgulākṛṣṭiprakārasya anuktatvātgranthāntaroktastatprakāraḥ pradarśyate tadyathā jambīrādirasaśodhitahiṅgulamadhaḥsthālyāṃ parṇopari saṃsthāpya kaṭhinīghṛṣṭatalabhāgāmuttānāṃ sthālīmaparāṃ tadupari dattvā mṛdambarādibhiḥ saṃdhimālipya ca adho jvālā deyā ūrdhvasthālyāṃ jalaṃ ca uṣṇe ca tasmin tatnikṣipya punardeyamevaṃ triṃśadvāraṃ kuryāt || 9.56;3
*     granthāntare'sya ūrdhvapātanamiti nāmāntaram || 9.56;4
*     kṛcidetadanantaram |
*     rasaṃ saṃmūrchitaṃ sthūlapātramāpūrya kāñjikaiḥ |
*     dviyāmaṃ svedayedenaṃ rasotthāpanahetave || 9.56;5
*     ityadhikaḥ pāṭho vidyate || 9.56;6



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha hiṅgulākṛṣṭividyādharayantramāha sthālikopari vinyasyeti || 9.56;1
*     antastale nihitaśuṣkahiṅgulāyā viśālamukhāyāḥ sthālikāyā uparyuttānāmeva laghusthālīṃ vinyasya sthāpayitvā saṃdhilepādinā ruddhvordhvasthālyāṃ kiṃcicchītalajalaṃ punaḥ punardadyāt || 9.56;2
*     adhaścullyāṃ vahniṃ jvālayetpraharatrayaparyantam || 9.56;3
*     etadyantraṃ pāradasya hiṅgulādviyujyordhvapātratala ākṛṣṭikaraṃ syāt || 9.56;4
*     etadākṛṣṭarasasya ṣoḍaśādhyāye caṇḍasaṃgrahagadaikakapāṭavidhāvanyatra copayogaṃ vakṣyati || 9.56;5

____________________


[ḍamaruyantra]
yantrasthālyupari sthālīṃ nyubjāṃ dattvā nirundhayet |
yantraṃ ḍamarukākhyaṃ tadrasabhasmakṛte hitam || 9.57 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha ḍamaruyantramāha pāradauṣadhagarbhitayantrasthālyupari nyubjāmeva sthālīṃ nidhāya nirundhayet || 9.57;1
*     mṛtkarpaṭādinā saṃdhirodhaṃ kuryāt || 9.57;2
*     yantrasyādhastādagniḥ kāryaḥ || 9.57;3
*     etadyantraṃ ḍamarukākhyamūrdhvatalagapāradabhasmakarmaṇyupayujyate || 9.57;4

____________________

[nābhiyantra]
mallamadhye caredgartaṃ tatra sūtaṃ sagandhakam |
gartasya paritaḥ kuḍyaṃ prakuryādaṅgulocchritam || 9.58 ||
tataścācchādayetsamyaggostanākāramūṣayā |
samyaktoyamṛdā ruddhvā samyagatrocyamānayā || 9.59 ||

[(ins.) toyamṛd]
lehavatkṛtababbūlakvāthena parimarditam |
jīrṇakiṭṭarajaḥ sūkṣmaṃ guḍacūrṇasamanvitam |
iyaṃ hi jalamṛtproktā durbhedyā salilaiḥ khalu || 9.60 ||

[(ins.) vahnimṛd]
khaṭikāpaṭukiṭṭaiśca mahiṣīdugdhamarditaiḥ |
vahnimṛtsnā bhavedghoravahnitāpasahā khalu || 9.61 ||
etayā mṛtsnayā ruddho na gantuṃ kṣamate rasaḥ |
vidagdhavanitāprauḍhapremṇā ruddhaḥ pumāniva || 9.62 ||
nandī nāgārjunaścaiva brahmajyotirmunīśvaraḥ |
vetti śrīsomadevaśca nāparaḥ pṛthivītale || 9.63 ||

[Fortsetśung: nābhiyantra]
tato jalaṃ vinikṣipya vahniṃ prajvālayedadhaḥ |
nābhiyantramidaṃ proktaṃ nandinā sarvavedinā |
anena jīryate sūto nirdhūmaḥ śuddhagandhakaḥ || 9.64 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     nābhiyantramāha mallamadhye iti || 9.64;1
*     kuḍyaṃ bhittiḥ ālavālākāramiti yāvat || 9.64;2
*     atrocyamānayā atra yantre ucyamānayā kathyamānayā toyamṛdā lehavadityādinā vakṣyamāṇayā paribhāṣikamṛdā ityarthaḥ || 9.64;3
*     atra prathamaṃ samyagācchādayeddvitīyaṃ samyakruddhvā tṛtīyaṃ samyagucyamānayā sambadhyate || 9.64;4
*     toyamṛtprakāramāha lehavaditi || 9.64;5
*     lepārthamṛttikāprastutaprasaṅgakrameṇa vahnimṛtprakāramāha khaṭiketi || 9.64;6
*     ayaṃ vidhiḥ pātramadhye kiṃcidgartaṃ kṛtvā tatra rasagandhau niveśya gartasya caturdikṣu aṅgulocchrāyamālavālaṃ kuryāttato gostanākṛtimūṣayā sālavālaṃ sarasagandhakaṃ gartamācchādya toyamṛdā sandhiṃ limpettatastatra jalaṃ dattvā yantrādho vahniṃ dāpayediti || 9.64;7



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha nābhiyantramāha mallamadhya iti || 9.64;1
*     dṛḍhasthūlavistṛtamṛtpātramadhye gartaṃ kuryāt || 9.64;2
*     tatra pāradaṃ gandhakaṃ ca nidadhyāt || 9.64;3
*     tato gartasya samantato'ṅgulocchrāyaṃ kuḍyākāraṃ bandhaṃ kuryāt || 9.64;4
*     tatastaṃ gartaṃ gostanākāramūṣayā nyubjayā pidadhyāt || 9.64;5
*     atrocyamānayā toyamṛdākhyayā mṛdā saṃdhiṃ ruddhvā saṃśoṣyoparisthitasarvamallabhāgaṃ kaṇṭhaparyantaṃ jalena pūrayet || 9.64;6
*     yantrasyādhastācca cullyāṃ vahniṃ prajvālayedityantimaślokena saṃbandho bodhyaḥ || 9.64;7
*     atra jalamṛcchabdabodhārthaṃ jalamṛdaṃ tatprasaṅgācca vahnimṛdaṃ ca madhyasthitagranthenāha lehavaditi || 9.64;8
*     ghanena babbūlatvakkaṣāyeṇa purāṇaṃ lohakiṭṭacūrṇaṃ sūkṣmaṃ kaṇaṃ yathā syāttathā saṃmardya tatra guḍacūrṇaṃ samaṃ dattvā punaḥ saṃmardya kṛteyaṃ mṛjjalamṛditi khyātā || 9.64;9
*     atha khaṭikāśvetacūrṇadravyaṃ khaḍū cāsa iti vākhyātam || 9.64;10
*     paṭu saindhavam || 9.64;11
*     lohakiṭṭaṃ ca sarvametanmithaḥ samaṃ gṛhītvā mahiṣīdugdhena saṃmardya vihiteyaṃ mṛdvahnimṛdākhyātā || 9.64;12
*     iyaṃ ca tīkṣṇaṃ vahnipātaṃ sahata ityanvarthākhyā || 9.64;13
*     etayā ruddho rasastaruṇyā kṛtasnehena baddhaḥ puruṣaḥ ivānyatra gantuṃ na śaknoti || 9.64;14
*     asya yantrasya jalayantramiti nāmāntaraṃ kecidvadanti || 9.64;15
*     pāṣāṇabhedirasavidhāvasyopayogaṃ vakṣyati || 9.64;16
*     rasasāre pāradabandhanārthamapyasya yantrasyopayogamudājahāra || 9.64;17

____________________


[grastayantra]
mūṣāṃ mūṣodarāviṣṭāmādyantaḥsamavartulām |
cipiṭāṃ ca tale proktaṃ grastayantraṃ manīṣibhiḥ |
sūtendrarandhanārthaṃ hi rasavidbhirudīritam || 9.65 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     grastayantramāha mūṣāmiti || 9.65;1
*     sūtendrarandhanārthaṃ rasapākārtham || 9.65;2




*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha grastayantramāha mūṣāmiti || 9.65;1
*     sthūlamūṣodare praveśayogyāmādyantamadhyabhāgeṣu tatsamavartulāṃ laghumūṣāṃ kuryāt || 9.65;2
*     etadyantraṃ grastayantramiti khyātam || 9.65;3
*     anena hi svodare ruddhagatiḥ pārado grastaḥ pīḍito mārakabheṣajena mārito vā bhavati || 9.65;4
*     kajjalīkṛtaṃ bheṣajāntareṇa saṃmarditaṃ vā pāradaṃ sthūlamūṣāntastale saṃbhṛtya tadrodhārthaṃ kiṃcillaghumūṣāṃ nyubjāṃ tadudare praveśya dṛḍhaṃ yathā syāttathā saṃdhirodhaṃ kṛtvā gajapuṭena pāradaṃ bhasmīkurvanti bhiṣajaḥ || 9.65;5
*     randhraṇaṃ bandhanam || 9.65;6

____________________


[sthālīyantra]
sthālyāṃ tāmrādi nikṣipya mallenāsyaṃ nirudhya ca |
pacyate sthālikādhastātsthālīyantramidaṃ smṛtam || 9.66 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha sthālīyantramāha sthālyāmiti || 9.66;1
*     sthālyāṃ mṛnmayapātryāṃ tāmralohādi nikṣipya mallena niruddhamukhaṃ kṛtvā tadbheṣajaṃ sthālikādhaḥsthavahninā pacyate ityetatsthālīyantraṃ prasiddham || 9.66;2

____________________


[dhūpayantra]
vidhāyāṣṭāṅgulaṃ pātraṃ lauhamaṣṭāṅgulocchrayam |
kaṇṭhādho hyaṅgule deśe galādhāre hi tatra ca || 9.67 ||
tiryaglohaśalākāśca tanvīstiryagvinikṣipet |
tanūni svarṇapattrāṇi tāsāmupari vinyaset || 9.68 ||
pattrādho nikṣipedvakṣyamāṇamihaiva hi |
tatpātraṃ nyubjapātreṇa chādayedapareṇa hi || 9.69 ||
mṛdā vilipya saṃdhiṃ ca vahniṃ prajvālayedadhaḥ |
tena pattrāṇi kṛtsnāni hatānyuktavidhānataḥ || 9.70 ||
rasaścarati vegena drutaṃ garbhe dravanti ca |
gandhālakaśilānāṃ hi kajjalyā vā mṛtāhinā || 9.71 ||
dhūpanaṃ svarṇapattrāṇāṃ prathamaṃ parikīrtitam |
tārārthaṃ tārapattrāṇi mṛtavaṅgena dhūpayet || 9.72 ||
dhūpayecca yathāyogyairanyairuparasairapi |
dhūpayantramidaṃ proktaṃ jāraṇādravyasādhane || 9.73 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     dhūpayantramāha vidhāyeti || 9.73;1
*     galādhāre śalākāsthāpanārthaṃ galadeśasthapālikāvadādhāraviśeṣe ityarthaḥ || 9.73;2
*     atra prathamaṃ tiryaklohaśalākā ityanena dvitīyaṃ ca vinikṣipetityanena sambadhyate || 9.73;3
*     tiryakvakrākārāḥ tanvīḥ sūkṣmāḥ lohaśalākāḥ tiryakvakrabhāvena vinikṣipetityanvayaḥ || 9.73;4
*     tenetyādi || 9.73;5
*     uktavidhinā hatāni kṛtsnāni patrāṇi drutaṃ garbhe dravanti tataśca rasaḥ sa svarṇapatradravaḥ vegena carati svakāryaṃ sādhayatītyarthaḥ || 9.73;6
*     patrādho nikṣipeddhūmamityuktaṃ kiṃtaddhūmamityāha gandheti || 9.73;7
*     prathamaṃ śreṣṭham || 9.73;8
*     dairghyavistārato'ṣṭāṅgulamānaṃ lohapātramekaṃ kārayitvā tasya kaṇṭhādhaḥ aṅgulidvayaparimitasthāne galādhāre sūkṣmatiryaglohaśalākāḥ tiryagbhāvena vinyasya tadupari kaṇṭakavedhyasvarṇapatrāṇi sthāpayettatpatrādhaḥ pātrābhyantare gandhakaharitālamanaḥśilābhiḥ kṛtakajjalīṃ mṛtanāgaṃ vā nikṣipya adhomukhapātrāntareṇa tatpātraṃ pidhāya mṛdādinā sandhiṃ ruddhvā ca pātrādho vahniṃ prajvālayettena saṃtaptakajjalyādito dhūmaṃ nirgatya svarṇapatre lagiṣyati patrāṇi tāni bhasmībhavanti garbhe dravanti ca || 9.73;9




*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha dhūpayantramāha vidhāyeti || 9.73;1
*     atra yathoktamānaṃ lauhaṃ pātraṃ vidhāya tatkaṇṭhādho dvyaṅgule deśe jalādhāraṃ laghupātraviśeṣaṃ nihitaṃ kuryāt || 9.73;2
*     jaḍadravyasya dhāraṇārthamāśrayabhūtaṃ pātraṃ jalādhāraśabdavācyam || 9.73;3
*     tatra lohaśalākāstiryaṅnidhāya tatra svarṇapatrāṇi vinyasetsthāpayet || 9.73;4
*     teṣāmadhobhāge vakṣyamāṇaṃ dhūmadravyaṃ nikṣipet || 9.73;5
*     tato yantrapātramapareṇa nyubjapātreṇa chādayet || 9.73;6
*     mṛdā saṃdhilepaṃ kṛtvā yantrasyādhastādvahniṃ kuryāt || 9.73;7
*     uktavidhānena hatāni patrāṇi kṛṣṇāni bhavanti || 9.73;8
*     tatastāni sarase taptakhalve yathāvidhi mardanena pārado bhakṣayati || 9.73;9
*     bhakṣitāni ca tāni pāradodare śīghraṃ dravanti || 9.73;10
*     atra dhūpadravyamāha || 9.73;11
*     pāradena sākaṃ gandhakaharitālamanaḥśilānāmanyatamasya samastānāṃ vā kṛtāyāḥ kajjalyā dhūpanaṃ tayā kajjalyā bhasmīkṛtanāgena dhūpanaṃ vā svarṇapatrāṇāṃ dhūpanasaṃskārārthaṃ mukhyaṃ dhūpanadravyamudāhṛtam || 9.73;12
*     tārārthaṃ dhūpanaṃ tūcyata iti śeṣaḥ || 9.73;13
*     tadāha tārapatrāṇyuktakajjalyā mṛtena vaṅgena dhūpayet || 9.73;14
*     yathāyogyairmāraṇayogyairuparasairhiṅgūlarasakamākṣikādibhirapi dhūpayet || 9.73;15
*     jāraṇādravasādhanaṃ patrāṇāṃ jāraṇopayogipāradagarbhadravasādhanamityarthaḥ || 9.73;16

____________________


[kandukayantra (1)]
sthūlasthālyāṃ jalaṃ kṣiptvā vāso baddhvā mukhe dṛḍham |
tatra svedyaṃ vinikṣipya tanmukhaṃ prapidhāya ca || 9.74 ||
adhastājjvālayedagniṃ yantraṃ tatkandukābhidham |
svedanīyantramityanye prāhuścedaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ || 9.75 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     spaṣṭam || 9.75;1
*     svedanīyantratayā prāguktamapi idaṃ saṃjñāntarapradarśanārthaṃ punaruktamathavā tatra sthālyā viśeṣo noktaḥ ataḥ yā kācitsthālī eva grāhyā atra tu sthūlasthālī eva grāhyā ataḥ svedanīyantrātasya vaiśiṣṭyamiti || 9.75;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     prāguktaṃ svedanīyantrameva kandukayantranāmnāha sthūlasthālyāmiti || 9.75;1
*     anye manīṣiṇo dhīrā vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa prāhuḥ yadveti || 9.75;2
*     tatkandukayantramiti || 9.75;3
*     evaṃ ca teṣāṃ mate svedanīyantramasmādbhinnameveti bhāvaḥ || 9.75;4
*     puṣpādīnāmatimṛdudravyāṇāṃ kalkādervā svedanārthamasya yantrasyopayogo bodhyaḥ || 9.75;5

____________________


[kandukayantra (2)]
yadvā sthālyāṃ jalaṃ kṣiptvā tṛṇaṃ kṣiptvā mukhopari |
svedyadravyaṃ parikṣipya pidhānaṃ pravidhāya ca |
adhastājjvālayedagniṃ yantraṃ tatkandukaṃ smṛtam || 9.76 ||

[khalva:: grindstone]
khallayogyā śilā nīlā śyāmā snigdhā dṛḍhā guruḥ |
ṣoḍaśāṅgulakotsedhā navāṅgulakavistarā || 9.77 ||

[khalva:: pestle]
caturviṃśāṅgulā dīrghā gharṣaṇī dvādaśāṅgulā |
viṃśatyaṅguladīrghā vā syādutsedhe daśāṅgulā |
khallapramāṇaṃ tajjñeyaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ syādrasakarmaṇi || 9.78 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha khalvasaṃjñāyogyā sā śilā syāt || 9.78;1
*     yā nīlā śyāmavarṇā vā snigdhādiguṇaviśiṣṭā ṣoḍaśāṅgulakocchrāyā tathā navāṅgulakavistārā caturviṃśāṅgulairā samantāddīrghā ca syāt || 9.78;2
*     sā khalvayogyeti pūrvatra saṃbandhaḥ || 9.78;3
*     gharṣaṇī tu dvādaśāṅguladīrghā syāt || 9.78;4
*     punaḥ khalvaśilāmānavikalpamāha viṃśatyaṅguleti || 9.78;5
*     athavā khalvaśilā viṃśatyaṅguladīrghā || 9.78;6
*     utsedha ucchrāye ca daśāṅgulā kāryeti viśeṣaḥ || 9.78;7
*     navāṅguleti vistāramānaṃ tu prāguktameva grāhyam || 9.78;8



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     khallayantramāha khallayogyeti || 9.78;1
*     utsedhaḥ pārśvata unnataḥ || 9.78;2
*     dīrghaḥ āyāmena unnataḥ iti dīrghotsedhayorbhedaḥ || 9.78;3
*     gharṣaṇī kaṇḍanī putrikā iti yāvatno¡ā iti bhāṣā || 9.78;4

____________________


[khalva:: subtypes]
khallayantraṃ tridhā proktaṃ rasādisukhamardane || 9.79 ||
nirudgārau sumasṛṇau kāryau putrikayā yutau || 9.80 ||

[khalva:: ardhacandra]
utsedhe sa daśāṅgulaḥ khalu kalātulyāṅgulāyāmavān vistāreṇa daśāṅgulo munimitairnimnastayaivāṅgulaiḥ |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     gharṣaḥ śilāputraḥ || 9.80;1

____________________


pālyāṃ dvyaṅgulivistaraśca masṛṇo'tīvārdhacandropamo gharṣo dvādaśakāṅgulaśca tadayaṃ khallo mataḥ siddhaye || 9.81 ||
asminpañcapalaḥ sūto mardanīyo viśuddhaye |
tattadaucityayogena khalleṣvanyeṣu yojayet || 9.82 ||

[khalva (2)]
dvādaśāṅgulavistāraḥ khallo'timasṛṇopalaḥ |
caturaṅgulanimnaśca madhye'timasṛṇīkṛtaḥ || 9.83 ||
mardakaścipiṭo'dhastātsugrāhaśca śikhopari |
ayaṃ tu vartulaḥ khallo mardane'tisukhapradaḥ || 9.84 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     mardanārthaṃ dvitīyaṃ vartulakhallamāha dvādaśeti || 9.84;1
*     mardaka iti || 9.84;2
*     putrikā adhobhāge cipiṭavadvistīrṇā uparibhāge ca sukhena yathā dhāraṇīyā bhavati tathā kartavyā ityarthaḥ || 9.84;3

____________________


[taptakhalva]
lauho navāṅgulaḥ khallo nimnatve ca ṣaḍaṅgulaḥ |
mardako'ṣṭāṅgulaścaiva taptakhallābhidho'pyayam || 9.85 ||
kṛtvā khallākṛtiṃ cullīmaṅgāraiḥ paripūritām |
tasyāṃ niveśya taṃ khallaṃ pārśve bhastrikayā dhamet || 9.86 ||
tadantarmarditā piṣṭiḥ kṣārairamlaiśca saṃyutā |
pradravatyativegena sveditā nātra saṃśayaḥ |
kṛtaḥ kāntāyasā so'yaṃ bhavetkoṭiguṇo rasaḥ || 9.87 ||


________________________________________________________


 10
[crucible:: synonyms]
mūṣā hi krauñcikā proktā kumudī karahāṭikā |
pācanī vahnimitrā ca rasavādibhirīryate || 10.1 ||

[crucible:: nirukti]
muṣṇāti doṣānmūṣā yā sā mūṣeti nigadyate || 10.2 ||

[crucible:: material]
upādānaṃ bhavettasyā mṛttikā lohameva ca || 10.3 ||
mūṣāmukhaviniṣkrāntā varamekāpi kākiṇī |
durjanapraṇipātena dhiglakṣamapi māninām || 10.4 ||

[saṃdhilepa]
mūṣāpidhānayorbandhe bandhanaṃ saṃdhilepanam |
andhraṇaṃ randhraṇaṃ caiva saṃśliṣṭaṃ saṃdhibandhanam || 10.5 ||

[ṭon|ḷehm fṃrṭiegel]
mṛttikā pāṇḍurasthūlā śarkarā śoṇapāṇḍurā |
cirādhmānasahā sā hi mūṣārthamatiśasyate |
tadabhāve ca vālmīkī kaulālī vā samīryate || 10.6 ||
yā mṛttikā dugdhatuṣaiḥ śaṇena śikhitrakairvā hayaladdinā ca |
lauhena daṇḍena ca kuṭṭitā sā sādhāraṇā syātkhalu mūṣikārthe || 10.7 ||
śvetāśmānastuṣā dagdhāḥ śikhitrāḥ śaṇakharpare |
laddiḥ kiṭṭaṃ kṛṣṇamṛtsnā saṃyojyā mūṣikāmṛdi || 10.8 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     mūṣārthaṃ praśastamṛttikāmāha mṛttiketi || 10.8;1
*     pāṇḍurasthūlā pāṇḍuvarṇā sthūlā ca || 10.8;2
*     athavā śoṇapāṇḍurā raktapāṇḍumiśravarṇā cirādhmānasahā dīrghakālaṃ vyāpya agnisaṃtāpaṃ prāpyāpi avidāraṇaśīlā evaṃvidhā yā śarkarā mṛttikā kaṅkararūpā mṛttikā || 10.8;3
*     śarkarāmṛttikābhāve grāhyamṛttikāmāha taditi || 10.8;4
*     vālmīkī kṛmiśailodbhavā mṛduimāṭī iti vaṅgabhāṣā || 10.8;5
*     kaulālī kumbhakāramṛdityarthaḥ || 10.8;6
*     hayaladdinā aśvapurīṣena || 10.8;7
*     upādānāntarāṇyāha śvetāśmāna iti || 10.8;8
*     śvetāśmānaḥ śvetaprastarāḥ || 10.8;9
*     kharparaḥ kapālakhaṇḍaḥ dagdhamṛttikā iti yāvat || 10.8;10
*     śaṇakharpare ityatra śaṇakarpaṭe iti pāṭhe karpaṭaṃ chinnavastram || 10.8;11
*     laddiḥ hayaladdiḥ || 10.8;12
*     kiṭṭaṃ maṇḍūram || 10.8;13
*     kṛṣṇamṛtsnā kṛṣṇavarṇamṛttikā || 10.8;14
*     mūṣikāmṛdā mūṣārthagrāhyamṛttikayā sahetyarthaḥ || 10.8;15



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     mūṣārthaṃ praśastamṛttikāyā lakṣaṇamāha mṛttiketi || 10.8;1
*     pāṇḍurā sthūlakaṇā śoṇamiśrapāṇḍurakaṇā vā || 10.8;2
*     kaulālī kulālabhāṇḍotpādanārthamupādānamṛttikā || 10.8;3
*     atha sarvamūṣopayogisādhāraṇamṛttikāmāha yā mṛttiketi || 10.8;4
*     dagdhatuṣeti || 10.8;5
*     caturthāṃśena dagdhatuṣayuktā pratyekaṃ tathā bhāgaiḥ śikhitraiḥ kokilairhayaladdināśvaśakṛtā ca yuktā sā praśastā || 10.8;6
*     mūṣikāmṛdi caturthāṃśena saṃyojyadravyāṇyāha śvetāśmāna iti || 10.8;7
*     gāretyaparaparyāyāḥ || 10.8;8
*     te ca dagdhā eva grāhyāḥ || 10.8;9

____________________


[vajramūṣā]
mṛdastribhāgāḥ śaṇaladdibhāgau bhāgaśca nirdagdhatuṣopalādeḥ |
kiṭṭārdhabhāgaṃ parikhaṇḍya vajramūṣāṃ vidadhyātkhalu sattvapāte || 10.9 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     vajramūṣāmāha mṛda iti || 10.9;1
*     mṛdaḥ pūrvoktarūpamṛttikāyāḥ || 10.9;2
*     śaṇaladdibhāgau śaṇasya laddeśca pratyekaṃ bhāgadvayam || 10.9;3
*     parikhaṇḍya cūrṇayitvā || 10.9;4

____________________


[yogamūṣā]
dagdhāṅgāratuṣopetā mṛtsnā valmīkamṛttikā |
tattadviḍasamāyuktā tattadviḍavilepitā || 10.10 ||
tayā yā vihitā mūṣā yogamūṣeti kathyate |
anayā sādhitaḥ sūto jāyate guṇavattaraḥ || 10.11 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     yogamūṣāmāha dagdheti || 10.11;1
*     dagdhaśabdo'tra aṅgāratuṣābhyāṃ pratyekaṃ sambadhyate || 10.11;2
*     mṛtsnā pāṇḍurasthūlādiguṇopetā śarkarāmṛttiketyarthaḥ || 10.11;3
*     tattadviḍasamāyuktā sūtajāraṇārthaṃ prāṅnirūpitaviḍamiśritā || 10.11;4
*     tattadviḍavilepitā tenaiva viḍenāliptā || 10.11;5
*     dagdhāṅgārādiviḍāntaṃ sarvamekatra saṃnīya mūṣāṃ viracayya viḍena liptvā śuṣkīkṛtya gṛhṇīyāditi || 10.11;6



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha yogamūṣāmāha dagdhagāreti || 10.11;1
*     dagdhā gārā vajraprasavāḥ śvetapāṣāṇā dagdhaṃ śālituṣaṃ ca pratyekaṃ caturthāṃśena taistathā tattadviḍacūrṇaiśca miśritā yā praśastā valmīkamṛttikā tayā vihitā ghaṭitā mūṣā yogamūṣeti kīrtyate || 10.11;2
*     iyaṃ cābhrakasattvāderjāraṇāyāṃ viśiṣṭo yo yo viḍastena vilepitā tenaiva kāryā || 10.11;3
*     atha jāraṇāyāṃ sā pāradagarbhitā koṣṭhīyantre bhastrikayā dhmātā cettayā sādhito jāritatāmrasattvādiḥ khoṭādirūpaśca pārado guṇavattaro bhavati || 10.11;4
*     evaṃ ceyaṃ yogavāhimūṣā yogamūṣetyanvarthanāmiketi bhāvaḥ || 10.11;5

____________________


[vajradrāvaṇikamūṣā]
gārabhūnāgadhautābhyāṃ śaṇairdagdhatuṣairapi |
samaiḥ samā ca mūṣāmṛnmahiṣīdugdhamarditā || 10.12 ||
krauñcikā yantramātraṃ hi bahudhā parikīrtitā |
tayā viracitā mūṣā vajradrāvaṇikocitā || 10.13 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     vajradrāvaṇopayogimūṣāmāha gāreti || 10.13;1
*     gāraśabdo'tra koṣṭhāgārikārthakaḥ nāmaikadeśenāpi nāmasākalyagrahaṇamiti nyāyātkoṣṭhāgārikā mṛttikāviśeṣaḥ kumīre pokāra moṭī iti bhāṣā || 10.13;2
*     bhūnāgaśabdenāpi tanmṛttikā grāhyā evaṃ ca gārabhūnāgadhautābhyāṃ dhautakoṣṭhāgārikākiñculūkamṛdbhyām || 10.13;3
*     mūṣāmṛtpūrvoktaśarkarāvālmīkikaulālyādīnāmanyatamā || 10.13;4
*     gārādituṣāntaṃ sarvaṃ samaṃ mṛttikā ca sarvaiḥ samānā grāhyā || 10.13;5
*     krauñciketi || 10.13;6
*     yantramātre hi krauñcikā saṃjñā vartate sā hi bahudhā vividhaprakārā ityanvayaḥ || 10.13;7



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha hīnajātīyanātikaṭhinavajrapramukhakaṭhinapāṣāṇasattvaratnānāṃ drāvaṇakarīṃ mūṣāmāha gāreti || 10.13;1
*     gārāḥ śvetā vajrotpādakāḥ pāṣāṇāḥ || 10.13;2
*     bhūnāgadhautaṃ paribhāṣādhyāyoktaṃ grāhyam || 10.13;3
*     śaṇā dagdhatuṣāścaite mithaḥ samāḥ || 10.13;4
*     taiḥ samā samabhāgā prāguktalakṣaṇā mūṣopādānamṛdekatra kṛtvā mahiṣīdugdhena saṃmardya saṃskṛtā ceyaṃ mṛtkrauñcikāpakṣamātraṃ nānāvidhamūṣārūpo yo bhāgo yantratulyastannirmāṇārthaṃ praśastatvena bahuṣu grantheṣu kathitā || 10.13;5
*     taddhitamūṣāyāḥ sattvāharaṇādikāryeṣvagninā cirakālaparyantaṃ durbhedyatvāt || 10.13;6
*     bhūnāgopetatvena śīghradrāvaṇopayogitvācca || 10.13;7
*     tayā saṃskṛtayā mṛdā vihitā mūṣā śāstre vajradrāvaṇaketi khyātā || 10.13;8

____________________


[gāramūṣā]
dugdhaṣaḍguṇagārāṣṭakiṭṭāṅgāraśaṇānvitā |
kṛṣṇamṛdbhiḥ kṛtā mūṣā gāramūṣetyudāhṛtā |
yāmayugmaparidhmānānnāsau dravati vahninā || 10.14 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     gāramūṣāmāha dugdheti || 10.14;1
*     gārasya ṣaḍguṇatvaṃ kiṭṭāṅgāraśaṇānāṃ ca pratyekamaṣṭaguṇatvaṃ kṛṣṇamṛdapekṣayā bodhyam || 10.14;2
*     dugdhaṃ ca mardanayogyam || 10.14;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha gāramūṣāmāha dagdheti || 10.14;1
*     dagdhā ye mṛttikāpekṣayā ṣaḍguṇā gārāstadviśiṣṭāstathā lohakiṭṭā aṅgārā nirvāṇāgnayaḥ kokilāḥ || 10.14;2
*     tathā śaṇāḥ tāga iti khyātāḥ || 10.14;3
*     te ca pratyekaṃ caturthāṃśamitāstairyuktā yā kṛṣṇavarṇā mṛttatkṛtā mūṣā śāstre gāramūṣeti parikīrtitā || 10.14;4
*     teṣāṃ pāṣāṇānāmatra sādhakamadhye hyadhikabhāgopetatvāditi bhāvaḥ || 10.14;5

____________________


[varamūṣā]
vajrāṅgāratuṣāstulyāstaccaturguṇamṛttikā |
gārā ca mṛttikātulyā sarvairetairvinirmitā |
varamūṣeti nirdiṣṭā yāmamagniṃ saheta sā || 10.15 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     varamūṣāmāha vajreti || 10.15;1
*     vajraṃ tadākhyalauhaṃ pūrvoktasaṃgatyā tatkiṭṭaṃ vā bodhyam || 10.15;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha varamūṣāmāha vajreti || 10.15;1
*     vajro hīrakaḥ || 10.15;2
*     tadalābhe vaikrāntaṃ grāhyam || 10.15;3
*     aṅgārā haṭhānmṛttikādhūliprakṣepeṇa niragnayaḥ kokilāḥ || 10.15;4
*     tathā dagdhatuṣāḥ || 10.15;5
*     sarve ca mithastulyabhāgāḥ || 10.15;6
*     sarvebhyaścaturguṇā mṛttikā tattulyā gārāḥ śvetapāṣāṇāḥ || 10.15;7
*     etannirmitā mūṣā varamūṣeti kathyate || 10.15;8
*     varasya śreṣṭhasya mahāmūlyasya hīrakasyātra ghaṭakatvāt || 10.15;9

____________________


[varṇamūṣā]
pāṣāṇarahitā raktā raktavargānusādhitā |
mṛttayā sādhitā mūṣā kṣitikhecaralepitā |
varṇamūṣeti sā proktā varṇotkarṣe niyujyate || 10.16 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     varṇamūṣāmāha pāṣāṇarahiteti || 10.16;1
*     raktā raktavarṇā || 10.16;2
*     raktavargānusādhitā vakṣyamāṇakusumbhādimākṣikāntavargakvāthena bhāvitā śṛtā vā || 10.16;3
*     kṣitikhecaralepitā kṣitiśca khaṃ ca kṣitikhe tatra carataḥ iti kṣitikhecarau bhūnāgamṛtkāśīśaṃ ca yadvā kṣitisthaḥ khecaraḥ kāśīśamityarthaḥ || 10.16;4
*     varṇotkarṣe praśastavarṇatāpādane raktavarṇajanane ityāśayaḥ || 10.16;5



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha varṇamūṣāmāha pāṣāṇeti || 10.16;1
*     pāṣāṇā raktapāṣāṇāḥ || 10.16;2
*     samabhāgaiśca taiḥ sahitā yā raktā raktavarṇā mṛttikā tayā sādhitā vihitā mūṣā varṇamūṣeti proktā || 10.16;3
*     yasmādiyaṃ niyujyata upayujyate nirvāhaṇena dhātoḥ pāradasya vā raktavarṇotpādanārtham || 10.16;4
*     uktaṃ ca rasahṛdaye || 10.16;5
*     nirguṇḍīrasabhāvitapuṭitaṃ śilayā vartitaṃ ślakṣṇam |
*     tāvanmṛditapuṭitaṃ nirutthabhāvaṃ vrajedyāvat || 10.16;6
*     tāre tannirvyūḍhaṃ yāvatpītaṃ bhavedruciram |
*     hemnā samena militaṃ mātrātulyaṃ bhavetkanakam || 10.16;7
*     iti || 10.16;8
*     atra nirvāhaṇaṃ cāsyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ kāryam || 10.16;9
*     raktavargaḥ śvetavargaścāsminnevādhyāye vakṣyamāṇaḥ || 10.16;10

____________________


[rūpyamūṣā]
pāṣāṇarahitā śvetā śvetavargānusādhitā |
mṛttayā sādhitā mūṣā kṣitikhecaralepitā |
raupyamūṣeti sā proktā varṇotkarṣe niyujyate || 10.17 ||

[viḍamūṣā]
tattadbhedamṛdodbhūtā tattadviḍavilepitā |
dehalohārthayogārthaṃ viḍamūṣetyudāhṛtā || 10.18 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     viḍamūṣāmāha tattaditi || 10.18;1
*     tattadbhedamṛdodbhūtā śarkarādīnāmanyatamamṛdā racitā ityarthaḥ || 10.18;2
*     tattadbhedamṛdodbhūtā ityatra tattadviḍamṛdodbhūtā iti pāṭhe pūrvoktaviḍena pūrvoktamṛdā ca udbhūtā nirmitā || 10.18;3
*     dehalohārthayogārthaṃ dehasya lauhavaddārḍhyasampādanārthā ye yogāḥ tadarthaṃ tatkarmasampādanārthamityarthaḥ || 10.18;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha viḍamūṣāmāha tattadviḍamṛdudbhūteti || 10.18;1
*     abhrasattvajāraṇasvarṇajāraṇopayogīni yāni vaḍavānalādiviḍāni yathoddiṣṭarasādibhāvitāni cūrṇāni tathā sarvalohādijāraṇopayogiviḍātmakaścūrṇaḥ paribhāṣādhyāye viḍavarṇanāvasare prāguktaḥ || 10.18;2
*     tathā pūrvoktā nānāvidhā pāṇḍurādimṛttadudbhūtā tatsiddhā tathā tattadviḍairgrāsajāraṇasādhanairviśiṣṭaiścūrṇairantarvilepitā yā mūṣā sā viḍamūṣetyucyate || 10.18;3
*     iyaṃ mūṣā dṛḍhakāyakararasāyanavidhau sādhanatvena dehārthā dehopayoginītyarthaḥ || 10.18;4
*     tathā lohamāraṇopayoginī || 10.18;5
*     ata eva lohārthā || 10.18;6
*     tathā artho dhanaṃ tathā suvarṇādyutpādanavidhau dhātuvāde sādhanatvenārthārthā || 10.18;7
*     tathā kāsaghnaratnakaraṇḍakarasārśonāśakasarvalokāśrayarasaprabhṛtinānāvidharogaghnarasavidhau sādhanatvena yogārthā cetīyaṃ caturvidhakāryopayoginī bhavati || 10.18;8

____________________


[vajradrāvaṇikamūṣā (2)]
gārabhūnāgadhautābhyāṃ tuṣamṛṣṭaśaṇena ca |
samaiḥ samā ca mūṣāmṛnmahiṣīdugdhamarditā || 10.19 ||
krauñcikā yantramātre hi bahudhā parikīrtitā |
tayā viracitā mūṣā liptā matkuṇaśoṇitaiḥ || 10.20 ||
bālābdadhvanimūlaiśca vajradrāvaṇakrauñcikā |
sahate'gniṃ caturyāmaṃ draveṇa vyādhitā satī || 10.21 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     pūrvoktavajradrāvaṇopayogimūṣāyāḥ prakārāntaramāha gāreti || 10.21;1
*     matkuṇaḥ chārapokā iti khyātaḥ krimiviśeṣaḥ tasya śoṇitaiḥ || 10.21;2
*     abdadhvaniḥ vanataṇḍulīyakaḥ kāṃṭānaṭe iti bhāṣā bālābdadhvanimūlaiḥ navotpannataṇḍulīyamūlaiḥ yadvā vālaḥ aśvapucchakeśaḥ tathā abdadhvanimūlaṃ taiḥ liptā ityanenānvayaḥ || 10.21;3
*     draveṇa vyādhitā viddhā spṛṣṭā ityarthaḥ dravapūrṇā ityāśayaḥ || 10.21;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     athāvyabhicāreṇa vajramātradrāvaṇārthaṃ vajramūṣāmāha gāreti || 10.21;1
*     vālābdeti || 10.21;2
*     vālā narakeśāḥ || 10.21;3
*     abdadhvanimūlaṃ tandulīyakamūlam || 10.21;4
*     iyaṃ mūṣā dravadraveṇa vyathitā satī antaḥsthataijasadravyayogasaṃyogena bāhyāgnisaṃyogena ca saṃtataṃ pīḍitā satyapyagniṃ sahate || 10.21;5

____________________


[mūṣāpyāyana]
drave dravībhāvamukhe mūṣāyā dhmānayogataḥ |
kṣaṇamuddharaṇaṃ yattanmūṣāpyāyanamucyate || 10.22 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     mūṣāpyāyanamāha drave iti || 10.22;1
*     dhmāyate aneneti dhmānamagniḥ tadyogataḥ agnisaṃyogāddrave drāvaṇopayogini dravye dravībhāvamukhe dravībhavitumārabdhe mūṣāyā yatkṣaṇamuddharaṇamagnitaḥ uttolanamavatāraṇamityarthaḥ tadāpyāyanaṃ tarpaṇaṃ sthāyitvasampādanamityarthaḥ || 10.22;2

____________________


[vṛntākamūṣā]
vṛntākākāramūṣāyāṃ nālaṃ dvādaśakāṅgulam |
dhattūrapuṣpavaccordhvaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ śliṣṭapuṣpavat || 10.23 ||
aṣṭāṅgulaṃ ca sacchidraṃ sā syādvṛntākamūṣikā |
anayā kharparādīnāṃ mṛdūnāṃ sattvamāharet || 10.24 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     vṛntākamūṣāmāha vṛntāketi || 10.24;1
*     vṛntākaṃ vārttākuḥ vārttākuphalasadṛśākārāṃ mūṣāṃ kṛtvā tatra dvādaśāṅguladīrghaṃ nālaṃ yojayetnālāgrabhāgaṃ ca dhustūrapuṣpavaduparyadho yugmarūpāvasthitamaṣṭāṅgulaṃ sacchidraṃ ca kuryāt || 10.24;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     vṛntākamūṣāmāha vṛntākākāreti || 10.24;1
*     dvādaśakāṅgulam || 10.24;2
*     dvādaśāṅgulaṃ dīrghamūrdhvamuttānamūṣāyā ūrdhvatanamukhabhāgo dhattūrapuṣpavatkrameṇa vistīrṇastadvadeva ca saṃśleṣeṇa tricatuṣkoṇayuto dhmānāvasare pihite'pi mukhe sati tatkoṇamārgeṇāntaḥsthadhūmasya bahirnirgamanārthaṃ koṇaghaṭanenaiva tanmukhaṃ sacchidraṃ bhavati || 10.24;3
*     tacca mukhamaṣṭāṅgulavistṛtaṃ vartulasūtraveṣṭanenāṣṭāṅgulamitamityarthaḥ || 10.24;4

____________________


[gostanamūṣā]
mūṣā yā gostanākārā śikhāyuktapidhānakā |
sattvānāṃ drāvaṇe śuddhau mūṣā sā gostanī bhavet || 10.25 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha gostanamūṣāmāha mūṣāyā iti || 10.25;1
*     śikhāyukteti || 10.25;2
*     śikharākārapidhānakasahiteyameva cāndhamūṣābhidhīyate || 10.25;3
*     dvaṃdvitabījamelāpādividhāvasyā upayogo bodhyaḥ || 10.25;4
*     yatra tu nyubjayā tayācchādanaṃ kriyate tatra pidhānarahitā grāhyā || 10.25;5
*     yathā nābhiyantre prāguktam |
*     tataścācchādayetsamyaggostanākāramūṣayā |
*     iti || 10.25;6

____________________


[mallamūṣā]
nirdiṣṭā mallamūṣā yā malladvitayasampuṭāt |
parpaṭyādirasādīnāṃ svedanāya prakīrtitā || 10.26 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     mallamūṣāmāha nirdiṣṭeti || 10.26;1
*     mallaṃ pidhānopayogi vistīrṇaṃ kiṃcidgabhīrodaraṃ mṛnmayaṃ pātraṃ śarāveti loke prasiddham || 10.26;2
*     saptaviṃśatitame'dhyāye'syā upayogaṃ vakṣyati madanasaṃjīvanarasavidhau || 10.26;3
*     anyatrāpi prabhūtasthale caiṣopayujyate || 10.26;4
*     tadevocyate parpaṭyādīti || 10.26;5

____________________


[pakvamūṣā]
kulālabhāṇḍarūpā yā dṛḍhā ca paripācitā |
pakvamūṣeti sā proktā poṭṭalyādivipācane || 10.27 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     pakvamūṣāmāha kulāleti || 10.27;1
*     ghaṭakapālayoḥ pṛthakpṛthaknirmāṇārthaṃ yaḥ pratirūpaḥ saḥ kulālabhāṇḍaśabdenocyate tadrūpā ityarthaḥ || 10.27;2
*     poṭalyādivipācane ratnagarbhapoṭalyādipāke ityarthaḥ || 10.27;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     trayodaśādhyāyoktaparpaṭīprabhṛtirasānāmalpasvedasādhyānāṃ pakvamūṣāmāha kulāleti || 10.27;1
*     vastramayapoṭalīva bheṣajagarbhitaṃ bheṣajamayamūṣā kapardikāśaṅkhādi bhūmau gajapuṭādinā pācayituṃ yatra pātrāntare dhriyate pākottaramācchādanasahitaṃ ca gṛhyate || 10.27;2
*     yathā rājayakṣmacikitsāyāṃ mṛgāṅkapoṭalīvidhau sa rasaḥ poṭalītyucyate || 10.27;3

____________________


[golamūṣā]
nirvaktragolakākārā puṭanadravyagarbhiṇī |
golamūṣeti sā proktā satvaradravarodhinī || 10.28 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     golamūṣāmāha nirvaktreti || 10.28;1
*     mukhavirahitagolākārā ityarthaḥ || 10.28;2
*     satvaradravarodhinī dravapadārthasrāvanivāriṇī ityarthaḥ || 10.28;3
*     madhyasthitapuṭanadravyā samyaṅniruddhānanā golākṛtimūṣā golamūṣā bodhyā || 10.28;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha golamūṣāmāha nirvaktreti || 10.28;1
*     gatvaradravyaṃ pāradarasakamanaḥśilāharitālaprabhṛti madhye dattvā kulālena yā nirmukhaiva vidhīyate etatsamā tāmramūṣā rasahṛdaye'ṣṭādaśāvabodhe'bhihitā || 10.28;2
*     ata eva tadudāharaṇamatra nopayogi || 10.28;3
*     mṛnmayodāharaṇaprayogo nopalabhyate || 10.28;4

____________________


[mahāmūṣā]
tale yā kūrparākārā kramādupari vistṛtā |
sthūlavṛntākavatsthūlā mahāmūṣetyasau smṛtā |
sā cāyo'bhrakasattvādeḥ puṭāya drāvaṇāya ca || 10.29 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     mahāmūṣāmāha tale iti || 10.29;1
*     kūrparākārā kūrparaḥ kaphoṇiḥ bhujamadhyasandhirityarthaḥ tadākārā || 10.29;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha mahāmūṣāmāha tala iti || 10.29;1
*     ayo'bhraketi || 10.29;2
*     atrāyaḥśabdena kāntaṃ grāhyam || 10.29;3
*     tasyāntaḥpītarekhāviśiṣṭasya chāgaraktena bhāvanayā śuddhasya || 10.29;4
*     piṇḍībaddhasya koṣṭhayantrodare mūṣāyāṃ laghubhastrayā dhamanādbhavati sattvanipātanam || 10.29;5

____________________


[maṇḍūkamūṣā]
maṇḍūkākārā yā nimnatāyāmavistarā |
ṣaḍaṅgulapramāṇena mūṣā maṇḍūkasaṃjñikā |
bhūmau nikhanya tāṃ mūṣāṃ dadyātpuṭamathopari || 10.30 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     maṇḍūkamūṣāmāha maṇḍūketi || 10.30;1
*     maṇḍūkaḥ bhekaḥ tadākārā || 10.30;2
*     ṣaḍaṅgulapramāṇena nimnatāyāmavistarā ṣaḍaṅgulapramāṇagabhīratādairghyavistārayuktā || 10.30;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     maṇḍūkamūṣāmāha maṇḍūketi || 10.30;1
*     tadākāramūṣāyā gāmbhīryadairghyapariṇāhāḥ ṣaḍaṅgulamitāḥ kāryāḥ || 10.30;2
*     upari mṛttikayā tāṃ caturaṅgulamitamācchādya bhūmipṛṣṭhoparītyarthaḥ || 10.30;3

____________________


[mūsala-|muśalamūṣā]
mūṣā yā cipiṭā mūle vartulāṣṭāṅgulocchrayā |
mūṣā sā mūsalākhyā syāccakribaddharase hitā || 10.31 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     muṣalamūṣāmāha mūṣeti || 10.31;1
*     vartulā mūlādūrdhvamiti bodhyam || 10.31;2
*     cakribaddharase strīrogādhikārokta auṣadhaviśeṣaḥ || 10.31;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     muśalamūṣāmāha mūṣeti || 10.31;1
*     cakribaddharaso dvāviṃśādhyāye vakṣyamāṇaḥ || 10.31;2

____________________


[koṣṭhī (generaldefinition)]
sattvānāṃ pātanārthāya pātitānāṃ viśuddhaye |
koṣṭhikā vividhākārāstāsāṃ lakṣaṇamucyate || 10.32 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     alpāgnisādhyasya dravyapākasya sādhanāya mūṣā nānāvidhā uktāḥ || 10.32;1
*     saṃprati prabhūtāgnisādhyasya dravyapākasya sādhanārthaṃ vividhāḥ koṣṭhīrvaktukāmaḥ prathamaṃ tāsāṃ prayojanamāha sattvānāmiti || 10.32;2
*     mūṣāmṛdaiva koṣṭhīrvidadhyādityabhiprāyeṇaivātra koṣṭhīnāṃ mṛdviśeṣo noktaḥ || 10.32;3
*     rasaratnākare tu mṛttikādravyāṇāṃ pramāṇamapi spaṣṭaṃ kṛtvābhihitam || 10.32;4
*     tathā ca tadgranthaḥ |
*     gārā dagdhāstuṣā dagdhā valmīkamṛttikā |
*     ajāśvānāṃ malaṃ dagdhaṃ yāvattatkṛṣṇatāṃ gatam || 10.32;5
*     pāṣāṇabhedapatrāṇi kṛṣṇā mṛcca samaṃ samam |
*     vajravallyā dravairmardyaṃ dinaṃ vā peṣayeddṛḍham || 10.32;6
*     tena koṣṭhīrvaṅkanālā vajramūṣāśca kārayet |
*     mṛnmayakośasya koṣṭhīti nāma prasiddham || 10.32;7
*     sā ca dvividhā || 10.32;8
*     bhūmikoṣṭhī calatkoṣṭhī ca || 10.32;9
*     yatkoṣṭhīyantraṃ prāguditaṃ sā calatkoṣṭhīti nigadyate || 10.32;10
*     idānīmucyamānāstu sarvā bhūmikoṣṭhyaḥ || 10.32;11
*     tāśca dvividhāḥ || 10.32;12
*     prakāśakoṣṭhī sapidhānakoṣṭhī ceti bhedena || 10.32;13

____________________


[aṅgārakoṣṭhī]
rājahastasamutsedhā tadardhāyāmavistarā |
caturasrā ca kuḍyena veṣṭitā mṛnmayena ca || 10.33 ||
ekabhittau careddvāraṃ vitastyābhogasaṃyutam |
dvāraṃ sārdhavitastyā ca saṃmitaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ śubham || 10.34 ||
dehalyadho vidhātavyaṃ dhamanāya yathocitam |
prādeśapramitā bhittiruttaraṅgasya cordhvataḥ || 10.35 ||
dvāraṃ copari kartavyaṃ prādeśapramitaṃ khalu |
tataśceṣṭikayā ruddhvā dvārasaṃdhiṃ vilipya ca || 10.36 ||
śikhitraistāṃ samāpūrya dhamedbhastrādvayena ca |
śikhitrān dhamanadravyamūrdhvadvāreṇa nikṣipet || 10.37 ||
sattvapātanagolāṃśca pañca pañca punaḥ punaḥ |
bhavedaṅgārakoṣṭhīyaṃ kharāṇāṃ sattvapātinī || 10.38 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     aṅgārakoṣṭhikālakṣaṇamāha rājahasteti || 10.38;1
*     rājahastasamutsedhā hastadvayonnatā || 10.38;2
*     rājahastasya vistṛtavyākhyā gajapuṭavyākhyāyāṃ draṣṭavyā || 10.38;3
*     caturasrā catuṣkoṇā || 10.38;4
*     vitastyābhogasaṃyutaṃ dvādaśāṅgulavistṛtam || 10.38;5
*     dvāraṃ sārdhavitastyā ca ityasya dehalyadhaḥ ityanena saṃbandhaḥ || 10.38;6
*     dehalī dvāranimne piṇḍikā || 10.38;7
*     prādeśapramitā aṅguṣṭhasya pradeśinyā vyāsaḥ prādeśa ucyate ityuktalakṣaṇā tarjanīsahitavistṛtāṅguṣṭhapramāṇā || 10.38;8
*     uttaraṅgasya dvārordhvasthadāruṇaḥ || 10.38;9
*     hastadvayotsedhaṃ hastamitāyāmavistāraṃ catuṣkoṇaṃ samantātmṛnmayabhittiveṣṭitaṃ ca cullyākāramekaṃ mārttikaṃ yantraṃ kṛtvā tasya ekabhittau vitastivistaraṃ dvāraṃ dvārapiṇḍikādhaḥ aṣṭādaśāṅgulamānaṃ dvārāntaraṃ ca kuryāt || 10.38;10
*     dvārordhvabhāge aṅguṣṭhatarjanyormadhyavatvistṛtāṃ bhittiṃ sthāpayitvā tadupari tadvadvistṛtaṃ dvāramanyatvidadhyāttataḥ iṣṭakayā dvārasandhiṃ ruddhvā ālipya ca koṣṭhīmaṅgāraiḥ paripūrya dvābhyāṃ bhastrābhyāṃ dhamet || 10.38;11
*     bhasmībhūte ca aṅgāre punarapi aṅgāraṃ sattvapātanagolādikaṃ ca pañca pañca kṛtvā ūrdhvadvāreṇa punaḥ punaḥ nikṣipet || 10.38;12
*     iyamaṅgārakoṣṭhī kharadravyāṇāṃ sattvapātane śubhā jñeyā || 10.38;13



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     prathamaṃ sapidhānakoṣṭhīmāha rājahasteti || 10.38;1
*     sapādahastastriṃśadaṅgulātmakastanmita utsedha ucchrayo yasyāstathoktā || 10.38;2
*     tadardhamātrau dairghyavistārau yasyāstathoktā || 10.38;3
*     tathā caturasrā catuṣkoṇā sarvato dārḍhyāya mṛnmayena kuḍyena bhittyā bahirveṣṭitā kāryā || 10.38;4
*     tena catasṛṣu dikṣu catasro bhittayaḥ sambhavanti || 10.38;5
*     ekabhittāviti sāmānyata uktamapi yogyatayā pūrvabhittau paścimabhittau veti viśeṣārthe paryavasyati || 10.38;6
*     tasyāṃ diśi sthitāyāṃ bhittau dvāraṃ vitastyā saṃmitaṃ kāryam || 10.38;7
*     yadvitastimitaṃ dīrghaṃ sūtraṃ tadvartulaṃ nidhāyāntaravakāśo yāvān sambhavati tanmitaṃ vartulaṃ caturaṅgulamitamityarthaḥ || 10.38;8
*     antaḥsthitajvālāyāḥ sattvanirgamakālabodhikāyāḥ parīkṣārthaṃ caretkuryāt || 10.38;9
*     tadarthaṃ prakāśa āvaśyakaḥ || 10.38;10
*     sa tu prācyāṃ pratīcyāṃ vādhikyena labhyata iti prakāśayogyatvādviśiṣṭā digevātra gṛhītā || 10.38;11
*     taddvārasyādhobhāgo dehalī dvāradehasaṃrakṣikā sā dvitryaṅgulamitā kāryā || 10.38;12
*     tādṛgdehalyā adhobhāge bhūtalaṃ saṃlagnaṃ pūrvavadeva sārdhavitastimitasūtreṇa paricchinnamarthātṣaḍaṅgulaṃ vartulaṃ dvāraṃ bhastrāyojanāya kāryam || 10.38;13
*     atra vitastidīrghā tāvadbhittireva sārdhavitastidīrghā ca dvāradvayārthaṃ paryāptā na bhavati || 10.38;14
*     ata uktaṃ sūtramitaṃ vartulaṃ ceti || 10.38;15
*     kecittu kramanimnabhūminikhātatiryaggartābhāgo'pi sārdhavitastimānārthaṃ saṃgrāhya iti noktasūtradairghyamānāpekṣā lohakārāṇāṃ tathaiva saṃpradāyo'pi saṃprati dhmānavidhau dṛśyata iti vadanti || 10.38;16
*     tattu nocitam || 10.38;17
*     tādṛśagartābhāgasya pātālakoṣṭhikāyāṃ vakṣyamāṇatvāt || 10.38;18
*     ūrdhvabhāge koṣṭhikāyā uttarāṅgasyordhvāṅgasya ca kartavyā yā bhittiḥ sā caturvidhāpi prādeśapramitā daśāṅgulamitaivārthācchikhākāravatsaṃkucitā kāryā || 10.38;19
*     śikhāsthāna ūrdhvaṃ dvāraṃ tu prādeśamitāyāmavistārameva kāryam || 10.38;20
*     tatastaddvāraṃ ceṣṭikayā ruddhvā prāguktavitastimitaṃ dvāraṃ ca kokilaiḥ samāpūrya dhamet || 10.38;21
*     kiṃ kṛtvā ruddhvā dhamettadāha prathamaṃ śikhitrān kokilān dhmānārhamabhrakādidravaṃ cordhvadvāreṇa krameṇa nikṣipet || 10.38;22
*     kiṃ taddhmānadravyaṃ piṇḍīkṛtaṃ bhāgaśaḥ sakṛdeva vā nikṣipedityākāṅkṣāyāmāha sattvapātanagolāniti || 10.38;23
*     punaḥ punaḥ pratiprakṣepakālāvasaraṃ saṃtataṃ dhmātvā yadāṅgārāḥ kārśyaṃ prāpnuyuḥ śvetabhasmāvṛtāśca bhaveyustadā punaḥ kokilān dattvā punardhamanaṃ kāryam || 10.38;24
*     evaṃ bhastrādvayena praharaparyantaṃ saṃtataṃ dhmānena prāyaḥ kaṭhinadravyāṇāṃ sattvanirgamanakālaḥ samupajāyate || 10.38;25
*     paraṃ tu nāyaṃ kālaḥ sattvanirgamanajñāpakaḥ svātantreṇa kiṃtu śuklā vahnijvālaiva jñāyamānā || 10.38;26
*     uktaṃ hi paribhāṣādhyāye |
*     yadā hutāśo dīptārciḥ śuklotthānasamanvitaḥ |
*     śuddhāvartastadā jñeyaḥ sa kālaḥ sattvanirgame |
*     iti || 10.38;27
*     tadā sphuṭipiṇḍīrbahirniṣkāsya kiṭṭaṃ pṛthakkṛtya gurumārgeṇa sattvamātraṃ grāhyam || 10.38;28
*     atha dvitīyaḥ sattvapiṇḍānāṃ prakṣepakālaḥ prāpnotīti bodhyam || 10.38;29
*     atha mṛdudravyāṇāṃ sattvapātārthamalpadhmānāpekṣā || 10.38;30

____________________


[pātālakoṣṭhī]
dṛḍhabhūmau caredgartaṃ vitastyā saṃmitaṃ śubham |
vartulaṃ cātha tanmadhye gartamanyaṃ prakalpayet || 10.39 ||
caturaṅgulavistāranimnatvena samanvitam |
gartāddharaṇiparyantaṃ tiryaṅnālasamanvitam || 10.40 ||
kiṃcitsamunnataṃ bāhyagartābhimukhanimnagam |
mṛccakrīṃ pañcarandhrāḍhyāṃ garbhagartodare kṣipet || 10.41 ||
āpūrya kokilaiḥ koṣṭhīṃ pradhamedekabhastrayā |
pātālakoṣṭhikā hyeṣā mṛdūnāṃ sattvapātinī |
dhmānasādhyapadārthānāṃ nandinā parikīrtitā || 10.42 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     pātālakoṣṭhikāmāha dṛḍhabhūmāviti || 10.42;1
*     kaṭhinamṛttikāyāṃ vitastimānaṃ vartulaṃ gartamekaṃ kṛtvā tanmadhye caturaṅgulavistāraṃ caturaṅgulagabhīraṃ gartādbhūpṛṣṭhaparyantayāyivakrākṛtinālasaṃyuktamīṣaducchritaṃ ca gartamanyaṃ kuryāt || 10.42;2
*     tataḥ abhyantaragartamadhye pañcarandhrasaṃyuktāṃ mṛccakrīṃ sthāpayitvā aṅgāraiḥ koṣṭhīṃ paripūrya ca ekabhastrayā pradhamet || 10.42;3
*     mṛdudravyāṇāṃ sattvapātanārthamiyaṃ pātālakoṣṭhīti jñeyamiti niṣkarṣaḥ || 10.42;4



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atastadupayogikoṣṭhīviśeṣamāha dṛḍhabhūmāviti || 10.42;1
*     tatra dvādaśāṅgulaṃ gartaṃ vidhāya tattalamadhye caturaṅgulagāmbhīryavistāramanyaṃ vartulaṃ gartaṃ kṛtvā garbhagartatalamārabhya pṛṣṭhabhāgaparyantaṃ bāhyagartābhimukhaṃ tatsallagnaṃ kiṃcitsamunnataṃ tiryaṅnālasamanvitaṃ dvāraṃ vidhāya garbhagartopari mṛccakrīṃ pañcarandhraviśiṣṭāṃ vāyorūrdhvagamanārthaṃ kṣipet || 10.42;2
*     taduparyaṅgārāṃstadupari sattvapātanagolāṃśca nikṣipya sarvāṃ koṣṭhīṃ kokilaiḥ pūrayitvaikabhastrayā dhamet || 10.42;3
*     eṣā pātālakoṣṭhiketyucyate || 10.42;4

____________________


[gārakoṣṭhī]
dvādaśāṅgulanimnā yā prādeśapramitā tathā |
caturaṅgulataścordhvaṃ valayena samanvitā || 10.43 ||
bhūricchidravatīṃ cakrīṃ valayopari nikṣipet |
śikhitrāṃstatra nikṣipya pradhamedvaṅkanālataḥ |
gārakoṣṭhīyamākhyātā mṛṣṭalohavināśinī || 10.44 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     adhodhmānaviśiṣṭāṃ koṣṭhīmuktordhvadhamanaviśiṣṭāṃ koṣṭhīmāha dvādaśeti || 10.44;1
*     prādeśāyāmavistārā koṣṭhī bhūtalopari kāryā || 10.44;2
*     sā ca talabhāgamārabhyopari caturaṅgulabhāgaṃ vihāya tadupari valayena kaṭakena samanvitāṃ tāṃ kṛtvā valayopari prabhūtacchidrayuktāṃ cakrīṃ nikṣipya tatra kokilāṃśca dattvā vaṅkanālataḥ pradhamet || 10.44;3
*     mṛttikāpekṣayā adhikagārabhāgamiśritamṛdupādāneyaṃ koṣṭhī gārakoṣṭhītyucyate || 10.44;4
*     iyaṃ kāṃsyarītipramukhaṃ yatsṛṣṭalohaṃ tanmadhye saṃsargaghaṭakayormadhya ekasyāvaśeṣakarī dvitīyaṃ vināśyetyarthaḥ || 10.44;5



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     gārakoṣṭhīmāha dvādaśeti || 10.44;1
*     dvādaśāṅgulagabhīrā prādeśapramāṇavistāraviśiṣṭā koṣṭhī ekā kāryā tasyā ūrdhvaṃ caturaṅgule valayākāramālavālamekaṃ dattvā tadupari bahucchidrāṃ cakrīṃ nidhāya aṅgāraṃ nikṣipya ca vaṅkanālena pradhametiti || 10.44;2
*     mṛṣṭalohavināśinī śodhitalauhamāriṇī ityarthaḥ || 10.44;3

____________________


[vaṅkanāla]
mūṣāmṛdbhirvidhātavyamaratnipramitaṃ dṛḍham |
adhomukhaṃ ca tadvaktre nālaṃ pañcāṅgulaṃ khalu |
vaṅkanālamidaṃ proktaṃ dṛḍhadhmānāya kīrtitam || 10.45 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     gārakoṣṭhikāyāṃ vaṅkanālamityuktaṃ kiṃ tatvaṅkanālamityāśaṃsāyāmāha mūṣāmṛdbhiriti || 10.45;1
*     śarkarādīnāmanyatamābhiḥ mūṣopayogimṛdbhiḥ aratnipramitaṃ dṛḍhaṃ nālaṃ kṛtvā tanmukhe pañcāṅgulapramāṇamadhomukhaṃ nālamekaṃ yojayetdṛḍhadhmānāya tadvaṅkanālaṃ veditavyam || 10.45;2

____________________


[mūṣākoṣṭhī|tiryakpradhamanakoṣṭhī]
koṣṭhī siddharasādīnāṃ vidhānāya vidhīyate |
dvādaśāṅgulakotsedhā sā budhne caturaṅgulā |
tiryakpradhamanāsyā ca mṛdudravyaviśodhinī || 10.46 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha siddharasānāṃ khoṭabaddhādīnāmabhrakādisattvānāṃ ca kācaṭaṅkaṇasauvīrādinā śodhayituṃ sādhanabhūtāṃ koṣṭhīmāha dvādaśāṅguleti || 10.46;1
*     asyāṃ koṣṭhyāṃ sthāpitamūṣāmadhye siddharasādi prakṣipya saṃtatadhmānāvasare'ntarāntarā muhuḥ kācacūrṇādi dattvā dhamet || 10.46;2
*     mṛdudravyaṃ sattvādi || 10.46;3
*     iyaṃ koṣṭhī budhnabhāgamārabhya mukhabhāgaparyantaṃ kramavistṛtā prādeśapramitavartulamukhī kāryetyanuktamapi bodhyam || 10.46;4
*     pūrvasyāstathāmānamukhasyoktatvāt || 10.46;5

____________________


[puṭa]
rasādidravyapākānāṃ pramāṇajñāpanaṃ puṭam |
neṣṭo nyūnādhikaḥ pākaḥ supākaṃ hitamauṣadham || 10.47 ||
lohāderapunarbhāvo guṇādhikyaṃ tato'gratā |
anapsu majjanaṃ rekhāpūrṇatā puṭato bhavet || 10.48 ||
puṭādgrāvṇo laghutvaṃ ca śīghravyāptiśca dīpanam |
jāritādapi sūtendrāllohānāmadhiko guṇaḥ || 10.49 ||
yathāśmani viśedvahnirbahiḥsthapuṭayogataḥ |
cūrṇatvāddhi guṇāvāptistathā loheṣu niścitam || 10.50 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     saṃprati mūṣākoṣṭhyapekṣyādhikatarāgnijanakapuṭān vaktuṃ teṣāṃ prayojanamāha raseti || 10.50;1
*     rasalohaprabhṛtikaṭhoradravyāṇāṃ samyakpāko bheṣajārthamapekṣyate || 10.50;2
*     sa ca pākaḥ kriyārūpastulayā parimāṇena vā paricchettuṃ na śakyate || 10.50;3
*     supākasya pramāṇabodhakaṃ puṭamevāgnidīpakopalatuṣagorvarasaṃpūryagartādiviśeṣa eva sambhavati || 10.50;4
*     etadgartāpūritopalāgninaikaḥ supāko'bhavaditi tatpuṭaviśeṣāvṛttiḥ kāryā || 10.50;5
*     yathoktaguṇalābhaparyantamiti bhāvaḥ || 10.50;6
*     nyūnapākena gandhavarṇarasasparśaḥ samyaṅna jāyate || 10.50;7
*     adhipākena tu dravyaṃ niḥsāraṃ dagdhaṃ bhavatyodanavat || 10.50;8
*     atha puṭasya yogato guṇāṃllohasthānāha lohāderiti || 10.50;9
*     apunarbhāvo na prākṛtasvarūpāpādanaṃ pañcamitrasaṃskāreṇāpi || 10.50;10
*     guṇādhikyamāmāvasthāpanne tasmin bhakṣite jāṭharāgnyapākena rogavārakā guṇāstathā puṣṭyādayo mārdavādayaścānudbhūtāsteṣāmudbhavenādhikyābhāsaḥ || 10.50;11
*     tena cāgratā śreṣṭhatādhikamūlyatā || 10.50;12
*     tathāpsu prakṣiptasya tasya na majjanamuparitaraṇam || 10.50;13
*     aṅgulibhyāṃ pīḍitasya tasya sūkṣmāṅgulirekhāsu praviṣṭatvaṃ ca || 10.50;14
*     abhrakavajraharitālādirūpapāṣāṇaviśeṣeṣu tu laghutvādayo guṇāḥ puṭātprādurbhavanti || 10.50;15
*     kiṃ bahunā guṇavarṇanena || 10.50;16
*     yatra guṇāḥ sarve sambhūyotkaṭā nivasanti tasmātpāradādapi jīrṇarasoparasamaṇilohātsusiddhānāṃ lohānāṃ guṇā yathāvidhisevino narasya śarīre'dhikā eva || 10.50;17
*     bhūriphalayuto'pi siddharasasya hi krāmaṇārthaṃ kiṃcitsphuṭitayauvanā kāminī saṃnihitāpekṣyate bhāṣaṇacumbanāliṅganārthaṃ tatstanābhyāmaṅgamardanārthaṃ ca || 10.50;18
*     tadasaṃnidhau sarvaśarīre krāmaṇābhāvena na manmathaḥ || 10.50;19
*     sāṃnidhye tu pramādena maithunam || 10.50;20
*     tataśca māraṇamāpadyate || 10.50;21
*     kiṃca rasasevino varṣaparyantaṃ parihāraviśeṣaḥ || 10.50;22
*     ādau kṣetrīkaraṇaṃ cāvaśyakam || 10.50;23
*     pāradasiddhāvanubhūtasarvakriyāsādhakaḥ prabhūtadravyādisaṃpaccāpekṣyate || 10.50;24
*     ata eva nātinirapāyasya lohasya guṇānāmādhikyamupacaryate || 10.50;25
*     nanu pāṣāṇato'pi kaṭhināṇāṃ lohaviśeṣāṇāṃ nāvayavaviśleṣa iti kathamuktalābha ityāśaṅkya nidarśanena punarlohaguṇān dṛḍhīkaroti yathāśmanīti || 10.50;26
*     antaragnipraveśena sarvaṃ sambhavatīti bhāvaḥ || 10.50;27

____________________


[mahāpuṭa]
nimnavistarataḥ kuṇḍe dvihaste caturasrake |
vanotpalasahasreṇa pūrite puṭanauṣadham || 10.51 ||
krauñcyāṃ ruddhaṃ prayatnena piṣṭikopari nikṣipet |
vanotpalasahasrārdhaṃ krauñcikopari vinyaset |
vahniṃ prajvālayettatra mahāpuṭamidaṃ smṛtam || 10.52 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     saṃprati gartāviśeṣaṃ mahāpuṭamāha bhūmimadhya iṣṭikādibhiḥ kṛte kuḍye kuḍyamaye garte gāmbhīryavistārābhyāṃ dvihaste tathā catuṣkoṇe tādṛggarte vanyacchagaṇaiḥ sahasrasaṃkhyākaiḥ pūrite sati śarāvasaṃpuṭitaṃ bheṣajaṃ piṣṭikopari pūritacchagaṇopari sthāpayet || 10.52;1
*     tādṛśamūṣikoparyardhasahasraṃ chagaṇānāṃ pūrayet || 10.52;2
*     tato dīpayet || 10.52;3

____________________


[gajapuṭa]
rājahastapramāṇena caturasraṃ ca nimnakam |
pūrṇaṃ copalasāṭhībhiḥ kaṇṭhāvadhyatha vinyaset || 10.53 ||
vinyasetkumudīṃ tatra puṭanadravyapūritām |
pūrṇacchagaṇato'rdhāni giriṇḍāni vinikṣipet |
etadgajapuṭaṃ proktaṃ mahāguṇavidhāyakam || 10.54 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha gajapuṭamāha rājahasteti || 10.54;1
*     rājahastastriṃśadaṅgulimitaḥ || 10.54;2
*     tanmitaṃ nimnaṃ gambhīraṃ tanmitavistāraṃ ca gartaṃ bodhyam || 10.54;3
*     nimnamityatra vistāraśabdo luptanirdiṣṭaḥ || 10.54;4
*     tadupalaśāṭhībhiḥ kaṇṭhaparyantaṃ pūrayet || 10.54;5
*     upalāḥ kaṭhinā mahānto vanyacchagaṇāstato'lpadehāḥ śāṭhyaḥ || 10.54;6
*     tatra mūṣāṃ bheṣajagarbhitāṃ vinyasya pūrvavinyastacchagaṇato'rdhamānāni giriṇḍāni vanyacchagaṇāni vinikṣipetityetadgajapuṭākhyaṃ matam || 10.54;7

____________________


[vārāhapuṭa]
itthaṃ cāratnike kuṇḍe puṭaṃ vārāhamucyate || 10.55 ||

[kukkuṭapuṭa]
puṭaṃ bhūmitale tattadvitastidvitayocchrayam |
tāvacca talavistīrṇaṃ tatsyātkukkuṭakaṃ puṭam || 10.56 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha kukkuṭapuṭamāha || 10.56;1
*     bhūmipṛṣṭhabhāge kuḍyena nirmitaṃ caturviṃśatyaṅgulocchrāyaṃ tāvanmānameva tale mukhe ca vistṛtametādṛśaṃ yadgartaṃ bheṣajaṃ paktumupalādibhiḥ pūryate tatkukkuṭapuṭasaṃjñaṃ bhavati || 10.56;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     kukkuṭapuṭamāha puṭamiti || 10.56;1
*     vitastidvitayocchrayaṃ hastapramāṇonnatam || 10.56;2
*     tāvacca talavistīrṇamadhobhāge'pi hastapramāṇavistṛtam || 10.56;3
*     etattu bhūmerupari eva kāryaṃ na tu gartaṃ khanitvā || 10.56;4

____________________


[kapotapuṭa]
yatpuṭaṃ dīyate bhūmāvaṣṭasaṃkhyairvanopalaiḥ |
baddhvā sūtārkabhasmārthaṃ kapotapuṭamucyate || 10.57 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     yacca gartaṃ bhūmitale mṛdādibhiḥ kṛtamaṣṭasaṃkhyairvanopalairdīyate jvālayā prajvalitaṃ kriyate baddhapāradasya bhasmakaraṇārthaṃ tatkapotapuṭamucyate || 10.57;1



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     baddhvā mūṣāyāṃ ruddhvetyarthaḥ || 10.57;1
*     sūtārkabhasmārthaṃ rasabhasmārthaṃ tāmrabhasmārthaṃ ca || 10.57;2

____________________


[govara]
goṣṭhāntargokṣurakṣuṇṇaṃ śuṣkaṃ cūrṇitagomayam |
govaraṃ tatsamādiṣṭaṃ variṣṭhaṃ rasasādhane || 10.58 ||

[govarapuṭa]
govarairvā tuṣairvāpi puṭaṃ yatra pradīyate |
tadgovarapuṭaṃ proktaṃ rasabhasmaprasiddhaye || 10.59 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     gorvaraistuṣairvā yatra gajapuṭagartāyāṃ puṭaṃ dīyate bheṣajaṃ pakvaṃ kriyate tadgorvarapuṭamucyate || 10.59;1

____________________


[bhāṇḍapuṭa]
sthūlabhāṇḍe tuṣāpūrṇe madhye mūṣāsamanvite |
vahninā vihite pāke tadbhāṇḍapuṭamucyate || 10.60 ||

[vālukāpuṭa]
adhastādupariṣṭācca krauñcikācchādyate khalu |
vālukābhiḥ prataptābhiryatra tadvālukāpuṭam || 10.61 ||

[bhūdharapuṭa]
vahnimitrāḥ kṣitau samyaṅnikhanyāddvyaṅgulādadhaḥ |
upariṣṭātpuṭaṃ yatra puṭaṃ tadbhūdharāhvayam || 10.62 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     vahnimitrāḥ mūṣāḥ dīrghakālaṃ vahninā sahāvasthānātasyā vahnimitratvaṃ jñātavyam || 10.62;1
*     spaṣṭamanyat || 10.62;2




*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha bhūdharapuṭamāha vahnimitrāmiti || 10.62;1
*     vahnimitrā mūṣā śarāva iti yāvat || 10.62;2
*     jayasundarādirasavidhāvasyā upayogo bodhyaḥ || 10.62;3
