[lāvakapuṭa]
ūrdhvaṃ ṣoḍaśikāmūtraistuṣairvā govaraiḥ puṭam |
yatra tallāvakākhyaṃ syātsumṛdudravyasādhane || 10.63 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha lāvakapuṭamāha ūrdhvamiti || 10.63;1
*     śarāvākāramūṣāṃ saṃpuṭitāṃ bhūmau nidhāya tadupari ṣoḍaśavanopalamitaistuṣairgorvarairvā yatpuṭaṃ dīyate pākaḥ kriyate tallāvakamiti khyātam || 10.63;2
*     ṣoḍaśa eva ṣoḍaśikā || 10.63;3
*     sā saṃkhyā cātra vanyopalānāmeva grāhyā || 10.63;4
*     tanmātraistadvanyacchagaṇatulitairityarthaḥ || 10.63;5

 11
[weightunits]
ṣaṭtruṭyaścaikalikṣā syātṣaḍlikṣā yūkamucyate |
ṣaḍyūkāstu rajaḥsaṃjñaṃ kathitaṃ tava suvrate || 11.1 ||
ṣaḍrajaḥ sarṣapaḥ sākṣātsiddhārthaḥ sa ca kīrtitaḥ |
ṣaṭsiddhārthena deveśi yavastvekaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 11.2 ||
ṣaḍyavairekaguñjā syāttriguñjo valla ucyate |
ṣaḍbhireva tu guñjābhirmāṣa ekaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
māṣā dvādaśa tolaḥ syādaṣṭau tolāḥ palaṃ bhavet || 11.3 ||

[weightunits]
truṭiḥ syādaṇubhiḥ ṣaḍbhistairlikṣā ṣaḍbhirīritā |
tābhiḥ ṣaḍbhirbhavedyūkā ṣaḍyūkāstadrajaḥ smṛtam || 11.4 ||
ṣaḍrajaḥ sarṣapaḥ proktastaiḥ ṣaḍbhiryava īritaḥ |
ekā guñjā yavaiḥ ṣaḍbhirniṣpāvastu dviguñjakaḥ || 11.5 ||
syādguñjātritayaṃ vallo dvau vallau māṣa ucyate |
dvau māṣau dharaṇaṃ te dve śāṇaniṣkakalāḥ smṛtāḥ || 11.6 ||
niṣkadvayaṃ tu vaṭakaḥ sa ca kola itīritaḥ |
syātkolatritayaṃ tolaḥ karṣo niṣkacatuṣṭayam || 11.7 ||

[karṣa:: synonyms]
udumbaraṃ pāṇitalaṃ suvarṇaṃ kavalagrahaḥ |
akṣaṃ biḍālapadakaṃ śuktiḥ pāṇitaladvayam || 11.8 ||
śuktidvayaṃ palaṃ kecidanye śuktitrayaṃ viduḥ |

[pala:: synonyms]
tadeva kathitaṃ muṣṭiḥ prakuñco bilvamityapi || 11.9 ||
paladvayaṃ tu prasṛtaṃ taddvayaṃ kuḍavo'ñjaliḥ |
kuḍavau mānikā tau syātprastho dve mānike smṛtaḥ || 11.10 ||
prasthadvayaṃ śubhaṃ tau dvau pātrakaṃ dvayamāḍhakam |
taiścaturbhirghaṭonmānanalvanārmaṇaśūrpakāḥ || 11.11 ||
droṇasya śabdāḥ paryāyāḥ palānāṃ śatakaṃ tulā |
catvāriṃśatpalaśatatulā bhāraḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 11.12 ||
rasārṇavādiśāstrāṇi nirīkṣya kathitaṃ mayā |
rasopayogi yatkiṃciddiṅmātraṃ tatpradarśitam || 11.13 ||
adhunā rasarājasya saṃskārān sampracakṣmahe || 11.14 ||

[18 saṃskāras]
syātsvedanaṃ tadanu mardanamūrchane ca utthāpanaṃ patanarodhaniyāmanāni |
saṃdīpanaṃ gaganabhakṣaṇamānamatra saṃcāraṇā tadanu garbhagatā drutiśca || 11.15 ||
bāhyā drutiḥ sūtakajāraṇā syādgrāsastathā sāraṇakarma paścāt |
saṃkrāmaṇaṃ vedhavidhiḥ śarīre yogastathāṣṭādaśadhātra karma || 11.16 ||
na yojyo marmaṇi chinne na ca kṣārāgnidagdhayoḥ || 11.17 ||

[mercury:: propertiesduringsaṃskāras]
śuddhaḥ sa mṛdvagnisaho mūrchito vyādhināśanaḥ || 11.18 ||
niṣkampavegastīvrāgnāvāyurārogyado mṛtaḥ || 11.19 ||

[tridoṣa]
viṣaṃ vahnirmalaśceti doṣā naisargikāstrayaḥ |
rase maraṇasaṃtāpamūrchānāṃ hetavaḥ kramāt || 11.20 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     viṣamiti || 11.20;1
*     ete doṣā naisargikāḥ || 11.20;2
*     tadviyogakaraṇaṃ doṣāntarāpekṣayātidurghaṭamiti svābhāvikā ityupacaryante || 11.20;3
*     vastutastu devaprārthitamahādevena teṣāṃ saṃbandhaḥ pārade yojita iti bhāvaḥ || 11.20;4

____________________


[yaugikadoṣāḥ]
yogikau nāgavaṅgau dvau tau jāḍyādhmānakuṣṭhadau || 11.21 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     yogikau doṣāvāha yaugikāviti || 11.21;1
*     nikaṭavartināgavaṅgakhaniyogena miśraṇājjāto nāgākhya eko doṣo vaṅgākhya ekaśceti to dvau jāḍyamādhmānaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ ca kurutaḥ || 11.21;2

____________________


[aupādhikāḥ =kañcukāḥ]
aupādhikāḥ punaścānye kīrtitāḥ saptakañcukāḥ |
bhūmijā girijā vārjāste ca dve nāgavaṅgajau || 11.22 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     upādhinā saṃnihitavastusaṃbandhamātreṇa vastuni bahireva vyāpya tiṣṭhanti kiṃcitkālāvasthāyinaśca ye doṣāste aupādhikāḥ || 11.22;1
*     te ca saptavidhāḥ || 11.22;2
*     kañcukākhyayā śāstra uditāḥ || 11.22;3
*     teṣāṃ madhye bhūmijā ekaprakārāḥ || 11.22;4
*     teṣāmavāntarabhedena śāstre'nupayogānnāmato'nādṛtena bahuvacanam || 11.22;5
*     girijā ekavidhāḥ || 11.22;6
*     vārjā jalajāścānyavidhāḥ || 11.22;7
*     tathā nāgena vaṇijādibhirmiśrīkṛtena jātau doṣau dvau || 11.22;8
*     vaṅgajāvapi dvau || 11.22;9
*     ityevaṃ sapta kañcukāḥ pūrvoktāśca pañca doṣāḥ || 11.22;10
*     itthaṃ pārade dvādaśa doṣā rasajñaiḥ proktāḥ || 11.22;11

____________________


dvādaśaite rase doṣāḥ proktā rasaviśāradaiḥ || 11.23 ||

[saptakañcukanāmāni]
parpaṭī pāṭanī bhedī drāvī malakarī tathā |
andhakārī tathā dhvāṅkṣī vijñeyāḥ sapta kañcukāḥ || 11.24 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     parpaṭyādi sapta kañcukānāṃ saṃjñāḥ tatra parpaṭīsadṛśaśoṣakatvātparpaṭī parpaṭī yathā śoṣiṇī grāhiṇī ca pāradasya parpaṭyākhyakañcuko'pi naradehe tatkriyājananī vidārakatvātpāṭanī malabhedakatvādbhedinī śārīradhātūnāṃ dravatvasaṃpādanāddrāvī doṣavardhakatvātmalakarī andhatvajananādandhakārī dhvāṅkṣo yathā karkaśasvaro bhavati tathā svarapāruṣyajananāddhvāṅkṣīti jñeyam || 11.24;1



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     uktasaptakañcukānāṃ krameṇa nāmāntarāṇyāha parpaṭīti || 11.24;1
*     rasopari parpaṭī parpaṭākārā bhavati || 11.24;2
*     sā parpaṭītyucyate || 11.24;3
*     pāṭanī tvacovidāraṇakarī || 11.24;4
*     bhedī tvaci randhrakarī || 11.24;5
*     drāvī lohādidravakarī || 11.24;6
*     malakarī vātādidoṣakarī || 11.24;7
*     andhakārī kṛṣṇatvakarī || 11.24;8
*     dhvāṅkṣī kālimā || 11.24;9

____________________


[kañcukas:: medic. symptoms]
bhūmijāḥ kurvate kuṣṭhaṃ girijā jāḍyameva ca |
vārijā vātasaṃghātaṃ doṣāḍhyaṃ nāgavaṅgayoḥ || 11.25 ||
tasmātsūtavidhānārthaṃ sahāyairnipuṇairyutaḥ |
sarvopaskaramādāya rasakarma samārabhet || 11.26 ||

[saṃskāras:: defaultweights]
dve sahasre palānāṃ tu sahasraṃ śatameva vā |
aṣṭāviṃśatpalānyeva daśa pañcaikameva vā || 11.27 ||
palārdhenaiva kartavyaḥ saṃskāraḥ sūtakasya ca |
sudine śubhanakṣatre rasaśodhanamārabhet || 11.28 ||

[1. svedana]
tryūṣaṇaṃ lavaṇāsūryau citrakārdrakamūlakam |
kṣiptvā sūto muhuḥ svedyaḥ kāñjikena dinatrayam || 11.29 ||

[2. mardana]
gṛhadhūmeṣṭikācūrṇaṃ tathā dadhi guḍānvitam |
lavaṇāsurīsaṃyuktaṃ kṣiptvā sūtaṃ vimardayet || 11.30 ||
ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ tu taddravyaṃ sūtamānānniyojayet |
sūtaṃ kṣiptvā samaṃ tena dināni trīṇi mardayet || 11.31 ||
jīrṇābhrakaṃ tathā bījaṃ jīrṇasūtaṃ tathaiva ca |
nairmalyārthaṃ hi sūtasya khalle dhṛtvā vimardayet || 11.32 ||
gṛhṇāti nirmalo rāgān grāse grāse vimarditaḥ |
mardanākhyaṃ hi yatkarma tatsūtaguṇakṛdbhavet || 11.33 ||

[3. mūrchana]
gṛhakanyā malaṃ hanyāttriphalā vahnināśinī |
citramūlaṃ viṣaṃ hanti tasmādebhiḥ prayatnataḥ || 11.34 ||
miśritaṃ sūtakaṃ dravyaiḥ saptavārāṇi mūrchayet |
itthaṃ saṃmūrchitaḥ sūto doṣaśūnyaḥ prajāyate || 11.35 ||

[saṃskāra:: utthāpana]
asmādvirekātsaṃśuddho rasaḥ pātyastataḥ param |
uddhṛtaḥ kāñjikakvāthātpūtidoṣanivṛttaye || 11.36 ||

[saṃskāra:: pātana]
tāmreṇa piṣṭikāṃ kṛtvā pātayedūrdhvabhājane |
vaṅganāgau parityajya śuddho bhavati sūtakaḥ || 11.37 ||

[saṃskāra:: ūrdhva-, adhaḥpātana]
śulvena pātayetpiṣṭīṃ tridhordhvaṃ saptadhā tvadhaḥ || 11.38 ||

[saṃskāra:: adhaḥpātana]
triphalāśigruśikhibhirlavaṇāsurīsaṃyutaiḥ |
naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ rasaṃ kṛtvā lepayeccordhvabhājane |
tato dīptairadhaḥ pātamutpalaistatra kārayet || 11.39 ||

[saṃskāra:: pātana]
haridrāṅkolaśampākakumārītriphalāgnibhiḥ |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     śampākaḥ āragvadhaḥ || 11.40 b;1

____________________


taṇḍulīyakavarṣābhūhiṅgusaindhavamākṣikaiḥ || 11.40 ||
piṣṭaṃ rasaṃ salavaṇaiḥ sarpākṣyādibhireva vā |
pātayedathavā devi vraṇaghno yakṣalocanaiḥ || 11.41 ||
itthaṃ hyadhaūrdhvapātena pātito'sau yadā bhavet |
tadā rasāyane yogyo bhaveddravyaviśeṣataḥ || 11.42 ||

[saṃskāra:: adhaḥpātana]
athavā dīpakayantre nipātitaḥ sarvadoṣanirmuktaḥ || 11.43 ||

[saṃskāra:: tiryakpātana]
tiryakpātanavidhinā nipātitaḥ sūtarājastu |
ślakṣṇīkṛtamabhradalaṃ rasendrayuktaṃ tathāranālena || 11.44 ||
khalle dattvā mṛditaṃ yāvattannaṣṭapiṣṭatāmeti |
kuryāttiryakpātanapātitasūtaṃ krameṇa dṛḍhavahnim || 11.45 ||
saṃsvedyaḥ pātyo'sau na patati yāvaddṛḍhaścāgnau |
tadāsau śudhyate sūtaḥ karmakārī bhaveddhruvam || 11.46 ||
mardanairmūrchanaiḥ pātairmandaḥ śānto bhavedrasaḥ || 11.47 ||

[saṃskāra:: nirodhana]
sṛṣṭyambujairnirodhena tato mukhakaro rasaḥ |
svedanādivaśātsūto vīryaṃ prāpnotyanuttamam || 11.48 ||

[saṃskāra:: niyamana]
niyamyo'sau tataḥ samyakcapalatvanivṛttaye |
karkoṭīphaṇinetrābhyāṃ vṛścikāmbujamārkavaiḥ |
samaṃ kṛtvāranālena svedayecca dinatrayam || 11.49 ||

[saṃskāra:: niyamana]
maricairbhūkhagayuktairlavaṇāsurīśigruṭaṅkaṇopetaiḥ |
kāñjikayuktaistridinaṃ grāsārthī jāyate svedāt || 11.50 ||

[saṃskāra:: dīpana]
trikṣārasindhukhagabhūśikhiśigrurājītīkṣṇāmlavetasamukhairlavaṇoṣaṇāmlaiḥ |
nepālatāmradalaśoṣitamāranāle sāmlāsavāmlapuṭitaṃ rasadīpanaṃ tat || 11.51 ||

[saṃskāra:: dīpana]
svedayedāsavāmlena vīryatejaḥpravṛddhaye |
yathopayogaṃ svedyaḥ syānmūlikānāṃ raseṣu ca || 11.52 ||

[rasamūlikās]
sarpākṣī kṣīriṇī vandhyā matsyākṣī śaṅkhapuṣpikā |
kākajaṅghā śikhiśikhā brahmadaṇḍyākhukarṇikā || 11.53 ||
varṣābhūḥ kambukī dūrvā sairyakotpalaśimbikāḥ |
śatāvarī vajralatā vajrakandāgnikarṇikā || 11.54 ||
śvetārkaśigrudhattūramṛgadūrvārasāṅkuśāḥ |
rambhā raktābhanirguṇḍī lajjāluḥ suradālikā || 11.55 ||
maṇḍūkaparṇī pātālī citrakaṃ grīṣmasundarā |
kākamācī mahārāṣṭrī haridrā tilaparṇikā || 11.56 ||
jātī jayantī śrīdevī bhūkadambaḥ kusumbhakaḥ |
kośātakī nīraṃ kaṇā lāṅgalī kaṭutumbikā || 11.57 ||
cakramardo'mṛtā kandaḥ sūryāvarteṣu puṅkhikā |
vārāhī hastiśuṇḍī ca prāyeṇa rasamūlikāḥ || 11.58 ||
rasasya bhāvane svede mūṣālepe ca pūjitāḥ |
ityaṣṭau sūtasaṃskārāḥ samā dravye rasāyane |
kāryāste prathamaṃ śeṣā noktā dravyopayoginaḥ || 11.59 ||

[mercury:: bandhana:: introd.]
pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyākān rasabandhān pracakṣmahe |
yena yena hi cāñcalyaṃ durgrahatvaṃ ca naśyati |
rasarājasya samprokto bandhanārtho hi vārttikaiḥ || 11.60 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha prathamādhyāye mūrchitvā harati rujamitipadyoktā viṣayāstraya evātra granthe vaktavyāḥ || 11.60;1
*     mūrchanaṃ bandhanaṃ māraṇaṃ ca || 11.60;2
*     tatra mūrchanaṃ tu mardanapūrvakaṃ puṭena prāguktameva || 11.60;3
*     yantramūrchanaṃ tvasminnadhyāye dehopayogisarvabandhottaraṃ vakṣyāmi || 11.60;4
*     dvitīyo viṣayo'tra bandharūpo varṇyate || 11.60;5
*     pañcaviṃśatīti || 11.60;6
*     nanu granthāntare pāṭakhoṭajalūkābhasmeti bandhaścaturvidha evākhyātaḥ || 11.60;7
*     atra tu pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyāka iti pratijānāti || 11.60;8
*     etadviruddhamiva bhātītyāśaṅkya cāñcalyadurgrahatvābhāvarūpasāmānyasya sarvabandheṣu sattve'pi bandhajanakakāraṇabhedānurodhena bandhabhedo'pi vārtikairādṛta ityāha yena yeneti || 11.60;9

____________________


[bandhana:: subtypes]
haṭhāroṭau tathābhāsaḥ kriyāhīnaśca piṣṭikā |
kṣāraḥ khoṭaśca poṭaśca kalkabandhaśca kajjaliḥ || 11.61 ||
sajīvaścaiva nirjīvo nirbījaśca sabījakaḥ |
śṛṅkhalādrutibandhau ca bālakaśca kumārakaḥ || 11.62 ||
taruṇaśca tathā vṛddho mūrtibaddhastathāparaḥ |
jalabandho'gnibandhaśca susaṃskṛtakṛtābhidhaḥ |
mahābandhābhidhaśceti pañcaviṃśatirīritāḥ || 11.63 ||
kecidvadanti ṣaḍviṃśo jalūkābandhasaṃjñakaḥ |
sa tāvanneṣyate dehe strīṇāṃ drāve'tiśasyate || 11.64 ||

[bandhana:: haṭha]
haṭho rasaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ samyakśuddhivivarjitaḥ |
sa sevito nṛṇāṃ kuryānmṛtyuṃ vā vyādhimuddhatam || 11.65 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     pāradamāpaṇādānīya nimbūrasena saṃmardya gālayitvā mayūratutthādisamabhāgaṃ caturthāṃśaṃ vā tatra dattvā mardanena baddho rasaḥ kriyate || 11.65;1
*     sa sevitaścenmṛtyuṃ kuryādvyādhiṃ vā || 11.65;2

____________________


[bandhana:: āroṭa]
suśodhito rasaḥ samyagāroṭa iti kathyate |
sa kṣetrīkaraṇe śreṣṭhaḥ śanairvyādhivināśanaḥ || 11.66 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     āroṭalakṣaṇamāha suśodhita iti || 11.66;1
*     trividhapātanena śuddhaṃ paścācchuddhaṃ cūrṇīkṛtamabhrakadalaṃ samabhāgaṃ dattvā kāñjikena mardanapūrvakaṃ pāradaṃ naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ kṛtvordhvādhastiryakpātanenāsakṛtkṛtenāgnisahaḥ pārada āroṭa iti nigadyate || 11.66;2
*     taṃ ca mākṣīkaśilājatulohacūrṇapathyākṣaviḍaṅgaghṛtamadhubhiḥ saṃyutaṃ kṛtvā kṣetrīkaraṇāya yuñjīteti rasahṛdaye || 11.66;3
*     mākṣīkasahito naṣṭapiṣṭirūpaḥ pātanenāgnisthāyyapi pārado'pi āroṭa iti kathyate || 11.66;4

____________________


[ābhāsabandha]
puṭito yo raso yāti yogaṃ muktvā svabhāvatām |
bhāvito dhātumūlādyairābhāso guṇavaikṛteḥ || 11.67 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     ābhāsabandhamāha puṭita iti || 11.67;1
*     dhātumūlādyaiḥ prāguktasvarṇādidhātubhistathā sarpākṣyādimūlikābhiḥ bhāvitaḥ puṭitaśca rasaḥ guṇavaikṛteḥ dravyāntarasaṃyogena svābhāvikaguṇaviparyayātsvabhāvatāṃ svābhāvikaguṇādikaṃ muktvā yogaṃ yogavāhitāṃ yāti sa ābhāsaḥ kathyate iti śeṣaḥ || 11.67;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     ābhāsalakṣaṇamāha yaḥ pārado dhātubhirmanaḥśilāgandhakādibhistathā mūlikādyaiḥ sarpākṣyādimūlikābhiḥ patrapuṣpādibhiśca saha bhāvito dravaṃ dattvā marditastato bhūdharayantre puṭena puṭito bhasmīkṛtaḥ svabhāvataḥ svabhāvaṃ cāñcalyadurgrahatvādi muktvā dhātvādiyogaṃ yāti tattadroganāśakayogaguṇaṃ ca yāti guṇaprado bhavati || 11.67;1
*     alpakālaparyantaṃ tataḥ pathyasevino'pi narasya guṇavaikṛte sati guṇavikriyāyāṃ satyāṃ sa baddhapārada ābhāsa iti kīrtitaḥ || 11.67;2
*     guṇābhāsakaratvāt || 11.67;3
*     vikṛtireva vaikṛtam || 11.67;4

____________________


[kriyāhīna]
asaṃśodhitalohādyaiḥ sādhito yo rasottamaḥ |
kriyāhīnaḥ sa vijñeyo vikriyāṃ yātyapathyataḥ || 11.68 ||

[piṣṭikābandha]
tīvrātape gāḍhatarāvamardātpiṣṭī bhavetsā navanītarūpā |
sa rasaḥ piṣṭikābandho dīpanaḥ pācanastarām || 11.69 ||

[kṣārabandha]
śaṅkhaśuktivarāṭādyairyo'sau saṃsādhito rasaḥ |
kṣārabandhaḥ paraṃ dīptipuṣṭikṛcchūlanāśanaḥ || 11.70 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     śaṅkhaśuktīti || 11.70;1
*     bhasmīkṛtānāṃ śaṅkhaśuktyādīnāṃ vṛkṣakṣārādīnāṃ ca mūṣāṃ kṛtvā tatsaṃpuṭe pāradaṃ prakṣipya saṃpuṭitaḥ pārado laghupuṭadānena bhasmībhavatīti kṣārabandho'sāvagnidīptyādikṛdbhavet || 11.70;2



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     kṣārabandhamāha śaṅkheti || 11.70;1
*     saṃsādhitaḥ mardanapuṭanādibhiḥ susampāditaḥ || 11.70;2
*     spaṣṭamanyat || 11.70;3

____________________


[khoṭabandha]
bandho yaḥ khoṭatāṃ yāti dhmāto dhmātaḥ kṣayaṃ vrajet |
khoṭabandhaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ śīghraṃ sarvagadāpahaḥ || 11.71 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     khoṭabandhamāha bandha iti || 11.71;1
*     khoṭatāṃ vahnistho'pi anuḍḍayanasvabhāvatāṃ yaduktam |
*     agnimadhye yadā tiṣṭhetkhoṭabaddhasya lakṣaṇam |
*     iti || 11.71;2
*     dhmāto dhmātaḥ bhastrayā punaḥ punarādhmāpita ityarthaḥ kṣayaṃ vrajetdravyāntareṇa saha ekībhāvātadarśanatāṃ gacchedityarthaḥ || 11.71;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha khoṭabandhamāha bandha iti || 11.71;1
*     khoṭakaraṇaprakārastu rasasāra uvāca |
*     bandhane yāśca vikhyātā aṣṭāveva mahauṣadhīḥ |
*     tābhiṣṭaṅkaṇayuktābhirbhāvayecca raseśvaram |
*     tābhirviliptamūṣāyāṃ dhamanātkhoṭatāṃ vrajet || 11.71;2
*     iti || 11.71;3
*     sa evātidhmānātkṣayaṃ gacchati || 11.71;4
*     sa ca khoṭabandho bodhyaḥ || 11.71;5
*     atra pārado jīrṇaṣaḍguṇagandho grāhyaḥ || 11.71;6
*     abhrakadānena piṣṭīkṛto vā grāhyaḥ || 11.71;7
*     somavallīrase piṣṭvā dāpayecca puṭatrayam |
*     somavallīrasenaiva saptavārāṃśca bhāvayet || 11.71;8
*     tadabhraṃ mṛdbhāṇḍe dadyādrasena saha saṃyutam |
*     mūlaṃ tu śarapuṅkhāyā gavyakṣīreṇa gharṣayet || 11.71;9
*     kalkena melayetsūtaṃ gaganaṃ ca tadadhordhvagam |
*     sthāpayedravitāpe tu nirmukho grasate kṣaṇāt |
*     jāyate piṣṭikā śīghraṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 11.71;10
*     iti || 11.71;11
*     piṣṭībandhastu khoṭaka iti varṇanādatra piṣṭikārūpamucyate || 11.71;12
*     khoṭalakṣaṇaṃ granthāntare tu |
*     dhmāto druto bhavetkhoṭa āhataścūrṇatāṃ vrajet |
*     punardhmāto drutaḥ khoṭa iti khoṭasya lakṣaṇam || 11.71;13
*     ghanasattvapādajīrṇaḥ kāntapādajīrṇaḥ samatīkṣṇajīrṇaścedvāyaṃ kṣetrīkaraṇārthamapi praśastaḥ || 11.71;14

____________________


[poṭabandha]
drutakajjalikā mocāpattrake cipiṭīkṛtā |
sa poṭaḥ parpaṭī saiva bālādyakhilaroganut || 11.72 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     pāṭabandhaṃ lakṣayati druteti || 11.72;1
*     adho'gninā kaṭāhe tāpena drutā jātadravā yā kajjalī sā tatkṣaṇe kadalīdale prakṣipya taddalenācchādya pīḍanena cipiṭīkṛtā pāṭabandhaḥ parpaṭikābandhaśceti khyātā bālavṛddhādīnāmanupānabhedena sarvarogaghnī || 11.72;2

____________________


[kalkabandha]
svedādyaiḥ sādhitaḥ sūtaḥ paṅkatvaṃ samupāgataḥ |
kalkabaddhaḥ sa vijñeyo yogoktaphaladāyakaḥ || 11.73 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     yogoktaphaladāyakaḥ yasmin yoge sa prayojyaḥ tasya phalautkarṣyaprada ityarthaḥ athavā svedanamardanārthaṃ gṛhītakalkadravyāṇāmupayoge yatphalaṃ tatphalaprada ityarthaḥ || 11.73;1

____________________


[kajjalībandha]
kajjalī rasagandhotthā suślakṣṇā kajjalopamā |
tattadyogena saṃyuktā kajjalībandha ucyate || 11.74 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     kajjalībandhamāha kajjalīti || 11.74;1
*     suślakṣṇā cikkaṇā || 11.74;2
*     tattadyogena saṃyuktā rasagandhakaśodhakadravyasaṃyogena śuddhā ityarthaḥ || 11.74;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     kajjalībandhaṃ lakṣayati || 11.74;1
*     tattadyogena rogavārakaviśiṣṭauṣadhīmiśraṇamardanayogena yuktā satī kajjalībandha ucyate || 11.74;2
*     atra tattadyogena saṃyukteti na lakṣaṇaghaṭakam || 11.74;3
*     tena vinā kajjalyāḥ siddhatvāt || 11.74;4
*     kiṃtu yogavāhitveneyaṃ sarvaroganāśiketi bodhanāya taduktiḥ || 11.74;5

____________________


[sajīva]
bhasmīkṛto gacchati vahniyogādrasaḥ sajīvaḥ sa khalu pradiṣṭaḥ |
saṃsevito'sau na karoti bhasmakāryaṃ javādrogavināśanaṃ ca || 11.75 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     sajīvabaddhamāha bhasmīkṛteti || 11.75;1
*     tāmbūlīdalarasamarditaḥ paścādvandhyākarkoṭakīkandamadhye prakṣipya saṃpuṭito bahirmṛdvastrābhyāṃ saṃpuṭito bhūmau gajapuṭena puṭito bhasmībhavati || 11.75;2
*     evaṃ prakārāntareṇāpi bhasmīkṛtaḥ pārado'gnau pātre dhṛtaścedākāśe gacchati || 11.75;3
*     pakṣacchedābhāvāt || 11.75;4
*     bhasmakāryaṃ jarāmaraṇābhāvarūpaṃ sa tu na karoti || 11.75;5
*     vyādhināśamapi na || 11.75;6

____________________


[nirjīva]
jīrṇābhrako vā parijīrṇagandho bhasmīkṛtaścākhilalohamauliḥ |
nirjīvanāmā hi sa bhasmasūto niḥśeṣarogān vinihanti sadyaḥ || 11.76 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     nirjīvabandhamāha jīrṇābhraka iti || 11.76;1
*     jīrṇābhrakaḥ grāsīkṛtābhra ityarthaḥ || 11.76;2
*     parijīrṇagandhaḥ samyaggrāsitagandhaka ityarthaḥ || 11.76;3
*     akhileti || 11.76;4
*     akhilānāṃ sarveṣāṃ lohānāṃ svarṇādīnāmityarthaḥ mauliḥ śirobhūṣaṇasvarūpaḥ sarvalohopayoge ye guṇā bhavanti tebhyo'pyadhikaguṇaprada ityarthaḥ || 11.76;5
*     vahniyoge'pi nirgamanāsāmarthyātasya nirjīvatvaṃ bodhyam || 11.76;6

____________________


[nirbīja]
rasastu pādāṃśasuvarṇajīrṇaḥ piṣṭīkṛto gandhakayogataśca |
tulyāṃśagandhaiḥ puṭitaḥ krameṇa nirbījanāmā sakalāmayaghnaḥ || 11.77 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     nirbījabandhaṃ lakṣayati rasastviti || 11.77;1
*     atra suvarṇaṃ na tu bījīkṛtaṃ kiṃtu śuddhaṃ jāraṇāyantre saṃskṛtaṃ pattrarūpaṃ grāhyam || 11.77;2
*     piṣṭīkṛto dvādaśāṃśe pārada ekāṃśaṃ gandhakaṃ truṭiśo muhurdattvāmlena saṃmarditastatastulyāṃśagandhaiḥ krameṇa puṭitaḥ prathamaṃ gandhakaṃ caturthāṃśaṃ dattvā bhūdharayantre puṭitastato'rdhāṃśaṃ gandhakaṃ dattvā tathā puṭitastataḥ pādonaṃ gandhakaṃ dattvā puṭitastadūrdhvaṃ samabhāgamitaṃ dattvā puṭita iti kramaśabdārthaḥ || 11.77;3
*     gandhairiti bahuvacanamatantram || 11.77;4

____________________


[sabījabandhana]
piṣṭīkṛtairabhrakasattvahematārārkakāntaiḥ parijārito yaḥ |
hatastataḥ ṣaḍguṇagandhakena sabījabaddho vipulaprabhāvaḥ || 11.78 ||

[śṛṅkhalābandha]
vajrādinihataḥ sūto hataḥ sūtaḥ samo'paraḥ |
śṛṅkhalābaddhasūtastu dehalohavidhāyakaḥ |
citraprabhāvāṃ vegena vyāptiṃ jānāti śaṃkaraḥ || 11.79 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     vajreṇa bhasmīkṛtena cūrṇīkṛtena vā nihato mārito yaḥ sūtastanmadhye māritamanyaṃ sūtakamapi nikṣipet || 11.79;1
*     so'pi raso baddhaḥ khoṭarūpaḥ śṛṅkhalābaddha ityucyate || 11.79;2
*     etanmāraṇaprakārastu rasārṇave uktaḥ || 11.79;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     śṛṅkhalābandhamāha vajrādīti || 11.79;1
*     vajrādinihataḥ hīrakādisahayogena māritaḥ sūtaḥ tadvā samaḥ samaparimitaḥ aparaśca hataḥ prakārāntareṇa māritaḥ sūtaḥ śṛṅkhalābaddhasūtaḥ ubhayormardanāditi śeṣaḥ || 11.79;2
*     citraprabhāvāmalaukikasāmarthyāṃ vegena vyāptiṃ śṛṅkhalābandhasūtasya dehe iti śeṣaḥ || 11.79;3

____________________


[drutibandha]
yukto'pi bāhyadrutibhiśca sūto bandhaṃgato vā bhasitasvarūpaḥ |
sa rājikāpādamito nihanti duḥsādhyarogān drutibaddhanāmā || 11.80 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     atha drutibaddhamāha yukto'pīti || 11.80;1
*     bījānāṃ jāraṇena yadi raso mahāprabhāvo bhavatīti tanna citram || 11.80;2
*     rasoparasadhātūnāṃ yā bahirdrutayastābhiryukto'pi tābhirjārito'pi pārado baddhamātro bandhottaraṃ bhasmīkṛto vā drutibaddhanāmā sarṣapacaturthāṃśamātrayā sevitaścedduḥsādhyarogānnihanti || 11.80;3



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     drutibandhamāha yukta iti || 11.80;1
*     bāhyadrutibhiḥ rasasya dravatvasampādakakriyāviśeṣaiḥ yuktaḥ api bandhaṃ gataḥ bandhanatāṃ prāptaḥ vā athavā bhasitasvarūpaḥ bhasmībhūtaḥ sūtaḥ drutibaddhanāmā jñeyaḥ || 11.80;2
*     saḥ drutibaddhasūtaḥ rājikā rāī iti khyātaḥ raktavarṇasarṣapaviśeṣaḥ tasyāḥ pādamitaḥ caturthabhāgaparimitaḥ || 11.80;3

____________________


[bālabandha]
samābhrajīrṇaḥ śivajastu bālaḥ saṃsevito yogayuto javena |
rasāyano bhāvigadāpahaśca sopadravāriṣṭagadānnihanti || 11.81 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     bālākhyabaddhaṃ lakṣayati sameti || 11.81;1
*     abhraṃ kṛṣṇavajrābhrasattvam || 11.81;2
*     śivajaḥ pāradaḥ || 11.81;3
*     samābhrajīrṇaścatuḥṣaṣṭidvātriṃśatṣoḍaśādibhāgakrameṇa || 11.81;4
*     asyopayogaḥ pattralepe'pi bodhyaḥ || 11.81;5

____________________

[kumārabandha]
harodbhavo yo dviguṇābhrajīrṇaḥ sa syātkumāro mitataṇḍulo'sau |
triḥ saptarātraiḥ khalu pāparogasaṃghātaghātī ca rasāyanaṃ ca || 11.82 ||

[taruṇabandha]
caturguṇavyomakṛtāśano'sau rasāyanāgryastaruṇābhidhānaḥ |
sa saptarātrātsakalāmayaghno rasāyano vīryabalapradātā || 11.83 ||

[vṛddhabandha]
yasyābhrakaḥ ṣaḍguṇito hi jīrṇaḥ prāptāgnisakhyaḥ sa hi vṛddhanāmā |
dehe ca lohe ca niyojanīyaḥ śivādṛte ko'sya guṇān pravakti || 11.84 ||

[mūrtibandha]
yo divyamūlikābhiśca kṛto'tyagnisaho rasaḥ |
vinābhrajāraṇātsa syānmūrtibandho mahārasaḥ || 11.85 ||
ayaṃ hi jāryamāṇastu nāgninā kṣīyate rasaḥ |
yojitaḥ sarvayogeṣu niraupamyaphalapradaḥ || 11.86 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     mūrtibaddhaṃ lakṣayati ya iti || 11.86;1
*     divyamūlikāścatuḥṣaṣṭimūlikāḥ prāguktāstābhistadrasena mardanasvedanādinā pakṣacchedāt || 11.86;2
*     abhrajāraṇādvināpyatyagnisaho baddhaḥ kṛto dravyāntarānabhivyāptyā kevalaṃ dehenaiva baddho mūrtibaddha ityucyate || 11.86;3
*     tathā vaikrāntavajrasaṃsparśāt || 11.86;4
*     tena cūrṇīkṛtena saha mardanādapi raso baddho bhavatīti so'pi mūrtibaddha ityucyate || 11.86;5
*     evaṃ ca mūrtibandho dvividhaḥ sambhavati || 11.86;6

____________________


[jalabandha]
śilātoyamukhaistoyairbaddho'sau jalabandhavān |
sa jarārogamṛtyughnaḥ kalpoktaphaladāyakaḥ || 11.87 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     jalabandhaṃ lakṣayati || 11.87;1
*     śilātoyamukhaiḥ śilātoyacandratoyapramukhairjalaiḥ saha mardanādinā baddhaḥ pārado jalabaddha iti kīrtitaḥ || 11.87;2
*     kalpokteti || 11.87;3
*     pāradakalpoktaphaletyarthaḥ || 11.87;4
*     etasya jalasya vistaro rasārṇave spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ || 11.87;5
*     kṣiptaṃ jale bhavetkāṣṭhaṃ śilābhūtaṃ ca dṛśyate |
*     bahirantaśca deveśi vedhakaṃ tatprakīrtitam || 11.87;6
*     hiṅgulaṃ haritālaṃ ca gandhakaṃ ca manaḥśilā |
*     eṣāṃ gandhāpahāraṃ yatkurute tacca vedhakam || 11.87;7

____________________


[agnibandha]
kevalo yogeṣu vā dhmātaḥ syādguṭikākṛtiḥ |
akṣīṇaścāgnibaddho'sau khecaratvādikṛtsa hi || 11.88 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     agnibandhamāha kevala iti || 11.88;1
*     kevalaḥ dravyāntarāsaṃyukta ityarthaḥ || 11.88;2
*     yogayuktaḥ dravyāntarasaṃyuktaḥ || 11.88;3
*     dhmātaḥ agnau saṃtaptaḥ || 11.88;4
*     akṣīṇaḥ agnitāpe'pi yathāmātrāyāṃ sthitaḥ na tu kiṃcidapyūnaḥ || 11.88;5
*     khecaratvādikṛtmukhe dhṛtaḥ nabhogatisāmarthyapradaḥ ityarthaḥ || 11.88;6
*     uddeśagranthe jalabandho'gnibandhaśca susaṃskṛtakṛtābhidhaḥ ityanena agnibandhānantaraṃ mahābandhācca prāksusaṃskṛtakṛtākhyabandhāntarasya samullekho vidyate vivaraṇagranthe tu tadullekhādarśanātbandhaḥ saḥ lipikarapramādātpramādāntarādvā adarśanatāṃ gata iti manye || 11.88;7
*     kiṃca susaṃskṛtakṛtābhidhaḥ ityasya agnibandhasya viśeṣaṇatvamapi na yujyate tathātve bandhasya caturviṃśatisaṃkhyatvātpañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyākān rasabandhān pracakṣmahe iti pratijñāhānyāpatteriti || 11.88;8



*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     athāgnibaddhaṃ pāradamāha kevala iti || 11.88;1
*     ādau ṣaḍguṇenābhrakajāraṇenāgnisahaḥ pārado maṇijīrṇaḥ pātanāyantreṇotthāpitaḥ sa druto bhavati || 11.88;2
*     sa ca kevalastīkṣṇalohayukto vā dhmātaḥ san guṭikākṛtiścāgnāvakṣayaśca bhavati || 11.88;3
*     ata evāyamagnibaddha ityucyate || 11.88;4
*     ayaṃ mukhe dhṛtaścetpuruṣasyākāśagāmitvaśastrāgnyabhedyatvasāyujyasārūpyādimuktikaro bhavet || 11.88;5

____________________


[mūrchana]
viṣṇukrāntāśaśilatākumbhīkanakamūlakaiḥ |
viśālānāginīkandavyāghrapādīkuruṇṭakaiḥ || 11.89 ||
vṛścikālībhaśuṇḍībhyāṃ haṃsapādā sahāsuraiḥ |
aprasūtagavāṃ mūtraiḥ piṣṭaṃ vā kulake pacet || 11.90 ||
pakvamevaṃ mṛtairlohairmarditaṃ vipacedrasam |
yantreṣu mūrchā sūtānāmeṣa kalpaḥ samāsataḥ || 11.91 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     nibiḍaḥ nonnatānataḥ samānasarvāvayavaḥ ityarthaḥ || 11.91;1

____________________


[mahābandha]
hemnā vā rajatena vā sahacaro dhmāto vrajatyekatāmakṣīṇo nibiḍo guruśca guṭikākāro'tidīrghojjvalaḥ |

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayabodhinī:
*     nirbandhaḥ yathāyathamasaṃpāditabandhanakriyaḥ cetkṣaṇātdravati agnau iti śeṣaḥ || 11.92 b;1

____________________


cūrṇatvaṃ paṭuvatprayāti nihato ghṛṣṭo na muñcenmalaṃ nirgandho dravati kṣaṇātsa hi mahābandhābhidhāno rasaḥ || 11.92 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     mahābandhamāha hemnā veti || 11.92;1
*     atra hema caturthāṃśamardhāṃśaṃ vā grāhyam || 11.92;2
*     rajatamapi tathaiva || 11.92;3
*     bandhauṣadhiliptavajramūṣāmadhye tayoranyatareṇa yukto dhmāto'gnibaddha eva pārado vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇaviśiṣṭaḥ susaṃskṛtakṛtasaṃjñāṃ mahābandhasaṃjñāṃ ca labhate || 11.92;4
*     tānyeva lakṣaṇānyāha dhmāto vrajatyekatāmityādinā || 11.92;5
*     paṭuvat || 11.92;6
*     saindhavatulyaṃ cūrṇatvaṃ yāti || 11.92;7
*     nihataḥ saṃdravati || 11.92;8
*     agniyogāditi śeṣaḥ || 11.92;9

____________________


[jalūkābandha]
sūte garbhaniyojitārdhakanake pādāṃśanāge'thavā pañcāṅguṣṭhakaśālmalīkṛtamadāśleṣmātabījaistathā |
tadvattejinīkolakākhyaphalajaiścūrṇaṃ tilaṃ pattrakaṃ tapte khallatale nidhāya mṛdite jātā jalūkā varā || 11.93 ||

[jalūkābandha]
saiṣā syātkapikacchūromapaṭale candrāvatītailake candre ṭaṅkaṇakāmapippalījale svinnā bhavettejinī |
tapte khallatale vimardya vidhivadyatnādvaṭī yā kṛtā sā strīṇāṃ madadarpanāśanakarī khyātā jalūkā varā || 11.94 ||
bālye cāṣṭāṅgulā yojyā yauvane ca daśāṅgulā |
dvādaśaiva pragalbhānāṃ jalaukā trividhā matā || 11.95 ||
dhṛtvā sūtamukhe pātraṃ meṣīkṣīraṃ pradāpayet |
sthāpayedātape tīvre vāsarāṇyekaviṃśatiḥ || 11.96 ||
dvitīyātra mayā proktā jalaukā drāvaṇe hitā |
puruṣāṇāṃ sthitā mūrdhni drāvayedbījamadbhutam || 11.97 ||

[jalūkābandha]
munipattrarasaṃ caiva śālmalīvṛntavāri ca |
jātīmūlasya toyaṃ ca śiṃśapātoyasaṃyutam || 11.98 ||
śleṣmātakaphalaṃ caiva triphalācūrṇameva ca |
kokilākṣasya cūrṇaṃ ca pāradaṃ mardayedbudhaḥ || 11.99 ||
jalūkā jāyate divyā rāmājanamanoharā |
sā yojyā kāmakāle tu kāmayetkāminī svayam || 11.100 ||

[jalūkābandha]
triphalābhṛṅgamahauṣadhamadhusarpiśchāgadugdhagomūtre |
nāgaṃ saptaniṣiktaṃ samarasajāritaṃ jalūkā syāt || 11.101 ||

[jalūkābandha]
bhānusvaradinasaṃkhyāpramāṇasūtaṃ gṛhītadīnāram |
aṅkolarājavṛkṣakakanyārasataśca śodhanaṃ kuryāt || 11.102 ||
śaśirekhāvaravarṇinīsakokilākṣāpāmārgakanakānām |
cūrṇaiḥ sahaikaviṃśatidināni saṃmardayetsamyak || 11.103 ||
niśāyāḥ kāñjikaṃ yūṣaṃ dattvā yonau praveśayet |
bālamadhyamavṛddhāsu yojyā vijñāya tatkramāt |
nīrasānāmapi nṝṇāṃ yoṣā syātsaṃgamotsukā || 11.104 ||

[jalūkabandha]
rasabhāgaṃ catuṣkaṃ ca vaṅgabhāgaṃ ca pañcamam |
surasārasamaṃ yuktaṃ ṭaṅkaṇena samanvitam || 11.105 ||
tridinaṃ mardayitvā ca golakaṃ taṃ rasodbhavam |
liṅgāgre yoninikṣiptaṃ yāvadāyurvaśaṃkaram || 11.106 ||

[jalūkabandha]
karpūrasūraṇasubhṛṅgasumeghanādairnāgaṃ niṣicya tu mitho valayedrasena |
liṅgasthitena valayena nitambinīnāṃ svāmī bhavatyanudinaṃ sa tu jīvahetuḥ || 11.107 ||

[jalūkabandha]
ṭaṅkaṇapippalikābhiḥ sūraṇakarpūramātuluṅgarasaiḥ |
kṛtvā svaliṅgalepaṃ yoniṃ vidrāvayetstrīṇām || 11.108 ||

[madanavalaya]
agnyāvartitanāge harabījaṃ nikṣipettato dviguṇam |
munikanakanāgasarpairdantyātha siñcyācca tanmadhyam || 11.109 ||
takreṇa mardayitvā gaṇena madanavalayaṃ kuryāt |
ratisamaye vanitānāṃ ratigarvavināśanaṃ kurute || 11.110 ||

[madanavalaya]
vyāghrībṛhatīphalarasasūraṇakandaṃ ca caṇakapattrāmlam |
kapikacchuvajravallīpippalikāmlikācūrṇam || 11.111 ||
agnyāvartitanāgaṃ navavāraṃ mardayeddhimairdravyaiḥ |
smaravalayaṃ kṛtvaitadvanitānāṃ drāvaṇaṃ kurute || 11.112 ||

[mercury:: māraṇa]
palāśabījakaṃ raktajambīrāmlena sūtakam |
sajīvaṃ marditaṃ yantre pācitaṃ mriyate dhruvam || 11.113 ||

[mercury:: māraṇa]
kharamañjari bījānvitapuṣkarabījaiḥ sucūrṇitaiḥ kalkam |
kṛtvā sūtaṃ puṭayeddṛḍhamūṣāyāṃ bhavedbhasma || 11.114 ||

[mercury:: māraṇa]
kākodumbarikāyā dugdhena subhāvito hiṅguḥ |
mardanapuṭanavidhānātsūtaṃ bhasmīkarotyeva || 11.115 ||

[mercury:: māraṇa]
devadālīṃ harikrāntāmāranālena peṣayet |
taddravaiḥ saptadhā sūtaṃ kuryānmarditamūrchitam || 11.116 ||
tatsūtaṃ kharpare dadyāddattvā dattvā tu taddravam |
cullyopari paceccāhni bhasma syāllavaṇopamam || 11.117 ||

[mercury:: māraṇa]
apāmārgasya bījāni tathairaṇḍasya cūrṇayet |
taccūrṇaṃ pārade deyaṃ mūṣāyāmadharottaram |
ruddhvā laghupuṭaiḥ pacyāccaturbhirbhasmatāṃ nayet || 11.118 ||

[mercury:: māraṇa]
kaṭutumbyudbhave kande garbhe nārīpayaḥplute |
saptadhā sveditaḥ sūto mriyate gomayāgninā || 11.119 ||

[mercury:: māraṇa]
aṅkolasya śiphāvāripiṣṭaṃ khalle vimardayet |
sūtaṃ gandhakasaṃyuktaṃ dinānte taṃ nirodhayet |
puṭayedbhūdhare yantre dinānte sa mṛto bhavet || 11.120 ||

[mercury:: māraṇa]
vaṭakṣīreṇa sūtābhrau mardayetpraharatrayam |
pācayettena kāṣṭhena bhasmībhavati tadrasaḥ || 11.121 ||

[rasa:: bhasma:: sevana]
athāturo rasācāryaṃ sākṣāddevaṃ maheśvaram |
sādhitaṃ ca rasaṃ śṛṅgadantaveṇvādidhāritam || 11.122 ||
arcayitvā yathāśakti devagobrāhmaṇānapi |
parṇakhaṇḍe dhṛtaṃ sūtaṃ jagdhvā syādanupānataḥ || 11.123 ||
ghṛtasaindhavadhānyakajīrakārdrakasaṃskṛtam |
taṇḍulīyakadhānyakapaṭolālambuṣādikam || 11.124 ||
gojīrṇaśālyannaṃ gavyaṃ kṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ dadhi |
haṃsodakaṃ mudgarasaḥ pathyavargaḥ samāsataḥ || 11.125 ||
bṛhatī bilvakūṣmāṇḍaṃ vetrāgraṃ kāravellakam |
māṣaṃ masūraṃ niṣpāvaṃ kulatthaṃ sarṣapaṃ tilam || 11.126 ||
laṅghanodvartanasnānatāmrasurāsavān |
ānūpamāṃsaṃ dhānyāmlaṃ bhojanaṃ kadalīdale |
kāṃsye ca guruviṣṭambhi tīkṣṇoṣṇaṃ ca bhṛśaṃ tyajet || 11.127 ||

[kakārādigaṇa]
kaṇṭārīphalakāñjikaṃ ca kamaṭhastailaṃ tathā rājikām |
nimbūkaṃ katakaṃ kaliṅgakaphalaṃ kūṣmāṇḍakaṃ karkaṭī |
kārī kukkuṭakāravellakaphalaṃ karkoṭikāyāḥ phalam |
vṛntākaṃ ca kapitthakaṃ khalu gaṇaḥ proktaḥ kakārādikaḥ || 11.128 ||
devīśāstroditaḥ so'yaṃ kakārādigaṇo mataḥ |

[kakārādigaṇa]
śāstrāntaravinirdiṣṭaḥ kathyate'nyaprakārataḥ || 11.129 ||
kaṅguḥ kandukakolakukkuṭakalakroḍāḥ kulatthāstathā |
kaṇṭārī kaṭutailakṛṣṇagalakaḥ kūrmaḥ kalāyaḥ kaṇā |
karkāruśca kaṭhillakaṃ ca katakaṃ karkoṭakaṃ karkaṭī kālī kāñjikameṣakādikagaṇaḥ śrīkṛṣṇadevoditaḥ || 11.130 ||
yasminrase ca kaṇṭhoktyā kakārādirniṣedhitaḥ |
tatra tatra niṣeddhavyaṃ tadaucityamato'nyataḥ || 11.131 ||
udgāre sati dadhyannaṃ kṛṣṇamīnaṃ sajīrakam |
abhyaṅgamanilakṣobhe tailairnārāyaṇādibhiḥ || 11.132 ||
aratau śītatoyena mastakopari secanam |
tṛṣṇāyāṃ nārikelāmbu mudgaṃ saśarkaram || 11.133 ||
drākṣādāḍimakharjūrakadalīnāṃ phalaṃ bhajet |
rasavīryavivṛddhyarthaṃ dadhikṣīrekṣuśarkarāḥ |
śītopacāramanyaṃ ca rasatyāgavidhau punaḥ || 11.134 ||
bhakṣayedbṛhatīṃ bilvaṃ sakṛtsādhāraṇo vidhiḥ || 11.135 ||


________________________________________________________


 12
jvarasya raktapittasya kāsasya śvāsahidhmayoḥ |
vaisvaryasya kṣayasyāpi tathārocaprasekayoḥ || 12.1 ||
chardihṛdrogayoścaiva tṛṣṇāmadyodbhavārśasām |
udāvartātisārāṇāṃ grahaṇyartipravāhiṇoḥ || 12.2 ||
visūcyā vahnimāndyasya mūtrakṛcchrāśmarujām |
mehasya somarogasya piṭikānāṃ ca vidradheḥ || 12.3 ||
vṛddhigulmādirogāṇāṃ śūlānāmudarasya ca |
pāṇḍuśophavisarpāṇāṃ kuṣṭhaśvitranabhasvatām || 12.4 ||
vātāsrasyāvṛtānāṃ ca vandhyānāṃ garbhiṇīrujām |
sūtikābālarogāṇāmunmāde'pasmṛtāvapi || 12.5 ||
netraroge karṇaroge nāsārogāsyarogayoḥ |
śiraḥsaṃjātarogeṣu vraṇe bhaṅge bhagaṃdare || 12.6 ||
granthyādau kṣudrarogeṣu guhyaroge viṣeṣu ca |
jarāyāstvanapatyānāṃ bījapoṣaṇahetave || 12.7 ||
paripāṭyānayā sarvaṃ rogāṇāṃ hi cikitsanam |
rasalohaviṣairatra yogairvakṣye yathāgamam || 12.8 ||
romāñcakampau vadane madhutvamujjṛmbhaṇaṃ mastakatodadāhau |
vātajvarasyoktamidaṃ hi lakṣma bhuktottaraṃ syādyadi śaśvadeva || 12.9 ||
virekaśoṣāsyakaṭutvatīvratāpapralāpabhramamūrchanāni |
etāni pittajvaralakṣaṇāni vamiḥ satṛṣṇāṅgavidāhitā ca || 12.10 ||
kāsaśvāsau mukhe jāḍyaṃ mādhuryaṃ bahunidratā |
prasvedaḥ svalpadāhaśca śleṣmajajvaralakṣaṇam || 12.11 ||
miśritaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ yattu dvayostriṣu bhavecca tat || 12.12 ||
vimarditābhyāṃ rasagandhakābhyāṃ nīreṇa kuryādiha golakaṃ tam |
bhāṇḍe navīne viniveśya paścāttadgolakasyopari tāmrapātram || 12.13 ||
sārdhaṃ muhūrtaṃ vinirudhya dhīmānuddīpayeddīptakṛśānunāsya |
adhastataḥ sidhyati parpaṭīyaṃ navajvarāraṇyakṛśānumeghaḥ || 12.14 ||
vilipya pūrvaṃ rasanāṃ ca tāludeśaṃ ca sindhūdbhavajīrakārdraiḥ |
vallonmitāṃ cārdrakatoyamiśrāmenāṃ niyojya sthagayetpaṭena || 12.15 ||
gharmodgamo yāvadataḥ paraṃ ca takraudanaṃ pathyamiha prayojyam |
kuryāddinānāṃ tritayaṃ yadītthaṃ jvarasya śaṅkāpi tadā bhavetkim || 12.16 ||
sūtārkagandhacapalājayapālatiktāpathyātrivṛcca viṣatindukajān samāṃśān |
saṃbhāvya vajripayasā madhunā trivallastrailokyaḍambararaso'bhinavajvaraghnaḥ || 12.17 ||
pādāṃśakaṃ sāraraviḥ samāṃśagandho vipakvaḥ svakaṣāyapiṣṭaḥ |
rasaḥ kramānmāṣamito'nilādijvareṣu nāmnā kila meghanādaḥ || 12.18 ||
daradajaladayuktaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ ca gandhaṃ praharamatha supiṣṭaṃ vallayugmaṃ ca dadyāt |
jvaragajaharisaṃjñaṃ śṛṅgaverodakena prathamajanitadāhī kṣīrabhaktena bhojyaḥ || 12.19 ||
saṃtaptasīsabhāgaṃ ca pāradaṃ gandhakaṃ kaṇām |
samabhāgaṃ pṛthaktatra melayecca yathāvidhi || 12.20 ||
jambīrasya rase sarvaṃ mardayecca dinatrayam |
meghanādakumāryośca rase cāpi dinatrayam || 12.21 ||
dinadvayamajāmūtre gavāṃ mūtre dinatrayam |
bhāvayecca yathāyogyaṃ tasminnetāni dāpayet || 12.22 ||
saindhavaṃ citrakaṃ bhāgaṃ sauvarcalalavaṇaṃ tathā |
tena sammelanaṃ kṛtvā bhāvayecca punaḥ punaḥ || 12.23 ||
anena vidhinā samyaksiddho bhavati tadrasaḥ |
śarkarāghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ dadyādvallatrayaṃ rasam || 12.24 ||
godhūmasyaudanaṃ pathyaṃ māṣasūpaṃ ca vāstukam |
dhātrīphalasamāyuktaṃ sarvajvaravināśanam |
dīpikārasa ityeṣa tantrajñaiḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 12.25 ||
pāradaṃ rasakaṃ tālaṃ tutthaṃ gandhakaṭaṅkaṇam |
sarvametatsamaṃ śuddhaṃ kāravallyā dravairdinam || 12.26 ||
mardayettena kalkena tāmrapātrodaraṃ limpet |
aṅgulārdhārdhamānena taṃ pacetsikatāhvaye || 12.27 ||
yantre yāvatsphuṭantyevaṃ vrīhayastasya pṛṣṭhataḥ |
tataḥ suśītalaṃ grāhyaṃ tāmrapātrodarādbhiṣak || 12.28 ||
śītabhañjī raso nāma cūrṇayenmaricaiḥ samam |
māṣaikaṃ parṇakhaṇḍena bhakṣayennāśayejjvaram |
tridinairviṣamaṃ tīvramekadvitricaturthakam || 12.29 ||
sūtatālaśilāstulyā mardayetkarkaṭīrase |
tāmrapātre vinikṣipya tatkalkaṃ kajjalīkṛtam || 12.30 ||
vipacedvālukāyantre yathoktavidhinā tataḥ |
dadyānmaricacūrṇena māṣamātraṃ bhiṣagvaraḥ || 12.31 ||
prapibeduṣṇatoyasya culukaṃ śītakajvare |
śītabhañjī rasaḥ so'yaṃ śītajvaranivāraṇaḥ || 12.32 ||
kūṣmāṇḍacūrṇatilajaiḥ praviśuddhatālaṃ gāḍhaṃ vimardya suṣavīsalilena tulyam |
sūtena hiṅgulabhuvā sikatākhyayantre golaṃ vidhāya parivṛttakapālamadhye || 12.33 ||
pattreṇa taṃ dinapateśca pidhāya ruddhvā saṃdhiṃ tayorguḍasudhākhaṭikāśivābhiḥ |
vahnau pacenmṛduni pātraśiraḥsthaśālīvaivarṇyamātramavadhiṃ pravidhāya dhīmān || 12.34 ||
vallaṃ tataḥ surasamiśramamuṣya dadyātsarpiḥ sitākaṇapayomadhu cānupeyam |
jetuṃ jvarān praviṣamāniha vāntiśāntyai maulau suśītalajalasya dadīta dhārām || 12.35 ||
athāmayāntaṃ rasarājamaulibhūṣāmaṇiṃ taṃ mṛtajīvanākhyam |
sudhāraseneva rasena yena saṃjīvanaṃ syātsahasāturāṇām || 12.36 ||
rasahiṅgulajepālairvṛddhyā dantyambumarditaiḥ |
dinārdhena jvaraṃ hanyādguñjaikaṃ sitayā saha || 12.37 ||
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ viṣaṃ gandhaṃ dhūrtabījaṃ tribhiḥ samam |
caturbhiśca samaṃ vyoṣaṃ cūrṇīkṛtya nidhāpayet || 12.38 ||
dantabhāṇḍe'tha vā śārṅge kāṣṭhe naiva kadācana |
vātaśleṣmajvare deyaṃ dvaṃdvaje vā tridoṣaje || 12.39 ||
rasena śṛṅgaverasya jambīrasyāthavā punaḥ |
guñjādvayaṃ ca jīrṇe'smindadhibhaktaṃ prayojayet || 12.40 ||
ekadvitridinairhanyājjvarān doṣakrameṇa tu |
mahājvarāṅkuśo nāma raso'yaṃ śambhunoditaḥ || 12.41 ||
tālaṃ tāmrarajo rasaśca gaganaṃ gandhaśca nepālakaṃ dīnārapramitaṃ tadardhamuditaṃ ṭaṅkaṃ śilāmākṣikam |
dīnāradvitayaṃ viṣasya śikhinaḥ piṣṭvā rasaiḥ pācito yaścintāmaṇivajjvaraughavijayī nāmnā tu mṛtyuṃjayaḥ || 12.42 ||
tālaṃ tāmramayorajaśca capalā tutthābhrakaṃ kāntakaṃ nāgaṃ syācca samāṃśakaṃ sumṛditaṃ mūlaṃ ca paunarnavam |
bhṛṅgīkāsaharīpunarnavāmahāmandārapattrodbhavaiḥ kalkaṃ vālukayantrapācitamidaṃ sarvajvarasyāntakṛt || 12.43 ||
tutthena tulyaḥ śivajaśca gandho jambīranīreṇa vimardanīyaḥ |
dinatrayaṃ melaya tena tulyaṃ vyoṣaṃ tataḥ sidhyati candrasūryaḥ || 12.44 ||
vallo vijetuṃ viṣamāvalambi dalena deyo bhujagākhyavallyāḥ |
dugdhaṃ hitaṃ syādiha śṛṅgaverarasena śaityeṣu niṣevaṇīyaḥ || 12.45 ||
takraṃ sagarbhājvaraśūlayostu drākṣāmbunā pathyamanantaroktam |
rodhaṃ varāyāḥ salilena śūlaṃ jambīranīreṇa varājalena || 12.46 ||
apasmṛtāvatra niyojanīyamabhyañjanaṃ bimbapayobhavābhyām |
ghṛtaudanaṃ syādiha bhojanāya jambīranīreṇa nihanti gulmam || 12.47 ||
hiṅgvamlikānimburasena deyaṃ plīhodare syādiha takrabhaktaḥ |
stambhārthamasminsasitaṃ payaḥ syādguḍo niyojyo vamanapraśāntyai || 12.48 ||
aśītiryasya varṣāṇi vasuvarṣāṇi yasya vā |
viṣaṃ tasya na dātavyaṃ dattaṃ ceddoṣakārakam || 12.49 ||
meghapāradagandhāśmaviṣavyoṣapaṭūni ca |
jīrakadvayametāni samabhāgāni kārayet || 12.50 ||
sinduvārarasenāpi laśunasya rasena ca |
apāmārgarasenāpi saptarātraṃ vimardayet || 12.51 ||
tatpakvaṃ vālukāyantre guñjāmātraṃ prayojayet |
sanāgavallīmaricaṃ tataḥ śītāmbu pāyayet || 12.52 ||
umāprasādano nāma rasaḥ śītajvarāpahaḥ |
cāturthikaṃ trirātraṃ vā nāśayetkimutāparān || 12.53 ||
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ rasagandhau ca samabhāgānprakalpayet |
nepālaṃ dviguṇaṃ dattvā mardayetkhallamadhyataḥ || 12.54 ||
ślakṣṇatāṃ yāti tadyāvattāvattanmardayecchanaiḥ |
saindhavaṃ maricaṃ śaṅkhaṃ ciñcākṣāraṃ samākṣikam || 12.55 ||
tattulyametatkṛtvātha nimbūtoyena mardayet |
caṇapramāṇavaṭikāṃ bhakṣayeddivasatrayam || 12.56 ||
aikāhikaṃ dvyāhikaṃ ca tryāhikaṃ ca caturthakam |
sarvajvaravināśāya jvarāṅkuśa iti smṛtaḥ || 12.57 ||
abhrakaṃ gandhakaṃ sūtaṃ tolakaikaṃ pṛthakpṛthak |
gṛhītvā viṣatolārdhaṃ tolārdhaṃ tintiḍīphalam || 12.58 ||
etatsarvaṃ samaṃ kṛtvā mardayetkhallamadhyataḥ |
ślakṣṇatāṃ yāti tadyāvattāvatsaṃmardayecchanaiḥ || 12.59 ||
vistāre pariṇāhe ca gartāṃ kṛtvā ṣaḍaṅgulām |
phaṇivallīdalānyantargartāyāṃ prakṣipennaraḥ || 12.60 ||
parṇeṣu sūtakalkaṃ taṃ gartāyāṃ sthāpayeddṛḍham |
kalkādupari tatparṇairgartāvaktraṃ prapūrayet || 12.61 ||
gartāyāṃ tu tato deyaṃ puṭamāraṇyakotpalaiḥ |
svāṅgaśītalatāṃ jñātvā samākarṣettataḥ param || 12.62 ||
sūtaliptadalaiḥ sārdhaṃ kalkaṃ khalle vimardayet |
tolārdhamamṛtaṃ kṣiptvā tolārdhaṃ tintiḍīphalam || 12.63 ||
sthāpayetkhallitaṃ kalkaṃ yojayedguñjamātrayā |
śṛṅgaverāmbhasā yuktaṃ tīkṣṇacitrakasaindhavaiḥ || 12.64 ||
saṃnipāte tathā vāte tridoṣe viṣamajvare |
agnimāndye grahiṇyāṃ ca tathā deyo'tisāriṇi || 12.65 ||
bhojanaṃ dadhibhaktaṃ ca rase'smin saṃprayojayet |
vyādhyādikaṃ yathā kuryādudakaṃ ḍhālayettataḥ || 12.66 ||
eṣa yogavaraḥ śrīmānprāṇināṃ prāṇadāyakaḥ |
cintāmaṇiritikhyāto rasaḥ sarvāṅgasundaraḥ || 12.67 ||
sūtendraṃ parimardya pañcapaṭubhiḥ kṣāraistribhistaṃ tataḥ piṇḍe hiṅgumahauṣadhāsurīmaye saṃsvedya dhānyodake |
nirguṇḍyambuhutāśamanthatilaparṇyunmattabhṛṅgārdrakaṃ kāmātāgirikarṇikāplavadalāpañcāṅgulotthairjalaiḥ || 12.68 ||
sūtendreṇa samairvimardya sahajaiḥ pittaistato bhāvayeddaṃṣṭricchāgalulāyamatsyaśikhināṃ sā saṃnipātāñjayet |
vikhyātā bhuvi lokanāthaguṭikā mārīcamātrā hitā syādasyāḥ sahitaṃ dadhīkṣuśakalaṃ vīryaṃ bhavecchītalam || 12.69 ||
vajravaikrāntayorbhasma pratyekaṃ niṣkasammitam |
śṛṅgīviṣaṃ dviniṣkaṃ ca triniṣkaṃ cūlikāpaṭu || 12.70 ||
pañcaniṣko'gnijāraśca sarvamekatra melayet |
tāvadbhasma rasaṃ yāvanmardayeddivasatrayam || 12.71 ||
śārṅgaṣṭādikavargasya kṣāranīreṇa bhāvayet |
trayoviṃśativārāṇi vimardya ca viśoṣya ca || 12.72 ||
tato vimardya divasaṃ kṣipeddantakaraṇḍake |
mṛtasaṃjīvanākhyo'yaṃ sūcikābharaṇo rasaḥ || 12.73 ||
saṃnipātena tīvreṇa mumurṣorbhūgatasya ca |
tāluni vṛścayitvātha rasamenaṃ vinikṣipet || 12.74 ||
sūcyātisūkṣmayā toyabhinnayātiprayatnataḥ |
tatastailena taṃ liptvā nirvāte saṃniveśayet || 12.75 ||
tato'rdhapraharādūrdhvaṃ muktamūtrapurīṣakam |
labdhasaṃjñaṃ pratāpāḍhyaṃ dolayantaṃ śiro muhuḥ || 12.76 ||
āyuṣmantaṃ vijānīyādanyathā cānyathā khalu |
tataḥ śītāmbusampūrṇe kaṭāhe taṃ niveśayet || 12.77 ||
tatra cotkvathitaṃ toyamapanīyāparaṃ kṣipet |
yācamānamamuṃ paścātpāyayetsasitaṃ payaḥ || 12.78 ||
dadhi vā sitayopetaṃ nārikelajalaṃ tathā |
rambhāphalāni dadyācca mriyate so'nyathā khalu || 12.79 ||
labdhasaṃjñaṃ prabhāṣantaṃ yācamānaṃ phalādikam |
tasmādākṛṣya tailāktaṃ tailaṃ piṣṭvāpanīya ca || 12.80 ||
lepayedgandhakarpūrairā pādatalamastakam |
ityādiśiśirairdravyaiḥ saptarātramupācaret || 12.81 ||
karṇākṣināsikāvaktre kṣipetpotāśrayaṃ muhuḥ |
aṣṭame'hani samprāpte dardurīmūlajaṃ rasam || 12.82 ||
sasitaṃ pāyayedvegamavatārayituṃ rasam |
rase'vatārite paścādyatheṣṭaṃ bhojanaṃ dadhi || 12.83 ||
śvāsocchvāsayutaṃ cānyairmuktajīvanalakṣaṇaiḥ |
kaṭāhe jalasampūrṇe nikṣipedbodhalabdhaye || 12.84 ||
labdhabodhaṃ tamākṛṣya pūrvavatsamupācaret |
jīvitvā yāvadāyuṣyaṃ mriyate tadanantaram || 12.85 ||
saṃnipāte mahāghore majjantaṃ mṛtyusāgare |
uddharettasya dharmasya brahmāpyantaṃ na vindati || 12.86 ||
saṃnipātamahāmṛtyubhayanirmuktamānavaḥ |
api sarvasvadānena prāṇācāryaṃ prapūjayet || 12.87 ||
anyathā narake tāvadyāvatkalpavikalpanā |
ityājñā śāṃkarī jñeyā śambhunā parikīrtitā || 12.88 ||
prakāśā naiva kartavyā rasottaraṇamūlikā |
śāstraṃ vinā prayacchante mandā vittābhikāṅkṣayā |
guruprasādamāsādya saṃnipāte prayujyatām || 12.89 ||
śārṅgaṣṭā ca tathā vyāghrī karīrastilaparṇikā |
indravāruṇikā mustā haridrāṅkolamūlikā || 12.90 ||
apāmārgaḥ kaṇā svarṇaṃ kaṭutumbī ca tintiḍī |
śārṅgaṣṭādikavargo'yaṃ saṃnipātaharaḥ param || 12.91 ||
sūtaṃ gandhakatālakaṃ maṇiśilāṃ tāpyaṃ lavaṃ tutthakaṃ jepālaṃ viṣaṭaṅkaṇaṃ madhuphalaṃ kṛtvā samāṃśaṃ dṛḍham |
kṛtvā kajjalikāṃ viṣolbaṇaphaṇeḥ pittaiśca saṃbhāvayetkṣiptvā sīsakakūpike rasavaraṃ sūcīmukhaṃ nāmataḥ || 12.92 ||
brahmadvāri vikīrṇalohitalave guñjaikamātraṃ dadeddattvā sampuṭabaddhatandrikadhanurvāte saśākhāhime |
kāsaṃ śvāsamarocakaṃ pralapanaṃ kampaṃ ca hikkāturaṃ mūkatvaṃ badhiratvamunmadamapasmāraṃ jayettatkṣaṇāt || 12.93 ||
rasagandhakatāmrābhraṃ lāṅgalīvahnirāmaṭham |
vandhyāpaṭolanirguṇḍīsugandhānimbapallavāḥ || 12.94 ||
pāṭhākṣāratrayaṃ kṣveḍaboladhattūrataṇḍulaiḥ |
śṛṅgīmadhukasāraṃ ca jambīrāmlena mardayet || 12.95 ||
kuryāddhi niṣkamānena vaṭikā sā niyacchati |
sasvedadāhābhinyāsaṃ saṃnipātagajāṃkuśaḥ || 12.96 ||
sasārā vaiṣṇavī senā acalā kādi kaṅkaṇā |
rāgarudropamopetā prauḍhā mastakaśālinī || 12.97 ||
tribhāgaṃ tālakaṃ vidyādekabhāgaṃ tu pāradam |
tadardhaṃ gandhakaṃ caiva tadardhaṃ tu manaḥśilā || 12.98 ||
kāravallīdalarasairmardayettatpraharatrayam |
pācito vālukāyantre cāturthikaharo rasaḥ || 12.99 ||
syādrasena samāyukto gandhakaḥ sumanoharaḥ |
hiyāvallitriguṇito nirguṇḍīrasamarditaḥ || 12.100 ||
saptavārāṇi tadyojyamārdrakasvarasena tu |
saṃtatādijvaraṃ hanyāccāturthikagajāṃkuśaḥ || 12.101 ||
tāpyatālakajepālavatsanābhamanaḥśilāḥ |
tāmragandhakasūtaṃ ca musalīrasamarditaḥ |
mṛtyuṃjaya iti khyātaḥ kukkuṭīpuṭapācitaḥ || 12.102 ||
valladvayaṃ prayuñjīta yatheṣṭaṃ dadhibhojanam |
navajvaraṃ saṃnipātaṃ hanyādeṣa mahārasaḥ || 12.103 ||
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ viṣaṃ gandhaṃ maricaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ kaṇām |
mardayeddhūrtajadrāvairdinamekaṃ tu śoṣayet || 12.104 ||
pañcavaktro raso nāma dviguñjaḥ saṃnipātajit |
arkamūlakaṣāyaṃ ca satryūṣamanupāyayet |
dadhyodanaṃ hitaṃ tatra jalayogaṃ ca kārayet || 12.105 ||
rasagandhakatulyāṃśaṃ dhattūraphalajadravaiḥ |
mardayeddinamekaṃ tu tattulyaṃ trikaṭu kṣipet |
unmattākhyo raso nāmnā nasye syātsaṃnipātajit || 12.106 ||
nistvagjepālajaṃ bījaṃ daśaniṣkaṃ pracūrṇayet |
maricaṃ pippalīṃ sūtaṃ pratiniṣkaṃ vimiśrayet || 12.107 ||
bhāvyaṃ jambīrajairdrāvaiḥ saptāhaṃ tatprayatnataḥ |
saṃnipātaṃ nihantyāśu añjane yaḥ śivaḥ smṛtaḥ || 12.108 ||
madanaphalaṃ viḍalavaṇaṃ sarṣapāḥ pratiniṣkadvayam |
cūrṇayitvā triphalākvāthena saṭaṅkaṇaṃ pibet || 12.109 ||
kuṣṭhe jvare kāmalāyāṃ kaṇṭharoge hyajīrṇake |
nasye ca girikarṇyutthabījaikaṃ śītavāriṇā || 12.110 ||
pratyekaṃ rasagandhayordvipalayoḥ kṛtvā maṣīṃ śuddhayorramyāṃ mlecchalulāyalocanamanodhātrīprakuñcatrayam |
pathyāyā badaratrikaṃ trikaṭu ṣaṭśāṇaṃ vacā dharmiṇī vellāmbhodharapattrakadviradakiñjalkāśvagandhāhvayam || 12.111 ||
piṣṭvaitatsamasāramakhilaṃ karṣonmitaṃ nyasya tatpronmardyārdhakarañjakāmṛtayutaṃ sāgastikatryūṣaṇaiḥ |
bhūdhātrīvijayāsaritpatiphalaṃ jvālāmukhīmārkavaiḥ pratyekaṃ vidadhīta niścalamatiḥ sapta kramādbhāvanāḥ || 12.112 ||
pittairatho pañca vidhāya pañcabhiḥ karañjapattrāmṛtadhūpanaṃ tataḥ |
dattvārdrakasya svarasena taṇḍulākṛtiṃ vidadhyādguṭikāṃ bhiṣagvaraḥ || 12.113 ||
deyaikā saṃnipāte pratihataviṣaye mohanetraprasuptyoḥ syādgulme sājamodā pavanavikṛtiṣu tryūṣaṇena grahaṇyām |
dātavyā jīrakeṇa dvipaturaganṛṇāṃ prāṇasaṃrakṣaṇāya kāyāmbhodhiretaṃ rasakasamarasaṃ vaidyanātho'bhyadhatta || 12.114 ||
gandhakābhrasamaḥ sūto vārāhīrasamarditaḥ |
pācito vālukāyantre triphalāvyoṣacitrakaiḥ || 12.115 ||
trikṣāraṃ pañcalavaṇahiṅgugugguludīpyakaiḥ |
sajīrakaiḥ sendrayavaiḥ pṛthagrasasamairyutaḥ || 12.116 ||
māṣamātro'nupānena dvipalasyoṣṇavāriṇaḥ |
abhinyāsānalabhraṃśagrahaṇīpāṇḍugulminām || 12.117 ||
kuryātprāṇaparitrāṇamataḥ prāṇeśvaraḥ smṛtaḥ |
vyādhivṛddhau prayogo'sya dvau vārau vaidyasaṃmataḥ || 12.118 ||
rasāyovyoṣakaṅkuṣṭhaśilātālābhrahiṅgulān |
kumbhyagnibhṛṅgamārītaṇḍulīyakamākṣikān || 12.119 ||
hastiśuṇḍīyutāṃstulyāṃstadardhaśivagandhakān |
tryahamārdrāmbunā piṣṭvā kūpīsthaṃ vālukāgninā || 12.120 ||
jayājambīranirguṇḍīcāṅgerīvāri nikṣipet |
paktvā caturdaśāhāni piṣṭvārdrāktaṃ viśoṣayet || 12.121 ||
mṛtasaṃjīvanākhyo'yaṃ raso vallamito'śitaḥ |
drāgjayedauṣadhaṃ saṃnipātādīn sakalān gadān || 12.122 ||
rasabhāgo bhavedeko gandhako dviguṇo mataḥ |
viṣatālakakaṅkuṣṭhaśilāhiṅgulalohakam || 12.123 ||
vahnitrikaṭubhṛṅgāhvahemamākṣikamabhrakam |
hastiśuṇḍī viṣaṃ kumbhī taṇḍulīyakatāmrakau || 12.124 ||
eṣāṃ pratyekamekaikaṃ bhāgamādāya cūrṇayet |
ārdrakasya draveṇaiva mardayecca dinatrayam || 12.125 ||
jambīrasya raso grāhyaḥ palatrayaparīkṣitaḥ |
triphalāyāśca nirguṇḍyāḥ pratyekaṃ ca palatrayam || 12.126 ||
rasasya palamātraṃ tu cāṅgeryāḥ parikīrtitam |
kācakūpyāṃ vinikṣipya yantre kṣiptvā prayatnavān || 12.127 ||
uddhṛtyārdrakaniryāsairmardayitvā viśoṣayet |
mṛtasaṃjīvano nāma raso'yaṃvidito bhuvi |
guñjādvayaṃ dadītāsya saṃnipātāpanuttaye || 12.128 ||
vaṅgaṃ nāgaṃ ca sūtaṃ ca nepālaṃ gandhakaṃ tathā |
śulbaṃ viṣaṃ samāṃśena rasenārdreṇa mardayet || 12.129 ||
punarmardyeta nirguṇḍyāścāṅgeryā rasamarditaḥ |
vallaprayogeṇa raso'yaṃ saṃnipātanut || 12.130 ||
gandhakaṃ ca rasaṃ śuddhaṃ pratyekaṃ karṣasammitam |
ekatra kajjalīṃ kṛtvā tataḥ kurvīta golakam || 12.131 ||
navabhāṇḍe vinikṣipya tāmrapātreṇa gopayet |
dṛḍhaṃ nirudhya tatpātramagnāvāropayettataḥ || 12.132 ||
vrīhisphuṭanamātreṇa svāṅgaśītaṃ samuddharet |
navajvare prayuñjīta rasaṃ parpaṭikāhvayam || 12.133 ||
ārdrakasya rasenaiva trivallaṃ tridinaṃ bhiṣak |
jvaritaṃ chādayedgāḍhaṃ yāvatsvedaḥ samudbhavet || 12.134 ||
takrabhaktaṃ bhavetpathyaṃ jvaramuktasya dehinaḥ |
navajvarārirityeṣa rasaḥ paramadurlabhaḥ |
vātajvare viśeṣeṇa rasaḥ sādhāraṇo mataḥ || 12.135 ||
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ rasagandhau ca maricāni samāṃśakam |
sarvaṃ jambīranīreṇa dināni trīṇi mardayet || 12.136 ||
saṃśoṣya śarkarāyuktaṃ matsyapittena bhāvayet |
bhāvitaṃ tadrasaṃ siddhamārdrakasvarasaistryaham || 12.137 ||
vallaṃ vāratrayaṃ deyaṃ pānārthaṃ vāri śītalam |
takrabhaktaṃ bhavetpathyaṃ vṛntākaphalasaṃyutam |
sarvānnavajvarān hanti raso'yaṃ jalamañjarī || 12.138 ||
kāntasya kaṇṭavedhyānāṃ pātrāṇāṃ bhasma kārayet |
tatsamaśca raso gandhaṣṭaṅkaṇo nimbavāriṇā |
tataḥ saṃpeṣya tatkalkaṃ mardayettridinaṃ punaḥ || 12.139 ||
rasatulyena matsyasya pittena paribhāvayet |
siddhaḥ kāntaraso hyeṣa prayojyo'bhinavajvare |
śṛṅgaverānupānena mātrayā bhiṣaguttamaiḥ || 12.140 ||
rasagandhau tathā vaṅgamabhrakaṃ samabhāgataḥ |
melayitvātha vaṅgena samaṃ sūtaṃ vimardayet || 12.141 ||
tatraikīkṛtya gandhābhre peṣyaṃ jambīravāriṇā |
sāmānyaṃ puṭamādadyātsaptadhā sādhitaṃ rasam || 12.142 ||
kumāryā citrakeṇāpi bhāvayitvātha saptadhā |
guḍena jīrakeṇāpi jvare jīrṇe prayojayet || 12.143 ||
kāse śvāse kumāryā ca triphalākvāthayogataḥ |
unmādaṃ ca dhanurvātamamṛtākvāthayogataḥ |
ityevaṃ rogatāpaghno rasaścandrodayābhidhaḥ || 12.144 ||
nāgaṃ vaṅgaṃ rasaṃ tāmraṃ gandhakaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ tathā |
viṣaṃ ca nepālaṃ haritālaṃ samaṃ tathā || 12.145 ||
vaṭakṣīreṇa saṃmardya sarvaṃ kuryāttu golakam |
taṃ golakaṃ bhāṇḍamadhye pācayeddīpavahninā || 12.146 ||
taṃ golaṃ śītalaṃ kṛtvā bhṛṅgarājena mardayet |
ārdrakasya rasenāpi mardayecca punaḥ punaḥ || 12.147 ||
caṇapramāṇavaṭakān rasenārdrasya dāpayet |
guñjādvayapramāṇena jvaraṃ jīrṇaṃ haratyasau || 12.148 ||
haraśca gandhakaṃ caiva kunaṭī ca samaṃ samam |
mardyaṃ karkoṭikāyāśca rasena viniyojayet || 12.149 ||
navajvaramurāriḥ syādvallaṃ śarkarayā saha |
taṇḍulīyarasenānupānaṃ śarkarayāpi vā |
guñjādvayapramāṇena jvarānhanti navānhaṭhāt || 12.150 ||


________________________________________________________


 13
kaṭvamlatīkṣṇalavaṇoṣṇavidāhirūkṣaiḥ pittaṃ praduṣṭamaśanairatisevitaistaiḥ |
saṃdūṣya raktamamunobhayamārgavarti niryātyasṛksthalayakṛtplihato'timātram || 13.1 ||
pāradaṃ hiṅgulukaṃ ca ūrdhvayantreṇa melayet |
kukkuṭāṇḍarasaṃ bhāgaṃ ṭaṅkaṇakṣārameva ca || 13.2 ||
gandhakasya tathā bhāgaṃ ghṛtena parimardayet |
siddhaṃ rasaṃ samādāya jīratoyena dāpayet || 13.3 ||
dināni trīṇi māṣaṃ ca grahaṇīraktadoṣajit |
jvaradāhavināśaṃ ca raktapittavināśanam || 13.4 ||
pratyekaṃ tolamānena sūtakaṃ tāmrabhasmakam |
dināni trīṇi guṭikāṃ kṛtvā cāgnau vinikṣipet || 13.5 ||
tataḥ śuṣkaṃ samādāya punareva ca mardayet |
samastaiḥ samagandhaiśca kṛtvā kajjalikāṃ ca taiḥ || 13.6 ||
mustādāḍimadūrvābhiḥ ketakīstanavāribhiḥ |
sahadevyāḥ kumāryāśca parpaṭasyāpi vāriṇā || 13.7 ||
rāmaśītalikātoyaiḥ śatāvaryā rasena ca |
bhāvayitvā prayatnena divase divase pṛthak || 13.8 ||
tiktaṃ guḍūcikāsattvaṃ parpaṭīśīramāgadhīḥ |
śṛṅgāṭaṃ sārivā caiṣāṃ samānaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇakam || 13.9 ||
drākṣādikakaṣāyeṇa saptadhā paribhāvayet |
tataḥ potāśrayaṃ kṣiptvā vaṭyaḥ kāryāścaṇopamāḥ || 13.10 ||
ayaṃ candrakalānāmā rasendraḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
sarvapittagadadhvaṃsī vātapittagadāpahaḥ || 13.11 ||
antarbāhyamahādāhavidhvaṃsanamahākṣamaḥ |
grīṣmakāle śaratkāle viśeṣeṇa praśasyate || 13.12 ||
kurute nāgnimāndyaṃ ca mahātāpajvaraṃ haret |
śramaṃ mūrchāṃ haratyāśu strīṇāṃ raktamahāsravam || 13.13 ||
ūrdhvādho raktapittaṃ ca raktavāntiṃ viśeṣataḥ |
mūtrakṛcchrāṇi sarvāṇi nāśayennātra saṃśayaḥ || 13.14 ||
paṭolamāyasaṃ cūrṇaṃ sūtendraṃ samacāritam |
lohārivargasaṃghṛṣṭaṃ raktapittaharaṃ param || 13.15 ||
vṛṣādalānāṃ svarasasya karṣaṃ rasendraguñjāmadhuśarkarāyutam |
lihanprabhāte manujo nihanyādduḥkhākaraṃ dāruṇaraktapittam || 13.16 ||
sapaṭolakahiṅgūlaḥ sakṣaudro raktapittanut || 13.17 ||
navanītaṃ sitā lājā drākṣayā saha bhakṣayet || 13.18 ||
mastake ca ghṛtaṃ dadyādraktapittaharaṃ param || 13.19 ||
drākṣāvāsāyutaṃ kvāthaṃ śarkarābhāvitaṃ pibet || 13.20 ||
vāsārasaṃ sitākṣaudrairlājānvā śarkarāsamān |
bhakṣayan raktapittārtastṛṣṇādāhajvaraṃ jayet || 13.21 ||
dhātrīcūrṇaṃ sitāyuktaṃ bhakṣayedraktapittanut || 13.22 ||
doṣāḥ śoṣamano'bhitāpakupitāḥ kurvanti kāsaṃ tataḥ pītaṃ pūtikaphaṃ pratīpanayanaḥ pūyopamaṃ ṣṭhīvati |
śītoṣṇecchurakāraṇena bahubhuksnigdhaprasannānanaḥ pārśvārtyalpabalakṣayākṛtirapi prādurbhavatyanyathā || 13.23 ||
sārkatīkṣṇābhrako'gastyakāsamardavarārasaiḥ |
mardito vetasāmlena piṇḍitaḥ kāsanāśanaḥ || 13.24 ||
tāre piṣṭaśilāṃ kṣiptvā haritālāccaturguṇām |
vāsāgokṣurasārābhyāṃ mardayetpraharadvayam || 13.25 ||
prasvinno vālukāyantre guñjādvitayasaṃmitaḥ |
kāsaṃ trikaṭunirguṇḍīmūlacūrṇayuto haret || 13.26 ||
bhūnāgābhrakayoḥ sattvaṃ kāntahemābhrarūpyakam |
muktāphalāni ratnāni tāpyaṃ vaikrāntameva ca || 13.27 ||
bhasmīkṛtamidaṃ sarvaṃ pṛthaṅmāṣamitaṃ matam |
niṣkamātramitaṃ śuddhaṃ rājāvartarajastathā || 13.28 ||
etatsarvaṃ samaṃ yojyaṃ mardayitvāmlavetasaiḥ |
ruddhvā mūṣodare koṣṭhyāṃ dhamedākāśadarśanam || 13.29 ||
śatavāraṃ dhamedevaṃ mardayitvāmlavetasaiḥ |
tataḥ saṃcūrṇite cāsminmuktābhasma dviśāṇakam || 13.30 ||
maricaṃ pañca śāṇeyaṃ kṣiptvā saṃmardya yatnataḥ |
ramye karaṇḍake kṣiptvā sthāpayettadanantaram || 13.31 ||
so'yaṃ ratnakaraṇḍako rasavaro madhvājyasaṃkrāmaṇo hanyācchvāsagadaṃ jvaraṃ grahaṇikāṃ kāsaṃ ca hidhmāmayam |
śūlaṃ śoṣamahodaraṃ bahuvidhaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ ca hanyādgadān balyo vṛṣyakaraḥ pradīpanatamaḥ svasthocito vegavān || 13.32 ||
śuddhasūtasya bhāgaikaṃ bhāgaikaṃ śuddhagandhakam |
bhāgatrayaṃ mṛtaṃ tāmraṃ maricaṃ pañcabhāgikam || 13.33 ||
mṛtābhrasya caturbhāgaṃ bhāgamekaṃ viṣaṃ kṣipet |
bhūtāṅkuśasya bhāgaikaṃ sarvaṃ cāmlena mardayet || 13.34 ||
yāmaṃ bhūtāṅkuśo nāma māṣaikaṃ vātakāsajit |
anupānaṃ lihetkṣaudrairvibhītakaphalatvacaḥ |
pittakāsāruciśvāsakṣayakāsāṃśca nāśayet || 13.35 ||
rasabhasma viṣaṃ tulyaṃ gandhakaṃ dviguṇaṃ matam |
bolatālakavāhlīkakarkoṭīmākṣikaṃ niśā || 13.36 ||
kaṇṭakārī yavakṣāralāṅgalīkṣārasaindhavam |
madhūkasāraṃ saṃcūrṇya saptāhaṃ cārdrakadravaiḥ || 13.37 ||
guṭikāṃ badarākārāṃ śleṣmakāsāpanuttaye |
bhakṣayedbolabaddho'yaṃ rasaḥ saśvāsapāṇḍujit || 13.38 ||
rasagandhakapippalyo harītakyakṣavāsakam |
ṣaḍuttaraguṇaṃ cūrṇaṃ babbulakvāthabhāvitam || 13.39 ||
ekaviṃśativārāṇi śoṣayitvā vicūrṇayet |
bhakṣayenmadhunā hanti kāsamagniraso hyayam || 13.40 ||
trikaṭu triphalā cailā jātīphalalavaṃgakam |
eteṣāṃ samabhāgānāṃ samaḥ pūrvaraso bhavet || 13.41 ||
sampūryāloḍayetkṣaudre bhakṣyo niṣkadvayaṃ dvayam |
svayamagniraso nāmnā kṣayakāsanikṛntanaḥ || 13.42 ||
bhṛṅgarājasya pattrāṇi madhunā cūrṇitāni hi |
golakaṃ dhārayedvaktre kāsaviṣṭambhaśāntaye || 13.43 ||
arkairaṇḍasya pattrāṇāṃ rasaṃ pītvā ca kāsajit || 13.44 ||
dantīmūlasya dhūmaṃ vā nirguṇḍyā vā pibejjayet || 13.45 ||
indravāruṇikāmūlaṃ bhṛṅgīkṛṣṇātilaiḥ saha |
bhakṣayetkṣayakāsārto niṣkamātraṃ praśāntaye || 13.46 ||
śleṣmoparuddhamanaḥ pavano'tiduṣṭaḥ saṃdūṣayannanu jalānnavahāśca nāḍīḥ |
āmāśayodbhavamimaṃ vidadhātyurasthaḥ śvāsaṃ ca vakragamano hi śarīrabhājām || 13.47 ||
indravāruṇikāmūlaṃ devadāru kaṭutrayam |
śarkarāsahitaṃ khādedūrdhvaśvāsapraśāntaye || 13.48 ||
sūtārdhaṃ gandhakaṃ mardyaṃ yāmaikaṃ kanyakādravaiḥ |
dvayoḥ samaṃ tāmrapattraṃ pūrvakalkena melayet || 13.49 ||
dinaikaṃ haṇḍikāyantre pakvamādāya cūrṇayet |
sūryāvartaraso hyeṣa dviguñjaḥ śvāsajitbhavet || 13.50 ||
sūtaḥ ṣoḍaśa tatsamo dinakarastasyārdhabhāgo baliḥ sindhustasya samaḥ susūkṣmamṛditaḥ ṣaṭpippalī cūrṇitaḥ |
jambīrasvarasena marditamidaṃ taptaṃ supakvaṃ bhavetkāsaśvāsasagulmaśūlajaṭharaṃ pāṇḍuṃ lihannāśayet || 13.51 ||
sādhāraṇaṃ tu vaṭakaṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu tattvataḥ |
pāradaṃ gandhakaṃ caiva palamekaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 13.52 ||
palatrayaṃ trikaṭukaṃ vaṅgamekapalaṃ kṣipet |
sarvamekatra saṃyojya dināni trīṇi mardayet || 13.53 ||
gomūtreṇa tathā trīṇi dināni parimardayet |
akṣapramāṇavaṭakaṃ chāyāśuṣkaṃ tu kārayet || 13.54 ||
nityamekaṃ tu vaṭakaṃ dināni triṃśadeva ca |
śvāsakāsajvaraharamagnimāndyārucipraṇut || 13.55 ||
rasabhāgo bhavedeko gandhako dviguṇo mataḥ |
tribhāgā pippalī grāhyā caturbhāgā harītakī || 13.56 ||
vibhītaḥ pañcabhāgastu vāsā ṣaḍguṇitā bhavet |
bhārṅgīsaptaguṇā grāhyā sarvaṃ cūrṇaṃ prakalpayet || 13.57 ||
babbulakvāthamādāya bhāvayedekaviṃśatiḥ |
vibhītakapramāṇena madhunā guṭikāṃ caret || 13.58 ||
ekaikāṃ bhakṣayetprātarvaṭī saptāmṛtābhidhā |
śvāsakāsādikaṃ vyādhiṃ tatkṣaṇānnāśayediyam || 13.59 ||
sūtaṃ śulbaṃ sulohaṃ balimamṛtayutaṃ tritrikaṃ reṇukābdaṃ gaṇḍīraṃ kesarāgniṃ dviguṇaguḍayutaṃ mardayitvā samastam |
kuryātkolāsthimātrān suruciravaṭakān bhakṣayetprāgdinādau pathyāsīsarvarogān harati ca nitarāṃ nīlakaṇṭhābhidhānaḥ || 13.60 ||
tāratāmrarasapiṣṭikā śilā gandhatālasamabhāgikaṃ rasaiḥ |
āṭarūṣasurasārdrasambhavairmardaya prakuru golakaṃ tataḥ || 13.61 ||
mṛtsnayā ca pariveṣṭya golakaṃ yāmayugmamatha bhūdhare pacet |
gandhakena kuru tatsamaṃ tataścāṭarūṣakaṭukairvibhāvayet || 13.62 ||
śvāsakāsakarikesarīraso vallayasya parisevayedbudhaḥ || 13.63 ||
rasagandhakatāmrābhraṃ kaṇāśuṇṭhyūṣaṇaṃ samam |
bhūtamekaṃ viṣaṃ caikaṃ sūryaḥ kāsādināśanaḥ || 13.64 ||
gandhakaṃ maricaṃ sājyaṃ pibecchvāsakaphāpaham |
śilā hiṅgu viḍaṅgaṃ ca maricaṃ kuṣṭhasaindhavam |
madhvājyābhyāṃ lihetkarṣaṃ śvāsakāśakaphāpaham || 13.65 ||
vidāhiguruviṣṭambhirūkṣābhiṣyandibhojanaiḥ |
śītapānāśanasthānarajodhūmātapānilaiḥ |
vyāyāmakarmabhārādhvavegāghātāpatarpaṇaiḥ |
hikkā śvāsaśca kāsaśca nṛṇāṃ samupajāyate |
rasagandhakadhānyābhratālatāpyopalaṃ kramāt |
bhāgavṛddhaṃ vacākuṣṭhaharidrākṣāracitrakaiḥ || 13.66 ||
sapāṭhā lāṅgalī vyoṣasaindhavākṣaviṣaiḥ samam |
bhāvitaṃ bhṛṅganīreṇa hikkāvaisvaryakāsanut || 13.67 ||
pakvatāmre rasaḥ piṣṭo balinā hidhmināṃ hitaḥ || 13.68 ||
cūrṇaṃ pāṭhendravāruṇyorbhāṇḍe dattvātha kunaṭīm |
tatpṛṣṭhe śuddhasūtaṃ ca kunaṭyaṃśaṃ pradāpayet || 13.69 ||
sūtārdhaṃ kunaṭīcūrṇaṃ tasyārdhaṃ pūrvamūlikā |
cūrṇaṃ dattvā paceccullyā yāmāṣṭa mṛduvahninā |
śilāpūto raso nāma hanti hikkāṃ triguñjakaḥ || 13.70 ||
mṛtasūtaṃ mṛtaṃ tāmraṃ hiṅgu puṣkaramūlakam |
saindhavaṃ gandhakaṃ tālaṃ kaṭukaṃ cūrṇayetsamān || 13.71 ||
devadālīpunarnavayornirguṇḍīmeghanādayoḥ |
tiktakoṣātakīdrāvairdinaikaṃ mardayeddṛḍham || 13.72 ||
māṣamātraṃ lihetkṣaudraiḥ rasaṃ manthānabhairavam |
kapharogapraśāntyarthaṃ nimbakvāthaṃ pibedanu || 13.73 ||
viśvāditrikanirgatadravaniśā kīrapriyotthaṃ dalaṃ nīlagrīvagalālayaṃ surapatestārtīyanetrābhidham |
vidvatpuñjavatī kṛmipratibhaṭaṃ nirguṇḍikāvāriṇā tulyāṃśāścaṇakapramāṇavaṭikāḥ saśvāsakāsaghnikāḥ || 13.74 ||
kvāthaṃ rāsnābṛhatyagnibalādugdhaiśca pāyayet || 13.75 ||
hikvinaṃ pāyayeddhūmaṃ pattraiḥ śikhiniśodbhavaiḥ || 13.76 ||
karṣaikaṃ gandhakaṃ śuddhaṃ ghṛtaiścoṣṇodakaiḥ pibet |
kaphaṃ hantyatha vā kṣaudraiḥ pañcavaktrarasaḥ khalu || 13.77 ||
atyuccabhāṣaṇaviṣādhyayanābhighātasaṃdūṣaṇaiḥ prakupitāḥ pavanādayastu |
srotaḥsu te svaravaheṣu gatāḥ pratiṣṭhāṃ hanyuḥ svaraṃ bhavati cāpi hi ṣaḍvidhaḥ saḥ || 13.78 ||

[parpaṭī:: production]
rasaṃ dviguṇagandhena mardayitvā sabhṛṅgakam |
lohapātre ghṛtābhyakte drāvitaṃ badarāgninā || 13.79 ||
ūrdhvādho gomayaṃ dattvā kadalyāḥ komale dale |
snigdhayā lohadarvyā ca parpaṭākāratāṃ nayet || 13.80 ||
lohapātre vinikṣiptā lohaparpaṭikā bhavet |
tāmrapātre vinikṣiptā tāmraparpaṭikā bhavet || 13.81 ||

----------

*     ṭīkā - rasaratnasamuccayaṭīkā:
*     parpaṭīrasamāha rasaṃ dviguṇeti || 13.81;1
*     sabhṛṅgakaṃ bhṛṅgarājarasena mardayitvā taptaṃ drāvitaṃ tadgomayoparisthe kadale snigdhayā lohadarvyā prakṣipya tadupari kadalīdale nyubje gomayaṃ dattvā karatalādinā nipīḍya parpaṭākāraṃ kuryāt || 13.81;2
*     kadalīdalasthāne snigdhe lohapatre vinikṣiptā cellohaparpaṭikā bhavet || 13.81;3
*     athavā tāmrapatre snigdhe vinikṣiptā tāmraparpaṭikā bhavet || 13.81;4
*     rasopari tu kadalīdalādyeva deyam || 13.81;5

____________________


viṣapādaṃ ca yuñjīta tatsādhyeṣvāmayeṣu ca |
surasāyā jayantyāśca kanyakāṭarūṣakayoḥ || 13.82 ||
triphalāyā munerbhārṅgyā muṇḍyāstrikaṭucitrayoḥ |
bhṛṅgarājasya vahneśca pratyahaṃ dravabhāvitam || 13.83 ||
ārdrakasya rasenāpi saptadhā bhāvayetpunaḥ |
aṅgāraiḥ svedayedīṣatparpaṭarasamuttamam || 13.84 ||
guñjāṣṭakaṃ dadītāsya tāmbūlīpattrasaṃyutam |
pippalīdaśakaiḥ kvāthaṃ nirguṇḍyāścānupāyayet |
svarabhaṅge kaphe śvāse prayojyaḥ sarvadā rasaḥ || 13.85 ||
trikaṇṭakasya mūlāni śuṇṭhīṃ saṃkṣudya nikṣipet |
ajākṣīre sanīrārdhaṃ yāvatkṣīraṃ vipācayet || 13.86 ||
tatkṣīraṃ pāyayedrātrau sakaṇaṃ bhojane'pi ca |
kūṣmāṇḍaṃ varjayecciñcāṃ vṛntākaṃ karkaṭīmapi || 13.87 ||
āranālaṃ ca tailaṃ ca saṃsargaṃ ca vivarjayet |
māsatrayaṃ ca seveta kāsaśvāsanivṛttaye || 13.88 ||
sajīrahiṅgukavyoṣaiḥ śamayedgrahaṇīṃ rasaḥ |
daśamūlāmbhasā vātajvaraṃ trikaṭunā kapham || 13.89 ||
jvaraṃ madhukasāreṇa pañcakolena sarvajam |
yakṣmāṇaṃ madhupippalyā gomūtreṇa gudāṅkurān || 13.90 ||
śūlameraṇḍatailena pāṇḍuśophaṃ sagugguluḥ |
kuṣṭhāni bhṛṅgabhallātavākucī pañcanimbakaiḥ || 13.91 ||
dhattūrabījasaṃyogānmehonmādavināśinī |
apasmāraṃ nihantyāśu vyoṣanimbudalaiḥ saha || 13.92 ||
stanaṃdhayaśiśūnāṃ tu raso'yaṃ nitarāṃ hitaḥ |
pathyākṣacūrṇādivaśādvyādhīṃścānyān sudustarān || 13.93 ||
sajātīphalaśītodaṃ yojayetparpaṭīrasam |
pittājīrṇe śiraścāsya śītatoyena secayet || 13.94 ||
nasyaṃ niṣṭhīvanaṃ dhūmaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ vamanarecanam |
annaṃ rūkṣālpatīkṣṇoṣṇaṃ kaṭutiktakaṣāyakam |
cirakālasthitaṃ madyaṃ yojayetkapharogiṇe || 13.95 ||


________________________________________________________


 14
agnimāndyaṃ jvaraḥ śaityaṃ vāntiḥ śoṇitapūyayoḥ |
sattvahāniśca daurbalyaṃ rājarogasya lakṣaṇam || 14.1 ||
rasasya tulyabhāgena hemabhasma prakalpayet |
tālakaṃ gandhakaṃ tutthaṃ mākṣikaṃ rasakaṃ śilām || 14.2 ||
rasasāmyena yuñjīta tutthaṃ bhasmīkṛtaṃ nyaset |
vahnau bhasmīkṛtaṃ cetthaṃ mayūrakasututthakam || 14.3 ||
kiṃciṭṭaṅkaṇakaṃ dattvā mārjārasya viśā yutam |
prathamaṃ puṭayeddadhnā dvitīyaṃ madhunā saha || 14.4 ||
tālakaṃ śodhayedagre kūṣmāṇḍakṣārapācanāt |
taile pacettataḥ samyakcūrṇe vā pariśodhayet || 14.5 ||
gandhakaṃ śodhayeddugdhe rasakaṃ naravāriṇā |
mākṣikaṃ sindhusaṃyuktaṃ bījapūrarase pacet || 14.6 ||
jayantīdravasampiṣṭāṃ śilāṃ tatraiva pācayet |
ekīkṛtya tataḥ sarvamarkakṣīreṇa mardayet || 14.7 ||
jayantībhṛṅgarājābhyāṃ vāsāpāṭhākṛśānubhiḥ |
agastilāṅgalībhyāṃ ca pratyekaṃ divasaṃ śanaiḥ || 14.8 ||
tatastu golakaṃ baddhvā pacetpūrvavadāhṛtaḥ |
cūrṇayitvā tataḥ samyagbhāvayedārdrakāmbunā || 14.9 ||
saptadhā vyoṣaniryāsai rasaḥ kanakasundaraḥ |
guñjādvayaṃ trayaṃ vāsya rājayakṣmāpanuttaye || 14.10 ||
madhunā pippalībhiśca maricairvā ghṛtānvitaiḥ |
lehayedrogiṇaṃ vaidyo balāvasthāviśeṣavit || 14.11 ||
jayapālarajobhirvā śuṇṭhyā gavyaghṛtāktayā |
dadīta śūline prājño gulmine ca viśeṣataḥ || 14.12 ||
kādivarjyaṃ caretpathyaṃ hṛdyaṃ balyaṃ ca pūrvavat |
sannipāte dadītainamārdrakadravasaṃyutam |
guḍūcītriphalākvāthaiḥ saṃskṛto guggulurvaraḥ || 14.13 ||

[rājamṛgāṅkarasaḥ]
rasabhasma trayo bhāgā bhāgaikaṃ hemabhasmakam |
mṛtatāmrasya bhāgaikaṃ śilāgandhakatālakam || 14.14 ||
pratibhāgadvayaṃ śuddhamekīkṛtya vicūrṇayet |
varāṭānpūrayettena ajākṣīreṇa ṭaṅkaṇam || 14.15 ||
piṣṭvā tena mukhaṃ ruddhvā mṛdbhāṇḍe tannirodhayet |
śuddhaṃ gajapuṭe pacyāccūrṇayetsvāṅgaśītalam || 14.16 ||
raso rājamṛgāṅko'yaṃ caturguñjaḥ kṣayāpahaḥ |
daśapippalikākṣaudrairmaricaikonaviṃśatiḥ |
saghṛtairdāpayedvaidyo rogarājapraśāntaye || 14.17 ||

[śaṅkheśvararasaḥ]
śaṅkhasya valayānniṣkaṃ caturniṣkaṃ varāṭakam |
niṣkārdhaṃ nīlatutthasya sarvatulyaṃ tu gandhakam || 14.18 ||
gandhatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ nāgaṃ nāgatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ rasam |
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ rasatulyaṃ syānmardyaṃ pācyaṃ mṛgāṅkavat |
rājayakṣmaharaḥ so'yaṃ nāmnā śaṅkheśvaro mataḥ || 14.19 ||

[mṛgāṅkapoṭalīrasaḥ]
śaṅkhanābhiṃ gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ peṣayenniṣkaṣoḍaśa |
tena mūṣā prakartavyā tanmadhye bhasmasūtakam || 14.20 ||
niṣkārdhaṃ gandhakāttrīṇi cūrṇīkṛtya vinikṣipet |
ruddhvā tadveṣṭayedvastre mṛttikāṃ lepayedbahiḥ || 14.21 ||
śoṣyaṃ gajapuṭe pacyānmūṣayā saha cūrṇayet |
guñjaikamanupānena kṣayaṃ hanti mṛgāṅkavat || 14.22 ||

[hemagarbhapaṭolīrasaḥ]
dviniṣkaṃ bhasma sūtasya niṣkaikaṃ svarṇabhasmakam |
śuddhagandhakaniṣkau dvau cūrṇayitvā citrakadravaiḥ || 14.23 ||
dviyāmānte viśoṣyātha tena pūryā varāṭikāḥ |
varāṭānmṛṇmaye bhāṇḍe ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet || 14.24 ||
svāṅgaśītaṃ vicūrṇyātha poṭalīṃ hemagarbhitām |
mṛgāṅkavaccaturguñjaṃ bhakṣitaṃ rājayakṣmanut || 14.25 ||
svayamagnirasaṃ khādettriniṣkaṃ rājayakṣmanut || 14.26 ||

[pañcāmṛtarasaḥ]
bhasmasūtābhralohānāṃ śilājatuviṣaṃ samam |
guḍūcītriphalākvāthaiḥ saṃskṛtaṃ gugguluṃ tathā || 14.27 ||
mṛtaṃ nepālatāmraṃ ca sūtasthāne niyojayet |
ekīkṛtya dviguñjaṃ tadbhakṣayedrājayakṣmanut || 14.28 ||
pañcāmṛtaraso nāma hyanupānaṃ ca pūrvavat || 14.29 ||
haretkṣīrājagandhābhyāṃ jayantī vā kṣayāpahā || 14.30 ||

[kṣayaśāmakarasaḥ]
tulyaṃ pāradagandhakaṃ trikaṭukaṃ tābhyāṃ rajaḥ kambujaṃ taistulyaṃ ca bhavetkapardabhasitaṃ syātpāradāṭṭaṅkaṇam |
pādāṃśaṃ sakalaiḥ samānamaricaṃ lihyātkramātsājyakaṃ yāvanniṣkamitaṃ bhavetpratidinaṃ māsātkṣayaḥ śāmyati || 14.31 ||

[lokanātharasaḥ]
rasasya bhasmanā hema pādāṃśena prakalpayet |
gandhakaṃ dviguṇaṃ dattvā mardayeccitrakāmbunā || 14.32 ||
carācarāsye sampūrya ṭaṅkaṇena nirudhya ca |
bhāṇḍe cūrṇapralipte'tha kṣiptvā rundhīta mṛtsnayā || 14.33 ||
śoṣayitvā puṭedgarte'ratnimātre'parāhṇake |
svāṅgaśītalamuddhṛtya cūrṇayitvātha vinyaset || 14.34 ||
eṣa lokeśvaro nāma puṣṭivīryavivardhanaḥ |
guñjācatuṣṭayaṃ sājyaṃ maricaiśca samanvitam || 14.35 ||
khādetparamayā bhaktyā lokeśe sarvadarśinī |
aṅgakārśye'gnimāndye ca raso'yaṃ kāsahikkayoḥ || 14.36 ||
marīcairghṛtasaṃyuktaiḥ pradātavyo dinatrayam |
lavaṇaṃ varjayettatra sājyaṃ sadadhi bhojanam || 14.37 ||
ekaviṃśaddinaṃ yāvanmaricaṃ saghṛtaṃ pibet |
pathyaṃ mṛgāṅkavaddeyaṃ śayītottānapādataḥ || 14.38 ||
vamane sampravṛtte tu guḍūcīdravamāharet |
madhunā pāyayetsārdhaṃ dagdhavṛntākamāśayet || 14.39 ||
snānaṃ śītalatoyena mūrdhni dhārāṃ vinikṣipet |
jāte śleṣmavikāre tu kadalīphalamāharet || 14.40 ||
bhṛṣṭvā tanmaricaiḥ sārdhaṃ bhojayecchleṣmanuttaye |
ārdrakaṃ madhumiśraṃ vā guḍārdrakamatho'pi vā || 14.41 ||
bhṛṣṭvā kustumbarīmāṣānnistuṣāṃścūrṇayettataḥ |
śarkarāghṛtasammiśrāndadītāruciśāntaye || 14.42 ||
bhṛṣṭvā kustumbarīṃ samyagghṛte śarkarayā pibet |
elāṃ maricasaṃyuktāṃ yāvadvāntiḥ praśāmyati || 14.43 ||
ajamodaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ ca piṣṭvā takreṇa pāyayet |
kṛmikopapraśāntyarthaṃ kvāthaṃ vātaghnamustayoḥ || 14.44 ||
saṃskṛtya dugdhikāṃ vahnau vireke ca prayojayet |
īṣadbhṛṣṭvā jayācūrṇaṃ madhunā khādayenniśi || 14.45 ||
aṅgatode ghṛtenāṅgaṃ mardayitvoṣṇavāriṇā |
snāpayedrogiṇaṃ vaidyo lokanāthaṃ rasaṃ smaran || 14.46 ||

[vaidyanātharasaḥ]
śaṃkhasya valayaṃ niṣkaṃ caturniṣkaṃ varāṭikāḥ |
karṣāṃśaṃ nīlatutthaṃ ca tālaṃ gandhāśmaṭaṅkaṇam || 14.47 ||
tutthaṃ nāgarasaṃ cārdhaṃ niṣkāṃśaṃ pūrvavatpuṭet |
varāṭacūrṇaṃ maṇḍūrakalpitālepane pacet || 14.48 ||
asyārdhamāṣaṃ maricārdhamāṣaṃ tāmbūlavallīrasabhāvitaṃ ca |
tatpatraliptaṃ madhunāvalihyāddhaiyaṅgavīnena ghṛtena vāpi || 14.49 ||
nāḍīmārge nirgate cālpamalpaṃ pathyaṃ bhojyaṃ lokanāthopadiṣṭam |
yāme yāme caivamā maṇḍalāntātsiddhaṃ sadyaḥ śoṣajidvaidyanāthaḥ || 14.50 ||

[lokanātharasaḥ]
adhyardhaniṣkau rasatutthabhāgau pṛthakpṛthaggandhakaṭaṅkakarṣam |
śaṃkhasya karṣaṃ mṛtatāmrato dvau varāṭikānāṃ nava sampuṭasthān || 14.51 ||
paktvā pacedarkadaladravārdrān bhūyo'rdhabhāgena karīṣakāṇām |
asyārdhapādaṃ maricārdhabhāgaṃ gandhāśmaniṣkaṃ ca ghṛtena lihyāt || 14.52 ||
aśnīyātpūrvavatpathyaṃ vāsarāṇyekaviṃśatiḥ |
lokanātharaso nāmnā rogarājanikṛntanaḥ || 14.53 ||

[prāṇanātharasaḥ]
ayorajo viṃśatiniṣkamānaṃ vibhāvitaṃ bhṛṅgarasāḍhakena |
dhattūrabhārṅgitriphalārasārdraṃ tulyāṃśatāpyaṃ vipacetpuṭeṣu || 14.54 ||
sūtaṃ ca niṣkaṃ samabhāgatutthaṃ gandhopalau dvau caturo varāṭān |
paktvā puṭāgnau samalohacūrṇānpacettathā pūrvarasairvimiśrān || 14.55 ||
cūrṇe'sminmaricāḥ sapta tutthaṭaṅkaṇayordaśa |
saṃsṛjettatpṛthaṅniṣkān prāṇanāthāhvayoditaḥ || 14.56 ||
ardhapādo rasādbhakṣyaḥ kevalādrājayakṣmibhiḥ |
śoṣodarārśograhaṇījvaragulmādyupadrutaiḥ || 14.57 ||

[vajrarasaḥ]
karṣaṃ kharparasattvasya ṣaṇmāse hemni vidrute |
ṣaṇniṣkasūtaṃ gandhāśmanyaṣṭaniṣke praveśitam || 14.58 ||
pravālamuktāphalayoścūrṇaṃ hemasamāṃśayoḥ |
kramāddvitricaturniṣkaṃ mṛtāyaḥ sīsabhāskaram || 14.59 ||
cāṅgeryamlena yāmāṃstrīnmarditaṃ cūrṇitaṃ pṛthak |
dvau niṣkau nīlavaṭakavyomāyaskāntatālakāt || 14.60 ||
aṅkollakaṅguṇībījatutthebhyaścaturaḥ pṛthak |
aṣṭau ca ṭaṃkaṇakṣārādvarāṭānāṃ ca viṃśatiḥ || 14.61 ||
mahājambīranīrasya prasthadvandvena peṣayet |
etadaṣṭaśarāvasthaṃ śuddhaṃ khāryāstuṣasya ca || 14.62 ||
karīṣabhāre ca pacedatha māṣadvayaṃ tataḥ |
etāvadgandhakātpādaṃ maricādbhāvitādapi || 14.63 ||
madhunāloḍitaṃ lihyāttāmbūlīpatralepitam |
gate'sya ghaṭikāmātre pratiyāmaṃ ca pathyabhuk || 14.64 ||
no ceduddīpito vahniḥ kṣaṇāddhātūnpacatyataḥ |
dinamekaṃ niṣevyainaṃ tyājyānyāmaṇḍalaṃ tyajet || 14.65 ||
tataḥ paraṃ yatheṣṭāśī dvādaśābdaṃ sukhī bhavet |
ekamekaṃ dinaṃ bhuktvā varṣe varṣe mahārasam || 14.66 ||
varṣādau ca tyajettyājyaṃ dvādaśābdaṃ jarāṃ jayet |
eṣa vajraraso nāma kṣayaparvatabhedanaḥ || 14.67 ||

[mahāvīrarasaḥ]
niṣkau dvau tutthabhāgasya rasādekaṃ susaṃskṛtāt |
niṣkaṃ viṣasya dvau tīkṣṇātkarṣāṃśaṃ gandhamauktikāt || 14.68 ||
agniparṇīharilatābhṛṅgārdrasurasārasaiḥ |
marditaṃ lāṅgalīkandapralipte sampuṭe pacet || 14.69 ||
ardhapādaṃ ca poṭalyāḥ kākinyau dve viṣasya ca |
lihenmaricacūrṇaṃ ca madhunā poṭalīsamam || 14.70 ||
kṣayagrahaṇyatīsāravahnidaurbalyakāsinām |
pāṇḍugulmavatāṃ śreṣṭho mahāvīro hito rasaḥ || 14.71 ||
atisthūlasya pūyāsṛkkaphānudvamatākṣaye |
na yojayetkṣīrarasānviruddhakramatattvataḥ || 14.72 ||

[sāṃānyopāyaḥ]
mātuluṅgasya mūlāni lājacūrṇaṃ sasaindhavam |
pippalīmadhunā yuktaṃ khādedvāntipraśāntaye || 14.73 ||
rajanīśaṅkhapūgaṃ ca niṣkaikaṃ vāntināśanam |
niṣkārdhaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ vātha kākamācīdravaiḥ pibet || 14.74 ||
sugandhāṃ vā pibetkhādetsarvavāntipraśāntaye |
alaktakarasaṃ kṣaudrai raktavāntiharaṃ param || 14.75 ||

[tṛṣṇāhararasaḥ]
yuktaṃ gandhakapiṣṭyāyastālakaṃ svarṇamākṣikam |
yuktyā tadbhasmatāṃ nītaṃ tṛṣṇāchardinivāraṇam || 14.76 ||

[rājāvartarasaḥ]
rājāvarto rasaḥ śulbaṃ mākṣikaṃ ghṛtapācitam |
madhvājyaśarkarāyuktaṃ hanti sarvānmadātyayān || 14.77 ||
rājāvartarasaḥ śulbaṃ sūtagarbhe niyojitam |
yaṣṭīmadhurasairghṛṣṭaṃ ghṛtamadhye vipācitam || 14.78 ||
madhvājyaśarkarāyuktaṃ hanti sarvānmadātyayān || 14.79 ||

[bhairavanāthī pañcāmṛtaparpaṭī]
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ tāmraṃ sattvābhraṃ kāntalohakam |
kramavṛddhamidaṃ sarvaṃ śāṇeyau nāgavaṅgakau || 14.80 ||
drāvayitvaikataḥ sarvaṃ retayitvā tataścaret |
pṛthakpalamitaṃ gandhaṃ śilālaṃ vinidhāya ca || 14.81 ||
sarvaṃ khalle vinikṣipya mardayedamlavargataḥ |
tāpyaṃ nīlāñjanaṃ tālaṃ śilāgandhaṃ ca cūrṇitam || 14.82 ||
dattvā dattvā puṭettāvadyāvadviṃśativārakam |
lohāddviguṇasūtena tato dviguṇagandhataḥ || 14.83 ||
vidhāya kajjalīṃ ślakṣṇāṃ kṣiptvā tāṃ lohapātrake |
drāvayedbadarāṅgārairmṛdubhiścātha nikṣipet || 14.84 ||
hemādipañcalohānāṃ bhasma cātha viloḍayet |
atha tatkadalīpatre gomayasthe vinikṣipet || 14.85 ||
patreṇānyena saṃchādya kuryādyatnena cippiṭīm |
tasyopari kṣipetsadyo gomayaṃ stokameva ca || 14.86 ||
svataḥ śītaṃ samāhṛtya paṭacūrṇaṃ vidhāya ca |
nikṣipedūrdhvadaṇḍāyāṃ pālikāyāṃ tataḥ param || 14.87 ||
pūrvavadbadarāṅgārairmṛdubhirdrāvayecchanaiḥ |
tulyālakaśilāgandhaṃ palārdhaṃ viṣabhāvitam || 14.88 ||
pūrvaparpaṭikātulyaṃ tasmādalpaṃ muhurmuhuḥ |
jārayetpalikāmadhye yathā dahyenna parpaṭī || 14.89 ||
paliketi vinirdiṣṭā snehakṣepaṇayantrikā |
jīrṇe tālādike cūrṇe paṭacūrṇaṃ vidhīyatām || 14.90 ||
pūtīkarañjaṣaṭkolavyāghrīsaubhāñjanāṅghribhiḥ |
etaiḥ pañcapalaiḥ kvāthaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśāvaśeṣitam || 14.91 ||
tena kvāthena saṃsvedya śoṣayetsaptadhā hi tām |
viṣatinduphalodbhūtai rasairnirguṇḍikārasaiḥ || 14.92 ||
vibhāvya palikāmadhye kṣiptvā badaravahninā |
īṣatprasvedanaṃ kṛtvā sthāpayedatiyatnataḥ || 14.93 ||
uktā bhairavanāthena syātpañcāmṛtaparpaṭī |
vyoṣājyasahitā līḍhā guñjābījena saṃmitā || 14.94 ||
sarvalakṣaṇasampūrṇaṃ vinihanti kṣayāmayam |
śvāsaṃ kāsaṃ viṣūcīṃ ca pramehamudarāmayam || 14.95 ||
arocakaṃ ca duḥsādhyaṃ prasekaṃ chardihṛdbhavam |
sarvajaṃ gudarogaṃ ca śūlakuṣṭhānyaśeṣataḥ || 14.96 ||
vātajvaraṃ ca viḍbandhaṃ grahaṇīṃ kaphajāngadān |
ekadvandvatridoṣotthān rogānanyānmahāgadān || 14.97 ||
agnimāndyaṃ viśeṣeṇa hantīyaṃ parpaṭī dhruvam |
evaṃ samūhya dātavyā rogeṣu bhiṣaguttamaiḥ || 14.98 ||
tattadrogaharairyogaistattadrogānupānataḥ |
kṣayādisarvarogaghnī syātpañcāmṛtaparpaṭī || 14.99 ||
tilasarṣapabilvāmlakāravellakusumbhakam |
tyajetpārāvataṃ māṃsaṃ vṛntākaṃ kukkuṭaṃ tathā || 14.100 ||


________________________________________________________


 15
[athārśaḥ]
gudasya bahirantarvā jāyante carmakīlakāḥ |
sarvarogakarāḥ puṃsāmarśāṃsīti hi viśrutāḥ || 15.1 ||
rudhirasrāviṇasteṣāṃ pittajāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 15.2 ||
vātajā niḥsahotthānā udāvartaṃ prakurvate || 15.3 ||
śvayathuṃ śleṣmajāḥ kuryuḥ sarvaṃ kuryustridoṣajāḥ || 15.4 ||

[arśaḥkuṭhārarasaḥ]
śuddhasūtaṃ palaikaṃ tu dvipalaṃ śuddhagandhakam |
mṛtaṃ tāmraṃ mṛtaṃ lohaṃ pratyekaṃ tu palatrayam || 15.5 ||
tryūṣaṇaṃ lāṅgalī dantī pirukaṃ citrakaṃ tathā |
pratyekaṃ dvipalaṃ yojyaṃ yavakṣāraṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇam || 15.6 ||
ubhau pañcapalau yojyau saindhavaṃ palapañcakam |
dvātriṃśatpalagomūtraṃ snuhīkṣīraṃ ca tatsamam || 15.7 ||
mṛdvagninā pacetsthālyāṃ sarvaṃ yāvatsupiṇḍitam |
māṣadvayaṃ sadā khādedraso hyarśaḥkuṭhārakaḥ |
takreṇa dāḍimāmbhobhiḥ pakvakandena vātha tat || 15.8 ||
mṛtasūtārkahemābhratīkṣṇamuṇḍaṃ sagandhakam |
maṇḍūraṃ mākṣikaṃ tulyaṃ mardyaṃ kanyādravairdinam || 15.9 ||
andhamūṣāgataṃ pācyaṃ tridinaṃ tuṣavahninā |
cūrṇitaṃ sitayā māṣaṃ khādetpittārśasāṃ jayet || 15.10 ||

[sarvalokāśrayarasaḥ]
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ palaṃ gandhaṃ gandhārdhaṃ tālatāpyakam |
amṛtaṃ rasakaṃ caiva tālakārdhavibhāgikam || 15.11 ||
eteṣāṃ kajjalīṃ kuryāddṛḍhaṃ saṃmardya vāsaram |
tridinaṃ mardayeccātha dattvā nimbujalaṃ khalu || 15.12 ||
vaṭīkṛtya viśoṣyātha kācakupyāṃ nidhāpayet |
niṣkatulyārkapatreṇa pidhāyāsyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 15.13 ||
sārdhāṃgulamitotsedhaṃ mṛtsnayā tāṃ vilepya ca |
nato bhāṇḍatṛtīyāṃśe sikatāparipūrite || 15.14 ||
nidhāya sikatāmūrdhni sikatābhiḥ prapūrayet |
ruddhvāsyaṃ tadadho vahniṃ jvālayetsārdhavāsaram || 15.15 ||
svāṃgaśītalitaṃ kācaghaṭādākṛṣya taṃ rasam |
paṭacūrṇaṃ vidhāyātha tāmramabhraṃ paladvayam || 15.16 ||
palārdhamamṛtaṃ caiva maricaṃ ca catuṣpalam |
ekīkṛtya kṣipetsarvaṃ nārikelakaraṇḍake || 15.17 ||
sājyo guñjādvimāno harati rasavaraḥ sarvalokāśrayo'yaṃ vātaśleṣmottharogāngudajanitagadaṃ śoṣapāṇḍvāmayaṃ ca |
yakṣmāṇaṃ vātaśūlaṃ jvaramapi nikhilaṃ vahnimāndyaṃ ca gulmaṃ tattadrogaghnayogaiḥ sakalagadacayaṃ dīpanaṃ tatkṣaṇena || 15.18 ||

[arśoghnavaṭaka]
arśoghnaṃ vaṭakaṃ vakṣye putrakaṃ śṛṇu bhadraka |
pippalī pippalīmūlavanasūraṇacitrakam || 15.19 ||
māricaṃ kaṇṭakārī ca raktapuṣpī samāṃśakā |
palamekaṃ pṛthaksarvaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ dṛṣadi peṣayet || 15.20 ||
gajājapaśumūtreṣu śubhe bhāṇḍe vinikṣipet |
mṛdvagninā pacetsarvaṃ cūrṇaśeṣaṃ yathā bhavet || 15.21 ||
loṇatrayaṃ ca tatraiva palamekaṃ tu nikṣipet |
akṣapramāṇavaṭakānkuryādevaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 15.22 ||
triṃśaddināni matimānarśoghnaṃ dīpanaṃ param |
ghṛtatakrasamāyuktaṃ bhojanaṃ saṃpradāpayet || 15.23 ||

[arśoghnī vaṭikā]
gandhakaṃ tāratāmraṃ ca kṛtvā caikatra piṣṭikām |
tatsamaṃ cābhrakaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ gandhakātpañcamāṃśakam || 15.24 ||
viṣaṃ ca ṣoḍaśāṃśena dvau bhāgau sūtakasya ca |
ekīkṛtya prayatnena jambīradravamarditam || 15.25 ||
bhājane mṛṇmaye sthāpya varākvāthena bhāvayet |
daśamūlaśatāvaryoḥ kvāthe pācyaḥ krameṇa hi || 15.26 ||
athottārya prayatnena vaṭikāṃ kārayedbudhaḥ |
guñjātrayapramāṇena hanti śūlaṃ gudāṅkuram || 15.27 ||

[mūlakuṭhārarasaḥ]
varanāgaṃ tathā vyomasattvaṃ śulbaṃ ca tīkṣṇakam |
sarvamekatra vidrāvya kṣiptvālaṃ cālpamalpakam || 15.28 ||
cālayedaniśaṃ yāvattālakaṃ triguṇaṃ khalu |
tatastena vimardyātha piṣṭīṃ kuryādrasena tu || 15.29 ||
tato bhallātakīvṛkṣamūlāntasthāṃ khanecca tām |
māsādākṛṣya tāṃ piṣṭīṃ gavyadugdhe vinikṣipet || 15.30 ||
tato bhallātakītailaṃ hṛtaṃ pātālayantrataḥ |
āyase bhājane snigdhe piṣṭikāṃ tāṃ niveśya ca || 15.31 ||
prasthamātraṃ hi tattailaṃ jārayedatiyatnataḥ |
tattailabhāvitairgandhaiḥ puṭitvā bhasmatāṃ vrajet || 15.32 ||
tataḥ kārtikamāsotthakoraṃṭadalajai rasaiḥ |
rasaṃ saṃmardya saṃmardya gharme saṃsthāpya mārayet || 15.33 ||
tadbhasma melayetpūrvabhasmanā samabhāgikam |
vanasūraṇanirguṇḍīmahārāṣṭrībhakaṇṭikā || 15.34 ||
vajravallī śikhī caiṣāṃ rasaiḥ piṣṭvā viśoṣayet |
trivāraṃ mārkavadrāvairdrāvayitvā viśoṣayet |
cūrṇīkṛtya prayatnena kṣipetkācakaraṇḍake || 15.35 ||
so'yaṃ mūlakuṭhārako rasavaro dīpyāgnivellottamāsaṃyuktaḥ saghṛtaśca vallatulitaḥ saṃsevito nāśayet |
arśāṃsyānananāsikākṣigudajānyatyugrapīḍāni ca plīhānaṃ grahaṇīṃ ca gulmayakṛtau māndyaṃ ca kuṣṭhāmayān || 15.36 ||
rasagrastasamudgīrṇagandhakasya palatrayam |
mṛtasūtābhratāmrāyaḥ karṣaṃ karṣaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 15.37 ||
palaṃ hiṅgulacūrṇasya mākṣikasya palatrayam |
palaṃ kampillakasyāpi viṣasyārdhapalaṃ tathā || 15.38 ||
saptāhaṃ mardayetsarvaṃ dattvā cūrṇodakaṃ muhuḥ |
tatastadgolakaṃ kṛtvā saptāhaṃ cātape kṣipet || 15.39 ||
guḍacūrṇaṃ śilācūrṇaṃ limpedaṅgulikāghanam |
tripalaṃ gandhakaṃ dattvā krauñcyāmatha ca golakam || 15.40 ||
golakasyopariṣṭācca kṣipettālapalatrayam |
saṃrudhyātiprayatnena dadyādgajapuṭaṃ khalu || 15.41 ||
svāṅgaśītalamāhṛtya golakaṃ lepanaiḥ saha |
vicūrṇya saptavāraṃ hi viṣatinduphalodbhavaiḥ || 15.42 ||
dravairathātape śuṣkaṃ kṣipedramye karaṇḍake |
triṃśadaṃśena vaikrāntabhasma tasminvinikṣipet || 15.43 ||
ayaṃ hi nandīśvarasampradiṣṭo raso viśiṣṭaḥ khalu rogahantā |
niḥśeṣarogeṣvahatapratāpo mahodayapratyayasāranāmā || 15.44 ||
hanyātsarvagudāmayānkṣayagadaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ ca mandāgnitāṃ śūlādhmānagadaṃ kaphaṃ śvasanatāmunmādakāpasmṛtī |
sarvā vātarujo mahājvaragadānnānāprakārāṃstathā vātaśleṣmabhavaṃ mahāmayacayaṃ duṣṭagrahaṇyāmayam || 15.45 ||

[kanakasundararasaḥ]
syādrasaṃ dhautamākṣīkaṃ kāntābhraṃ nāgahāṭakam |
pṛthvībhaṭena saṃtulyaṃ sarvatulyaṃ ca gandhakam || 15.46 ||
dattvā vidyādhare yantre puṭedāraṇyakotpalaiḥ |
svāṅgaśītalamuddhṛtya tryūṣaṇena vimiśrayet || 15.47 ||
arśovyādhau kaṭīśūle cakṣuḥśūle ca dāruṇe |
sannipāte kṣaye kāse śvāse mandānale jvare || 15.48 ||
karṇaśūle śiraḥśūle dantaśūle prayojayet |
pīnase plīhni hṛcchūle granthivāte ca dāruṇe || 15.49 ||
ekāṅge vā dhanurvāte kampavāte ca mūrchite |
jvarāṃśca viṣamān sarvānhanti rogānanekadhā || 15.50 ||
sevitaḥ pathyayogena rasaḥ kanakasundaraḥ |
guñjāmātraṃ dadītāsya yathāyuktānupānataḥ || 15.51 ||
ghṛtena saṃyuto vāte madhunā paittike jvare |
pippalyā ślaiṣmike deyaḥ pittodbhūte sacandanaḥ || 15.52 ||
takreṇa śleṣmavātotthe vātapitte ghṛtānvitaḥ |
śleṣmapitte cārdrakeṇa nirguṇḍyā sānnipātike || 15.53 ||
phalatrayeṇa śūleṣu viṣameṣu jvareṣvapi |
ārdrakeṇātha vā dadyādvahnimāndye viśeṣataḥ || 15.54 ||
abhiṣyande śiraḥśūle gāyatrībolasaṃyutam |
pakṣimāṃsasamāyuktaṃ kaphavāte ca mūrchite || 15.55 ||
ekāṅge ca dhanurvāte kṣīrayuktaṃ ca pīnase |
pāṇḍuroge kṣaye kāse maricājyaiśca kāmale || 15.56 ||
ajamodāviḍaṅgaiśca nābhiśūle'gnimāndyajit |
rūkṣajvare'rucau deyaḥ kadalīphalasaṃyutaḥ |
bolenārdhakaṭīśūle bhāṣitaṃ nāgabodhinā || 15.57 ||

[arkeśaḥ]
nāgaṃ pāradagandhakaṃ trilavaṇaṃ vāyarkajaṃ melayedekaikaṃ ca palaṃ palaṃ trayamataḥ pañca kramānmardayet |
sarvaṃ taddivasatrayaṃ tadanu taddattvā puṭaṃ bhāvanāḥ kuryātsatriphalāgnivetasarasaiḥ pañcādhikā viṃśatiḥ || 15.58 ||
pañcaitatkramaśastato guḍabhavairdatto'sya vallo jalairhantyarśāṃsyakhilāni sūraṇaghṛtaistasyānnamasminhitam |
arkeśaḥ parivarjyatāmiti muniḥ śrīvāsudevo'vadatkūṣmāṇḍīphalamāṣapāyasamativyāyāmamarkātapam || 15.59 ||

[tīkṣṇamukharasaḥ]
rasendrahemārkabiḍālagolasurāyasalohamalābhragandhāḥ |
tāpyaṃ ca kanyārasamardito'yaṃ pakvaḥ puṭe tīkṣṇamukho'rśasāṃ syāt || 15.60 ||

[arśaḥkuṭhārarasaḥ]
śreṣṭhā dantyagniyugmatrikaṭukahalinīpīlukumbhaṃ vipakvaṃ prasthe mūtrasya sasnukpayasi rasapalaṃ dve pale gandhakasya |
lohasya trīṇi tāmrātkuḍavamatha rajaḥkṣārayoścāpi pañca kṣiptvā sthālyāṃ pacettu jvalati dahanataścūrṇamarśaḥkuṭhāraḥ || 15.61 ||

[trailokyatilakarasaḥ]
vajrakṛṣṇābhrajaṃ sattvaṃ śodhitaṃ kācaṭaṅkaṇam |
