retayitvā rajaḥkṛtvā bharjayitvā ghṛtena tat || 15.62 ||
aṣṭāṃśasasyakopetaṃ puṭedvāratrayaṃ tataḥ |
trivāraṃ nṛpavartena luṅgasvarasayoginā || 15.63 ||
caturvāraṃ ca varṣābhūvāsāmatsyākṣikārasaiḥ |
guggulutriphalākvāthaistriṃśadvārāṇi yatnataḥ || 15.64 ||
tulyāṃśarasagandhotthakajjalyāṣṭāṃśabhāgayā |
puṭetpañcāśataṃ vārānmardayecca puṭe puṭe || 15.65 ||
śodhitaṃ retitaṃ kāntasatvaṃ ca ghṛtamarditam |
puṭedaṣṭāṃśadaradaiḥ saṃyuktaṃ lakucāmbunā || 15.66 ||
daśavāraṃ tathā samyaktāraṃ śuddhaṃ manohvayā |
tathā viṃśativārāṇi balinā mīnadṛgrasaiḥ || 15.67 ||
daśavārāṇi tāpyena kṛṣṇagoghṛtayoginā |
ubhayaṃ samabhāgaṃ tatpuṭennirguṃḍikārasaiḥ || 15.68 ||
rasagandhotthakajjalyā daśavāraṃ puṭetpunaḥ |
tasminnaṣṭāṃśabhāgena kṣipedvaikrāntabhasmakam || 15.69 ||
rājāvartakalāṃśena samabhāgena parpaṭī |
tatsarvaṃ parimardyātha bhāvayitvārdrakāmbunā || 15.70 ||
guḍūcyāḥ svarasenāpi bhūkadambarasena vā |
bhṛṅgarājarasenāpi citramūlarasena ca || 15.71 ||
vyoṣagañjākinīkandairbhūyo'pyārdradraveṇa ca |
paṭacūrṇamataḥ kṛtvā kṣipecchuddhakaraṇḍake || 15.72 ||
trailokyatilakaḥ so'yaṃ khyātaḥ sarvarasottamaḥ |
sarvavyādhiharaḥ śrīmāñchambhunā parikīrtitaḥ || 15.73 ||
udāvartaṃ ca viḍbandhaṃ vyathāṃ ca jaṭharodbhavām |
lohalaṃ mandabuddhitvaṃ śūlitvamapi vandhyatām || 15.74 ||
sūtirogānaśeṣāṃśca śūlaṃ nānāvidhaṃ tathā |
pariṇāmākhyaśūlaṃ ca tathā bhindyātsamutkaṭam || 15.75 ||
raktagulmaṃ ca nārīṇāṃ rajaḥśūlaṃ ca duḥsaham |
anupānaṃ ca pathyaṃ ca tattadrogānurūpataḥ || 15.76 ||

[sāmānyopacāraḥ]
vacāhiṅguviḍaṅgāni saindhavaṃ jīranāgaram |
maricaṃ pippalī kuṣṭhaṃ pathyā vahnyajamodakam || 15.77 ||
kramottaraguṇaṃ cūrṇaṃ sarveṣāṃ dviguṇaṃ guḍam |
karṣaṃ coṣṇajalenānupibedvātārśasāṃ jaye || 15.78 ||
mṛtaṃ lohaṃ cendrayavaṃ śuṇṭhībhallātacitrakam |
bilvamajjāviḍaṅgāni pathyā tulyaṃ vicūrṇayet || 15.79 ||
sarvatulyaṃ guḍaṃ yojyaṃ karṣaṃ bhuktvārśasāṃ jayet |
śleṣmārśasāṃ praśāntyarthaṃ deyamānandabhairavam |
mṛtatāmreṇa santulyaṃ deyaṃ guñjātrayaṃ hi tat || 15.80 ||
kusumbhamṛdupatrāṇi kāñjikenaiva pācayet |
śākavadbhakṣayennityamarśorogapraśāntaye || 15.81 ||

[sāmānyalepaḥ]
devadālyāśca bījāni saindhavena sucūrṇitaḥ |
āranālena lepo'yaṃ mūlaroganikṛntanaḥ || 15.82 ||
kāñcanīkusumaṃ cūrṇaṃ śaṅkhacūrṇaṃ manaḥśilām |
gajapippalikātoyairlepo hyarśaḥkuṭhārakaḥ || 15.83 ||
devadālyāḥ kaṣāyeṇa hyarśoghnaṃ śaucamācaret |
gudaniḥsaraṇaṃ cāpi śāntimāyāti nānyathā || 15.84 ||
āranālena sampiṣṭā sabījā kaṭutumbikā |
saguḍā hanti lepena durnāmāni samūlataḥ || 15.85 ||
pīlutailena saṃliptā vartikā gudamadhyagā |
ghātayatyarśasāṃ śīghraṃ sakalāṃ vedanāṃ tathā || 15.86 ||
arkakṣīraṃ snuhīkāṃḍaṃ kaṭulāvupatrakam |
karaṃjaṃ chāgamūtreṇa lepaḥ srāvyarśasāṃ hitaḥ || 15.87 ||
śigrumūlārkajaiḥ patrairlepanaṃ hitamarśasām || 15.88 ||


________________________________________________________


 16
rūkṣaiḥ kodravajīrṇamudgacaṇakaiḥ kruddho'nilo'dho vahan ruddhvā vartma malaṃ viśoṣya kurute viṇmūtrasaṃgaṃ tataḥ |
hṛtpṛṣṭhodaravastimastakarujaḥ saśvāsakāsaṃ jvaraṃ gacchannūrdhvamasau hi nūnamaniśaṃ kopādudāvartayet || 16.1 ||
kaṃkuṣṭhahiṃgusiṃdhūtthatrivṛddaṃtīvacābhayāḥ |
citrakasya tu mūlaṃ ca kalkīkṛtya pacedghṛtam || 16.2 ||
caturguṇe gavāṃ kṣīre yuktaṃ snukkṣīramātrayā |
udāvartodarān hanti pānena sarvadā || 16.3 ||

[atisāraḥ]
atyambupānatilapiṣṭavirūḍharūkṣaśuṣkāmiṣādhyaśanabaddhamalagrahādyaiḥ |
kruddho'nilo'tisaraṇāya ca kalpate'gniṃ hatvā malaṃ śithilayannapi toyadhātūn || 16.4 ||

[dardurarasaḥ]
suślakṣṇatīkṣṇacūrṇaṃ tu rasendrasamabhāgikam |
kāṃcanārarasairghṛṣṭaṃ sarvātīsāranāśanam || 16.5 ||
piṣṭaḥ samena tīkṣṇena kāñcanārāṃbumarditaḥ |
puṭapakvo'tisāraghnaḥ sūto'yaṃ dardurāhvayaḥ || 16.6 ||
hiṃgulaṃ vatsanābhaṃ ca maricaṃ ṭaṃkaṇaṃ kaṇā |
mardayetsamabhāgaṃ ca raso hyānandabhairavaḥ || 16.7 ||
guṃjaikāṃ vārdhaguñjāṃ vā balaṃ jñātvā pradāpayet |
madhunā lehayeccānu kuṭajasya phalaṃ tvacam || 16.8 ||
cūrṇitaṃ karṣamātraṃ tu tridoṣotthātisārajit |
dadhyannaṃ dāpayetpathyaṃ gavājyaṃ takrameva vā |
pipāsāyāṃ jalaṃ śītaṃ vijayā ca hitā niśi || 16.9 ||

[sudhāsārarasaḥ]
pṛthakpalikagaṃdhāśmasūtasaṃjātakajjalīm |
pradrāvya nikṣipedvyoma palaikaṃ gatacandrikam || 16.10 ||
kāṣṭhenāloḍya tatsarvaṃ kṣipetkuṭajapatrake |
punaḥ saṃcūrṇya yatnena bhāvayettadanantaram || 16.11 ||
bālatinduphaladrāvaiḥ kṣīrairauduṃbaraistathā |
aralutvagrasaiścāpi dugdhinīsvarasaistathā || 16.12 ||
puṭapakvasya bālasya dāḍimasya rasaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
kṛṣṇakāmbojikāmūlarasaiḥ kuṭajavalkajaiḥ || 16.13 ||
tulyāṃśaviśvagāṃdhārīcūrṇaṃ dvipalikaṃ kṣipet |
mustāvatsakadīpyāgnimocasāraṃ sajīrakam || 16.14 ||
vatsanābhaṃ ca karṣāṃśaṃ pratyekaṃ tatra nikṣipet |
vicūrṇya bhāvayedbhūyaḥ śuṇṭhīkvāthena saptadhā || 16.15 ||
itthaṃ siddho rasaḥ piṣṭaḥ karaṇḍe viniveśayet |
sudhāsāra iti khyātaḥ sudhārasasamadyutiḥ || 16.16 ||
dīpanaḥ pācano grāhī hṛdyo rucikarastathā |
doṣatrayātisāraṃ ca durjayaṃ bheṣajāntaraiḥ || 16.17 ||
āmaṃ caivamāraktaṃ ca jvarātīsārameva ca |
sātisārāṃ viṣūcīṃ ca pratibadhnāti tatkṣaṇāt |
mānyamānavyatikrāntiriva puṇyaphalodayam || 16.18 ||

[rasottamaḥ]
piṣṭaviśvābdakalkena vidhāya khalu cakrikām |
nikṣipetsvedanīyantre paktvārdhaghaṭikāvadhi || 16.19 ||
ākṛṣya tajjalairevaṃ saṃpramardyāharedrasam |
sudhāsārarasaṃ tatra kṣiptvā dhānyakasaṃmitam || 16.20 ||
pūrvoditeṣu rogeṣu pradadīta bhiṣagvaraḥ |
gotakreṇātha dadhnā vā pathyaṃ deyaṃ hitaṃ mitam || 16.21 ||
bālarambhāphalaṃ gurvīphalaṃ bilvaphalaṃ tathā |
āmrapeśī ca madhukaṃ vṛntākaṃ ca praśasyate || 16.22 ||
sarvātisāraṃ grahaṇīṃ ca hikkāṃ mandāgnimānāhamarocakaṃ ca |
nihanti sadyo vihitāmapāke dvitriprayogeṇa rasottamo'yam || 16.23 ||
sāṃbusthālīmukhābaddhe vastre pākyaṃ nidhāya ca |
pidhāya pacyate yatra svedanīyantramucyate || 16.24 ||

[lokeśvararasaḥ]
dvau bhāgau gaṃdhakasyāṣṭau śaṃkhacūrṇasya yojayet |
ekameva rasasyāṃśamarkakṣīreṇa mardayet || 16.25 ||
citrakasya draveṇaivaṃ śoṣayitvā punaḥ punaḥ |
ekīkṛtya rasenātha kṣāraṃ dattvā tadardhakam || 16.26 ||
arkakṣīreṇa kurvīta golakānatha śoṣayet |
nirudhya cūrṇalipte'tha bhāṇḍe dadyātpuṭaṃ tataḥ || 16.27 ||
lokeśvararaso hyeṣa grahaṇīrogakṛntanaḥ |
guñjācatuṣṭayaṃ cāsya marīcājyasamanvitam |
dadīta dadhibhaktaṃ ca pathyaṃ lokeśvare tathā || 16.28 ||

[lokanātharasaḥ]
mṛtapāradabhāgaikaṃ catvāraḥ śuddhagaṃdhakāt |
yāmaṃ ca mardayetkhalle tena pūrvā varāṭakāḥ || 16.29 ||
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ tu gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ piṣṭvā tena mukhaṃ lipet |
varāṭānāṃ prayatnena ruddhvā bhāṇḍe puṭe pacet || 16.30 ||
svāṅgaśītaṃ samuddhṛtya tataścūrṇya varāṭakāḥ |
lokanātharaso nāmnā kṣaudrairguñjācatuṣṭayam || 16.31 ||
nāgarātiviṣāmustādevadāruvacānvitam |
kaṣāyamanupānaṃ syādvātātīsāranāśanaḥ || 16.32 ||
nāgabhasmarasavyomagandhairardhapalonmitaiḥ |
kurvīta kajjalīṃ ślakṣṇāṃ prakṣipettadanantaram || 16.33 ||
dvipalonmitarālāyāṃ drutāyāṃ parimiśritām |
bhṛṣṭairyakṣākṣasiṃdhūtthavacāvyoṣadvijīrakaiḥ || 16.34 ||
sapathyāvijayādīpyaistulyāṃśairavacūrṇitaiḥ |
melayetprāktanaṃ kalkaṃ bhāvayettadanantaram || 16.35 ||
mahāniṃbatvacāsāraiḥ kāṃbojīmūlajadravaiḥ |
rasairnāgabalāyāśca guḍūcyāśca tridhā tridhā |
tataśca guṭikāḥ kāryā badarāsthipramāṇataḥ || 16.36 ||
hanyādeva hi nāgasundararaso vallonmitaḥ sevitaḥ |
nānātīsaraṇāmayaṃ gudaparibhraṃśaṃ tathā biṃbiśim || 16.37 ||

[ṣaṇniṣkatailam]
ṣaṇniṣkaṃ tilatailasya niṣkaṃ jambīrajaṃ rasam |
lavaṇaṃ pañcaguñjaṃ ca aṃgulyā mardayeddṛḍham |
āmavātārtasāraghnaṃ lihetpathyaṃ ca pūrvavat || 16.38 ||
[saṃgrahaṇī]
malaṃ saṃgṛhya saṃgṛhya kadācidatirecayet |
aruciḥ śvayathurmāndyaṃ grahaṇīrogalakṣaṇam || 16.39 ||

[vajrakapāṭarasaḥ]
mṛtasūtābhrakaṃ gandhaṃ yavakṣāraṃ saṭaṅkaṇam |
vacā jayā samaṃ sarvaṃ jayantī bhṛṃgajadravaiḥ || 16.40 ||
sajaṃbīraistryahaṃ mardyaṃ śoṣayettaṃ ca golakam |
mandavahnau śanaiḥ svedyaṃ yāmārdhaṃ lauhapātrake || 16.41 ||
rasasāmye pratiniśā deyā mocarasastathā |
bhāvayedvijayādrāvaiḥ śoṣyaṃ peṣyaṃca saptadhā |
raso vajrakapāṭo'yaṃ niṣkārdhaṃ madhunā lihet || 16.42 ||

[agnikumārarasaḥ]
dagdhāṃ kapardikāṃ piṣṭvā tryūṣaṇaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ viṣam |
gandhakaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ ca tulyaṃ jambīrajairdravaiḥ || 16.43 ||
mardayedbhakṣayenmāṣaṃ maricājyaṃ lihedanu |
nihanti grahaṇīrogaṃ pathyaṃ takraudanaṃ hitam || 16.44 ||
hiṅgulaṃ maricaṃ gandhaṃ pippalī ṭaṅkaṇaṃ viṣam |
kanakasya ca bījāni samāṃśaṃ vijayādravaiḥ || 16.45 ||
mardayedyāmamātraṃ tu caṇamātraṃ vaṭīkṛtam |
bhakṣayedgrahaṇīṃ hanti rasaḥ kanakasundaraḥ || 16.46 ||
agnimāndyaṃ jvaraṃ tīvramatisāraṃ ca nāśayet |
dadhyannaṃ dāpayetpathyaṃ gavyājaṃ takrameva vā || 16.47 ||

[grahaṇīhararasaḥ]
rasābhragandhāḥ kramavṛddhabhāgā jayārasena tridinaṃ vimardyāḥ |
gadyāṇakārdhaṃ madhunā sametaṃ dadīta pathyaṃ dadhibhaktakaṃ ca || 16.48 ||
hiṃgulasthitamaheśvarabījaṃ pātayantravidhinā haraṇīyam |
gaṃdhaṭaṃkaṇamṛtābhrakatulyaṃ kokilākṣamatha cāyasakhalle || 16.49 ||
mardanīyamabhidhāraṇayukte dhūmahīnadahanopari saṃsthe |
yāvadeṣa jalaśoṣaṇadakṣo jīrakārdrakayutena sa vallaḥ || 16.50 ||
saṃgrahajvaramatisrutigulmānarśasāṃ ca vinihanti samūham |
vāsudevakathito rasarājaścaṇḍasaṃgrahagadaikakapāṭaḥ || 16.51 ||

[laghusiddhābhrakarasaḥ]
samāṃśaṃ rasagaṃdhābhradaradaṃ ca viśodhitam |
lohakhalle vinikṣipya gavyājyena samanvitam || 16.52 ||
droṇīcullyāṃ nyasetkhallaṃ sāṅgārāyāṃ prayatnataḥ |
mardakenāpi lauhena mardayeddivasadvayam || 16.53 ||
iti siddho raseṃdro'yaṃ laghusiddhābhrako mataḥ |
vallatulyo raso jīravāriṇā sahitaḥ prage || 16.54 ||
pīto harati vegena grahaṇīmatidurdharām |
atisāraṃ mahāghoraṃ sātisāraṃ jvaraṃ tathā || 16.55 ||
pācano dīpano hṛdyo gātralāghavakārakaḥ |
nāgārjunena kathitaḥ sadyaḥ pratyayakārakaḥ || 16.56 ||

[sarvarogarasaḥ]
rasaṃ palamitaṃ tulyaṃ śuddhanāgena saṃyutam |
drāvayitvāyase pātre sataile nikṣipetkṣitau || 16.57 ||
tato drute vinikṣipya gaṃdhake tadviloḍya ca |
punarāyasapātre tatkṣiptvā pradrāvya nikṣipet || 16.58 ||
tattulyaṃ jārayettālaṃ punaḥ saṃcūrṇya pūrvavat |
tattulyāṃ jārayetsamyakkunaṭīṃ pariśodhitām || 16.59 ||
tattulyaṃ cūrṇite tasminkṣipennāgaṃ nirutthakam |
tāvadeva mṛtaṃ tāpyaṃ sarvamanyacca tatsamam || 16.60 ||
tīkṣṇāyaḥ kharparaṃ vyoma hiṃgulaṃ ca śilājatu |
pṛthakkarṣāṃśamānena ṣaṭkolaṃ kaṭphalaṃ miśīm || 16.61 ||
dīpyakaṃ ca caturjātaṃ reṇukośīravellakam |
tuṃbaruṃ bhārṅgikāṃ rāsnāṃ kaṅkolaṃ corapuṣkaram || 16.62 ||
riṅgiṇīṃ ciratiktaṃ ca bījānyunmattakasya ca |
paladvayaṃ ca lāṃgalyāḥ sarveṣāṃ dvādaśāṃśakam || 16.63 ||
vatsanābhaṃ sitaṃ bhūri vinikṣipya tataḥ param |
triphalānāṃ daśāṅghrīṇāṃ kaṣāyeṇa tataḥ param || 16.64 ||
jayantyārdrakavāsānāṃ mārkavasya rasaistathā |
bhāvayitvā ca kartavyā vaṭakāścaṇakopamāḥ || 16.65 ||
ekaikā vaṭikā sevyā kuryāttīvratarāṃ kṣudhām |
viṣūcīmaratiṃ hikkāṃ sevyaṃ svādu ca śītalam || 16.66 ||
sāmāṃ ca grahaṇīṃ sadāṅgatudanaṃ śoṣotkaṭaṃ pāṇḍutāmārtiṃ vātakaphatridoṣajanitāṃ śūlaṃ ca gulmāmayam |
hikkādhmānaviṣūcikāṃ ca kasanaṃ śvāsārśasāṃ vidradhiṃ sarvāropyavaṭī kṣaṇādvijayate rogāṃstathānyānapi || 16.67 ||

[grahaṇīgajakesarīrasaḥ]
rasagaṃdhakayoḥ kṛtvā kajjalīṃ tulyabhāgayoḥ |
drāvayitvāyase pātre rasatulyaṃ vinikṣipet || 16.68 ||
carācarabhavaṃ bhasma tatra mākṣikasaṃbhavam |
gaṃdhapāṣāṇasahitaṃ pātre lohamaye kṣipet || 16.69 ||
tatkāṣṭhena viloḍyātha nikṣipetkadalīdale |
tata ācchādya saṃcūrṇya nidhāyāyasabhājane || 16.70 ||
akṣamātraṃ kṣipedbhasma tatra mākṣikasambhavam |
samyaṅniścandratāṃ nītaṃ vyomabhasma palonmitam || 16.71 ||
viṣaṃ viṣāṃ ca gandhārīṃ mocasāraṃ sajīrakam |
sarvaṃ samāṃśikaṃ kṛtvā rase cārdhāṃśikaṃ kṣipet || 16.72 ||
sarvametanmardayitvā bhāvayedatiyatnataḥ |
jayaṃtyā ca mahārāṣṭryā gañjākinyāśvagandhayā || 16.73 ||
pañcakolakaṣāyaiśca kuryāccūrṇaṃ tataḥ param |
itthaṃ siddho rasaḥ so'yaṃ grahaṇīgajakesarī || 16.74 ||
nāmato nandinā proktaḥ karmataśca sudhānidhiḥ |
vallena pramitaścāyaṃ rasaḥ śuṇṭhyā ghṛtāktayā || 16.75 ||
sevito grahaṇīṃ hanti satsaṅga iva vigraham |
pathyamatra pradātavyaṃ svalpājyaṃ dadhitakrayuk || 16.76 ||
hitaṃ mitaṃ ca viśadaṃ laghu grāhi rucipradam |
pācano dīpano'tyarthamāmaghno rucikārakaḥ || 16.77 ||
tattadauṣadhayogena sarvātīsāranāśanaḥ |
badhnannapi malaṃ śīghraṃ nādhmānaṃ kurute nṛṇām || 16.78 ||

[śīghraprabhāvarasaḥ]
pāradaṃ gandhakaṃ vyoma tīkṣṇaṃ tālaṃ manaḥśilā |
sauvīramañjanaṃ śuddhaṃ vimalaṃ ca samāṃśakam || 16.79 ||
ebhiḥ kajjalikāṃ kṛtvā svalpatailena bharjayet |
granthikaṃ jīrakaṃ citraṃ dīpyakaṃ mustakaṃ viṣam || 16.80 ||
bālāmraṃ bālabilvaṃ ca mocasāraṃ samāṃśakam |
vicūrṇya pūrvavatkalkaṃ tadardhena vinikṣipet || 16.81 ||
punarvimardayedyatnādekarūpaṃ bhavedyathā |
bhāvayetsaptavārāṇi pañcakolakaṣāyataḥ || 16.82 ||
aralutvagrasenāpi daśavārāṇi bhāvayet |
proktena kramayogena raso niṣpadyate hyayam || 16.83 ||
jagdho viśvaghanāmbunā sa hi rasaḥ śīghraprabhāvābhidho niṣkārdhapramito mahāgrahaṇikāroge'tisārāmaye |
ādhmāne grahaṇībhave rucihate vāte ca mandānale mukte cāpi male punaścalamalāśaṅkāsu hikkāsu ca || 16.84 ||

[poṭalīrasaḥ]
kapardatulyaṃ rasagandhakalkaṃ lohaṃ mṛtaṃ ṭaṅkaṇakaṃ ca tulyam |
jayārasenaikadinaṃ vimardya cūrṇena sampiṣya puṭettu bhāṇḍe || 16.85 ||
dadīta tāṃ poṭalikāṃ ca doṣatrayapradhānagrahaṇīnivṛttyai || 16.86 ||

[vahnijvālāvaṭīrasaḥ]
naṣṭapiṣṭau caturmāṣamekaikaṃ rasagaṃdhakau |
abhrakaṃ māṣamānaṃ ca mātuluṃgāmlamarditam || 16.87 ||
śodhitaṃ saptadhā caiva dvimāṣaṃ tryūṣaṇaṃ pṛthak |
triśūlī bhṛṃgacāṅgerī sātalā tīkṣṇaparṇikā || 16.88 ||
śvetāparājitā kanyā matsyākṣī grīṣmasundarā |
kariṇī karṇamoṭī ca rudaṃtī citrakārdrakāt || 16.89 ||
dhustūrakākamācībhyāṃ musalyāśca pṛthagrasaiḥ |
marditaṃ dvipalaiḥ kuryādvaṭikā māṣasaṃmitā || 16.90 ||
grahaṇyāṃ parṇakhaṇḍena vyoṣayuktā niṣevitā |
aruciṃ rājayakṣmāṇaṃ mandāgniṃ sūtikāgadān |
śamayedvaṭikā nāmnā vahnijvāleti gīyate || 16.91 ||

[vajradhararasaḥ]
rasagaṃdhakatāmrābhraṃ kṣārāṃstrīnvaruṇo vṛṣam |
apāmārgasya ca kṣāraṃ lavaṇaṃ dvidvimāṣakam || 16.92 ||
cāṅgeryā hastiśuṃḍyāśca rasaiḥ piṣṭaṃ pacetpuṭe |
bhakṣayitvā tato guñjāṃ grahaṇyāṃ kāṃjikaṃ pibet || 16.93 ||
paktiśūle ca kāse ca mandāgnāvārdrakadravam |
amlapitte ca dhāroṣṇaṃ kṣīraṃ vajradharo hyayam || 16.94 ||

[grahaṇīkapāṭarasaḥ]
raseṃdragandhātiviṣābhayābhraṃ kṣāradvayaṃ mocaraso vacā ca |
jayā ca jaṃbīrarasena piṣṭaṃ piṇḍīkṛtaṃ syādgrahaṇīkapāṭaḥ || 16.95 ||
tasyārdhamāṣaṃ madhunā prabhāte śambukabhasmājyamadhūni lihyāt |
sakṣīriṇījīrakamāṇimanthatīkṣṇāni cādau dadhibhojanaṃ ca || 16.96 ||

[sauvarcalādicūrṇam]
sauvarcalaṃ jīrakayugmadhānyajayāyavānī kaṇanāgaraṃ ca |
kapitthasāreṇa samaṃ pragṛhya dadīta cūrṇaṃ niśi tīvrapittaiḥ || 16.97 ||
gadyāṇamātraṃ madhukhaṇḍayuktaṃ takreṇa yuktaṃ tvarucipraśāntyai |
vātapradhāne ca kaphapradhāne rātrau kaṣāyaṃ kuṭajasya dadyāt || 16.98 ||

[mustādicūrṇam]
mustāvatsakapāṭhāgnivyoṣaprativiṣāviṣam |
dhātakīmocaniryāsaścūtāsthigrahaṇīharam || 16.99 ||
vahniśuṃṭhībiḍaṃ bilvaṃ lavaṇaṃ peṣayetsamam |
pibeduṣṇāṃbhasā cānu vātotthāṃ grahaṇīṃ jayet || 16.100 ||
dagdhaśaṃbūkasiṃdhūtthaṃ tulyaṃ kṣaudreṇa lehayet |
niṣkaikaikaṃ nihantyāśu grahaṇīrogamutkaṭam || 16.101 ||
kṛśānvajājīdvayamākṣikeṇa kaṭutrayeṇāpi yutaṃ tvanuṣṇam |
cāṅgerikājīrakayugmadhānyaṃ dugdhenduśākāya dadīta dadhnā || 16.102 ||

[ajīrṇam]
vireko jaṭhare śūlaṃ vamanaṃ ca muhurmuhuḥ |
hastapādādisaṃkocaḥ sarvājīrṇasya lakṣaṇam || 16.103 ||

[ajīrṇakaṇṭakarasaḥ]
śuddhasūtaṃ viṣaṃ gandhaṃ samaṃ sarvavicūrṇitam |
maricaṃ sarvatulyāṃśaṃ kaṃṭakāryā phaladravaiḥ || 16.104 ||
mardayedbhāvayetsarvamekaviṃśativārakam |
vaṭīṃ guñjātrayīṃ khādetsarvājīrṇapraśāntaye || 16.105 ||
ajīrṇakaṇṭakaḥ so'yaṃ raso hanti viṣūcikām |
vāriṇā tilaparṇyutthamūlaṃ piṣṭvā pibedanu || 16.106 ||

[vidhvaṃsarasaḥ]
vimardya gandhopalaṭaṃkaṇena saṃbhāvya vārānatha saptajātyāḥ |
toyaiḥ phalānāmatha siddhasūto vidhvaṃsanāmā śamano viṣūcyāḥ || 16.107 ||
amuṣya guñjā nava dāpanīyā hantuṃ viṣūcīṃ sitayā sametāḥ |
takraudanaṃ syādiha bhojanāya pathyaṃ ca śākaṃ kila vāstukasya || 16.108 ||

[viṣūcīvijayarasaḥ]
rasagaṃdhaṭaṃkabhasitaṃ samāṃśakaṃ parimardya jātiphalasaptabhāvitam |
sitayopayujya navaraktikonmitaṃ mathitānnabhugvijayate viṣūcikām || 16.109 ||

[agnikumārarasaḥ]
haṃsapādīrasaiḥ siddhaṃ rasagaṃdhakayoḥ palam |
kolaṃ ca viṣacūrṇasya vālukāyaṃtrapācitam || 16.110 ||
śāṇaṃ viṣasyārdhapalaṃ maricasya vimiśrayet |
dīpano'gnikumāro'yaṃ grahaṇyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ || 16.111 ||
savātaśleṣmajānrogānkṣaṇādevāpakarṣati |
sannipātajvaraśvāsakṣayakāsāṃśca nāśayet || 16.112 ||

[vaḍabāgnirasaḥ]
ṭaṃkaṇaṃ maricaṃ tutthaṃ pṛthakkarṣatrayaṃ bhavet |
sundaraṃ dvādaśaṃ niṣkaṃ triṃśanniṣkamayomalam || 16.113 ||
cūrṇānyetāni saṃyojya sthāpayecchuddhabhājane |
śuddhadeho narastasya pānaṃ yadbhojanottaram || 16.114 ||
adyātpathyaṃ tataḥ svalpaṃ tatastāṃbūlabhāgbhavet |
udarāgnirnarasyāsya vaḍavāgnisamo bhavet |
bahunātra kimuktena rasāyanamayaṃ nṛṇām || 16.115 ||
kāntaṃ padmarase ghṛṣṭaṃ puṭapakvaṃ varārase |
mārkavasvarase ghṛṣṭaṃ saptakṛtvastvayomalam || 16.116 ||
śuddhau sūtabalī carācararajaḥ karṣāṃśataḥ kajjalīṃ kṛtvā gopayasā vimardya divasaṃ ruddhvā ca mūṣodare |
siddhaṃ kumbhapuṭe svataśca śiśirā piṣṭā karaṇḍe sthitā syādvaiśvānarapoṭalīti kathitā tīvrāgnidīptipradā || 16.117 ||
ekonaviṃśateścūrṇairmaricānāṃ ghṛtānvitaiḥ |
deyeyaṃ vallamānena vayobalamavekṣyatām || 16.118 ||
giledgalaviśuddhyarthaṃ dadhibhaktamanuttamam |
kavalatrayamānena durgandhodgāraśāntaye || 16.119 ||
madhyaṃdine tato bhojyaṃ ghṛtatakropadaṃśayuk |
rātrau ca payasā sārdhaṃ yadvā rogānusārataḥ || 16.120 ||
vidāhi dvidalaṃ bhūrilavaṇaṃ tailapācitam |
bilvaṃ ca kāravellaṃ ca vṛṃtākaṃ kāṃjikaṃ tyajet || 16.121 ||
iyaṃ hi poṭalī proktā siṅghaṇena mahībhṛtā |
maṃdāgniprabhavāśeṣarogasaṃghātaghātinī || 16.122 ||
siṃghaṇasya vinirdiṣṭā bhairavānaṃdayoginā |
lokanāthoktapoṭalyā upacārā iha smṛtāḥ |
poṭalyo dīpanāḥ snigdhā maṃdāgnau nitarāṃ hitāḥ || 16.123 ||
pītavarṇā gurusnigdhā pṛṣṭhato granthilāmalā |
carācareti sā proktā varāṭī naṃdinā khalu || 16.124 ||
sārdhaniṣkamitā śreṣṭhā madhyamā niṣkamānikā |
pādonaniṣkamānā ca kaniṣṭhātra varāṭikā || 16.125 ||
niṣphalāśca tato nyūnāḥ puṃvarāṭāśca pittalāḥ |
dattvā dattvā guṇānbhūyo vikārānkurvate hi te || 16.126 ||

[vaḍavāmukhī guṭī]
śulbāyoghanabhasmavellahalinīvyoṣāmbunimbacchadaiḥ saṃyuktaiśca haridrayā samalavaiḥ sārdhaṃ saśubhrāmṛtaiḥ |
bhṛṅgāmbhoviṣatindukārdrakarasaiḥ sampiṣya guṃjāmitā saṃśuṣkā vaḍavāmukhīti guṭikā nāmnoditā tārayā || 16.127 ||
kṣipraṃ kṣutparibodhinī khalu matā sarvāmayadhvaṃsinī śleṣmavyādhividhūnanī kasanahṛcchvāsāpahā śūlanutkṣudvaiṣamyaharā ca gulmaśamanī mūlārtimūlaṃkaṣā śophavyādhiharātra kiṃ bahugirā sarvāmayotsādinī || 16.128 ||

[kravyādarasaḥ]
dvipalaṃ gandhakaṃ śuddhaṃ drāvayitvā vinikṣipet |
pāradaṃ palamānena mṛtaśulvāyasaṃ punaḥ || 16.129 ||
tolamānena saṃkṣipya pañcāṅguladale kṣipet |
tato vicūrṇya yatnena nikṣipyāyasabhājane || 16.130 ||
cullyāṃ niveśya yatnena jvālayenmṛduvahninā |
pātramātraṃ hi jaṃbīrarasaṃ samyagvijārayet || 16.131 ||
saṃcūrṇya pañcakolotthaiḥ kaṣāyaiḥ sāmlavetasaiḥ |
bhāvanāḥ khalu kartavyāḥ pañcāśatpramitāstataḥ || 16.132 ||
bhṛṣṭaṭaṃkaṇacūrṇena tulyena saha melayet |
tadardhaṃ kṛṣṇalavaṇaṃ sarvatulyaṃ marīcakam || 16.133 ||
saptadhā bhāvayetpaścāccaṇakakṣāravāriṇā |
tataḥ saṃśoṣya sampiṣya kūpikājaṭhare kṣipet || 16.134 ||
atyarthaṃ gurumāṃsāni gurubhojyānyanekaśaḥ |
bhuktvā ca kaṃṭhaparyantaṃ caturvallamitaṃ rasam || 16.135 ||
paṭvamlatakrasahitaṃ pibettadanupānataḥ |
kṣipraṃ tajjīryate bhuktaṃ jāyate dīpanaṃ punaḥ || 16.136 ||
rasaḥ kravyādanāmāyaṃ prokto manthānabhairavaiḥ |
siṃghaṇakṣoṇipālasya bhūrimāṃsapriyasya ca |
diṣṭo grāmaṃ samāsādya bhairavānandayoginā || 16.137 ||
kuryāddīpanamuddhataṃ ca pacanaṃ duṣṭāmasaṃśoṣaṇaṃ tundasthaulyanibarhaṇaṃ garaharaṃ mūlārtiśūlāpaham |
gulmaplīhavināśanaṃ grahaṇikāvidhvaṃsanaṃ sraṃsanaṃ vātagranthimahodarāpaharaṇaṃ kravyādanāmā rasaḥ || 16.138 ||

[rājaśekharavaṭī]
bhāgo mṛtarasasyaiko vatsanābhāṃśakadvayam |
rasatulyaṃ śivācūrṇaṃ gandhakaṃ tryūṣaṇaṃ tathā || 16.139 ||
vicūrṇyātiprayatnena bhāvayetsaptavāsaram |
tāmbūlapatratoyena svarṇadhustūrajadravaiḥ |
piṣṭvā caṇamitāḥ kuryācchāyāśuṣkāstu golikāḥ || 16.140 ||
uṣṇāṃbhoyutarājaśekharavaṭī mandāgninirṇāśinī nānākāramahājvarārtiśamanī niḥśeṣamūlāpahā |
pāṇḍuvyādhimahodarārtiśamanī śūlāntakṛtpācinī śophaghnī pavanārtināśanapaṭuḥ śleṣmāmayadhvaṃsinī || 16.141 ||

[agnikumārarasaḥ]
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ viṣaṃ gaṃdhaṃ dvikṣāraṃ paṭupañcakam |
daśakaṃ tulyāṃśaṃ bharjitā vijayā navā || 16.142 ||
daśānāṃ tulyabhāgā ca tasyārdhaṃ śigrumūlakam |
tatsarvaṃ vijayādrāvaiḥ śigrucitrakabhṛṃgajaiḥ || 16.143 ||
drāvairdinatrayaṃ mardyaṃ ruddhvā bhāṇḍe pacellaghu |
dīpāgninā tu yāmaikaṃ śuṣkaṃ yāvatsamuddharet || 16.144 ||
saptadhā cārdrakadrāvairbhāvayeccūrṇayedbhiṣak |
dīpako'gnikumāro'yaṃ niṣkaikaṃ madhunā lihet |
pratikarṣaṃ guḍaṃ śuṃṭhī hyanupānaṃ ca dīpanam || 16.145 ||
[amṛtavaṭī]
kuṣṭhagaṃdhaviṣavyomatriphalāpāradaiḥ samaiḥ |
mṛgāmbumarditairmudgamānāmṛtavaṭī śubhā |
ajīrṇaśleṣmavātaghnī dīpanī rucivardhinī || 16.146 ||

[rākṣasanāmā rasaḥ]
tāmraṃ pāradagaṃdhakau trikaṭukaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca sauvarcalaṃ khalle mardya dṛḍhaṃ vidhāya sikatākumbhe'ṣṭayāmaṃ tataḥ |
svinnaṃ tasya ca raktaśākinibhavaṃ kṣāraṃ samaṃ melayetsarvaṃ bhāvitamātuluṃgajarasairnāmnā raso rākṣasaḥ || 16.147 ||
mandāgnau satataṃ dadīta munaye prātaḥ purā śaṃkaraḥ sakhya asmai cyavanāya maṃdahutabhugvaryāya naṣṭaujase |
tenādāya samastalokagurave sūryāya tasmai namo martyānāmapi cāsya dānasamaye guṃjāṣṭakaṃ varjayet || 16.148 ||

[jīvanāmā rasaḥ]
rasagaṃdhau sindhukaṇāṭaṅkaṇamabhayāgnihiyāvalīkatakaphalam |
kramaśa uttaraṃ ca vicūrṇitayā bṛhatīrasasaṃyutabhāvanayā || 16.149 ||
ārdrakahiṃgupunarnavapūticchinnarasaiḥ kramaśastu bhāvanayā |
tatra kalāṃśaviśaṃ ca vimiśraṃ tadrasamānaṃ mānavidhayā || 16.150 ||
sarvamajīrṇaṃ kaphamārutapāṇḍuśophahalīmakakāmalāśūlam |
nāśayate hyudarāgnikaro'yaṃ dīpanajīvananāmarasendraḥ || 16.151 ||

[vaḍavānalarasaḥ]
śulbaṃ tālakagandhakau jalanidheḥ pheno'gnigarbhāśayaḥ kāntāyo lavaṇāni hemapavayo nīlāṃjanaṃ tutthakam |
bhāgo dvādaśako rasasya tu dinaṃ vallyaṃbughṛṣṭaṃ śanaiḥ siddho'yaṃ vaḍavānalo gajapuṭe rogānaśeṣāñjayet || 16.152 ||

[agnijananī vaṭī]
kaṇanāgaragandhakapāradakaṃ garalaṃ maricaṃ samabhāgayutam |
lakucasya rasaiścaṇakapramitā guṭikā janayatyacirādanalam || 16.153 ||

[sarvarogāntakā vaṭī]
śuddhasūtaṃ viṣaṃ gaṃdhamajamodaṃ phalatrayam |
sarjīkṣāraṃ yavakṣāraṃ vahnisaindhavajīrakam || 16.154 ||
sauvarcalaṃ viḍaṅgāni sāmudraṃ tryūṣaṇaṃ samam |
viṣamuṣṭiḥ sarvatulyā jaṃbīrāmlena marditam || 16.155 ||
maricābhāṃ vaṭīṃ khādedvahnimāṃdyapraśāṃtaye |
pathyā śuṇṭhī guḍaṃ cānu palārdhaṃ bhakṣayetsadā || 16.156 ||
agnimāṃdye vaṭī khyātā sarvarogakulāntakā || 16.157 ||

[sāmānyopāyaḥ]
mṛtaṃ tāmraṃ kaṇātulyaṃ cūrṇaṃ kṣaudravimiśritam |
niṣkārdhaṃ bhakṣayennityaṃ naṣṭavahnipradīptaye || 16.158 ||
ārdrakasvarasaḥ kṣaudraṃ palamātraṃ pibedanu |
yatheṣṭaṃ ghṛtamāṃsāśī śakto bhavati pāvakaḥ || 16.159 ||


________________________________________________________


 17
[atha mūtrakṛcchrāśmaryādicikitsanam]
kaṭau kukṣipradeśe ca śūlaṃ prathamato bhavet |
paścādrodho jvalanmūtramaśmarīrogalakṣaṇam || 17.1 ||

[pāṣāṇabhedīrasaḥ]
rasaṃ dviguṇagaṃdhena mardayitvā prayatnataḥ |
vasuḥ punarnavā vāsā śvetā grāhyā prayatnataḥ || 17.2 ||
taddravairbhāvayedenaṃ pratyekaṃ tu dinatrayam |
pakvaṃ mūṣāgataṃ śuṣkaṃ svedayejjalayantrataḥ || 17.3 ||
pāṣāṇabhedī nāmāyaṃ niyuñjītāsya vallayuk |
gopālakarkaṭīdugdhaṃ bhūmyāmalakamūlikā |
kulatthakvāthatoyena piṣṭvā tadanupāyayet || 17.4 ||

[dvitīyaḥ pāṣāṇabhedīrasaḥ]
rasena sitavarṣābhvā rasaṃ dviguṇagaṃdhakam |
ghṛṣṭaṃ pacecca mūṣāyāṃ dvau māṣau tasya bhakṣayet || 17.5 ||
pātālakarkaṭīmūlaṃ kulatthodaiḥ pibedanu |
gokaṇṭakasadābhadrāmūlakvāthaṃ pibenniśi |
ayaṃ pāṣāṇabhinnāmnā rasaḥ pāṣāṇabhedakaḥ || 17.6 ||
gokṣurabījasamutthaṃ cūrṇamavikṣīrasaṃyuktam |
rasavaramiśraṃ pibataścūrṇībhūtvāśmarī patati || 17.7 ||

[trivikramarasaḥ]
mṛtatāmramajākṣīraiḥ pācyaṃ tulyaṃ gate drave |
tattāmraṃ śuddhasūtaṃ ca gaṃdhakaṃ ca samaṃ samam || 17.8 ||
nirguṇḍyutthadravairmardyaṃ dinaṃ tadgolamandhrayet |
yāmaikaṃ vālukāyaṃtre pācyaṃ yojyaṃ dviguñjakam || 17.9 ||
bījapūrasya mūlaṃ tu sajalaṃ cānupāyayet |
rasastrivikramo nāmnā māsaikenāśmarīpraṇut || 17.10 ||

[ānandabhairavīvaṭī]
tilāpāmārgakāṇḍaṃ ca kāravellyā yavasya ca |
palāśakāṣṭhasaṃyuktaṃ sarvaṃ tulyaṃ dahetpuṭe || 17.11 ||
tanniṣkaikamajāmūtrairvaṭīṃ cānandabhairavīm |
pāyayedaśmarīṃ hanti saptāhānnātra saṃśayaḥ || 17.12 ||

[sāmānyopāyaḥ]
pāṇḍuraṃ phalikāmūlaṃ jalenaivāśmarīharam || 17.13 ||
madhunā ca yavakṣāraṃ līḍhaṃ syādaśmarīharam || 17.14 ||
haridrāguḍakarṣaikaṃ cāranālena vā pibet || 17.15 ||
vandhyākarkoṭakīkandaṃ bhakṣya kṣaudraṃ sitāyutam |
aśmarīṃ hanti no citraṃ karṣamātraṃ śivoditam || 17.16 ||

[prameha]
śoṣastāpo'ṅgakārśyaṃ ca bahumūtratvameva ca |
asvāsthyaṃ sarvagātreṣu mūtramehasya lakṣaṇam || 17.17 ||

[guḍamārkaṇḍī]
mārkaṇḍīcūrṇamādāya saguḍaṃ khādayenniśi || 17.18 ||

[laghulokeśvararasaḥ]
mṛtasūtasya bhāgaikaṃ catvāraḥ śuddhagandhakāt |
piṣṭvā varāṭakaṃ tena rasapādaṃ ca ṭaṃkaṇam || 17.19 ||
kṣīraiḥ piṣṭvā mukhaṃ ruddhvā varāṭāṃścāndhrayetpuṭet |
svāṃgaśītaṃ vicūrṇyātha laghulokeśvaro rasaḥ || 17.20 ||
caturguṃjārasaścāyaṃ maricaikonaviṃśatiḥ |
jātimūlapalaikaṃ tu ajākṣīreṇa peṣayet |
śarkarābhāvitaṃ cānu pītvā kṛcchraharaṃ param || 17.21 ||


________________________________________________________


 18
bhedā vandhyābalānāṃ hi navadhā parikīrtitāḥ |
tatrādivandhyā prathamā pāpakarmavinirmitā || 18.1 ||
raktena ca pṛthagdoṣaiḥ samastaiḥ pañcadhā bhavet |
bhūtadevābhicāraiśca tisro vandhyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 18.2 ||
pumānapi bhavedvandhyo doṣairetaiśca śukrataḥ || 18.3 ||
garbhasrāvī smṛtā pūrvaṃ mṛtavatsā dvitīyakā |
tṛtīyā strīprasūtiḥ syātkākavandhyā sakṛtprasūḥ || 18.4 ||

[jayasundararasa]
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ tāmraṃ tāpyasattvaṃ ca vaikṛtam |
ekaikaṃ niṣkamānena saṃśuddhaṃ parimāritam || 18.5 ||
etaccaturguṇaṃ sūtaṃ sūtāddviguṇagandhakam |
mardayellakṣmaṇātoyairbandhujīvarasairapi || 18.6 ||
kācakūpyāṃ tataḥ kṣiptvā tāmrapātraṃ mukhe nyaset |
vilimpedabhitaḥ kūpīmaṅgulotsedhayā mṛdā || 18.7 ||
viśoṣya ca puṭaṃ dadyādbhūmau nikṣipya kūpikām |
gajākhyapuṭaparyāptiḥ śāṇakarṣamitotpalaiḥ || 18.8 ||
svāṃgaśītaṃ vicūrṇyātha bhāvayellakṣmaṇādravaiḥ |
saptavāraṃ viśoṣyātha karaṇḍāntarvinikṣipet || 18.9 ||
aśvagandhārajoyuktaratāmragokṣīrasaṃyutaḥ |
sevito guñjayā tulyaḥ sitayā ca rasottamaḥ || 18.10 ||
māsatrayaprayogeṇa vandhyā bhavati putriṇī |
putriṇyai snānaśuddhāyai jaratkauśikacakṣuṣī || 18.11 ||
gavyājyena ca saṃsādhya tattadānīṃ hi bhojayet |
ṛtāvṛtāvidaṃ deyaṃ yāvanmāsatrayaṃ bhavet || 18.12 ||
rasendraḥ kathitaḥ so'yaṃ campakāraṇyavāsibhiḥ |
pūrṇāmṛtākhyayogīndrairnāmato jayasundaraḥ || 18.13 ||
sevite'sminrase strīṇāṃ na bhavetsūtikāgadaḥ |
bhavetputraśca dīrghāyuḥ paṇḍito bhāgyamaṇḍitaḥ || 18.14 ||

[ratnabhāgottararasa]
vajraṃ marakataṃ padmarāgaṃ puṣpaṃ ca nīlakam |
vaidūryaṃ cātha gomedaṃ mauktikaṃ vidrumaṃ tathā || 18.15 ||
pañcaguñjāmitaṃ sarvaṃ ratnaṃ bhāgottaraṃ param |
tattantroktavidhānena bhasmīkuryātprayatnataḥ || 18.16 ||
sarvasmādaṣṭaguṇitaṃ bhasma vaikrāntasambhavam |
tattulyaṃ tāpyajaṃ bhasma tadvadvimalabhasma ca || 18.17 ||
sarvatastriguṇāṃ tulyāṃ rasagandhakakajjalīm |
sarvamekatra saṃmardya chāgīdugdhena taddvyaham || 18.18 ||
vidhāya parpaṭīṃ yatnātparicūrṇya prayatnataḥ |
vandhyākarkoṭakīparṇakvāthena parimardayet || 18.19 ||
kānanotpalaviṃśatyā puṭetṣoḍaśavārakam |
evaṃ raso viniṣpanno ratnabhāgottarābhidhaḥ || 18.20 ||
mahāvandhyādivandhyānāṃ sarvāsāṃ saṃtatipradaḥ |
devīśāstre vinirdiṣṭaḥ puṃsāṃ vandhyatvaroganut || 18.21 ||
so'yaṃ pācanadīpano rucikaro vṛṣyastathā garbhiṇīsarvavyādhivināśano ratikaraḥ pāṇḍupracaṇḍārtinut |
dhanyo buddhikaraśca putrajananaḥ saubhāgyakṛdyoṣitāṃ nirdoṣaḥ smaramandirāmayaharo yogādaśeṣārtinut || 18.22 ||

[cakribandharasa]
gandhakaḥ palamātraśca pṛthagakṣau śilālakau |
tridinaṃ mardayitvātha vidadhyātkajjalīṃ śubhām || 18.23 ||
viṣāṇākāramūṣāyāṃ kajjalīṃ nikṣipettataḥ |
dvipalasya ca tāmrasya tanmukhe cakrikāṃ nyaset || 18.24 ||
saṃnirudhyātiyatnena saṃdhibandhe viśoṣite |
tataḥ karipuṭārdhena pākaṃ samyakprakalpayet || 18.25 ||
svataḥśītaṃ samuddhṛtya cakrikāṃ paricūrṇayet |
sthagayetkūpikāmadhye vastreṇa parigālitam || 18.26 ||
raso'yaṃ cakrikābandhastattadrogaharauṣadhaiḥ |
dātavyaḥ śūlarogeṣu mūle gulme bhagandare || 18.27 ||
grahaṇyāmagnimāṃdye ca vidradhau jaṭharāmaye |
nāgodare tathaivopaviṣṭake jalakūrmake || 18.28 ||
skandenāmandakṛpayā trilokatrāṇahetave |
